Abstract: Speculative decoding has achieved substantial lossless speedups for LLMs, but remains less effective for large vision-language models (LVLMs), where lightweight drafters struggle to use rich multimodal information. A second limitation is that standard distillation supervises the drafter only along the original training trajectory, without modeling how target predictions shift after the drafter's own proposals. As drafting moves away from this trajectory, the drafter can increasingly disagree with the target, reducing acceptance in later steps. We propose HAWK to address both limitations. HAWK uses representation similarity to select informative target layers and learns how to combine their hidden states. For visual information, it directly provides the drafter with compressed visual hidden states from the target model instead of raw visual tokens, making the visual information easier for a shallow drafter to use. HAWK also trains the drafter to capture how target predictions change after its own proposals, improving its agreement with the target during multi-step drafting. On SmolVLM-256M across ten multimodal benchmarks, HAWK raises average acceptance length from 3.32 to 4.08 and speedup from 2.19x to 2.60x over EAGLE-3 under greedy decoding, and from 2.89 to 3.41 and 1.92x to 2.19x under sampling.

</details>


### [34] [VisionQ: VLM-as-a-Judge Taxonomy, Dataset and Benchmark for Qualitative Analysis in Computer Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00666)
*Vu Dinh Xuan,Duc-Hai Nguyen,Minh-Dung Dao,Vu Quynh Giao,Quang Hong Nguyen,Binh-Son Hua,Barry O'Sullivan,David Murphy,Hoang D. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VisionQ 是首个基于同行评审 CV 对比图构建、将每次判断锚定在明确视觉标准上的基准，并提出标准条件下的视觉判别任务，同时发布了一个 DPO 微调的裁判模型，揭示了当前 VLM 裁判在按标准判别上准确率有限且可靠性随标准差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLM）裁判基准只对整体质量或综合偏好打分，导致裁判可能因错误的视觉理由选对图片而仍被奖励；需要将判断锚定到论文作者声明的具体视觉标准上，以更真实地衡量裁判的理解与判别能力。

Method: 提出标准条件下的视觉判别（criterion-conditioned visual discrimination）任务。构建 VisionQ 语料：收集 1,409 篇 CVPR/ICCV 论文、1,800+ 张经验证的对比图、3,911 个将方法裁剪块与作者视觉声明关联的人工标注数据点；梳理出六轴、51 叶的视觉标准分类体系；设计隐藏方法名、标题和论文身份、按标准报告准确率的评估协议。同时训练 VisionQ-Judge：一个基于对称证据对做 DPO 微调的 Gemma-4-E4B 裁判模型。

Result: 评估 20 个开源与闭源 VLM 裁判，最强模型准确率仅 63.1%（随机基线 32.2%），且可靠性随标准差异显著。VisionQ-Judge 将"末选项预测"降低 7.0 个百分点，并在留出测试集上提升 2.5 个百分点的准确率。

Conclusion: VisionQ 揭示了现有 VLM 裁判在按明确视觉标准做判别时能力有限且不均衡，为更细粒度、可解释的定性比较评估提供了基准、协议与改进示范（VisionQ-Judge）。

Abstract: Qualitative comparison figures are central evidence in computer vision papers, and vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used to judge them. Yet existing benchmarks score only scalar quality or overall preference, so a judge can be rewarded for picking the preferred image for the wrong visual reason. We introduce VisionQ, the first benchmark built from peer-reviewed CV comparison figures that grounds every judgment in a named visual criterion: each question states the criterion, and a judge is credited only when it selects the output the authors identify as best on that criterion. We call this task criterion-conditioned visual discrimination. VisionQ comprises (1) a corpus of 1,409 CVPR and ICCV papers with 1,800+ validated comparison figures and 3,911 hand-annotated data points linking method crops to author-stated visual claims; (2) a six-axis, 51-leaf taxonomy of the visual criteria behind qualitative judgment; (3) a criterion-conditioned evaluation protocol that hides method names, captions, and paper identity and reports accuracy per criterion; and (4) VisionQ-Judge, a DPO-tuned Gemma-4-E4B judge trained on symmetric evidence pairs, which reduces last-option predictions by 7.0pp and improves accuracy by 2.5pp on a held-out test set. Evaluating 20 open- and closed-source VLM judges, we find that the strongest reach only 63.1% accuracy (chance 32.2%) and that reliability varies sharply across criteria. Code: https://github.com/ReML-AI/visionq. Data: https://huggingface.co/datasets/visionq-anon-2026/VisionQ-1k.

</details>


### [35] [Harnessing Vision-Language Models for Perceptual Quality Assessment and Autonomous Content Adjustment in Augmented Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00677)
*Elias Rotondo,Lin Duan,Yanming Xiu,Sangjun Eom,Conrad Li,Maria Gorlatova*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的自动评估与优化框架，用于评估和预测 AR 场景的视觉保真度，并据此构建自动内容调整系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AR 头戴设备受限于场景几何约束、空间抖动和时间不稳定性，优化用户沉浸感与舒适度仍具挑战；而传统用户研究作为 AR 视觉质量评估标准方法，存在成本高、可扩展性差、迭代设计不灵活等瓶颈。

Method: 作者构建 RateAR 基准（跨场景与环境条件的 AR 图像/视频，感知因子信度 ICC(2,5)≥0.90），评估 11 个商用 VLM，提出上下文提示（contextual prompting）策略，并基于此构建自动 AR 内容调整系统，通过 21 名参与者的用户研究验证。

Result: VLM 质量预测与人类主观评分高度相关，Spearman 秩相关系数最高达 0.8695；消融研究表明上下文提示比其他提示策略更贴合人类评分且平衡了复杂线索；超 90% 参与者认为系统提升了虚拟内容的放置与尺寸一致性。

Conclusion: VLM 可作为 AR 视觉质量评估的自动化替代方案，所构建的自动调整系统能有效改善 AR 内容的放置与尺寸一致性，为 AR 应用迭代设计提供了可扩展、灵活的评估手段。

Abstract: Advancements in augmented reality (AR) continue to foster innovative solutions, facilitating novel methodologies within educational systems, healthcare delivery, and risk-mitigation protocols. However, optimizing for end-user immersion and comfort remains challenging, as AR head-mounted displays contend with constrained scene geometry, spatial jitter, and temporal instability. User studies are the standard AR evaluation method for visual quality, but their cost, diminishing scalability, and inflexibility pose bottlenecks during iterative application design. To address this problem, we present an automated framework for AR content evaluation and refinement, built on vision-language models (VLMs), to evaluate and predict the visual fidelity of AR scenes as perceived by users. First, we introduce RateAR, a benchmark of AR images and videos collected across diverse scenes and environmental conditions, with good-to-excellent reliability (ICC(2,5) >= .90) across perceptual factors, including object placement, scale, and shadow consistency. Subsequently, we evaluate eleven commercial VLMs on the crafted benchmark. Results support that VLM-based quality predictions strongly correlate with human subjective judgments, achieving Spearman's rank-order correlations of up to 0.8695. An ablation study further suggests that, compared to other prompting strategies, our contextual prompting yields better alignment with human ratings while balancing introduced complexity cues. Building on these findings, we construct an automated AR content adjustment system and conduct a 21-participant user study. More than 90% of participants found that the system improved placement and size coherence of virtual content.

</details>


### [36] [SemanTok: Predictable Semantic Tokens for Efficient Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00686)
*Mikhail Dereviannykh,Vikram Voleti,Simon Donne,Mallikarjun Byrasandra Ramalinga Reddy,Shimon Vainer,Mark Boss*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SemanTok，一种灵活长度的视频分词器，通过向编码器注入冻结的 DINO 特征、并从每个保留的 token 前缀单独重建这些语义特征，实现了更高的语义对齐与视频保真度，使更小的自回归模型即可匹敌更大规模的对比模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型中场景分词器的选择对保真度和语义至关重要。现有的灵活长度（粗到细）分词器只在解码器早期隐状态上施加 REPA 表示对齐损失，而解码器可以部分依赖其带噪输入来满足该目标，导致语义对齐不足。因此需要一种能更强、更可靠地注入并保持语义的灵活视频分词器。

Method: 提出 SemanTok 灵活视频分词器：将冻结的 DINO 特征输入编码器，并添加轻量级头，从每个保留的 token 前缀（而非仅早期隐状态）单独重建这些 DINO 特征。从而在多个层级上强制语义对齐，同时保持粗到细的灵活长度特性。

Result: SemanTok 在任意自回归模型规模下均实现高语义对齐与视频保真度：201M 参数的 SemanTok AR 模型达到或超过 3.4 倍体量的 VideoFlexTok AR 模型，更大的 SemanTok AR 模型进一步提升保真度。它在分布外类别上保持语义对齐，并在每个噪声水平（包括纯噪声）下为解码器提供更高语义对齐。其在重建与生成两方面均表现良好，短 token 前缀预测成本更低且生成保真度更高，像素细节延后到后续 token。

Conclusion: SemanTok 通过在编码器中注入冻结 DINO 特征并从各 token 前缀直接重建，显著增强了灵活视频分词器的语义对齐能力，在更小模型规模下即可实现与更大模型媲美甚至更优的保真度，验证了语义对齐对视频世界模型可扩展性与生成质量的关键作用。

Abstract: Recent video-based world models pair the scalability of autoregressive (AR) prediction with the visual quality of diffusion models. The choice of scene tokenizer is paramount for the optimal performance of each of these, both in terms of fidelity and semantics. Flexible-length, coarse-to-fine tokenizers yield exactly that: the first coarse tokens carry the clip's global semantics while later tokens further specify details. Existing flexible tokenizers only apply a representation-alignment (REPA) loss on early decoder hidden states, a target the decoder can partly meet from its noised input instead. We introduce SemanTok, a flexible video tokenizer that feeds frozen DINO features into its encoder and adds lightweight heads that reconstruct them from each retained token prefix alone. SemanTok achieves high semantic alignment and video fidelity at every AR model size: a 201M SemanTok AR model matches or beats a VideoFlexTok AR model $3.4\times$ its size, and larger SemanTok AR models further improve fidelity. It keeps semantic alignment on out-of-distribution classes and gives the decoder higher semantic alignment at every noise level, including pure noise. It performs well in both reconstruction and generation, and its short token prefixes are cheaper to predict and give better generation fidelity, with pixel detail deferred to later tokens.

</details>


### [37] [Soundwich: Video Generation with Layered and Controllable Audio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00691)
*Zhuo Ning,AmirHossein Naghi Razlighi,Sagi Polaczek,Daniel Cohen-Or,Ali Mahdavi-Amiri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Soundwich 是一个无需训练（training-free）的框架，将冻结的联合音视频流匹配模型改造为可生成多条同步、可独立编辑音轨（stem）的生成器，并耦合到同一视频上，支持对各音轨进行时间控制、静音、替换与混音。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联合音视频生成模型能合成与视频同步的逼真声音，但通常只输出单一混合音轨，缺乏音源级别的控制，难以满足实际音视频工作流中语音、音乐、音效、环境声作为独立可编辑轨道的需求。

Method: 提出 Soundwich，在冻结的联合音视频流匹配模型之上构建多音轨生成能力：引入共享场景表示以跨音轨传递全局音视频上下文，同时保持音源级分离；并将每个音频 stem 与其对应视觉源之间的跨模态交互进行路由，以提升音视频一致性；支持对各音轨的时序活动进行显式控制。

Result: 实验与人工评测表明，Soundwich 在时序控制、音源分离和自然度方面均有提升，并能在连贯的音视频生成中实现灵活的音源级编辑（如重新定时、静音、替换、重混），各音轨保持与视频同步。

Conclusion: Soundwich 以无需训练的方式将冻结的联合音视频流匹配模型扩展为多音轨、可独立编辑且与视频同步的生成框架，在保持整体连贯性的同时提供了实用的音源级控制，代码已开源。

Abstract: Recent joint audio-video generative models can synthesize realistic videos with synchronized sound, but typically generate audio as a single mixed track. This limits source-level control and differs from practical audiovisual workflows, where speech, music, sound effects, and ambient sounds are represented as separate editable tracks. We introduce Soundwich, a training-free framework that transforms a frozen joint audio-video flow-matching model into a generator of multiple synchronized, independently editable audio stems coupled to a shared video. Soundwich generates separate audio stems with explicit control over their temporal activity. To keep separately generated sounds coherent, we introduce a shared scene representation that communicates global audiovisual context across stems while preserving their source-level separation. We further route cross-modal interactions between each audio stem and its corresponding visual source, improving audiovisual consistency. The resulting stems remain synchronized with the video and can be independently retimed, muted, replaced, or remixed. Experiments and human evaluations show improved temporal control, source separation, and naturalness, while enabling flexible source-level editing within coherent audiovisual generation. Code is available at https://github.com/CodyNing/Soundwich.

</details>


### [38] [Joint Branch-Space Transform Coding for Diffusion Activation Quantization with Classifier-Free Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00930)
*Mingrun Jiang,Yuejia Liu,Zishan Shao,Ting Jiang,Qinsi Wang,Hancheng Ye,Yixiao Wang,Rui-Feng Wang,Kangning Cui,Yixuan Chen,Fan Yang,Xiang Cheng,Hai Li,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对扩散模型后训练量化提出分支空间变换编码，通过离线推导的2x2正交矩阵旋转匹配的CFG（无分类器引导）分支，并进一步提出引导-相关性分支变换（GCBT），联合利用CFG引导方向与跨分支二阶矩，在等速率量化噪声代理下给出无需梯度优化或角度搜索的逐层闭式解，叠加于现有扩散PTQ方法之上可获得统计显著的保真度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型量化虽已开始利用CFG结构，但激活量化仍在条件与无条件坐标上独立进行，未利用跨激活结构；而匹配的CFG激活构成强相关的二维源，在固定比特预算下分支编码基的选择会实质影响量化保真度。

Method: 提出分支空间变换编码，用离线推导的2x2正交矩阵旋转匹配的CFG分支，对模型参数与量化流程改动极小；进一步推导GCBT，联合CFG引导方向和跨分支二阶矩，在等速率量化噪声代理下得到逐层闭式解，不需梯度优化或角度搜索。

Result: 将GCBT叠加在现有扩散PTQ方法上，在大多数评测比较中取得统计显著的保真度增益，且未出现统计显著的性能退化，同时保持底层宿主量化流程不变。

Conclusion: 利用CFG分支间的相关性进行分支空间变换编码，可有效提升扩散模型后训练量化的保真度，且GCBT实现简单、无需优化，兼容现有PTQ流程。

Abstract: Post-training quantization for diffusion models increasingly exploits timestep, feature, and layer structure. While recent work has begun incorporating CFG structure into diffusion quantization, activation quantization still operates independently across conditional and unconditional coordinates, leaving cross-activation structure unexploited. We show that matched CFG activations form a strongly correlated two-dimensional source and that, under a fixed bit budget, the choice of branch coding basis materially affects quantization fidelity. Motivated by this observation, we introduce branch-space transform coding, which rotates matched CFG branches via an offline derived 2x2 orthogonal matrix, requiring minimal modifications to model parameters or the quantization pipeline. We further derive the Guidance-Correlation Branch Transform (GCBT), which jointly incorporates the CFG guidance direction and cross-branch second moments. Under an equal-rate quantization-noise surrogate, GCBT admits a closed-form per-layer solution without gradient optimization or angle search. Applied on top of existing diffusion PTQ methods, GCBT yields statistically significant fidelity gains in most evaluated comparisons with no statistically significant degradation, while leaving the underlying host quantization pipeline unchanged.

</details>

### [39] [On-Device Named-Entity Recognition: A Deployability Study of Accuracy, Cost, Reliability, and Confidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00007)
*Vinay Kumar Chaganti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了在端侧（无 API、低延迟、数据本地化）部署命名实体识别（NER）模型的可行性，比较了经典标注器、双向编码器专家模型与本地运行的生成式大模型三类共九个系统，重点考察准确性、延迟、输出有效性及置信度可信度，发现编码器模型在可部署性上最具优势，生成式模型存在输出无效与过度自信等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NER 在端侧部署需求日益增长（无 API、低延迟、数据不出本地），但实践者真正关心的问题并非排行榜排名，而是：哪个模型可实际部署、如何在没有人工标注的情况下评估模型、以及模型的置信度是否可信。现有文献往往只报告准确率，而忽略了延迟与输出有效性等关键部署指标。

Method: 作者将九个系统按三种范式分类：经典标注器（spaCy）、双向编码器专家模型（GLiNER，166M 至 460M）、本地运行的生成式 LLM（Qwen3-0.6B/1.7B/4B-Instruct，DeepSeek-R1-1.5B/8B），在三个特性不同的数据集上评估。由于 RSS-News 语料无黄金标注，作者用跨族 LLM 裁判组构建了"银标准"，再对照基准黄金标注与全量人工重验证（严格 F1 0.95）衡量其保真度，并考察标签来源对范式排名的影响。除准确率外，还测量延迟与输出有效性；随后刻画 GLiNER 的逐跨度置信度（AUROC、ECE、温度缩放、阈值化、级联路由等）。

Result: 标签来源会反转范式排名：从 LLM 生成的银标准切换到人工黄金标注后，所有编码器模型准确率上升，所有生成式模型准确率下降。单论准确率，4B Instruct LLM 具有竞争力（在干净新闻专线上领先）；但编码器模型的可部署性更优：以 1/9 至 1/24 的参数量达到持平或略逊的准确率，毫秒到秒级延迟，零格式错误输出，而最小的生成式模型在长输入上最高有 27% 的无效输出，该缺陷需靠扩大规模而非输出预算来修复。GLiNER 的逐跨度置信度对正确性排序良好（AUROC 0.76~0.86），但过度自信（ECE 0.24~0.47，温度缩放可减半）；阈值化带来小幅诚实的样本外 F1 提升；全本地的小到大级联相对成本匹配的随机路由仅有适度且依赖语料的增益；置信度与正确性相关，但与新颖性无关。

Conclusion: 端侧 NER 部署中，编码器专家模型（GLiNER）在准确率、延迟与输出有效性之间提供了最佳权衡，是可部署的首选；生成式 LLM 虽在干净数据上准确率有竞争力，但存在输出无效和样本外下降等问题。置信度校准（如温度缩放）可改善但其诚实性收益有限，级联策略的增益依赖于语料。所有指标均可从逐跨度记录离线重算。

Abstract: Named-entity recognition (NER) is increasingly wanted on-device (no API, low latency, data kept local). The practitioner's question is not the leaderboard but which model is deployable, how to evaluate it without human annotation, and whether its confidence can be trusted. We answer these jointly. We place nine systems across three paradigms and 13 M to 8 B parameters: a classical tagger (spaCy), bidirectional-encoder specialists (GLiNER, 166 to 460 M), and generative LLMs run locally (Qwen3-0.6B/1.7B/4B-Instruct, DeepSeek-R1-1.5B/8B), on three datasets of differing character, and report accuracy plus two axes the literature omits: latency and output validity. Because our corpus (RSS-News) had no gold, we built silver gold from a cross-family LLM judge panel, then measured its fidelity against benchmark gold and a full human re-validation of the corpus (strict F1 0.95, an upper bound since the human gold was silver-seeded); gold provenance flips the paradigm ranking, moving from LLM-authored silver to human gold raises every encoder and lowers every generative model. On accuracy alone a 4 B instruct LLM is competitive (it leads on clean newswire), so the encoder's case is deployability: it matches or slightly trails at one-ninth to one-twenty-fourth the size, at millisecond-to-second latency, with zero malformed output, while the smallest generative models emit up to 27% invalid output on long inputs, a failure fixed by scale, not output budget. We then characterize GLiNER's per-span confidence: it ranks correctness well (AUROC 0.76 to 0.86) but is overconfident (ECE 0.24 to 0.47, halved by temperature scaling); thresholding gives a small honest out-of-sample F1 gain; an all-local small-to-large cascade gives a modest, corpus-dependent gain over cost-matched random routing; and confidence tracks correctness but not novelty. Every number recomputes offline from per-span records.

</details>


### [40] [SCM-based Fairness and Faithful Explainability for Legal Document Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00045)
*Yasmina El Kacemi,Seyed Sahand Mohammadi Ziabari,Ali Mohammed Mansoor Alsahag*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究法律文本分类模型（LegalBERT）中公平性去偏干预与解释忠实度之间的关系，发现二者存在解离：去偏干预虽持续降低SHAP解释充分性，但并不改变人口统计学公平性与分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律决策支持中，模型公平性与解释透明性通常被分开评估，导致一个关键问题悬而未决：改变公平性的去偏干预是否也会改变解释忠实反映模型推理的程度。作者旨在厘清公平性与解释忠实度之间的关联。

Method: 在LexGLUE的ECtHR alleged-violations语料上，对比LegalBERT基线与一个公平性正则化变体（微调时惩罚关于温暖度与能力度的刻板印象表征）。覆盖五个随机种子，评估预测性能、人口统计学公平性及SHAP解释忠实度（充分性，三个阈值），并采用常规词对控制与打乱词对控制进行消融。

Result: 在性能最优正则化强度下，去偏干预未降低人口统计学差异，此零结果在两种公平性定义及性别轴词对控制下均成立；分类性能基本不变。但干预在全部五个种子与三个阈值下持续降低解释充分性。打乱词对控制复现了该降质，同时保持性能与公平性不变，说明效应源于对比式表征正则化本身，而非温暖度/能力度结构。

Conclusion: 公平性与解释忠实度之间存在解离：解释行为的变化并不必然意味着公平性变化，因此公平性必须被直接评估，不能以解释变化作为公平性变化的代理指标。

Abstract: Transformer models such as LegalBERT are increasingly used in legal decision support, raising concerns about both fairness and the transparency of model explanations. These properties are usually evaluated separately, leaving open whether a debiasing intervention that changes fairness also changes how faithfully explanations reflect model reasoning. This study investigates that relationship on the ECtHR alleged-violations corpus from LexGLUE. It compares a LegalBERT baseline with a fairness-regularized variant that penalizes stereotypical warmth and competence representations during fine-tuning. The evaluation covers predictive performance, demographic fairness, and SHAP explanation faithfulness across five random seeds. At the performance-optimal regularization strength, the intervention does not reduce demographic disparity. This null result holds across two fairness definitions and a conventional word-pair control on the gender axis. Classification performance is largely unchanged. However, the intervention consistently degrades explanation sufficiency across all five seeds and three thresholds. A shuffled-pair control reproduces this degradation while leaving performance and fairness unchanged, indicating that the effect arises from contrastive representational regularization rather than specifically from the warmth and competence structure. The results demonstrate a dissociation between fairness and explanation faithfulness: changes in explanation behavior do not necessarily indicate changes in fairness, and fairness must therefore be evaluated directly.

</details>


### [41] [The First Token Is Not the Verdict: Hidden Costs of Reading LLM Judges Without Generating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00054)
*Gnaneswar Villuri,Hashmath Shaik,Alex Doboli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出：从LLM法官首个生成token的logits直接读取判决（无需生成）会系统性地夸大位置偏差，因其强制性读取在法官未以判决token开头（占3%–49%）的配对上总是返回先展示的回答而非真实判断，导致位置偏差被高估约42个百分点；同时该失真仅影响审计者测得的位置偏差，对法官准确率影响不足1个点。文中建议在报告位置偏差时附报法官"以判决token开头"的比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多评估范式（受限解码、似然评分）从首个生成token的logits直接读取LLM法官的裁决，认为这样成本低、无需生成且结果等价。作者怀疑这种读取方式并未捕捉法官的真实判断，反而可能系统性扭曲位置偏差度量，而位置偏差正是审计LLM法官质量的关键指标。

Method: 作者对多个LLM法官（三个Qwen3模型、Llama-3.1-8B、Phi-3.5-mini）在成对比较任务上进行实验：比较"仅读取首个token"与"完整生成后读取判定"两种方式。统计各法官"未以判决token开头"的比例（Qwen3为12%–49%，另两模型低于3%）；在924个法官未给出裁决token的配对上，交换两回答顺序，测量强制读取与生成后读取的翻转率差异（配对差值+0.422，95% CI [+0.365, +0.467]）；并对比该失真对位置偏差与法官准确率两个指标的影响大小。

Result: 1) 强制读取首token在所有测试条件下都高估位置偏差，故所得数值是上界；2) 在法官未给出裁决token的924个配对上，交换回答顺序时强制读取翻转89.7%，而生成后读取仅翻转47.5%，配对差+0.422（95% CI [+0.365, +0.467]）；3) 该失真影响指标具有特异性：使位置偏差移动42个百分点，而在十之七个条件下使法官准确率仅移动不足1个点，因此误导的是审计者而非使用者；4) 即使法官确实以判决token开头，仍存在次级失效——部分案件先以某字母开头后推理至另一结论，发生率为0–5.5%，且与"合规"率不相关；5) 三位Qwen3法官未以判决token开头的比例为12%–49%，Llama-3.1-8B与Phi-3.5-mini则低于3%。

Conclusion: 从首个token的logits读取LLM法官判决会严重扭曲位置偏差（系统性高估、充当上界），但几乎不影响准确率，因而专门误导审计法官的人。作者建议：在报告任何位置偏差数据时，同时报告法官"以判决token开头"的比例——该指标仅需一次前向传播、无需任何标注即可测得。

Abstract: Reading an LLM judge's verdict from the logits of its first generated token is cheap, requires no generation, and is exactly what constrained decoding and likelihood-scoring evaluation harnesses produce. We show that this readout distorts position bias in one direction: it overstates it in every condition we test, so figures obtained this way behave as upper bounds. The mechanism is that judges do not always lead with a verdict token, on 12% to 49% of pairs for three Qwen3 judges and under 3% for Llama-3.1-8B and Phi-3.5-mini, and forcing a read on those pairs returns whichever response was shown first rather than a judgment. Pooled over the 924 pairs where a judge did not commit, the forced read flips on 89.7% of them when the responses are swapped, against 47.5% read after generation (paired difference +0.422, 95% CI [+0.365, +0.467]). The distortion is specific to what is measured: it moves position bias by 42 points while moving judge accuracy by under one point in seven of ten conditions, so it misleads whoever audits a judge rather than whoever uses one. A second, smaller failure occurs even when the judge does lead with a verdict token, since it sometimes opens with one letter and reasons its way to the other, on 0 to 5.5% of pairs at a rate uncorrelated with compliance. We recommend reporting the rate at which a judge leads with a verdict token, which costs one forward pass and no labels, alongside any position-bias figure.

</details>


### [42] [Measuring Human-Like Bias in LLMs? A Critique of Human-Derived Bias Constructs in LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00070)
*Antonela Tommasel,Markus Schedl*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文批判性地审视了将人类认知与社会偏见构念（如内隐偏见、刻板印象激活、锚定效应、框架效应、确认偏误等）直接迁移到LLM评估中的做法，指出其存在推断鸿沟，并提出了一个厘清目标构念、操作化、推断范围与人类类比界限的分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者日益采用源自人类的偏见构念来研究大语言模型，但这种迁移存在推断上的不匹配：心理测评工具是为研究人类认知与社会行为而设计的，而LLM评估依赖概率、文本补全、排序或模拟决策，二者存在结构性差异。直接套用可能混淆对模型偏见的多种解释，因而需要系统批判并建立规范框架。

Method: 本文采用理论分析与批判性论述的方法。首先梳理人类中心偏见评估在LLM中的应用现状，随后从三个维度剖析推断鸿沟的来源：人类源性构念的不匹配、人机之间的根本差异、以及评估情境的差异。在此基础上提出一个分析性框架，将目标构念、操作化方式、推断范围以及人类类比的局限性关联起来，以界定何种解释是合理的。

Result: 文章指出，人类源性构念与LLM评估方式之间存在系统的推断鸿沟，这种鸿沟会模糊对模型偏见的不同解读；所提出的框架能够帮助研究者厘清评估的目标与操作化之间的对应关系，明确推断的适用范围，并识别人类类比在方法论上的限制。

Conclusion: 将人类偏见构念迁移到LLM评估需要更加谨慎，研究者应当明确构念目标、操作化方式与推断范围之间的联系，并正视人机差异与人类类比的局限；该框架为未来更严谨的LLM偏见评估提供了分析工具。

Abstract: Researchers increasingly use human-derived bias constructs to study Large Language Models (LLMs), including social-cognitive constructs such as implicit bias and stereotype activation, and cognitive biases such as anchoring, framing effects, and confirmation bias. Such approaches offer alternatives to overt bias probes, particularly when direct questioning may obscure bias or when model behaviour appears normatively acceptable. However, adapting human bias constructs to LLMs introduces an inferential gap. Psychological instruments were developed to study human cognition and social behaviour, whereas LLM evaluations rely on probabilities, text completions, rankings, or simulated decisions. This paper critiques human-centered bias evaluation in LLMs. We show how this gap arises from mismatches pertaining to human-derived constructs, human-model differences, and evaluation contexts, which can blur distinct interpretations of model bias. We then introduce a framework providing an analytical lens for relating these elements to warranted interpretations, with attention to target constructs, operationalizations, scope of inference, and limits of human analogy.

</details>


### [43] [Legal text classification in Korean sexual offense cases: from traditional machine learning to large language models with XAI insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00087)
*Jeongmin Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究对比了从传统机器学习到大型语言模型的法律文本分类方法，针对十类韩国性犯罪判例进行评估，发现微调小规模模型 KLUE-BERT 以 99.3% 的准确率超越 GPT-3.5、GPT-4.0 及传统模型，并借助可解释 AI（XAI）分析预测与误分类原因，揭示了领域适应与可解释性在法律 AI 中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律文本复杂且类别间差异细微，使得准确分类法律文档极具挑战；同时，法律 AI 不仅需要高性能，还要求可解释性以提升透明度。因此，本研究旨在探讨模型规模、领域适应与可解释性对法律文本分类的实际影响。

Method: 以十类韩国性犯罪判例为数据集，对比传统机器学习、微调后的小规模模型（如 KLUE-BERT）与通用大语言模型（GPT-3.5、GPT-4.0）的分类性能；利用可解释 AI（XAI）技术分析模型预测、误分类案例及影响决策的语言特征；并使用与真实案件记录高度相似的 KICS 数据评估模型泛化能力。

Result: 微调后的 KLUE-BERT 达到最高准确率 99.3%，优于通用模型 GPT-3.5、GPT-4.0 及传统机器学习模型，表明在法律文档分类中领域适应与微调比模型规模更重要。XAI 分析识别出影响模型决策的语言特征，也暴露了模型在捕捉细微文本线索上的局限；在 KICS 数据上，模型难以解释隐性上下文线索，泛化能力受限。

Conclusion: 法律 AI 中性能与可解释性同样重要，XAI 能提升法律文本分类的透明度；领域适应和微调对法律文档分类至关重要。AI 辅助工具可支持法律专业人员开展文档分类、法律信息检索与案件评估等工作。

Abstract: The advancement of natural language processing (NLP) has expanded AI-based text classification in the legal domain. However, accurately classifying legal documents remains challenging due to the complexity of legal texts and subtle differences between legal categories. This study evaluates legal text classification models ranging from traditional machine learning techniques to large language models (LLMs) using ten categories of Korean sexual offense precedents. The results show that fine-tuning small-scale models such as KLUE-BERT on legal data outperforms general-purpose models such as GPT-3.5 and GPT-4.0, as well as traditional machine learning models. KLUE-BERT achieved the highest accuracy of 99.3%, indicating that domain adaptation and fine-tuning can be more important than model size for legal document classification. We further employ explainable AI (XAI) techniques to analyze model predictions and misclassification cases. XAI analysis identifies linguistic features influencing model decisions and limitations in capturing subtle textual cues. Using KICS data, which closely resembles real-world legal case records, we further evaluate the model's generalization capabilities and find that it struggles to interpret implicit contextual cues. These findings highlight the importance of both performance and interpretability in legal AI and demonstrate how XAI can improve transparency in legal text classification. AI-assisted tools can support legal professionals in tasks including document classification, legal information retrieval, and case assessment.

</details>


### [44] [BudgetSchemaBench: A Budget-Swept Diagnostic for Schema Context in Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00092)
*Chen Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 BudgetSchemaBench，一个基于执行结果的诊断基准，用于研究在结构化数据上运行的数据代理中，模式上下文预算与序列化方式对查询执行准确率的影响；研究发现当检索覆盖受限时，执行准确率对模式预算的敏感性远高于对序列化方式的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型数据目录可能包含多个数据库和数千列，受成本限制，必须远早于上下文窗口耗尽之前，在表覆盖范围与序列化细节之间做出权衡。现有研究缺乏对这种预算受限场景的系统诊断，尤其是缺乏无需人工或 LLM 标注、且能机械地从 gold SQL 派生相关性的评估方法。

Method: 构建 BudgetSchemaBench：从 gold SQL 机械地派生相关性标签，避免人工或 LLM 标注；采用 80 个数据库的池化目录，在固定各检索器表排序的前提下，扫描四种模式上下文预算并比较三种序列化表示；引入源命名空间检查，拒绝那些结果正确但来源数据库错误的查询；覆盖三种评测条件：端到端检索、frozen-gold（保证所需表存在）以及移除所需表的探测实验。在 1,279 条留出问题上对主求解器和来自不同家族的推理模型进行评测。

Result: 对使用原始序列化的主求解器，预算从目录的 2.5% 提升到 50% 时，词法检索下的执行准确率提升 18 个百分点，而稠密检索下仅提升 3 个百分点，因为稠密检索器在最小预算下已能找到大部分所需表。当所需表被移除时，94.6% 的正确预测精确命名了其中一个被移除的表，表明模型从参数化知识中重建了缺失模式。在 frozen-gold 条件下，两种主求解器在三种表示间的差异至多 2 个百分点，最宽的成对 95% 置信区间将差异控制在 +/-4 个百分点以内。另一家族推理模型呈现相同的定性模式。

Conclusion: 当检索覆盖受限时，执行准确率对模式预算比对所测序列化方式更敏感；三种序列化表示在 frozen-gold 条件下差异很小。该诊断基准及其构建、评测代码已公开。

Abstract: Data agents over structured sources must fit database schema into the model's context window. Large catalogs can span many databases and thousands of columns, so cost constraints may require choosing between table coverage and serialization detail well before the context window is full. We introduce BudgetSchemaBench, an execution-grounded diagnostic for this setting. Its construction derives relevance labels mechanically from gold SQL, without human- or LLM-authored ground truth. Using a pooled 80-database catalog, we sweep four schema-context budgets and compare three representations while keeping each retriever's table ranking fixed. A source-namespace check rejects queries that obtain the correct result from the wrong database. The evaluation covers three conditions: end-to-end retrieval; frozen-gold, in which the required tables are guaranteed; and a probe that removes those tables. For the primary solver with raw serialization, raising the budget from 2.5% to 50% of the catalog improves execution accuracy on 1,279 held-out questions by 18 percentage points under lexical retrieval but only 3 under dense retrieval; the dense retriever already finds most required tables at the smallest budget. When the required tables are removed, 94.6% of correct predictions name one of them exactly, consistent with reconstruction of absent schema from parametric knowledge. For the two main solvers in the frozen-gold condition, the three representations differ by at most 2 percentage points, and the widest paired 95% confidence interval bounds the difference within +/-4 points. We observe the same qualitative patterns with one reasoning model from a different family. When retrieval is coverage-limited, execution accuracy is more sensitive to the schema budget than to the tested serializations. The diagnostic and the code used to construct and evaluate it are publicly available.

</details>


### [45] [FACET at WMT 2026 Automated Translation Quality Evaluation Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00096)
*Ahrii Kim,Chanjun Park,Seong-heum Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FACET 是提交给 WMT26 自动翻译质量评估任务的无参考系统，将评估分解为流畅性、准确性和一致性三个通道，每个通道只使用其错误类型所需的上下文，通过三次提示单一固定模型合并错误片段，输出错误片段、质量分数和无错误标签，无需训练组件；其系统排名将人工译后编辑排在首位，一致性通道改变约十分之一的片段分数但几乎不影响排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器翻译中不同类型的错误需要不同的证据：语义是否保留只能对照源文判断，而译文是否通顺、是否一致地指称同一实体仅凭译文即可判断。因此，评估应针对每种错误类型提供其所需的上下文，而非不加以区分地使用全部或错误的上下文。

Method: 提出 FACET，一个无参考的评估框架，将评估分解为流畅性（Fluency）、准确性（Accuracy）和一致性（Consistency）三个通道（pass），每个通道只提供该错误类型所需的上下文（准确性对比源文，流畅性与一致性仅基于译文）。使用单一固定模型被提示三次，合并各通道产生的错误片段（error spans），从而输出三个任务结果：错误片段、质量分数和无错误标签（error-free labels），整个过程不含任何训练组件。此外还提交了省略一致性通道的变体 FACET-C。

Result: 在没有金标准标签的情况下对 FACET 的预测进行了刻画。其系统排名将人工译后编辑（post-edited human translation）排在首位；一致性通道改变了约十分之一的片段分数，但对整体系统排名几乎没有影响。

Conclusion: 按错误类型分解评估并只提供相应上下文的做法是有效的；一致性检查虽会修正部分片段分数，但对系统级排名的贡献有限，可作为一个可选组件。

Abstract: Different error types in machine translation require different evidence. Whether meaning is preserved can be judged only against the source, while whether the target is well-formed, or whether it names one entity consistently, can be judged from the target alone. We present FACET, our reference-free submission to the WMT26 Automated Translation Quality Evaluation Task, which decomposes evaluation into Fluency, Accuracy, and Consistency passes and gives each pass only the context its error type requires. A single fixed model is prompted three times, and the merged error spans yield the three task outputs, error spans, quality scores, and error-free labels, with no trained components. We also submit FACET-C, which omits the Consistency pass. Without gold labels, we characterize the predictions of FACET. Its system rankings place post-edited human translation first, and the Consistency pass changes about a tenth of segment scores while leaving the ranking nearly unchanged.

</details>


### [46] [Metric-Construction Coupling Inflates Measured Synthetic Dialect Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00164)
*Hyojung Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究在韩语方言语料不可再分发的限制下，合成数据能在多大程度上替代真实数据用于训练与评测，并检验这种“恢复程度”是否可独立于合成流程本身来衡量。作者发布 KoDialectBench（跨五地区、三轴、共 1,000 条，以标识符哈希和评分代码形式发布），发现恢复程度强烈依赖评测轴与指标，并揭示共享的构式/评测词表会显著夸大合成数据恢复的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 韩语方言语料虽可用但不可再分发，导致只能发布模型权重、无法复现基于底层数据的训练与评测。作者希望量化合成数据能在多大程度上弥补这一缺口的监督信号，并验证这种恢复能否独立于合成流程加以测量。

Method: 构建 KoDialectBench（1,000 条，覆盖五个地区、三个评测轴），以标识符哈希与评分代码发布，使用户可用自持许可副本重建条目。设计合成数据训练臂并与真实数据对照，测量各方言的相对增益比例。针对生成任务引入已部署的“标记词表”（marker lexicon）指标与基于参考的生成指标。为直接检验构式（construction）访问的影响，设置一个“构式不相交精确形式”臂，在规则中保留 20% 标记类型，并在恒定训练规模（8,600 条）下比较其方言性与地区匹配恢复度。最后通过评测器扫描定义“度量-构式覆盖率”（MCC），考察其与测量恢复度的关系。

Result: 恢复度强烈依赖评测轴：最佳合成臂在地区识别上达到真实数据增益的 91.2%，但在理解轴仅 63.7%。生成轴结果因指标而异：已部署标记词表报告方言性恢复 92.3%、地区匹配恢复 119.3%（后者超过真实数据参照），而基于参考的生成仅 72.4%。作者发现标记指标的评分词表完全包含于转换规则可输出的词表中。构式不相交臂在匹配训练规模（8,600 例）下，将保留标记词表上的方言性恢复从 91.8% 降至 8.1%、地区匹配恢复从 101.9% 降至 25.6%，而三项与流程无关的测量完全不下降。评测器扫描显示，随着 MCC 从 0 升至 1，测得的方言性恢复单调上升。

Conclusion: 合成数据的恢复程度并非单一数值，而是强烈依赖评测轴和所选指标；当构式（训练所用规则）与评测词表共享时，会显著夸大合成数据恢复的估计。作者据此主张评测应与合成流程解耦，以避免循环论证并得到可信的替代性评估。

Abstract: Korean dialect corpora are available but not redistributable: weights may be released, while reproducing training and evaluation from the underlying data cannot be. We ask how much of that supervision synthetic data recovers, and whether that recovery can be measured independently of the synthesis pipeline. We contribute KoDialectBench, 1,000 items across five regions on three axes, released as identifier hashes and scoring code so users reconstruct the items from their own licensed copy. Recovery is strongly axis-dependent: our best synthetic arm reaches 91.2% of the real-data gain on region identification but 63.7% on comprehension. On generation the answer depends on the metric: the deployed marker lexicon reports 92.3% on dialectness and 119.3% on region match, the latter exceeding the real-data reference, whereas reference-based generation reaches 72.4%. We find the marker metrics' scoring inventory is entirely contained in the inventory our transformation rules can emit. We test the effect of construction access directly with an exact-form construction-disjoint arm that withholds 20% of marker types from the rules. At exactly matched training size (8,600 examples) it reduces dialectness recovery from 91.8% to 8.1% and region-match recovery from 101.9% to 25.6% on the held-out marker inventory, while the three pipeline-independent measurements do not fall at all. A complementary evaluator sweep defines metric-construction coverage (MCC) and finds measured dialectness recovery increasing monotonically as overlap rises from MCC=0 to MCC=1. Shared construction and evaluation inventories can therefore substantially inflate estimates of synthetic-data recovery.

</details>


### [47] [When a Data Artifact Isn't a Shortcut: Causal Auditing of Synthetic RLVR Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00202)
*Esther Xin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文审计了RLVR训练数据中的一种构造性偏差：正确选项是人类真实文本，而干扰项全是合成文本，导致“正确性”与“来源”纠缠。作者通过表面统计分类与干预实验发现，这种可检测的artifact在训练中并未被策略模型利用，并发布了以CPU为主的审计协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期多条RLVR数据管线通过掩盖真实语料片段、让语言模型围绕其生成“似是而非的错误答案”来构建数据。这使得正确选项是真实人类文本，而所有干扰项都是合成内容，正确性与来源（provenance）发生纠缠。一个策略原则上可能学到“区分来源”而非“判定正确性”这一捷径，因此需要审计这种可能性。

Method: 作者在GooseReason-0.7M上分两步审计。第一步探测偏差是否可见：仅用五个表面统计特征（不含语义）训练分类器，在315,499个选项上评估。第二步通过干预实验检验模型是否真正利用该信号：构建一个经改写匹配的对照语料（paraphrase-matched control），保持两个实验分支训练集大小一致，在固定预算下各训练一个策略，并比较其“利用差距”（exploitation gap）。

Result: 表面统计分类器整体AUROC为0.562，仅略高于随机；但聚合结果掩盖了子域差异——代码类选项AUROC为0.416，低于随机。人工检查发现原因：代码干扰项实际上是对黄金答案的“单运算符突变”，而非自由书写的替代项，因此两类在构造上几乎相同。干预实验中，利用差距并不偏向未修改数据分支：未修改数据臂为0.021，对照臂为0.027，即在给定预算下，可检测的artifact未被模型利用。

Conclusion: 该解离现象（可检测artifact与未被利用之间的分离）以及特定领域的构造性发现，对策划此类语料的人员具有参考价值。作者将审计作为一套以CPU为主的协议发布。

Abstract: Several recent pipelines build RLVR training data by masking a span of real corpus text and asking a language model to invent plausible wrong answers around it. The correct option is therefore genuine human prose; every distractor is synthetic. Correctness and provenance become entangled, and a policy could in principle learn the second instead of the first. We audit that possibility in GooseReason-0.7M. First we ask whether the asymmetry is visible at all: a classifier reading only five surface statistics (never the meaning) reaches AUROC 0.562 over 315,499 options, barely above chance. The aggregate hides something, though. Code sits at 0.416, below chance, and manual inspection explains why: code distractors turn out to be single-operator mutations of the gold answer rather than freely written alternatives, so the two classes are nearly identical by construction. Detecting a signal is not the same as showing a model uses it, so we then run an intervention. We build a paraphrase-matched control corpus, hold training-set size identical across arms, and train two policies under one fixed budget. The exploitation gap does not favour the unmodified-data arm: 0.021 against 0.027 for the control. Under our budget, in other words, a detectable artifact went unexploited. We think that dissociation, along with the domain-specific construction finding, is worth knowing for anyone curating corpora of this kind, and we release the audit as a mostly CPU-only protocol.

</details>


### [48] [A Holistic Assessment of the Carbon Footprint of Noor, a Very Large Arabic Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00223)
*Imad Lakim,Ebtesam Almazrouei,Ibrahim Abu Alhaol,Merouane Debbah,Julien Launay*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对超大规模阿拉伯语多任务语言模型 Noor（高达 130 亿参数）的全生命周期碳足迹进行整体评估，覆盖数据收集与存储、研发、预训练、未来推理服务及国际合作所需的外部成本，发现推理与外部因素是碳预算的重要组成部分，并探讨减排路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型规模日益增大并广泛普及，其极高的计算资源消耗导致显著碳足迹，引发批评。然而现有机器学习论文中的碳影响报告通常仅局限于训练阶段的计算资源，缺乏对模型全生命周期（含推理、数据、研发及外部成本）的完整评估，难以真实反映超大规模模型的环境总成本。

Method: 本文以 Noor 项目（面向阿拉伯语的多任务语言模型，规模可达 13B 参数，利用零样本泛化实现多样下游任务）为案例，提出一种整体性足迹评估框架。评估范围涵盖从数据收集与存储、研究开发预算、预训练成本、未来推理服务估算到国际合作所需的各类外部成本，对项目整体碳账单进行测算。

Result: 研究发现推理（inference）成本与外部因素（exogenous factors）在总碳预算中占有显著比重，说明仅关注训练阶段会严重低估模型的实际环境影响；整体评估揭示了全生命周期中各环节的碳负担不可忽视。

Conclusion: 对超大规模模型的碳足迹评估应超越单一训练阶段，采用覆盖全生命周期的整体视角。文章最后讨论了降低超大规模模型碳足迹的可行路径，为后续可持续大模型开发提供指导。

Abstract: As ever larger language models grow more ubiquitous, it is crucial to consider their environmental impact. Characterised by extreme size and resource use, recent generations of models have been criticised for their voracious appetite for compute, and thus significant carbon footprint. Although reporting of carbon impact has grown more common in machine learning papers, this reporting is usually limited to compute resources used strictly for training. In this work, we propose a holistic assessment of the footprint of an extreme-scale language model, Noor. Noor is an ongoing project aiming to develop the largest multi-task Arabic language models -- with up to 13B parameters -- leveraging zero-shot generalisation to enable a wide range of downstream tasks via natural language instructions. We assess the total carbon bill of the entire project: starting with data collection and storage costs, including research and development budgets, pretraining costs, future serving estimates, and other exogenous costs necessary for this international cooperation. Notably, we find that inference costs and exogenous factors can have a significant impact on total budget. Finally, we discuss pathways to reduce the carbon footprint of extreme-scale models.

</details>


### [49] [CAVE-Mem: Boundary-Aware Experience Validation for Memory Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00238)
*Xinyu Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CAVE-Mem 是一个免训练框架，将经验表示为带条件（适用性、边界、效用）的类型化干预算子，仅在算子匹配当前基板、答案契约、证据边界和交叉拟合效用时才允许修改基础答案，否则弃权，避免了仅靠相关性复用经验带来的潜在危害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前经验记忆系统主要优化相关性，即检索与当前状态相似的过往搜索经验并注入提示。但相关经验仍可能有害：当记忆基板、问题意图、答案粒度或证据边界发生变化时，盲目复用相关经验会误导答案。

Method: 提出 CAVE-Mem 免训练框架，将经验表示为具有适用性、边界和效用条件的类型化干预算子；先生成基础记忆搜索答案，仅在算子与当前基板、答案契约、证据边界及交叉拟合效用都匹配时才允许修改答案，否则系统弃权。

Result: 在长期对话记忆、多跳问答和长文档叙事推理三个场景上的实验显示，相比仅基于相关性的经验复用，CAVE-Mem 取得了稳定的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过把经验复用从“相关性”升级为“条件化的类型化干预”，CAVE-Mem 有效避免了有害经验注入，并在多种长时记忆任务中一致优于仅相关性的方法。

Abstract: Long-term memory agents increasingly rely on it- erative search and reusable experience to answer questions over large personal, factual, or narrative histories. However, current experience-memory systems largely optimize relevance: they re- trieve past search lessons that appear similar to the current state and inject them into the prompt. A relevant experience can still be harmful when the memory substrate, question intent, answer granularity, or evidence boundary changes. We propose CAVE- Mem, a training-free framework that represents experience as a typed intervention operator with applicability, boundary, and utility conditions. CAVE-Mem first obtains a base memory-search answer, then allows an operator to change it only if the oper- ator matches the current substrate, answer contract, evidence boundary, and cross-fitted utility; otherwise the system abstains. Experiments across long-term conversational memory, multi-hop question answering, and long-document narrative reasoning show consistent gains over relevance-only experience reuse.

</details>


### [50] [Signed Lexical Confidence for Risk-Calibrated Intent Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00262)
*Yezhou Cheng,Zehua Yang,Bojun Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"符号化词汇门"方法，将句子分类器的logit边际与稀疏词汇模型对预测意图的支持相结合，通过有符号的词汇证据增强选择性意图路由的置信度估计，在保证基础分类器预测不变的前提下显著改善了风险-覆盖率性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准置信度分数主要反映基础模型的表征，未能充分利用互补证据来改善选择性意图路由中的不确定性判断。作者希望在不改变基础模型决策的前提下，引入词汇层面的证据来更准确地估计置信度，从而在可靠时行动、不确定时推迟请求。

Method: 提出一种符号化词汇门（signed lexical gate），将句子分类器的logit边际与稀疏词汇模型的证据结合：对词汇一致（与分类器预测意图相符）赋予正证据，对词汇偏向竞争意图（lexically favored competing intent）赋予负证据。随后通过独立的二项校准（binomial calibration）阶段，在指定风险目标下选择操作阈值。

Result: 在BANKING77、CLINC150、HWU64三个数据集上重复10次实验，所提分数相比仅语义学习的门分别将风险-覆盖率曲线下面积（AURC）相对降低15.8%、15.1%和11.8%。在5%标称错误目标下，BANKING77和HWU64的接受覆盖率分别提升1.83和5.14个百分点（CLINC150已接近全覆盖）。在更严格的2%目标下，同时二项校准过程在全部30个数据集-运行组合中产生非空策略。匹配对照显示所提特征优于无符号词汇置信度特征，相对二元一致性特征有数据集依赖性增益。

Conclusion: 所提双特征门为风险校准的意图路由提供了一种紧凑、可解释的置信度增强方法，在保持基础分类器预测不变的同时改善了路由性能，且符号化词汇证据优于无符号词汇置信度和简单的一致性规则。

Abstract: Selective intent routing allows an assistant to act on reliable predictions while deferring uncertain requests. Standard confidence scores primarily reflect the base model's representation, leaving an opportunity to incorporate complementary evidence without changing its decisions. We introduce a signed lexical gate that combines a sentence classifier's logit margin with a sparse lexical model's support for the classifier's predicted intent. By assigning positive evidence to lexical agreement and negative evidence to a lexically favored competing intent, the gate retains more information than either unsigned lexical confidence or a hard agreement rule. An independent binomial calibration stage selects an operating threshold for a specified risk target. Across ten runs on BANKING77, CLINC150, and HWU64, the proposed score reduces area under the risk-coverage curve by 15.8%, 15.1%, and 11.8% relative to a learned semantic-only gate. At a nominal 5% error target, it increases accepted coverage by 1.83 and 5.14 percentage points on BANKING77 and HWU64, while CLINC150 is already near full coverage. At a stricter 2% target, the simultaneous binomial procedure yields a nonempty policy in all 30 dataset-run combinations at the available calibration budgets. Matched controls show that the proposed feature improves average error ranking over the tested unsigned lexical-confidence feature, with dataset-dependent gains over binary agreement. The resulting two-feature gate provides a compact, interpretable confidence enhancement for risk-calibrated intent routing while preserving the base classifier's predictions.

</details>


### [51] [Certainty Is Not Just Correctness: Rethinking Token-Level Certainty in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00296)
*Yunfan Zhou,Ye Zhu,Zhihai Wang,Jianguo Yao,Haibing Guan,Xijun Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文对LLM中token级确定性（certainty）作为正确性代理的有效性进行了受控实证评估，揭示了确定性信息随预测目标、模型、度量指标和token位置而变化，并据此设计了一种在推理时更高效地分配计算资源的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: token级确定性被广泛用作LLM训练和推理中正确性的代理，但现有方法的效果同时取决于确定性分数本身所含信息以及这些分数如何使用，而这两方面缺乏系统的实证检验，导致对确定性预测能力的理解不足。

Method: 作者在多种模型和任务上进行受控实证评估，区分两种预测目标（识别模型更容易答对的问题 vs. 区分同一问题下正确与错误回答），分析确定性在不同token类型、词内位置以及响应中的位置分布规律，并据此提出推理时计算分配策略：在生成早期用确定性分配响应数量，在响应末尾用确定性加权答案投票。

Result: 确定性在识别“模型更可能答对的问题”上表现优于区分“同一问题下正确与错误回答”；确定性随token类型和词内位置系统性变化，反映词和文本形式的局部特性；关于问题难度的信息在生成早期出现，而关于答案正确性的较弱信息集中在末尾。基于此的推理时计算分配方法将总体准确率从78.71%提升至79.54%，同时将生成token成本降低82.4%。

Conclusion: 确定性所提供的决策信息依赖于预测目标、模型、确定性度量以及聚合时纳入的token位置；这一认识具有实际价值，可用于在推理时更有效地分配计算资源，在提升准确率的同时大幅降低成本。

Abstract: Token-level certainty is widely used as a proxy for correctness in LLM training and inference. However, the performance of certainty-based methods depends both on the information in certainty scores and on how those scores are used. We therefore directly assess certainty's predictive ability through controlled empirical evaluations across models and tasks. We distinguish two prediction targets: identifying questions a model is more likely to answer correctly and distinguishing correct from incorrect responses to the same question. In our experiments, certainty is generally better at identifying questions a model is likely to answer correctly than at distinguishing correct from incorrect responses to the same question. Certainty also varies systematically across token types and positions within words, reflecting local properties of words and text form. Information about question difficulty appears early in generation, while the weaker information about answer correctness is more concentrated near the end. These findings show that the information certainty provides for decisions depends on the prediction target, the model, the certainty metric, and which token positions in the response are included in aggregation. We further demonstrate the practical value of these findings for test-time compute. We allocate the number of responses using certainty early in generation and weight answer votes using certainty near the end of each response. Compared with a fixed-sampling majority-voting baseline, this approach increases overall accuracy from 78.71\% to 79.54\% while reducing generated-token cost by 82.4\%.

</details>


### [52] [DuplexSpeechBench-Document Grounding: Benchmarking Document Grounding and Hallucinations in Voice Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00316)
*Puneet Mathur,Nedim Lipka,Zeyu Jin,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 DSB-DG 基准，用于评估语音代理在五个专业领域的文档 grounding 能力，覆盖三种失败模式，并发现级联管线和专有实时系统表现优于开源语音到语音模型，后者存在上下文容量崩塌和多轮 grounding 衰减等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音代理能实现低延迟、自然交互，但其能否忠实地将回答 grounded 到外部文档尚缺乏系统研究。为此需建立一个基准，量化语音代理在不同上下文长度、多轮对话及上下文重注入条件下的文档 grounding 表现。

Method: 构建 DuplexSpeechBench-Document Grounding (DSB-DG) 基准，覆盖五个专业领域、50 篇文档、1,636 个经对抗性验证的问答对。基准针对三种失败模式：Context Saturation（衡量文档长度增加时的 grounding 能力）、Grounding Decay（衡量多轮对话中事实保留）、Proactive Grounding（评估上下文重注入是否缓解对话漂移）。支持全自动评估 grounding 准确率、幻觉和响应延迟，并对比级联式（ASR-LLM-TTS）、专有全双工/实时以及开源 speech2speech 架构。

Result: 不同系统在有效 grounding 容量上差异显著：级联管线（ASR-LLM-TTS）grounding 准确率最高，Gemini-Live 与 GPT-Realtime 紧随其后；开源权重系统表现出独特的失败模式，尤其是上下文容量突然崩塌和多轮 grounding 衰减。总体而言，grounding 保真度随上下文和对话负载增加而下降，失败常表现为无依据的生成而非拒绝回答。

Conclusion: 上下文 grounding 仍是可靠全双工语音代理面临的一个关键未解难题，需要在系统架构与评估方法上进一步改进。

Abstract: Voice agents enable low-latency, natural interaction, yet their ability to faithfully ground responses in external documents remains underexplored. We introduce DuplexSpeechBench-Document Grounding (DSB-DG), a benchmark for evaluating document grounding in voice agents across five professional domains. DSB-DG targets three failure modes: Context Saturation, which measures grounding under increasing document length; Grounding Decay, which measures retention of document facts across multi-turn dialogue; and Proactive Grounding, which evaluates whether context re-injection mitigates conversational drift. The benchmark contains 1,636 adversarially verified QA pairs from 50 documents covering five professional domains, and supports fully automatic evaluation of grounding accuracy, hallucination, and response latency. Across systems spanning cascaded, proprietary full-duplex and real-time, and open-weight speech2speech architectures, we find substantial differences in effective grounding capacity. While cascaded pipeline (ASR-LLM-TTS) achieves the highest grounding accuracy, Gemini-Live and GPT-Realtime closely trail behind. Open-weight systems exhibit distinct failure modes, most notably an abrupt context-capacity collapse and multi-turn grounding decay. More broadly, grounding fidelity degrades with context and conversational load, and failures frequently manifest as unsupported generations rather than abstention. We show that contextual grounding as a key unresolved challenge for reliable full-duplex voice agents.

</details>


### [53] [Refusal Localizes, the Damage Relocates: Safety Layers Under Few-Sample Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00320)
*Jungseob Lee,Dongyub Jude Lee,Sugyeong Eo,Seongtae Hong,Seungyoon Lee,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文测试了针对大语言模型（LLM）微调攻击的两类防御（分层冻结与谱修复）能否抵御自适应攻击，发现攻击者可以通过改变攻击方式绕过由恢复过程定位的区域，并击败跨多个检查点有效的修复方法，从而提出五项防御检查标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的防御工作通过定位安全相关行为到特定层、方向或 token，提出在攻击后修复或恢复拒绝能力。但尚不清楚这些基于成功定位与恢复的防御，在攻击方式发生变化（自适应攻击）时是否仍然有效。

Method: 第一项研究：在来自四个模型家族的六个检查点上，验证有害与良性提示在攻击后仍保持线性可分，并通过注入完整干净的隐藏状态恢复拒绝能力，确定可复现的"过渡深度"；然后基于此将过渡深度以下的所有层冻结，并用一百个有害样本重复攻击，观察拒绝率。第二项研究：移除微调更新中排名前二的奇异方向以恢复拒绝能力，在四个检查点上的短注意-only微调中测试；并在 Llama-3.1-8B 上研究普通训练变化对此修复的削弱以及攻击者分散更新的绕过效果，同时用良性微调校准谱检测器检验其漏检情况。

Result: 在全部六个检查点上，一百个有害样本攻击后拒绝率仍接近于零，恢复过渡深度位于冻结边界之上。在四个检查点上，移除更新前两个奇异方向能在短注意-only微调后恢复拒绝。但在 Llama-3.1-8B 上，普通训练变化会削弱该修复，且攻击者分散更新可使其失效；在良性 Llama 微调上校准的谱检测器漏掉了该检查点上的大多数修复失败。

Conclusion: 攻击者能够绕过由恢复过程识别出的区域，并击败在多个检查点上有效的修复方法，说明基于恢复定位的防御并不稳健。分层冻结仍可在少量有害样本意外混入训练数据时帮助保持拒绝能力。总体上，研究提出了针对自适应微调的五项防御检查标准，以指导未来更稳健的防御设计。

Abstract: Fine-tuning adapts aligned large language models (LLMs) to downstream tasks, but a few dozen harmful examples can remove their refusal of harmful requests. Prior work localizes safety-related behavior to specific layers, directions, and tokens, suggesting targets for protection. We test whether successful localization and recovery support defenses that survive changes in the attack. Across six checkpoints from four model families, harmful and benign prompts remain linearly separable after attack, and patching full clean hidden states into the compromised model restores refusal at a reproducible transition depth. Building on a prior layer-freezing defense, we freeze every layer up to this depth and repeat the attack. At a hundred harmful examples, refusal remains near zero on all six checkpoints, with recovery transitions above the frozen boundary. In a second study, removing the update's top two singular directions restores refusal after short attention-only fine-tunes on four checkpoints. On Llama-3.1-8B, ordinary training changes weaken this repair and an attacker who spreads the update defeats it. A spectral detector calibrated on benign Llama fine-tunes misses most repair failures on that checkpoint. Localized freezing can nevertheless help preserve refusal when a few harmful examples enter training data unintentionally. These results show that an attacker can bypass a region identified by recovery and defeat a repair that works across multiple checkpoints, motivating five checks for defenses against adaptive fine-tuning. Code is available at https://github.com/js-lee-AI/refusal-relocates.

</details>


### [54] [CAST: Cost-Aware Speculative Trees from One-Pass Block Drafters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00321)
*Jungseob Lee,Sugyeong Eo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CAST 是一种成本感知的投机树解码方法，将块草稿器生成的多条候选链打包成树，在单次目标模型前向传播中并行验证，并根据延迟测量动态决定树宽，在不改变目标模型、草稿器权重和解码规则的前提下加速大语言模型推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准投机解码中，块草稿器一次前向传播就能给整块未来 token 打分，但传统解码只验证得分最高的单条链，丢弃了其余已打分候选。这些候选已无需额外草稿成本，验证更多候选项只需增加目标计算，因此存在利用它们提升吞吐/加速的潜力；同时需要一种自动、部署自适应的方式决定验证多宽的树，以避免手工调参。

Method: CAST 将这些已打分的候选打包成一棵验证树，并在单次目标模型前向传播中验证整棵树，同时保持目标模型、草稿器权重和解码规则不变。树宽的确定基于成本感知准则：持续加入新候选，直到下一个候选带来的期望收益不再超过其引入的额外验证时间；该宽度依据一次延迟测量自动适应每个部署，无需对宽度进行穷举调参。

Result: 在三个 GPU 世代、两个模型家族、五个领域上评估，CAST 在其预测宽度下于全部八种设置中均快于标准链式解码，最高加速 43%。研究发现最佳宽度强烈依赖部署环境：在验证成本因核边界跃升时，128 token 树仅比标准链快 2%，而采用预测宽度的树快 20%。同时从理论上证明 CAST 在贪心和采样解码下都不改变目标输出分布。

Conclusion: CAST 通过把块草稿器的多条候选打包成树并以单次目标前向传播验证，有效利用了原本被丢弃的已打分候选，实现了显著加速；其成本感知的宽度选择机制使树宽能按部署自动适配，无需手工调参，且严格保持目标模型输出分布不变，是一种可即插即用的投机解码加速方案。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference by drafting future tokens cheaply and verifying them with the target model in parallel. Block drafters score a whole block of future tokens in one forward pass, yet standard decoding verifies only the top-scoring chain and discards the other candidates. Because these candidates are already scored, verifying more of them adds target computation but no extra drafting. We introduce CAST (Cost-Aware Speculative Trees), which packs these candidates into a tree and verifies it in a single target pass, leaving the target model, drafter weights, and decoding rule untouched. To decide how wide the tree should be, CAST adds candidates while the expected gain from the next one outweighs the verification time it adds. The width therefore adapts to each deployment from a latency measurement, without sweeping over widths. We evaluate CAST across five domains on three GPU generations and two model families. At its predicted width, CAST is faster than the standard chain in all eight settings, by up to 43%. We also find that the best width depends strongly on the deployment. Where verification cost jumps at a kernel boundary, a 128-token tree is only 2% faster than the standard chain, whereas the tree at the predicted width is 20% faster. Furthermore, we prove that CAST leaves the target output distribution unchanged under both greedy and sampled decoding. Code is available at https://github.com/js-lee-AI/CAST.

</details>


### [55] [Dissonant ballerinas and crafty carrots: a comparative multi-modal analysis of Italian brain rot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00402)
*Anca Dinu,Andra-Maria Florescu,Marius Micluta-Campeanu,Stefana-Arina Tabusca,Claudiu Creanga,Andreiana Mihail*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对意大利语与罗马尼亚语的"脑腐"（brain rot）梗视频进行多模态对比分析，探究其吸引力成因及两种语言版本间的语言与文化差异，并发布了手工构建的 CRIB 数据集（240 条 TikTok 视频）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究 TikTok 上"脑腐"梗受欢迎的原因，并揭示意大利语与罗马尼亚语版本在语言和文化层面的差异，弥补该新兴网络现象缺乏系统多模态研究的空白。

Method: 构建一个多模态数据集 CRIB，手工收集 240 条 TikTok 视频并按语言（意大利语、罗马尼亚语）和流行度分层；在文本、声学、视觉三个模态上分别提取特征，进行相关性分析与跨语言对比分析。

Result: 流行度与文本特征（情感、荒诞性、押韵）及声学特征（嗓音特征、声音情感）无显著相关性；罗马尼亚语中视频层面的动态特征（更快的剪辑速度和整体节奏）是成功的强预测因子。跨语言差异显著：意大利语脑腐文本更负面、困惑度更高、押韵更多，音频旋律范围和响度更大；罗马尼亚语音频频谱更明亮、音高变化更不规则。

Conclusion: 脑腐梗的流行主要受视频动态节奏（尤其罗马尼亚语）驱动，而非文本或静态声学特征；意大利语与罗马尼亚语版本在语言和音频特征上存在系统性文化差异。

Abstract: This paper presents a comparative multi-modal analysis of Italian and Romanian brain rot memes, investigating the factors that contribute to its appeal and the linguistic and cultural distinctions between the two versions. To conduct this analysis, we introduce a multi-modal brain rot dataset named CRIB (Collection of Romanian and Italian Brain rot), a manually curated collection of 240 TikTok videos stratified by language (Italian, Romanian) and popularity, on which we examine textual, acoustic, and visual features. Our findings indicate that popularity is not significantly correlated with textual elements like sentiment, absurdity, or rhyme, or acoustic elements such as vocal features or sentiment of the sound. Instead, in Romanian language, video-level dynamics, specifically faster cutting speeds and a more rapid overall pace, are strong predictors of a video's success. The cross-linguistic analysis reveals significant differences. Italian brain rot is textually more negative, exhibits higher perplexity, and uses more rhyme, while its sound is characterized by higher melodic range and loudness. Romanian audio is spectrally brighter with more erratic pitch variations.

</details>


### [56] [LLM-as-a-Judge for Low-Resource Languages: Adapting Ragas and Comparative Ranking for Romanian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00406)
*Claudiu Creanga,Liviu P. Dinu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探索了将"LLM-as-a-Judge"范式应用于低资源语言罗马尼亚语的RAG系统评估，通过适配Ragas框架并引入罗马尼亚语行政文档数据集AdminRo-Eval，比较三种评估方法，发现评估策略需针对具体指标选择，其中Gemini 2.5 Pro为罗马尼亚语自动评估提供了稳健且可迁移的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言（LRLs）的RAG系统评估面临挑战，标准的基于参考的指标效果不佳，需要探索能替代人工评估的自动化方案。作者旨在验证"LLM-as-a-Judge"范式在罗马尼亚语上的可行性，并为自动评估器建立可靠的真值基准。

Method: 作者通过适配Ragas框架并使用下一代模型（Gemini 2.5与Gemini 3）开展工作。首先构建了AdminRo-Eval数据集——由母语者标注的罗马尼亚语行政文档集，作为基准真值。随后比较三种评估方法：直接评分、比较排序和粒度分解，并在Faithfulness、Answer Relevance和Context Relevance三个指标上进行评测，以人类标注为对齐基准。

Result: 结果表明评估策略需按指标选择：粒度分解在Faithfulness上的人类对齐度最高（Gemini 2.5 Pro达到96%），而比较排序在Answer Relevance上表现更好（90%）。此外，轻量级模型在低资源语言的复杂推理上效果不佳，而Gemini 2.5 Pro架构为罗马尼亚语RAG自动评估建立了稳健、可迁移的基线。

Conclusion: "LLM-as-a-Judge"范式对罗马尼亚语等低资源语言的RAG评估具有可行性，但不存在对所有指标通用的最优评估策略，需针对具体指标选用对应方法。Gemini 2.5 Pro可作为可靠的自动评估基线。未来可进一步探索跨语言迁移与更广泛场景的泛化能力。

Abstract: Evaluating Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems remains a challenge for Low-Resource Languages (LRLs), where standard reference-based metrics fall short. This paper investigates the viability of the "LLM-as-a-Judge" paradigm for Romanian by adapting the Ragas framework using next-generation models (Gemini 2.5 and Gemini 3). We introduce AdminRo-Eval, a curated dataset of Romanian administrative documents annotated by native speakers, to serve as a ground truth for benchmarking automated evaluators. We compare three evaluation methodologies - direct scoring, comparative ranking, and granular decomposition - across metrics for Faithfulness, Answer Relevance, and Context Relevance. Our findings reveal that evaluation strategies must be metric-specific: granular decomposition achieves the highest human alignment for Faithfulness (96% with Gemini 2.5 Pro), while comparative ranking outperforms in Answer Relevance (90%). Furthermore, we demonstrate that while lightweight models struggle with complex reasoning in LRLs, the Gemini 2.5 Pro architecture establishes a robust, transferable baseline for automated Romanian RAG evaluation.

</details>


### [57] [UniBuc at SemEval-2024 Task 2: Tailored Prompting with Solar for Clinical NLI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00408)
*Marius Micluta-Campeanu,Claudiu Creanga,Ana-Maria Bucur,Ana Sabina Uban,Liviu P. Dinu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniBuc团队在SemEval 2024 Task 2（临床生物医学自然语言推理安全任务）中，不进行微调、仅使用SOLAR Instruct模型，通过针对临床试验报告（CTR）各章节定制提示词及输入处理，在零样本和少样本设置下取得0.72的一致性得分，排名第14，并在错误分析中发现模型倾向于走捷径、依赖简单启发式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参与SemEval 2024 Task 2安全生物医学自然语言推理（NLI）竞赛，目标是使模型在处理语义保持变化时仍能保持推理结果的一致性。作者不采用微调，转而探索输入处理与定制提示词等低成本方法，以验证大语言模型在该任务上的可行性并分析其失败模式。

Method: 使用SOLAR Instruct模型，不做任何微调；重点放在输入操控与定制化提示（prompting）上。针对临床试验（CTR）的不同章节分别定制提示词，并分别在零样本（zero-shot）与少样本（few-shot）设置下进行实验；随后开展细致的错误分析。

Result: 在零样本和少样本设置下取得0.72的一致性得分，在排行榜上排名第14。错误分析显示模型存在走捷径倾向，尤其在处理语义保持（semantic-preserving）变化时依赖简单启发式规则。

Conclusion: 不微调、仅靠输入处理和按章节定制提示词的方案在任务上表现中等（排名第14），说明提示工程可带来一定收益；但错误分析揭示模型偏好捷径与简单启发式，尤其在语义不变扰动下鲁棒性不足，表明该任务仍需更强的方法来保证推理一致性。

Abstract: This paper describes the approach of the UniBuc team in tackling the SemEval 2024 Task 2: Safe Biomedical Natural Language Inference for Clinical Trials. We used SOLAR Instruct, without any fine-tuning, while focusing on input manipulation and tailored prompting. By customizing prompts for individual CTR sections, in both zero-shot and few-shots settings, we managed to achieve a consistency score of 0.72, ranking 14th in the leaderboard. Our thorough error analysis revealed that our model has a tendency to take shortcuts and rely on simple heuristics, especially when dealing with semantic-preserving changes.

</details>


### [58] [EurekaBench: Measuring Agentic Ability to Discover New Scientific Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00492)
*Jiayi Geng,Zhengxuan Wu,Kevin S. Chen,Seungone Kim,Joseph Janssen,Zora Zhiruo Wang,Bhupalee Kalita,Runtian Gao,Aaron Ho,Andrew Oakleigh Nelson,Olexandr Isayev,Francisco Villaescusa-Navarro,Ching-Yao Lai,Howard Chen,Graham Neubig*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 EurekaBench，一个跨领域长时程科学发现基准，用于评估 AI 智能体通过实验发现解释观测现象的机制的能力。基准含 26 个任务、306 条科学洞察，覆盖神经科学、计算机科学、化学、天体物理、地球物理和等离子体物理。结果显示当前 AI 智能体在预测精度上超过人类科学家，但在派生科学洞察方面显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受牛顿通过迭代分析数据、建立数学方程并反复修正理论从而发现万有引力的启发，作者希望探究 AI 智能体能否进行类似的科学发现。现有评估往往只关注预测精度，缺乏对"能否从发现的机制中推导出科学洞察"这一更高层次能力的系统测量，因此需要构建一个能检验长期实验与机制发现的跨领域基准。

Method: 构建 EurekaBench 基准，包含经专家验证的 26 个长时程任务，横跨神经科学、计算机科学、化学、天体物理、地球物理和等离子体物理，共关联 306 条科学洞察。评估框架沿三个维度测试科学发现能力：能否遵循已知科学约束、所发现机制的预测准确度、以及这些机制能否产生科学洞察或指导未来研究。

Result: 实验表明，当前 AI 智能体过度聚焦于优化预测精度，在这项指标上甚至超越人类科学家；但在派生科学洞察这一维度上表现明显不足。

Conclusion: EurekaBench 揭示了 AI 智能体在科学发现中"高预测精度、低洞察能力"的失衡，为未来提升 AI 从机制中发现真正科学洞见的能力提供了基准与评估手段。

Abstract: When Isaac Newton discovered the law of gravitation, he did so through an iterative process of analyzing observed data such as planetary patterns, finding the underlying mechanisms by describing patterns in mathematical equations, and refining his theory against the Moon's orbit, revealing the startling insight that the same force governs both falling apples and orbiting planets. Would it be possible for AI agents to make similar discoveries? To measure this ability, we introduce EurekaBench, a cross-domain benchmark that tests AI agents' ability to conduct long-horizon experiments and discover mechanisms that explain observations. We evaluate these mechanisms by the scientific insights that can be derived from them. EurekaBench contains an expert-verified set of 26 long-horizon tasks across neuroscience, computer science, chemistry, astrophysics, geophysics, and plasma physics, with a total of 306 scientific insights that the discovered mechanisms are expected to support. Our evaluation framework tests three axes of scientific discovery: agents' ability to follow known scientific constraints, the predictive accuracy of the discovered mechanisms, and whether these mechanisms yield scientific insights or inform future research. Our results show that current AI agents often overly fixate on predictive accuracy optimization, surpassing human scientists, while falling substantially short in deriving scientific insights.

</details>


### [59] [Rules Amortize, Pairings Don't: Linguistic Structure Determines What Latent Task Representations Can Replace In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00526)
*Gunmay Jhingran*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究哪些语言学操作能在零样本推理成本下，从少样本支持集几何中被"摊销"成潜在任务表征。作者训练一个小网络读取支持集几何（质心、主子空间、谱）并生成输入条件的残差流更新，在冻结的GPT-2上测试，发现规则性形态屈折可被完美摊销甚至超越ICL，而任意配对（反义词）无法摊销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近理论表明静态任务向量等价于单个合成演示，无法处理高秩映射（如词级双射）。本文提出一个语言学版本的问题：哪些语言学操作可以从提示中摊销出来？旨在厘清可规则化的语言学操作与需要记忆的任意配对在可摊销性上的本质区别。

Method: 训练一个260万参数的变换网络（transform），读取少样本支持集一次性计算并缓存的几何特征（质心、主子空间、谱），对冻结的GPT-2-large/XL中层查询残差流施加输入条件的加性更新。在八个屈折方向和一种词汇关系上测试，采用规范分割防止方向间倒置对泄漏。对照实验包括：错误任务流形、仅查询变体、留一任务外推、不同容量与规模、种子。

Result: 出现三种机制：(1)正向屈折上，10-shot ICL强（0.67–0.89）而提取的任务向量崩溃（≤0.06），transform以严格零样本单查询成本匹配ICL；(2)词形还原方向上，冻结GPT-2能执行但10个演示系统性无法传达（1.5B模型ICL仅0.13–0.48），transform完全不受ICL上限约束，达0.78–0.92，最高比ICL高72个点（过去时变现在时0.85 vs 0.13）；(3)任意配对（反义词）上，所有摊销器在每种规模、容量和种子上都稳定在ICL的一半左右。对照显示支持流形是因果必要的任务指纹——错误任务流形使准确率崩溃至≤0.06，仅查询变体无法区分共享输入空间的任务，留一任务外推为零。

Conclusion: 可产生的规则（product rules）能摊销进潜在任务表征，有时比提示能传达的更好；而记忆性配对（memorized pairings）不能。支持集几何是因果必要的任务指纹，规则性与记忆性操作的摊销能力存在本质差别。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) can be amortized into latent objects (task vectors, function vectors, context vectors) that recover few-shot behavior at zero-shot inference cost, but recent theory shows a static vector acts as a single synthetic demonstration and must fail on high-rank mappings such as word-level bijections. We ask a linguistic version of this question: which linguistic operations can be amortized out of the prompt? We train a 2.6M-parameter network that reads the geometry of a few-shot support set (centroid, principal subspace, spectrum, computed once and cached) and produces an input-conditioned additive update to the query's residual stream at a mid-depth layer of a frozen GPT-2-large/XL. Across eight inflectional directions and one lexical relation, under a canonical split that bars inverted-pair leakage between directions, three regimes emerge. On forward inflection, where 10-shot ICL is strong (0.67-0.89) and extracted task vectors collapse (<=0.06), the transform matches ICL at strictly zero-shot per-query cost. On lemmatization directions, which frozen GPT-2 can execute but 10 demonstrations systematically fail to convey (ICL 0.13-0.48 at 1.5B), the transform is not capped by ICL at all: it reaches 0.78-0.92, up to +72 points over ICL (past to present: 0.85 vs. 0.13). On arbitrary pairings (antonymy) every amortizer plateaus near half of ICL at every scale, capacity, and seed tested. Controls show the support manifold acts as a causally necessary task fingerprint: wrong-task manifolds collapse accuracy to <=0.06, query-only variants cannot disambiguate tasks sharing an input space, and leave-one-task-out transfer is zero. Productive rules amortize into latent task representations, sometimes better than prompting can convey them; memorized pairings do not.

</details>


### [60] [Assessing the Impact of Language Disparity on Multilingual Linguistic Ability in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00540)
*Zhanyu Chen,Jaap Jumelet*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统考察了六族多语言模型的基座版与后训练版在覆盖101种语言的MultiBLiMP最小对立句法基准上的表现，发现评估范式、后训练与语言资源丰富度之间存在显著交互：后训练会损害语法能力且低资源语言受损最重，但隐藏的语法知识仅在低资源语言上无法通过显式提示或零概率探针恢复，而母语提示则可找回低资源语言的被掩盖能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于多语言模型语法能力的结论因测量方式不同而差异悬殊，但评估范式、后训练与语言资源丰富度三者间的交互关系尚缺乏系统性研究，导致低资源语言能力可能被系统性低估。

Method: 作者选取六族模型的基座版与后训练版，在覆盖101种语言的MultiBLiMP句法最小对立基准上，采用四种评估方法（显式提示、非提示零概率探针、母语提示等）进行对照实验，并分析后训练效应、模型规模与语言资源层级的交互。

Result: 得到三项主要发现：(1) 后训练降低语法能力，其幅度受模型规模不均等缓解，且低资源语言代价最高；(2) 后训练模型仍保留无法通过显式提示表述的语法知识，但这仅在高资源语言中可测，因为低资源场景下基线接近随机、几乎没有可隐藏的知识；(3) 母语提示能恢复低资源语言上被隐藏的能力，说明只有高资源语言可从非提示概率中被直接探测。

Conclusion: 多语言语法评估必须采用语言知情、多范式并行的协议，才能避免系统性低估低资源语言的能力；仅依赖单一评估方法或直接读取非提示概率都会得出有偏结论。

Abstract: Claims about the grammatical competence of multilingual language models vary sharply with how competence is measured, yet the interaction between evaluation paradigm, post-training, and language resource availability has not been systematically examined. We evaluate base and post-trained models from six families on MultiBLiMP, a syntactic minimal-pair benchmark covering 101 languages, using four evaluation methods. We report three principal findings. First, post-training degrades grammatical competence, but the magnitude of this effect is reduced unevenly by model scale, while low-resource languages bear the highest cost. Second, post-trained models retain grammatical knowledge they cannot articulate through explicit prompting, yet this is measurable only in high-resource languages, because near-chance baselines in low-resource settings leave little knowledge to hide. Third, native-language prompting recovers otherwise hidden competence on low-resource languages, demonstrating that only high-resource languages can be probed directly from unprompted probabilities. We conclude that multilingual grammatical evaluation must adopt language-informed, multi-paradigm protocols to avoid systematically underestimating low-resource abilities.

</details>


### [61] [Can LLMs Reason Over Long Horizons? An Empirical Evaluation of Context Strategies for Longitudinal Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00562)
*Taye Akinrele,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz,Subash Neupane,Sudip Mittal,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了五种上下文策略（Full、Recent、Episodic、Semantic、Hybrid）在纵向临床推理任务 MedLoCoMo 上的表现，发现 Episodic 和 Hybrid 策略整体准确率最高且对证据距离更稳健，但高准确率并不保证在面对证据不足的问题时能正确拒绝回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向临床推理要求 LLM 从冗长的病历历史中识别并整合分散的相关证据。尽管长上下文模型能处理更多信息，但"提供更多历史"并不等于让相关证据更易获取或更能提升推理质量。因此需要研究不同的历史信息选择与呈现策略如何影响推理能力。

Method: 在 MedLoCoMo 数据集上，对四种开源权重 LLM 比较五种上下文策略：Full（全部历史）、Recent（仅近期）、Episodic（按片段/episode 组织）、Semantic（语义检索相关证据）和 Hybrid（混合策略）。评估指标包括答案正确性、对查询-证据距离的鲁棒性，以及在前提不受支持的问题上的拒绝回答（abstention）表现。

Result: Episodic 和 Hybrid 策略整体准确率最高；Recent Context 在支持证据距离变远时性能下降最明显，而 Episodic 和 Hybrid 在长距离下仍保持最高准确率。对抗性问题分析表明，在可回答问题上表现强并不等同于在历史不足以支持结论时能正确拒绝回答。

Conclusion: 可靠的纵向推理不仅取决于 LLM 能访问多少历史，更关键地取决于如何选择并呈现相关证据供其推理使用。

Abstract: Longitudinal clinical reasoning requires large language models (LLMs) to identify and integrate relevant evidence distributed across extended patient histories. Although long-context models can process increasingly large amounts of information, providing more history does not necessarily make relevant evidence more accessible or improve reasoning. We compare five context strategies (Full, Recent, Episodic, Semantic, and Hybrid) on MedLoCoMo across four open-weight LLMs, examining answer correctness, robustness to query-evidence distance, and abstention on questions with unsupported premises. Episodic and Hybrid generally achieve the strongest overall accuracy, while Recent Context degrades most as supporting evidence becomes more distant; Episodic and Hybrid maintain the highest accuracy at long distances. Analysis of adversarial questions further shows that strong performance on answerable questions does not necessarily translate to successful abstention when the available history does not support the requested conclusion. These findings show that reliable longitudinal reasoning depends not only on how much history an LLM can access, but critically on how relevant evidence is selected and presented for reasoning.

</details>


### [62] [Emergent Unfaithfulness: How Alignment Training Causes Language Models to Silently Override Task Faithfulness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00568)
*Pardis Sadat Zahraei,Janvijay Singh,Gokhan Tur,Dilek Hakkani-Tur*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大语言模型中“对齐-忠实性”之间的第三种张力：对齐后的模型在处理不安全或敏感内容时会系统性地偏离输入且不披露修改，作者称之为“对齐导致的失实（AIU）”。通过构建 FaithConflict 数据集和双重分类法，研究发现 AIU 随模型规模增大而加剧（逆向缩放律），并在 DPO 后训练阶段最为显著且最隐蔽，提示性缓解手段无法解决，构成能力-对齐-忠实性的三难困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究主要关注“能力-对齐”与“能力-忠实性”两类权衡，而对“对齐-忠实性”之间的冲突研究不足。作者旨在系统揭示并刻画这一被忽视的失败模式：模型因对齐机制而非认知错误，在敏感内容上无声地偏离原始输入。

Method: 1) 提出“对齐导致的失实（AIU）”概念，与由知识或推理错误引起的能力驱动失实相区分；2) 构建受控数据集 FaithConflict，以隔离两类冲突；3) 建立两套互补分类法：行为层面（B1-B8）与思维链推理层面（C0-C6）；4) 跨多模型、多规模及后训练中间检查点进行实验，分析缩放行为与训练阶段影响，并测试提示性缓解策略。

Result: AIU 随模型规模增大而增强，且增速快于能力驱动失实，呈现“逆向缩放律”；中间检查点显示 AIU 在后训练阶段被放大，其中 DPO 阶段两类失实的差距增长最大且最不易被察觉；提示性缓解手段无法消除 AIU。研究由此揭示能力-对齐-忠实性三者间的三难困境。

Conclusion: 对齐会导致模型对不安全或敏感输入的无声失实，且这一现象与规模、后训练（尤其 DPO）密切相关，难以通过提示缓解。因此在 LLM 的设计与评估中必须正视能力-对齐-忠实性三难困境，将 AIU 纳入模型评估体系。

Abstract: Large language models are characterized by three key properties: capability, alignment, and faithfulness. Prior work studies the tradeoffs between capability and alignment, and between capability and faithfulness, but a third tension remains underexplored: the alignment-faithfulness conflict. We show that aligned models systematically deviate from their inputs on unsafe or sensitive content without disclosing the modification, a failure mode we call alignment-induced unfaithfulness (AIU). Unlike capability-driven unfaithfulness, which comes from errors in knowledge or reasoning, this is induced by post-training mechanisms that override adherence to the input. We introduce FaithConflict, a controlled dataset isolating both conflicts, and two complementary taxonomies: behavioral (B1-B8) and chain-of-thought reasoning (C0-C6). Across models, AIU increases with scale and more sharply than capability-driven unfaithfulness, a reverse scaling law; intermediate checkpoints show it is amplified during post-training, with DPO the stage at which the gap both grows most and becomes least visible. Prompting-based mitigation does not resolve it, revealing a capability-alignment-faithfulness trilemma in the design and evaluation of LLMs.

</details>


### [63] [Where's Waldo? Query-language Preference under Cross-lingual Knowledge Disparities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00606)
*Dayeon Ki,Ruochen Zhang,Silviu Cucerzan,Ryen W. White,Ning Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出多语言问答基准Waldo，系统研究大语言模型在跨语言知识差异（知识缺口与知识冲突）下表现出的"查询语言偏好"行为，并探索两种缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益作为跨语言知识密集型信息检索的接口，此前研究虽已发现其存在"查询语言偏好"（倾向于使用与查询相同语言的来源），但大多在跨语言知识等价可得的设定下考察。当不同语言来源对同一事实提供不完整或相互矛盾的描述时，用户获得的信息将取决于模型选择使用的来源，因此这种偏好变得至关重要。

Method: 作者构建了基于维基百科的多语言问答基准Waldo，包含12K个问答对，覆盖两类场景：知识缺口（事实仅在某语言中存在、另一语言缺失）和知识冲突（不同语言版本对同一事实给出矛盾说法）。在五种语言上评估八个模型，考察模型在两类场景下对查询语言的选择行为，并尝试两种缓解策略——针对查询语言偏好相关注意力头进行消融的机制干预方法，以及基于LoRA的训练方法。

Result: 在知识缺口场景下，当某语言版本仅仅缺失相关事实时，模型普遍会使用另一语言的证据，不受查询语言影响；但在知识冲突场景下，模型回答与查询语言文档高度一致，导致语义等价的查询因用户语言不同而产生不同答案。缓解实验显示，注意力头消融与LoRA训练均能降低该偏好，其中LoRA方法将偏好差距最多缩小61.5%。

Conclusion: 查询语言偏好并非在所有跨语言知识差异下都显现：知识缺失时模型能跨语言补全，但知识冲突时会系统性地偏向查询语言，产生语言依赖的不一致回答。针对性的机制干预与LoRA训练可在一定程度上缓解该偏好，为提升大模型跨语言信息检索的公平性与一致性提供了方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models increasingly serve as interfaces for knowledge-intensive information seeking tasks across languages by synthesizing multilingual evidence. Prior work has shown that they often exhibit query-language preference -- the tendency to favor sources written in the language of the query -- but has largely examined this behavior in settings where equivalent knowledge is available across languages. However, this bias becomes consequential when sources in different languages provide incomplete or inconsistent accounts of the same fact, since the information users receive then depends on the sources a model selects to use. To characterize query-language preference under such cross-lingual knowledge disparities, we introduce Waldo, a multilingual Question-Answering (QA) benchmark constructed from Wikipedia. Waldo contains 12K QA pairs targeting knowledge gaps, where a fact is available in one language but absent in another, and knowledge conflicts, where language editions provide conflicting versions of the same fact. Evaluating eight models across five languages, we find that when one language edition merely lacks the relevant fact, models generally use evidence from the other language regardless of the query language. Under conflicting accounts, however, model responses strongly align with the document in the query language, causing semantically equivalent queries to elicit different accounts depending on the user's language. Finally, we explore two different approaches that could mitigate this preference under knowledge conflicts: a mechanistic intervention that ablates attention heads associated with query-language preference, and LoRA-based training, which reduces the preference gap by up to 61.5%.

</details>


### [64] [Explainable Suicide Risk Assessment on Social Media with Multi-Task QLoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00610)
*Xuan Zhong Feng,Geoffrey Martin,Hexin Dong,Yifan Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向 IEEE BigData 2026 可解释自杀风险评估竞赛的系统，基于 Qwen2.5-Instruct 并通过 QLoRA 微调，针对风险分级、证据短语提取和多标签因子识别三项任务采用差异化训练与聚合策略，最终综合得分 0.7738。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释的自杀风险评估不仅需要估计风险严重程度，还要识别支撑语言及帖子中表达的风险因子与保护因子。为此需要同时解决风险分级、证据短语提取和多标签因子识别三个任务，且不同任务的输出结构差异要求针对性设计训练目标与聚合方式。

Method: 采用 Qwen2.5-Instruct 模型，使用 QLoRA（量化低秩适配）和答案掩码的因果语言模型目标进行微调。训练策略按任务定制：三项任务联合训练用于风险分类；任务1a与1b联合训练用于证据提取；任务2单独适配用于因子识别。聚合策略也分别设计：对32B与72B模型的风险概率取平均，通过跨折共识合并证据短语，并基于样本外工作点进行比率匹配来校准因子级决策。

Result: 官方排行榜上系统综合得分为 0.7738，其中任务1为 0.8089、任务2为 0.6919。消融显示：三任务联合训练对任务1a最优，任务1a与1b联合训练对任务1b最优，任务专用训练对任务2最优；当组件模型误差互补时，概率平均进一步提升任务1a表现。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在统一语言模型框架内，针对各任务输出结构量身定制训练目标与聚合策略具有重要价值，能有效提升可解释自杀风险评估中各子任务的性能。

Abstract: Explainable suicide-risk assessment requires models not only to estimate risk severity, but also to identify supporting language and the risk and protective factors expressed in a post. We present our system for the IEEE BigData 2026 Cup on Explainable Suicide Risk Assessment on Social Media, which addresses three tasks: risk-level classification, evidence phrase extraction, and multi-label factor identification. Our approach adapts Qwen2.5-Instruct models using quantized low-rank adaptation (QLoRA) and an answer-masked causal language-model objective. We jointly train across all three tasks for risk classification, jointly train on Tasks~1a and 1b for evidence extraction, and adapt Task~2 separately for factor identification. We also tailor aggregation to each output: we average risk-level probabilities from the 32B and 72B models, combine evidence phrases through cross-fold consensus, and calibrate factor-specific decisions through rate matching based on out-of-fold operating points. On the official leaderboard, the final system achieved a composite score of 0.7738, with 0.8089 on Task~1 and 0.6919 on Task~2. Across the evaluated configurations, three-task training performed best for Task~1a, joint training on Tasks~1a and 1b performed best for Task~1b, and task-specific training performed best for Task~2. Probability averaging further improved Task~1a when component models had complementary errors. These findings highlight the value of tailoring both training objectives and aggregation strategies to the output structure of each task within a unified language-model framework.

</details>


### [65] [Mixture of Decoders for Diverse Dialog Response Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00621)
*Wenchao Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在条件变分自编码器（CVAE）框架下，将多个解码器组成的混合模型引入序列到序列对话生成，使每个解码器学习专门的主题，从而提升回复的多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列到序列模型在对话回复生成中存在多样性低的问题，作者假设其根源在于模型倾向于学习一个退化的单模态回复分布。因此希望通过混合建模来捕捉多模态数据分布，改善生成多样性。

Method: 在条件变分自编码器（CVAE）框架下，引入多个解码器（mixture of decoders）构成混合模型，并让每个解码器针对特定主题进行专门化学习，从而对回复的多模态分布进行建模。

Result: 在开放域聊天语料上的实验表明，该方法在量化指标和人工评估两方面均优于强基线模型，有效提升了生成回复的多样性。

Conclusion: 通过将混合解码器融入CVAE并让各解码器学习专门主题，能够缓解序列到序列模型学习退化单模态分布的问题，从而显著提高对话回复生成的多样性。

Abstract: Mixture modeling is a long established machine learning technique for learning large sets of multi-modal data. While it is known that sequence-to-sequence models for dialog response generation suffer from the problem of low diversity, we hypothesize that it is because sequence-to-sequence models tend to learn a degenerate uni-modal distribution of responses. We then propose to incorporate a mixture of decoders into sequence-to-sequence models and try to make each decoder learn specialized topics in order to improve the diversity of generated responses. Our model is developed under the framework of conditional variational autoencoder (CVAE). We evaluate our approach on an open domain chat corpus and show improvement over strong baselines in quantitative measures and human evaluation.

</details>


### [66] [Self-Evolving Coding Rules for AI Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00650)
*Zhengyuan Jiang,Reachal Wang,Yuepeng Hu,Yupu Wang,Yuqi Jia,Neil Zhenqiang Gong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 RuleEvolve，一个让编码规则自我演化的框架，通过迭代变异与择优，自动改进 AI 编码代理的规则，从而提升其性能并降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 编码代理的性能高度依赖其编码规则，但现有规则通常是人工手工编写且固定不变，既费时费力又往往不是最优解。因此需要一种能自动、持续改进编码规则的方法。

Method: RuleEvolve 维护一个候选编码规则池并迭代优化：每轮迭代中，先用 LLM 驱动的变异器（mutator）从现有候选规则生成变体，再由评判器（judge）评估这些变体，并用表现最好的规则更新规则池。

Result: 在两个编码代理框架、四个骨干 LLM 和三个基准上的广泛评测表明，RuleEvolve 在生成代码的功能正确性、代码长度和/或生成成本（如 token 使用量）方面均优于人工工程和现有的 prompt 优化基线。

Conclusion: RuleEvolve 证明通过自我演化自动优化编码规则是可行且有效的，能够替代人工手工编写规则，在提升编码质量与功能正确性的同时控制甚至降低生成成本。

Abstract: The performance of AI coding agents is highly dependent on their underlying coding rules. However, existing coding rules are typically hand-crafted and fixed, making the process labor-intensive and often suboptimal. In this work, we propose RuleEvolve, a self-evolving framework for coding rules. RuleEvolve maintains a pool of candidate coding rules and iteratively improves them.
