In each iteration, it employs an LLM-powered mutator module to generate variants from existing candidates, and then uses a judge module to evaluate these variants and update the pool with the best-performing ones. Extensive evaluations across two coding-agent frameworks, four backbone LLMs, and three benchmarks demonstrate that RuleEvolve outperforms both manual engineering and existing prompt optimization baselines in terms of functional correctness of the generated code, code length, and/or generation cost (e.g., tokens used).

</details>


### [67] [Lingtai: What Concept Geometry Reveals--and Does Not Reveal--About LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00656)
*Jiangang Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Lingtai，一种免训练的概念遥测层，在自回归推理的每一步将残差状态投影到命名概念锚点上，生成结构化的逐步概念坐标信号。该信号与预测不确定性稳健相关，并展现出任务条件化的活动几何与执行特异性的轨迹身份特性，且计算开销极低、生成token不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察大语言模型在自回归推理过程中实际计算了什么，在当前仍然困难——尤其是希望在线进行、且不训练探针。现有方法往往依赖标注概念示例、结果标签、梯度拟合或激活空间优化，作者希望摆脱这些依赖，获得无需训练的、可解释的模型内部计算信号。

Method: 提出 Lingtai 概念遥测层：在每一步生成时，将残差状态投影到一个领域特定的命名概念锚点库上。锚点库的构建不需要标注概念示例、结果标签、梯度拟合或激活空间优化。由此产生结构化的逐步概念坐标信号。对照方法包括匹配的随机锚点、K-means 与 PCA 投影，以及 completion-only 弹性对齐用于轨迹检索实验。

Result: 该概念坐标信号与预测不确定性稳健相关：关联在控制问题身份与token位置后依然存在，不归因于单一token类型，无法用 GSM8K 上的简单对/错混合解释，且无法被匹配随机锚点复现；K-means 与 PCA 投影中的关联显著更弱或方向不一致。呈现出两种结构：一是与不确定性相关的重复活动信号，其功能几何是任务条件化的（在 HumanEval、MBPP、GSM8K 上呈现不同的活动熵形态）；二是执行特异性的轨迹身份，具有强局部惯性但弱再实例化不变性——在匹配重执行子集上，completion 的 k=32（约占补全中位四分之一）处进行弹性对齐损坏，仍能以 62.0% 检索到归档片段，而全新执行仅能检索到 11.7–16.0%。此外，匹配审计未发现所用的标量概念活动信号在测试协议下提供稳定的正确性坐标，故正确性被视为外部给定。161 个锚点的代码实现中，遥测仅增加 0.7–1.6% 的逐token解码开销，且生成token不变。

Conclusion: Lingtai 提供了一种免训练、在线、结构化的概念遥测手段，能在不改变生成行为的前提下揭示语言模型推理过程中与不确定性相关且任务条件化的内部活动结构与轨迹身份，为可解释性研究提供了低成本工具。

Abstract: Observing what a large language model computes during autoregressive inference--online and without training probes--remains difficult. We introduce Lingtai, a training-free concept telemetry layer: at each generation step, residual states are projected onto a domain-specific bank of named concept anchors, constructed without labeled concept examples, outcome labels, gradient fitting, or activation-space optimization, producing a structured per-step concept-coordinate signal. Across code generation and grade-school mathematical reasoning, this signal exhibits a robust association with predictive uncertainty: the association survives problem-identity and token-position controls and is not attributable to a single token type, is not explained by a simple correct/incorrect mixture on GSM8K, and is not reproduced by matched random anchors; it is markedly weaker or direction-inconsistent in K-means and PCA projections. Two structures emerge: a recurring uncertainty-linked activity signal whose functional geometry is task-conditioned (distinct activity-entropy shapes on HumanEval, MBPP, and GSM8K), and an execution-specific trajectory identity with strong local inertia but weak re-instantiation invariance--under completion-only elastic alignment, corruption at k=32 (approximately a median quarter of the completion) on the matched re-execution subset still retrieves the archived episode at 62.0%, while a fresh execution retrieves it only 11.7-16.0% of the time. Finally, a matched audit finds no evidence that the scalar concept-activity signal used here supplies a stable correctness coordinate under the tested protocol; we therefore treat correctness as externally supplied. Telemetry adds 0.7-1.6% per-token decode overhead for the 161-anchor code implementation, with unchanged generated tokens.

</details>


### [68] [PhysicsMate: A Curriculum-Grounded Bengali Benchmark for Secondary Physics QA with Small-Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00664)
*Rashid Azraf Jahin,Saadman Sajid,Khan Raiyan Ibne Reza,Sumaiya Tabassum Nimi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 PhysicsMate，一个基于孟加拉国 NCTB 九年级至十年级物理课程构建的 STEM 问答基准（1834 个问答对）与多关系知识图谱，并通过对 0.6B、1.7B、4B 模型进行 Low-Rank Adaptation（LoRA）微调，显著提升了闭卷答题准确率，同时将 4B 模型量化为可离线部署的小型二进制文件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉国中学教育缺少与课程大纲对齐的 STEM 问答基准，而通用语言模型难以处理物理问题所要求的精确术语、单位规范和推导过程。

Method: 依据 NCTB 九年级至十年级物理大纲构建 1834 个问答对，并建立包含 1760 个节点、2600 条边、十种本体类型的多关系知识图谱；采用统一流程对 0.6B、1.7B、4B 参数模型进行 Low-Rank Adaptation（LoRA）微调，并对 4B 模型进行量化以支持离线本地推理。

Result: 三个规模的模型闭卷准确率分别提升 +5.5、+15.0 和 +23.3 个百分点；节点类型分析显示，结构化课程知识（物理量与命名定律）从适配中获益最大，而松散规定的实体级知识获益最小。

Conclusion: 经适配并量化后的 4B 模型可作为小型离线二进制运行于资源受限环境，为连通性和硬件条件有限的场景提供了可行的、与课程对齐的物理学习支持路径。

Abstract: Bengali secondary education lacks curriculum-grounded benchmarks for STEM question-solving, and general-purpose language models struggle with the precise terminology, unit conventions, and derivations that physics problems demand. We introduce PhysicsMate, a benchmark of 1834 question-answer pairs built from the National Curriculum and Textbook Board (NCTB) Grade 9-10 physics syllabus and grounded in a multi-relational knowledge graph of 1760 nodes and 2600 edges across ten ontological types. We Low-Rank Adapt at 0.6B, 1.7B, and 4B parameters, with a unified recipe and demonstrate a significant increase in closed-book accuracy in all scales (+5.5, +15.0, and +23.3 percentage points). A node-type analysis shows that the most benefited by adaptation is the structured curricular knowledge, which consists of physical quantities and named laws, while the least benefited is the loosely specified entity-level knowledge. The 4B model has been adapted and quantized to a small offline binary that can be used for local inference in resource constrained environments and offers a viable path to curriculum aligned physics support in environments with limited connectivity and hardware.

</details>


### [69] [Closing the Loop: Practical Training Recipes for Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00673)
*Andrei Marchenko,Viacheslav Bezrukov,Oleg Kashurin,Inessa Fedorova,Dmitry Bocharov,Yuliana Shakhvalieva,Maria Tikhonova,Valerii Ternovskii*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出实用的循环语言模型（Looped LLM）训练方案，将从零训练的预算从7.7T tokens降至310B tokens，并证明循环结构本身在相同数据与算力下优于稠密模型，同时给出将现成稠密模型低成本转换为循环模型的极简配方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型通过反复应用共享层块来增加有效深度，但现有大规模方案需要数万亿tokens的多阶段训练，且循环带来的收益难以与数据和训练差异分离。作者旨在建立实用的循环语言模型训练配方，降低成本并隔离循环结构本身的增益。

Method: 作者开发了三条路径：(1) 从零训练的算力高效管线，结合预训练加高质量中期训练、学习率预热和更强的出口门正则化，实现无多阶段调度的稳定循环训练；(2) 在受控对比下训练1.4B LoopLM，与参数量匹配、相同数据和token预算的稠密模型比较；(3) 提出将预训练稠密模型转换为循环模型的极简配方：单一可学习的输入混合标量和平滑的出口损失，无需步级专用参数，并应用于Qwen3-1.7B-Base。

Result: 从零训练预算从7.7T降至310B tokens并保持推理性能；1.4B LoopLM在全部12个基准上超过同参数量稠密模型，GSM8K +14、MATH +10、DROP +22；在匹配推理算力下逼近3.9B稠密模型，仅用36%参数；Looped Qwen在两种数据机制下所有基准均优于继续训练的稠密基线，在精选混合数据上GSM8K、MATH、MMLU-Pro有显著提升。

Conclusion: 这些结果使循环语言模型从零训练大幅降本，并能实际引入现有预训练checkpoint，同时隔离了循环结构本身带来的增益，证明了循环模型在效率与性能上的实用价值。

Abstract: Looped language models increase effective depth by repeatedly applying a shared block of layers, but existing large-scale recipes require multi-stage training over trillions of tokens, while the benefits of recurrence remain difficult to separate from differences in data and training. In this work, we establish practical training recipes for looped language models, with three main results. (1) We develop a compute-efficient from-scratch pipeline that reduces the training budget from 7.7T tokens in Ouro to 310B tokens while retaining strong reasoning performance. Pretraining followed by high-quality mid-training, together with learning-rate warmup and stronger exit-gate regularization, enables stable recurrent training without prior multi-stage schedules. (2) Under controlled comparisons, our 1.4B LoopLM outperforms a parameter-matched dense model trained on the same data and token budget on all 12 evaluated benchmarks, including +14 points on GSM8K, +10 on MATH, and +22 on DROP. At matched inference compute, it approaches a 3.9B dense model on mathematical reasoning and reading comprehension while using only 36\% as many parameters. (3) We introduce a minimal recipe for converting pretrained dense models into looped ones: a single learned input-mixing scalar and a smoothed exit loss, with no step-specific parameters. Applied to Qwen3-1.7B-Base, Looped Qwen improves over an identically continued dense baseline on every evaluated benchmark across two data regimes, with statistically clear gains on GSM8K, MATH, and MMLU-Pro on the curated mixture. Together, these results make looped language models substantially cheaper to train from scratch and practical to introduce into existing pretrained checkpoints, while isolating the gains due to recurrence itself.

</details>


### [70] [How Divergence Becomes Decision Flips in Compressed Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00694)
*Beatriz Almeida Felicio*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 总变差（TV）距离能直接预测压缩语言模型相对稠密模型的决策变化率（arg-max token 翻转率），比率中位数为 1.05，无需拟合常数；而 KL 散度需先开方且乘上一个跨模型/语料波动四倍的因子才能转换成翻转率，因此容易误判两种压缩方式的相对影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压缩报告通常用 KL 散度度量压缩模型偏离稠密模型的程度，但实际部署（如依赖稠密模型输出的系统）真正关心的是有多少决策发生了改变。作者指出 KL 无法直接回答这一问题，需要一个能直接对应决策变化率的度量。

Method: 在 19 个开源模型、802 个压缩/扰动副本、5 个语料库及 9 类机械无关的扰动家族上，测量 arg-max token 翻转率，并考察其与总变差、KL 散度、Hellinger 距离等度量的关系；进行两项预注册检验（留出代码语料、新模型真实内核），并在 vLLM 投机解码中验证 TV 对贪婪草稿接受率的预测能力。

Result: 翻转率与总变差的比率中位数为 1.05，且无拟合常数；KL 仅在开方并乘以跨模型/语料波动四倍的因子后才能转换为翻转率。当两种压缩方式的翻转率相差至少 10% 时，KL 在 11% 的案例中给更小散度的反而是改变决策更多的一方，而 TV 仅 1%。在两个预注册测试中：留出代码语料上 TV 比率对全部 8 个模型成立，而关于 KL 的三个预测各有一半以上模型失败；新模型真实内核上 37/38 个检查点保持比率带宽，但两个模型在代码上低于 1。在 vLLM 投机解码中，教师强制下测得的总变差能以 1.1%–2.4% 的平均相对误差预测贪婪草稿接受率，无需 KL 所需的特定任务校准。

Conclusion: 总变差距离是报告压缩模型决策变化量的直接且稳健的度量，优于 KL 散度；其与翻转率近乎 1:1 对应，通用且无需校准，建议以 TV 作为压缩报告中度量模型偏离的主要指标。

Abstract: Compression reports summarize how far a compressed language model moved from the dense one, usually by a KL divergence; a deployment that relies on the dense model's outputs needs to know how many of its decisions changed. We show that total variation, not KL, answers this directly. Across 802 compressed and perturbed copies of 19 open models on five corpora and nine mechanically unrelated perturbation families, the rate at which the arg-max token changes (the \emph{flip rate}) tracks total variation at a ratio with median $1.05$, with no fitted constant. KL converts into flips only through its square root and a factor that varies fourfold across models and corpora, because KL averages over tokens before the root is taken; first-order statistics averaged per token, such as Hellinger distance, avoid this, but reports rarely give them. As a result, of two compressors reported on different models and corpora whose flip rates differ by at least $10%$, KL assigns the smaller divergence to the one that changes more decisions in $11%$ of cases, total variation in $1%$. Two pre-registered tests mark the limits: on a held-out code corpus the ratio held for all eight models while three predictions about KL each failed for half of them or more, and on three new models with real kernels it stayed in its band for 37 of 38 checkpoints but fell below one on code for two models. In vLLM speculative decoding, total variation measured under teacher forcing predicts greedy draft acceptance with a mean relative error of $1.1$--$2.4%$, without the task-specific calibration that KL needs.

</details>


### [71] [Bayesian Fine-tuning Yields Language Models that are as Bayesian as their Beliefs Allow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00679)
*Polina Tsvilodub,Andreas Waldis,Linlu Qiu,Tal Linzen,Michael Franke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对比了在贝叶斯输出上微调与在标准真实答案（oracle）上微调的语言模型，发现贝叶斯微调能在模型中间层编码并利用可用的贝叶斯信念，而 oracle 微调在信念表征及信念读出两方面都逊色，揭示了细化监督的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多地被用于需要从少量观测中推理隐藏变量的任务，而贝叶斯推理是这类任务的规范正确解。已有研究表明，用最优贝叶斯模型的输出做监督微调可使 LM 接近贝叶斯行为，而用真实答案做标准 SFT 则达不到这一水平。但仅凭行为无法解释二者差异的来源——即模型究竟是否表征了贝叶斯信念、是否据此行动、是否像贝叶斯规则那样把信念转化为决策。因此需要深入模型内部，比较两种训练信号在表征与计算层面的差异。

Method: 作者提出一套递进的评判标准来界定“贝叶斯决策者”，涵盖行为、表征与计算三个层面，并在航班推荐任务上加以检验。具体做法包括：训练贝叶斯微调 LM 与 oracle 微调 LM；检测模型中间层是否编码了贝叶斯规则的各组成部分（如先验、似然、后验等）；检验编码后的信念在多大程度上被读出并用于最终推荐；并通过在两个 LM 之间交换信念表征的干预实验，考察贝叶斯优势能否随之迁移。

Result: 贝叶斯微调的 LM 表现出贝叶斯式行为，在中间层编码了贝叶斯规则的各量，并能在一定程度上将编码后的信念读出用于推荐。oracle 微调的 LM 在持有的信念内容上、以及在是否将信念读出转化为推荐上，均与贝叶斯微调模型存在差异。在二者之间交换信念表征能够迁移一部分贝叶斯优势，表明贝叶斯微调植入的信念是“可用的”。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯微调能够在 LM 中植入可用的贝叶斯信念，使其在不确定性推理时真正利用这些信念；而标准 oracle SFT 无法做到这一点。这凸显了用更细化的（贝叶斯式）监督信号进行微调的优势。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) are increasingly used for tasks that require reasoning about hidden variables from a few observations, for which Bayesian inference is the normatively correct solution. While supervised fine-tuning of an LM on the outputs of an optimal $\textit{Bayesian}$ model leads to near-Bayesian behavior, standard supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on the true answers for the task falls short of it. But behavior alone does not tell us $\textit{why}$ tuning on a $\textit{Bayesian}$ or an $\textit{oracle}$ (true answers) signal differs: whether the resulting LM represents Bayesian beliefs, acts on them, or turns them into a choice the way Bayes' rule does. To compare them, we formulate increasingly demanding requirements for an LM to count as a Bayesian decision maker, spanning its behavior, representations, and computations, and test them on a flight recommendation task. The Bayes-trained LM acts Bayesian, encodes quantities of Bayes' rule in its middle layers, and uses the encoded belief for the recommendation to a certain extent. The oracle-trained LM differs from it both in the beliefs it holds and whether it reads beliefs out into recommendations. Exchanging beliefs between the LMs transfers a part of the Bayesian advantage. Bayes fine-tuning thus installs usable Bayesian beliefs in an LM for reasoning under uncertainty in a way standard SFT on oracle answers cannot, highlighting the advantage of nuanced supervision.

</details>


### [72] [Sequential Functional Structured Tucker Compression for Large Language Model Attentions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00717)
*Jiangfeng Chen,Xinyu Wang,Tianshuo Yan,Hanwei Wu,Xiao-Wen Chang,Yang Zhang,Lei Ding*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FTC 是一种针对 LLM 注意力的顺序结构化压缩框架，在固定存储预算下利用 Q/K/V 头结构联合近似，无需微调，在 6B–32B 模型上取得最优困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 注意力后训练压缩常被建模为独立的矩阵近似，忽略了两点：一是注意力各个投影矩阵之间共享的结构，二是早期压缩所引发的表示偏移。这导致压缩效果受限，尤其在激进压缩下性能下降明显。

Method: 提出 FTC（顺序结构化压缩框架）：① 将压缩目标适配到当前已压缩的模型状态，以应对表示偏移；② 在固定存储预算下联合利用 Q/K/V 原生头结构进行近似；③ 将输出投影单独处理，以适配注意力后表示的变化。整个过程既不依赖微调，也不需要基于梯度的恢复。

Result: 在 7 个参数规模为 6B–32B 的 decoder-only LLM 上，FTC 在 5 个现代 GQA 模型的每一个保留率下均取得对比方法中最优的 WikiText-2 困惑度，且在激进压缩下优势最大；该提升可迁移到下游任务，并在 32B 规模上依然显著。

Conclusion: 通过顺序地适配当前压缩状态并联合利用头结构，FTC 在无需微调或梯度恢复的前提下显著优于独立矩阵近似方法，验证了考虑结构共享与表示偏移对注意力压缩的重要性。

Abstract: Post-training compression of LLM attention is often formulated as independent matrix approximation, ignoring both the shared structure among attention projections and the representation shift introduced by earlier compression. We propose FTC, a sequential structured compression framework that adapts the approximation to the current compressed model while jointly exploiting the native Q/K/V head structure under a fixed storage budget. The output projection is handled separately to account for the changed post-attention representation. FTC requires neither fine-tuning nor gradient-based recovery. Across seven decoder-only LLMs from 6B to 32B parameters, FTC achieves the lowest WikiText-2 perplexity among the compared methods at every tested keep ratio on five modern GQA models, with the largest gains under aggressive compression. The improvements transfer to downstream tasks and remain substantial at the 32B scale.

</details>


### [73] [Towards Robust Numerical Claim Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00689)
*Peter Røysland Aarnes,Vinay Setty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型在声明验证任务中的数值推理脆弱性问题，发现通过对数值扰动样本进行对抗性微调，小模型可超越更大规模的前沿模型，并实现跨扰动类型、跨语言和证据侧扰动的鲁棒性迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在声明验证中数值推理脆弱，即使数值微小变化也会导致准确率大幅下降，且该脆弱性在先进前端模型中依然存在，亟需有效的缓解方法。

Method: 采用参数高效微调（PEFT），对Qwen3系列小模型（0.6B–8B）在数值扰动样本上进行对抗性微调，并在标签翻转扰动、未见扰动类型、跨语言（西班牙语）以及VitaminC数据集的证据侧扰动上评估鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Result: 小模型在标签翻转扰动上达到98.7%准确率，优于更大的零样本模型及GPT-5.4 Pro（74.0%）和Gemini 2.5 Flash（73.9%）；增益可泛化到未见扰动类型，且无需目标域数据即可迁移，显著提升西班牙语跨语言性能，同时对抗证据侧扰动也表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 对抗性微调能够有效建立鲁棒的数值决策边界而非记忆化修改，使小参数模型在数值推理鲁棒性上超越更大规模的前沿系统，并具备跨扰动类型、跨语言及证据侧的泛化迁移能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are widely used for claim verification, yet remain brittle for numerical reasoning: even small changes in value can sharply degrade accuracy. We show that this brittleness persists in frontier LLMs, but can be mitigated through adversarial fine-tuning on numerically perturbed examples. Using parameter-efficient fine-tuning, small Qwen3 models (0.6B$\unicode{x2013}$8B) reach 98.7% accuracy on label-flipping perturbations, outperforming larger zero-shot models and frontier systems (GPT-5.4 Pro (74.0%) and Gemini 2.5 Flash (73.9%)). The gains generalise to unseen perturbation types, indicating robust numerical decision boundaries rather than memorised edits. Robustness also transfers without target-domain data, significantly improving cross-lingual performance in Spanish. We further show that the same fine-tuning recipe confers robustness to evidence-side perturbations, using the VitaminC dataset.

</details>


### [74] [Reason in Style: Discovering and Controlling Style in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00724)
*Ioana Marinescu,Eric Karl Oermann,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究语言模型中内容与风格的分离，提出无监督算法发现模型输出中的重复风格，并通过微调使学生模型可显式控制风格，从而提升数学推理性能并实现风格多样化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型联合学习内容与风格，导致其输出中的风格变异难以识别和控制；作者希望探究能否在无监督条件下发现模型回复中的重复风格并显式加以控制。

Method: 设计算法将语言模型输出的内容表示与风格表示分离，并在受控的数学题目设置中验证有效性；将该方法应用于来自九个教师模型、超过10万条验证轨迹的数据，无监督发现六种重复但分布不均衡的风格；随后用重要性加权平衡语料中各类风格的贡献，微调较小学生模型使其在显式条件下遵循指定风格。

Result: 该方法在六个数学推理基准上相比同数据上的标准微调提升了 Pass@k；确认请求的风格与实际生成的风格之间具有强对应关系；同时发现风格影响正确性——解题概率取决于所条件化的风格，且不同题目受益于不同风格。

Conclusion: 模型生成数据中的风格变异可以被无监督地发现并显式化，这既能提供对输出的控制手段，也能改善推理性能。

Abstract: Language models learn content and style jointly, making stylistic variation in their outputs difficult to identify and control. We study whether recurring styles in model responses can be discovered without supervision and explicitly controlled. We design an algorithm that learns to separate representations of content and style from language models' outputs and validate its effectiveness on math questions in a controlled setting. By applying this method to over 100K verified traces from nine distinct teacher models, we discover six recurring yet imbalanced styles. We then fine-tune smaller student models to follow these styles when explicitly conditioned on them, using importance weighting to balance the contribution of the styles represented in the corpus. This approach improves Pass@$k$ over standard fine-tuning on the same data across six math reasoning benchmarks, demonstrating that we can diversify the style of answers effectively. We confirm that this also results in strong correspondence between requested and realized styles. We find that style affects correctness: the probability of solving a problem depends on the style we condition on, and different problems benefit from different styles. In summary, our results show that stylistic variation in model-generated data can be discovered in an unsupervised way, and made explicit, providing a source of both control and improved reasoning performance.

</details>


### [75] [Effective Synthetic Data Curation Requires Group-Level Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00779)
*Cathy Jiao,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文论证：对合成数据的有效筛选不能仅依赖样本个体级效用信号，必须引入考虑样本间交互的群体级信号；群体级信号在合成数据占比越高时越关键，并提供低成本诊断方法以定位最需群体级估计的数据组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据已广泛用于提升LLM的自主与长程任务能力，但近期工作发现规模训练合成数据可能损害模型生成质量。现有数据筛选依赖个体级信号（孤立估计每个样本的训练效用），作者认为这在合成数据场景下不足，需转向能刻画样本间交互的群体级信号。

Method: 在预训练与后训练两种设定下，比较个体级与群体级效用信号：构造组成不同但在个体级影响下不可区分、在群体级影响下差异显著的数据集，并对比两种筛选策略的下游性能（尤其生成能力）。同时考察多种常用数据筛选方法在训练管道日益合成化时的表现，并放大刻画样本间关系的权重以观察增益变化。

Result: 个体级信号对样本联合效应是盲目的：组成不同的数据集在个体级影响下不可区分，但群体级影响下差异显著，按群体级筛选获得更优下游性能（特别是在生成能力上）。训练管道越合成化，群体级信号越重要：在常用方法中，仅纳入群体级信号的方法优于基线，且当放大样本间关系权重时增益进一步增大。

Conclusion: 合成数据的有效筛选需要群体级信号而非仅个体级信号，且其重要性随训练数据合成化程度提高而增强。为便于落地，作者提出一种低成本诊断方法，帮助开发者在算力预算内优先确定最需群体级估计的数据组，从而以极小算力代价恢复大部分全量群体级评分的收益。

Abstract: Synthetic data now is essential to LLM training, used to strengthen advanced capabilities such as autonomous and long-horizon task execution. Yet recent work shows that training on it at scale can degrade model generation, making it important to decide what synthetic data is worth training on. While current data curation practices do so with individual-level signals (i.e., estimates of each data sample's training utility in isolation), across pre-training and post-training settings we show that this is insufficient for synthetic data, and that group-level signals (i.e., estimates of utility that account for interactions among data samples) are necessary for effective data curation. First, we show that individual-level signals are blind to how samples jointly affect training: synthetic datasets with different compositions can be indistinguishable under individual-level influence yet differ sharply under group-level influence, and curating by the latter yields better downstream performance, particularly in generative capability. Second, we find that group-level signals matter more as training pipelines become increasingly synthetic: among widely used data curation methods, only those incorporating them improve over baseline, with gains increasing when weights capturing relations among samples are amplified. Finally, we translate these findings into practice -- for model developers under a compute budget, we offer a cheap diagnostic that prioritizes which groups of synthetic data most need group-level estimation, recovering much of the benefit of full group-level scoring at a fraction of the compute cost.

</details>


### [76] [Can large language models unlock discrete data in ophthalmic diagnostic reports?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00795)
*Umair A. Zaidi,An-Lun Wu,Wei-Chun Lin,Thomas S. Hwang,Michelle R. Hribar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了大型语言模型（GPT-4o）通过两种提示策略从眼科诊断PDF报告中提取结构化数据的准确性与效率，发现其准确率高且能将人工处理时间减少约92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 眼科诊断PDF报告中的结构化数据提取通常依赖耗时的人工审查，亟需一种高效、准确且可自动化的替代方案，以提高研究和临床数据抽象的效率。

Method: 采用20份去标识化的四类眼科报告（视野、OCT青光眼总览、OCT视网膜神经纤维层单次检查、OCT厚度图，各5份），分别通过两种基于GPT-4o的流程处理：Schema-Constrained（使用Structured Output模式配合预定义JSON Schema）与Prompt-Only（详细指令提示后经Python转换为JSON）。以人工核对的地面真值为基准，比较数值准确率、格式准确率和提取时间。

Result: Schema-Constrained数值准确率：视野与RNFL单次检查100.00%，青光眼总览97.45%，厚度图98.00%；Prompt-Only四类报告均达100.00%。格式准确率：Schema-Constrained为100.00%，Prompt-Only除RNFL单次检查（90.14%）外均为100.00%。平均提取时间：人工审查每份56.51秒，Schema-Constrained为5.04秒，Prompt-Only为4.70秒，约减少92%。

Conclusion: 在该小规模概念验证数据集中，通用LLM辅助流程能以高准确率提取眼科诊断PDF的结构化数据并显著缩短处理时间。Prompt-Only数值准确率最高，而Schema-Constrained输出100%符合模式规范，两种策略优势互补，支持进一步评估融合验证感知的混合工作流用于科研与临床数据抽象。

Abstract: Objective: To assess the accuracy and efficiency of a large language model (LLM) using two prompt strategies to extract structured data from ophthalmic diagnostic PDF reports. Methods: Twenty deidentified reports across four types (Visual Field, OCT Glaucoma Overview, OCT retinal nerve fiber layer Single Exam, and OCT Thickness Map; n = 5 each) were processed using two GPT-4o-assisted pipelines and compared with a reconciled manual ground truth. Schema-Constrained used Structured Output mode with a predefined JSON Schema; Prompt-Only used a detailed instruction prompt followed by Python conversion to JSON. Outcomes were value accuracy, formatting accuracy, and extraction time. Results: Schema-Constrained value accuracy was 100.00% for Visual Field and RNFL Single Exam, 97.45% for Glaucoma Overview, and 98.00% for Thickness Map; Prompt-Only achieved 100.00% across all four report types. Formatting accuracy was 100.00% for Schema-Constrained across all report types and 100.00% for Prompt-Only except RNFL Single Exam (90.14%). Mean extraction time was 56.51 s per report for manual review versus 5.04 s for Schema-Constrained and 4.70 s for Prompt-Only, an approximately 92% reduction. Conclusions: In this small proof-of-concept dataset, general-purpose LLM-assisted pipelines extracted structured data from ophthalmic diagnostic PDFs with high accuracy and substantially reduced processing time. Prompt-Only achieved the highest value accuracy, while Schema-Constrained produced schema-compliant output with 100% formatting accuracy. These complementary strengths support further evaluation of hybrid, validation-aware workflows for research and clinical data abstraction.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [77] [Heavy-Tailed Memory Traces in Long-Horizon Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00010)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究长程语言智能体外部记忆的"使用形态"：在有限上下文与反复检索下，智能体的记忆检索会形成重尾分布（核心少数状态被高频访问，长尾稀有状态预测误差累积）。作者通过"保守尾审计"验证这一现象，并提出 Core–Tail World Model (CTWM) 记忆控制器，用单一指数 τ 分配提示预算并保留摘要化的尾部，在多个基准上实现 token 节省与尾部预测误差降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统通常只用任务成功率或 token 成本来评价，忽视了记忆使用的分布形态。作者认为，在有限上下文与重复检索条件下，记忆可能过度集中于小核心，而稀有状态落入长尾、预测误差累积，因此需要诊断并利用这种重尾痕迹作为控制信号。

Method: 首先通过"保守尾审计"（conservative tail audit）研究检索分布形态，发现随机游走智能体产生对数正态兼容的检索伪迹，而语义 LLM 策略产生最强的截断幂律兼容的核心-尾部痕迹。基于此提出 CTWM，一种基于秩的记忆控制器，用单一指数 τ 分配 prompt 预算，同时保留摘要化的尾部（summarized tail）。

Result: 在 Synthetic Graph World 上，CTWM 保持完整的状态与转移覆盖，相对图记忆基线减少 5.9% 的 prompt token，并将下半部分尾部预测误差降低 13.6%。相同的配对比较在 ALFWorld 上获得一致的 token 节省，在 LongMemEval 上实现 24.48% 的 token 减少，且总体准确率相当。

Conclusion: 重尾记忆痕迹不仅是有限检索的一种诊断特征，也可作为实际的控制信号，用于构建 token 高效的智能体世界模型。CTWM 展示了基于尾部结构进行记忆预算分配的实用价值。

Abstract: Long-horizon language agents increasingly rely on external memory as a frozen world model, yet current memory systems are usually judged only by task success or token cost. We argue that the missing object is the shape of memory use: under finite context and repeated retrieval, agent memory can concentrate on a small core while leaving rare states in a long tail where prediction errors accumulate. We study this effect through a conservative tail audit and find that concentration is reproducible but policy-dependent. Random-walk agents produce log-normal-compatible retrieval artifacts, whereas semantic LLM policies yield the strongest truncated-power-law-compatible core--tail traces. Motivated by this audit, we propose Core--Tail World Model (CTWM), a rank-based memory controller that allocates prompt budget with a single exponent $τ$ while retaining a summarized tail. On Synthetic Graph World, CTWM preserves full state and transition coverage, reduces prompt tokens by 5.9%, and lowers bottom-half tail prediction error by 13.6% relative to a graph-memory baseline. The same paired comparison gives consistent token savings on ALFWorld and a 24.48% token reduction on LongMemEval with aggregate accuracy parity. These results suggest that heavy-tailed memory traces are not only a diagnostic of finite retrieval, but also a practical control signal for token-efficient agent world models.

</details>


### [78] [When Do Causal World Models Help Modular LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00012)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究模块化LLM智能体中因果世界模型的必要性，提出FedCausalCompose框架，证明观察性世界模型存在不可约的干预误差，并发现因果结构仅在接口可识别且以决策相关形式呈现时才有帮助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体越来越多地通过订单、支付、库存、物流等模块化服务协同工作，一个模块中的动作会改变另一模块的有效状态转移。标准世界模型通常拟合观测轨迹，但观测轨迹（如"支付先于发货"）无法区分支付是否授权发货、库存是否中介、或是否存在隐藏触发因素。这种观测与干预之间的鸿沟是本文的核心动机。

Method: 提出FedCausalCompose框架，利用局部动作提供的干预-响应证据来恢复跨模块接口的因果结构。理论分析：证明在存在未阻断后门路径时，观察性世界模型产生不可约的干预误差；证明接口恢复精度随干预-响应覆盖率提升；证明在覆盖率与局部机制误差受控时，oracle因果组合可超越非因果下界。实验验证：在结构化工具环境（API签名暴露前置条件与下游效应）与对话/叙事环境等诊断性智能体场景中进行测试。

Result: 因果接口在结构化工具环境中帮助最大，因为API签名显式暴露前置条件和下游效应；而在对话与叙事环境中，原始边列表常被忽略，除非通过简短的注意力锚点使因果信息具有决策相关性。因果结构帮助的必要条件是：跨模块接口既统计可识别，又以智能体在动作时可利用的形式呈现。

Conclusion: 因果世界模型对LLM智能体的价值是有条件的：仅当跨模块接口在统计上可识别、并且以智能体在决策时能实际使用的形式呈现时，因果结构才能带来增益。这为在LLM智能体中何时以及如何引入因果建模提供了具体判据。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly act through modular systems, such as order, payment, inventory, and shipment services, where actions in one module change which transitions are valid in another. Standard world models usually fit observational traces, but this is not the quantity needed for intervention-time planning: a trace may show that payment precedes shipment without identifying whether payment authorizes shipment, inventory mediates the effect, or a hidden trigger explains both. We study this gap through FedCausalCompose, a causal world-model framework for modular LLM agents in which local actions provide intervention-response evidence for cross-module interfaces. We first show that observational world models incur an irreducible interventional error under unblocked back-door paths, that interface recovery improves with intervention-response coverage, and that an oracle causal composition can beat the non-causal lower bound when coverage and local mechanism errors are controlled. We then test the resulting prediction in diagnostic agent settings. Causal interfaces help most in structured tool environments, where API signatures expose preconditions and downstream effects. In contrast, dialogue and narrative environments often ignore raw edge lists unless a short attention anchor makes the causal information decision-relevant. These results identify a concrete condition for causal world models in LLM agents: causal structure helps when cross-module interfaces are both statistically identifiable and presented in a form the agent can use at action time.

</details>


### [79] [From Proposal to Verified Effect: Praxa, an Evidence-Bound Harness for Governed AI Agent Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00015)
*Stefan G. Creadore*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Praxa 是一个大语言模型智能体框架（agent harness），通过确定性准入（admission）、代理执行（brokered execution）、外部回读（external read-back）、对账（reconciliation）和评审后晋升（reviewed promotion）等机制，将"提案、授权、派发、可验证外部效果、服务晋升"等不同声明显式为可测试的状态。论文报告了四条证据路径，但均明确指出当前证据不足以支撑安全性、生产优势或通用优越性等更强结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体能够"提出并执行动作"，但提案（proposal）、授权（authority）、派发（dispatch）、经核实的外部效果（verified external effect）与服务晋升（serving promotion）在概念上是不同的声明，现有系统往往将其混为一谈。作者希望构建一个能显式区分这些状态、并使"从授权到实际效果的过渡"可审计、可测试的框架。

Method: 提出 Praxa 框架，通过五个显式机制区分并管理授权到效果的过渡：确定性准入（deterministic admission）、代理执行（brokered execution）、外部回读（external read-back）、对账（reconciliation）、评审后晋升（reviewed promotion）。通过四条证据路径评估：1) 作者在固定修订版上的仓库内审计（单元测试、Workerd 测试、源文件插桩、覆盖率下限）；2) 供应商支持的 Terminal-Bench Core 0.1.1 试点（12 个精选任务、基线臂与可靠性层臂）；3) 调试后的双阶协调代理开发对比（基线 vs 源码作者候选，180/180 试验）；4) 部署的源码/配置证据（有界反射、召回记账、内存编译、工具健康路径）。

Result: 证据一：固定修订版本地审计通过 1,027/1,027 单元测试与 89/89 Workerd 测试，插桩全部 363 个预期源文件，满足四项覆盖率下限；但原始逐测试转录和独立复现不可用。证据二：Terminal-Bench Core 0.1.1 试点中基线与可靠性层各通过 17/36 严格试验；可靠性层多消耗 37.49% 输入与 50.73% 输出 token，因此试点不支持"可靠性层更优"的结论。证据三：调试后对比中基线与源码作者候选各完成 180/180 试验，测量准确率相同，均具备完全密闭崩溃恢复、零保护违规；候选省 37.11% token、端点成本低 33.84%、步数少 11.63%，但这不证明质量、延迟或生产行为更优。证据四：部署证据显示有界反射、召回记账、内存编译与工具健康路径，但无生产结果提升。

Conclusion: Praxa 的核心贡献是一个"证据受限（evidence-bound）"的架构，它使从授权到实际效果的过渡显式且可测试。但当前证据不能确立对抗性安全、生产安全、通用专家优越性、自主递归优化或用户收益等更强主张。论文整体采取审慎、证据受限的立场，将可验证的架构设计与其尚未被证明的能力边界明确区分。

Abstract: Large-language-model agents can propose and execute actions, but proposal, authority, dispatch, verified external effect, and serving promotion are different claims. We present Praxa, an agent harness that represents these states explicitly through deterministic admission, brokered execution, external read-back, reconciliation, and reviewed promotion. We report four evidence lanes. First, an author-run repository-local audit at a pinned revision passed 1,027/1,027 unit tests and 89/89 Workerd tests, instrumented all 363 expected source files, and met four coverage floors; raw per-test transcripts and independent reproduction are unavailable. Second, in a provider-backed Terminal-Bench Core 0.1.1 pilot across 12 curated tasks, baseline and reliability-layer arms each passed 17/36 strict trials. The reliability layer used 37.49% more input and 50.73% more output tokens, so the pilot does not support superiority. Third, in a post-debug, two-order coordination-proxy development comparison, baseline and a source-authored candidate each completed 180/180 trials with equal measured accuracy, full hermetic crash recovery, and zero protected violations. The candidate used 37.11% fewer tokens, 33.84% lower estimated endpoint cost, and 11.63% fewer steps; this does not establish improved quality, latency, or production behavior. Fourth, deployed source/configuration evidence shows bounded reflection, recall accounting, memory compilation, and tool-health paths, but no production outcome lift. Praxa's supported contribution is an evidence-bound architecture that makes authority-to-effect transitions explicit and testable. Current evidence does not establish adversarial security, production safety, general specialist superiority, autonomous recursive optimization, or user benefit.

</details>


### [80] [What Do Rationales Communicate? A Message-Intervention Study in Role-Specialized QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00018)
*Jiameng Zhang,Hongqiu Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"消息干预诊断"方法，固定证据与候选答案、仅改变reasoner传给verifier的rationale，发现忠实rationale对答案准确率几乎无增益，但被篡改的rationale会强烈影响支持性判断；显式检查prompt会放大此效应，且模型对"有效但被篡改"的rationale存在过度信任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 角色分工的QA流程普遍将rationale从推理器传给验证器，但其实际作用不明：究竟带来更优答案、更强的支持评估，还是引入新的失败模式。作者希望厘清这一reasoner-to-verifier消息通道的真实功能。

Method: 提出消息干预（message-intervention）诊断：固定证据与候选答案，仅系统性地改变跨reasoner-verifier边界传递的rationale。在400个MuSiQue、HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA样本上，以DeepSeek同时作为生成器与验证器，比较无rationale、忠实rationale、被篡改rationale（包括无害改写与实质性损坏）及有无显式rationale-checking prompt下的支持判断与最终答案变化，并结合人工审计与跨模型、跨任务边界检查。

Result: 忠实rationale相对无rationale几乎不提升答案准确率；被篡改rationale强烈改变支持判断。盲验证prompt下无害改写仅使支持偏移0-2.5%，而损坏rationale偏移10-22%；显式rationale-checking prompt将同一模式放大到34-55%。最终答案变动较小（2-30%），仅2.9-35.3%的损坏支持翻转伴随答案变化。人工审计发现：16/42有效损坏属"损坏-过度信任"案例，且人类在9/10已审计的被模型接受的损坏rationale上做出拒绝或标记不明确。跨模型与任务边界检查显示该通道可呈激活、放大、惰性或折叠进任务标签等状态。

Conclusion: rationale共享应作为一种"验证消息机制"来评估，而不只是通往更高答案准确率的路径；否则可能忽视其对支持性判断的脆弱影响及由此产生的过度信任风险。

Abstract: Role-specialized QA pipelines increasingly pass rationales from a reasoner to a verifier, but it is unclear what this message actually buys: better answers, stronger support assessment, or a new failure surface. We introduce a message-intervention diagnostic that fixes the evidence and candidate answer while varying only the rationale passed across the reasoner-to-verifier boundary. On 400 MuSiQue, HotpotQA, and 2WikiMultiHopQA examples with DeepSeek as generator and verifier, faithful rationales add almost no answer accuracy over no rationale, while corrupted rationales strongly alter support judgments. Under a blind verifier prompt, harmless paraphrases shift support by only 0--2.5%, whereas corrupted rationales shift support by 10--22%; an explicit rationale-checking prompt amplifies the same pattern to 34--55%. Final answers move less (2--30%), and only 2.9--35.3% of corrupted support flips co-occur with answer changes. Human audits show why this matters: 16/42 valid corruptions are corruption-overtrust cases, and blind humans reject or mark unclear 9/10 audited corrupted rationales that the model accepts. Cross-model and task-boundary checks show when the channel is active, amplified, inert, or folded into the task label. Rationale sharing should be evaluated as a verification-message mechanism, not merely as a route to higher answer accuracy.

</details>


### [81] [Measuring the Microtask Eligibility Gap: When Is an Off-the-Shelf SLM Enough for an Agent Harness?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00025)
*Jundong Hu,Shekar Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统检验了围绕前沿大模型规划器的小型语言模型微任务能力，发现现成SLM普遍无法达到预设的合格阈值，且量化加重而非解决该问题，建议将SLM置于已通过阈值基线的后方使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent框架日益希望用小型语言模型处理前沿LLM规划器周边的微任务，以降低成本与延迟，但缺乏对这些SLM是否真正满足从业者设定质量阈值（而非仅为"可跑"）的系统性评估。

Method: 构建含4类微任务（自动批准shell命令、写记忆、选工具、排序历史轮次）的基准，固定提示词与自动指标，每任务预设阈值τ锚定于廉价的非LLM基线，并采用基于置信区间的合格判定规则（配置需置信界超过τ才算通过）。对Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B在最优配置（FP16、贪心解码、固定冻结提示、无微调）下扫描，结合logprob决策阈值诊断（T1/T3/T4，T2经上下文长度/级联探针）区分能力缺陷与可调阈值故障；再测试4-bit量化（RTN/GPTQ/AWQ）的影响，并在Llama-3.x与阈值扫描、提示改写层面做稳健性验证。

Result: 16个配置（4任务×4模型）中0个通过合格判定，存在"资格缺口"；诊断分离出能力缺陷与可通过调整解码阈值解决的两类失败（4种机制）。4-bit量化造成的损伤随模型规模变化，且未使任何配置合格（T1/T3在可重构硬标签任务上证得，T2/T4为诊断性/窗口化稳健性），合格缺口更随模型规模而非精度变化。该结果在Llama-3.x（12/12不合格）上复现，对锚点选择（τ扫描）与提示措辞（原文加3个中性改写，0/112合格）稳健。实践示例：4B重排序器叠加BM25短列表优于纯BM25（+0.047[0.020,0.073]），但自身仍不满足合格门槛。

Conclusion: 现成SLM在规划器微任务上尚不能独立达到从业者定义的合格标准，将其置于已通过CI阈值基线的后方（仅当基线不达标时启用SLM）是更可靠的部署策略；该结论对模型规模与量化精度稳健，提示"资格"评估需以置信区间而非单点性能为准。

Abstract: Agent harnesses increasingly want to run small language models (SLMs) on the microtasks around a frontier large language model (LLM) planner: auto-approving shell commands, writing memory, selecting tools, ranking past turns. We ask whether off-the-shelf SLMs meet practitioner-defined thresholds and, when they fail, why, and whether quantization changes the answer. We build a benchmark of 4 such microtasks with fixed prompts and automatic metrics, each with a pre-specified threshold $τ$ anchored to a cheap non-LLM baseline and a CI-aware eligibility rule (a configuration passes only if its confidence bound clears $τ$). Sweeping Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B at their best (FP16, greedy, one frozen prompt, no tuning), we find an eligibility gap: 0 of 16 (4 tasks $\times$ 4 models) configurations pass (verified by checking the raw outputs and parser behavior). A logprob decision-threshold diagnostic (T1/T3/T4; T2 via a context-length/cascade probe) separates the failures into capability deficits and failures that can be addressed by changing the decoding threshold (4 regimes). Quantization to 4-bit (RTN/GPTQ/AWQ) does damage that depends on model size and moves no configuration into eligibility (certified on the reconstructable hard-label tasks T1/T3, diagnostic/windowed robustness on T2/T4), so the gap tracks model size more than precision; it replicates on Llama-3.x (12/12 ineligible) and is robust to the anchor choice (a $τ$-sweep) and to prompt wording (0/112 eligible across the original plus 3 neutral paraphrases per cell). The practical implication: place SLMs behind a baseline that meets the CI-backed threshold, and use the SLM only where the baseline fails to meet the threshold; e.g. a 4B re-ranker over a BM25 shortlist beats BM25 ($+0.047$ [0.020, 0.073], without itself certifying eligibility).

</details>


### [82] [Characterizing a Configuration Where Inference-Time PRM-Pruned Fragment Grafting Is Inert: Evidence from Three Reasoning LMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00047)
*Khawaja Murad ul Hassan,Mehran Ebrahimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文系统检验了"PRM剪枝片段嫁接"（PPFG）这一推理时干预机制，发现其在所测试的操作点上与独立并行思维链基线在统计上等价（无增益），并定位了导致其失效的机制性原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前并行思维链会出现"多样性坍缩"，相关工作提出通过过程奖励模型（PRM）剪枝链时，将高PRM前缀提取并原样嫁接为上下文演示以传递跨轨迹步级信息，从而促进推理多样性。然而此前报道的片段嫁接增益往往依赖额外补偿成分。作者希望隔离出这一最省成本的机制（即PPFG），并在其宣称有效的操作点上严格检验：PPFG本身是否真的能带来任何增益。

Method: 在 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 上使用 Math-Shepherd PRM，在完整 MATH500（n=500，三个随机种子）上开展实验，比较 PPFG（停滞触发与随机触发两种变体）与独立并行思维链基线。通过四桶分类法对 322 个停滞规则注入事件进行归因，考察复合门控精化、随机对照（2.4 倍触发率）、兼容门扫描，并在三个基座模型、六个基准、第二个 PRM 上复现。采用双单侧检验（TOST）将"无差异"提升为等价性结论，并辅以每事件抽查、幸存兄弟链反事实分析和后见之明预言机（上界 +0.13 pp）。

Result: PPFG 在所有测量轴上都与独立并行思维链基线在统计上无差异。四桶分类显示 322 个停滞注入事件中仅 14% 真正针对处于挣扎状态的链，其余落在已成功、接近完成或处于 PRM 平台期的链上——而救援嫁接无法改变这些状态。复合门控未能同时实现精准触发与足够密度，随机对照在 2.4 倍触发率下同样无差异，表明失效并非启发式特有问题。该发现在三个基座模型、六个基准、第二个 PRM 及兼容门扫描中复现；TOST 分析在全部 12 个 Qwen/LLaMA 单元上支持等价性。每事件抽查显示注入链以匹配步 2.75 倍速率被剪枝，但幸存兄弟链反事实未发现群体层面的补偿。后见之明预言机将"选择 PPFG 优于独立"的单题增益上界定为 +0.13 个百分点。

Conclusion: 在本文测试的操作点上，PRM 剪枝片段嫁接（PPFG）这一最省成本的跨轨迹步级传递机制并未带来优于独立并行思维链基线的增益，且失效原因具有机制性（多数注入未指向真正挣扎的链）而非启发式特异。作者贡献了一套用于确立推理时机制"零效应"的等价性检验模板，所有结论均限定于其所测试的操作点。

Abstract: Diversity collapse in parallel chain-of-thought has motivated inference-time interventions built on a natural design: when a process reward model (PRM) prunes a chain, its high-PRM prefix is extracted and grafted verbatim as an in-context demonstration into a still-decoding sibling. We isolate this mechanism, PRM-Pruned Fragment Grafting (PPFG), as the most cost-minimal operationalization of cross-trajectory step-level transfer, and test it at the operating point where prior fragment-grafting work reports gains only under additional compensating ingredients. On Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct with Math-Shepherd on full MATH500 (n=500, three seeds), PPFG in both stagnation- and random-targeting variants is statistically indistinguishable from an independent parallel-CoT baseline on every measured axis. We characterize why: a four-bucket classification of 322 stagnation-rule injection events shows only 14% targeted a genuinely struggling chain; the rest landed on chains that had already succeeded, were near completion, or sat on a flat PRM plateau, states a rescue graft cannot change. No compound-gate refinement jointly achieves well-targeted firing and adequate density, and a random control matches the same parity at 2.4x the firing rate, so the inertness is not heuristic-specific. The finding replicates across three base LMs, six benchmarks, a second PRM, and a compatibility-gate sweep; two-one-sided-tests analysis promotes the parity to positive equivalence on all twelve Qwen/LLaMA cells. A per-event spot-check finds injected chains prune at 2.75x the matched-step rate, but a surviving-sibling counterfactual finds no population-level compensation. A hindsight oracle bounds any per-problem gain from choosing PPFG over independent at +0.13 pp. We contribute an equivalence-testing template for establishing inference-time mechanism nulls, with every claim scoped to its tested operating point.

</details>


### [83] [Gradient-Aligned Pair Selection for Personalized Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00061)
*Ruoming Jin,Xinyu Li,Hao Zhou,Jianfeng Zhu,Ruixin Guo,Feodor Dragan,Lei Xu,Haixun Wang,Yang Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 GAP-DPO，一种几何对齐的偏好对选择算法。作者通过分析 DPO 更新方向与用户效用的梯度交互，发现当偏好边际与效用梯度方向对齐时，DPO 更新会从误差纠正信号转变为类强化学习更新，从而将配对选择从启发式预处理提升为优化几何的固有组成部分，并在个性化文本生成任务上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化大语言模型需要对齐用户特定偏好而非全局质量。现有 DPO 方法在个性化场景中的效果高度依赖偏好对的选择，但常用启发式准则（如基于似然的极端采样）将优化与显式用户效用解耦，可能导致个性化退化。因此需要从几何角度理解配对选择对优化方向的影响。

Method: 将个性化偏好学习形式化为几何对齐优化问题，分析期望用户效用梯度与 DPO 更新方向之间的一阶交互；揭示在离策略采样下，当偏好边际与效用梯度方向对齐时，DPO 更新由误差纠正信号转变为类强化学习更新。据此提出 GAP-DPO 迭代算法，执行效用感知、几何对齐的配对选择，并通过逐轮重生成来控制分布偏移。

Result: 在个性化文本生成基准上，GAP-DPO 相比标准 DPO 变体在风格保真度、偏好对齐度和生成质量上均取得一致改进，验证了将梯度对齐作为个性化偏好优化统一原则的有效性。

Conclusion: 梯度对齐可作为个性化偏好优化的统一原则；配对选择是优化几何的内在组成部分，而非启发式预处理步骤。GAP-DPO 通过效用感知、几何对齐的配对选择显著提升了 DPO 在个性化场景中的性能。

Abstract: Personalizing large language models (LLMs) requires aligning generation behavior with user-specific preferences rather than aggregate quality. While Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) provides a stable framework for preference learning, its effectiveness in personalized settings critically depends on how preference pairs are selected. Existing approaches typically rely on heuristic criteria, such as likelihood-based extremes, which decouple optimization from explicit user utility and can lead to degraded personalization. We formalize personalized preference learning as a geometry-aligned optimization problem by analyzing the first-order interaction between gradients of expected user utility and DPO update directions. Our analysis reveals that, under off-policy sampling, the DPO update transitions from a purely error-corrective signal to a reinforcement-like update when preference margins are directionally aligned with utility gradients. This perspective exposes pair selection as a geometric decision that governs whether preference optimization advances or hinders personalization. Motivated by this insight, we propose GAP-DPO (Geometry-Aligned Preference DPO), an iterative algorithm that performs utility-aware, geometry-aligned pair selection while controlling distribution shift via epoch-wise regeneration. Experiments on personalized text generation benchmarks show that GAP-DPO consistently improves stylistic fidelity, preference alignment, and generation quality compared to standard DPO variants. Together, our results establish gradient alignment as a unifying principle for personalized preference optimization and demonstrate that pair selection is an intrinsic component of the optimization geometry rather than a heuristic preprocessing step.

</details>


### [84] [K-Dense BYOK: An Open-Source AI Research Assistant That Runs Locally and Keeps a Hash-Chained Lab Notebook](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00074)
*Aubrey M. Brueckner,Darshil Patel,Yuhuan He,Timothy Kassis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: K-Dense BYOK 是一个免费开源的 AI 科研助手，在研究者自己的电脑上运行，通过可核验的观察日志、Living Lab Notebook 和科学脚手架来减少模型过度声称，并在 20 个跨学科提示的评分中优于两个托管平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聊天助手和通用编码代理最普遍的失败模式是模型过度声称（overclaiming），且缺乏可核验的记录；研究者也担心数据、代码和结果离开自己掌控的机器。作者希望提供一个在本地运行、可追溯、可复现的科研助手，通过让声称可核验而非简单禁止来应对这一失败。

Method: K-Dense BYOK 以普通文件夹作为项目载体，研究者自行提供模型（托管或本地）。应用提供三层：一是科学脚手架——内置书面科学流程库、引导式工作流模板、研究数据目录，以及评审员和写作者角色；二是 Living Lab Notebook，条目链接进论证且只增不删；三是由应用旁观代理行为产生的观察日志，代理没有任何工具能写入该日志。评测采用事先固定的评分标准，在 20 个跨学科研究提示上比较 K-Dense BYOK 与两个托管平台（其中一个运行相同的 frontier 模型），分别评估科学质量与研究执行两方面。

Result: 在 20 个跨学科提示上，K-Dense BYOK 在科学质量和研究执行两项指标上均领先两个托管平台。其成果是唯一记录所用软件运行环境的，且通常附带可重新生成结果的命令；而运行相同前沿模型的托管平台两者都未提供。不过这些环境记录是代理写下的文件，不是观察日志的一部分——观察日志目前尚未捕获软件环境本身。

Conclusion: K-Dense BYOK 通过在本地运行、提供科学脚手架、只增不删的 Living Lab Notebook 以及代理无法篡改的观察日志，成功使模型声称变得可核验，并在科学质量与研究执行上优于托管平台；代码以 MIT 许可证开源，未来可进一步将软件环境纳入观察日志。

Abstract: K-Dense BYOK (bring your own keys) is a free, open-source AI research assistant for scientists in any field that runs on the researcher's own computer. The researcher supplies access to a model of their choice, hosted or running locally, and the application supplies everything else: a place for the work to run, a layer of scientific scaffolding, and a complete record. Each project is an ordinary folder, so the data, the code, the results, and the record stay on a machine the researcher administers and can be read years later without the application. Three things separate it from a chat assistant or a general-purpose coding agent. It ships a library of written scientific procedures, guided workflow templates, catalogs of where research data can be found, and reviewer and writer roles the agent can hand work to. It keeps a Living Lab Notebook whose entries link into an argument and are added to but never erased. And it records what happened by watching what the agent does rather than by taking the agent's word for it, in a log the agent has no tool that can write to. That choice targets the most common failure, model overclaiming, in our earlier benchmark of nine frontier models, by making claims checkable rather than preventing them. On twenty interdisciplinary research prompts, scored under a rubric fixed in advance, K-Dense BYOK led two managed platforms on both scientific quality and research execution. Its deliverables were the only ones that recorded the software they ran in, and the only ones that usually arrived with a command that regenerates the results. One of the managed platforms ran the same frontier model and supplied neither. Those environment records were files the agent wrote, not part of the observed log, which does not yet capture the software environment itself. The code is available under the MIT license at https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/k-dense-byok.

</details>


### [85] [Scientific Agents: Evaluating Profession-Specific System Prompts on Scientific Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00084)
*Timothy Kassis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了详细职业专用系统提示词对科学任务的实际价值，发现它们显著增加 token 消耗与成本，却未带来一致的准确率提升，甚至在某些任务上降低表现；仅有的优势（API 掉线时重试成功率更高）源于提示词长度/格式，而非领域专业性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社区中针对不同职业（如科学家）生成详细专用系统提示（AGENTS.md 配置文件）的做法日益流行，但缺乏系统性证据表明这些冗长的职业专用提示词是否真正提升模型在相应领域任务上的表现，还是仅仅推高推理成本。作者希望通过受控实验量化其准确率收益与成本代价。

Method: 使用 Scientific Agents 开源语料库的 503 个职业专用 AGENTS.md 配置文件，通过 OpenRouter 调用 Gemini 3.8 Flash，在 Pi agent 框架下进行测试。采用五种提示条件对比：极简基线（"You are a helpful assistant"）、配置文件开头的角色句、通用科学严谨性指南、不相关领域的配置文件，以及匹配的领域配置文件。在 9 个文本类科学基准（4531 道抽样题、100 个匹配配置文件）上评估，4488 项经 API 错误重试后完成全部五种条件，用自动化规则评分。另在 60 道使用工具的 BioMysteryBench 生物信息学问题上对基线/配置文件各运行三次比对。

Result: 匹配配置文件与基线的平均准确率差为 -0.6 个百分点（95% bootstrap 区间 [-1.5, +0.2]），无任何基准显示统计上明显的提升。匹配配置文件的输出 token 是基线的 1.5–2.3 倍，每次成功调用的成本为 2.2–4.5 倍。BioMysteryBench 工具任务中，配置文件平均解题率 46.7%、基线 56.7%，差 -10.0 个百分点（95% 区间 [-16.7, -3.3]），主因是配置文件下更频繁出现 token/时间限制中止。唯一意外优势出现在 SuperGPQA：因提供商频繁 API 掉线，短基线仅有 54.0% 题目有正确的首轮答案，而配置文件为 71.6%；但通用和不匹配提示同样可靠，表明该收益来自提示长度或格式而非领域专业知识。

Conclusion: 对被测模型与任务而言，默认加载完整职业配置文件并不能提高准确率，反而显著增加成本；更长的提示唯一的运营收益源于其长度/格式（影响重试后的正确率），与领域专业性无关。选择性检索配置文件片段、或面向开放式科学任务的效果，仍有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Detailed profession-specific system prompts raise token use and estimated cost per response without a consistent accuracy gain. We evaluate Scientific Agents, an open-source corpus of 503 profession-specific AGENTS.md profiles, with Gemini 3.8 Flash via OpenRouter in the Pi agent harness. We compare matched profiles with four controls: a minimal baseline ("You are a helpful assistant"), the profile's opening role sentence, a generic scientific rigor guide, and a profile from an unrelated domain. Across nine text-based science benchmarks (4,531 sampled questions, 100 matched profiles), 4,488 items completed all five conditions after API-error retries, scored with automated, rule-based grading. The average profile-baseline accuracy difference is -0.6 percentage points (95% bootstrap interval [-1.5, +0.2] across fixed tasks), and no benchmark shows a statistically clear improvement. Matched profiles produced 1.5-2.3 times as many output tokens and cost 2.2-4.5 times more per successful call. On 60 tool-using BioMysteryBench bioinformatics problems (three runs each for baseline and profile), mean solve rates were 46.7% with the profile and 56.7% at baseline, a difference of -10.0 percentage points (95% interval [-16.7, -3.3]) driven by more frequent token- and time-limit stops under the profile. Longer prompts had one unexpected operational advantage: on SuperGPQA, frequent provider API drops left the short baseline with a correct first-pass answer on only 54.0% of items, against 71.6% with the profile. Generic and mismatched prompts were about as reliable, so this gain comes from prompt length or formatting rather than domain expertise. For the tested model and tasks, loading full profession profiles by default does not improve accuracy and costs considerably more; whether selective retrieval of profile sections or open-ended scientific tasks would change this remains to be tested.

</details>


### [86] [Comedic Fool's Gold: Reward Exploits and Countermeasures in Conversational Humor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00197)
*Sam Larson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究对话幽默场景下语言模型训练的自动奖励设计，发现基于惊喜度和受众笑声预测的奖励均存在可被利用的捷径，通过修正后虽提升综合评分但幽默专项改进未达预设目标，揭示自动奖励设计中"防漏洞"与"保留目标行为"之间的根本张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为语言模型在对话幽默任务上训练自动奖励信号极具挑战：研究者需要奖励既能捕捉"可理解的惊喜"又能预测受众愉悦，但以往工作表明奖励函数容易被非目标捷径利用。本文旨在系统识别此类奖励的漏洞并检验对应对策是否有效。

Method: 作者提出两条奖励路径：一是基于嵌入的"惊喜度"奖励，二是训练"受众模型"预测笑声。控制实验检验两者：对惊喜度奖励，尝试用词序打乱（word-shuffled）回复测试其是否与机敏回复同等得分，并引入流畅度过滤器作为对策；对受众模型，检验其是否被说话双方消息中的笑声线索（laughter cues）所欺骗，并采用跨说话人归一化这些线索作为对策。最后通过三轮强化学习训练，逐步迭代奖励修订并评估效果。

Result: 基于嵌入的惊喜奖励对词序打乱回复与机敏回复给予同等认可，暴露其漏洞；流畅度过滤器虽能识别打乱样本，但组合奖励也误拒了部分机敏回复，且未能通过进一步验证。受众模型预测的笑声易被任一说话方消息中的笑声线索所利用；对线索做跨说话人归一化可阻断已覆盖的攻击，但未被匹配的表达仍可被利用。三轮强化学习中，最终一轮将综合评估分数提升0.0903、零分会话减少40%，但幽默专项改进仍低于预先注册的目标。

Conclusion: 自动奖励设计面临更广泛的挑战：任何对策都必须同时做到"阻断可利用的捷径"与"保留奖励本意鼓励的行为"，二者之间存在张力。即使经过多轮修订，奖励仍可能遗留漏洞或误伤目标行为，提示研究者需审慎设计并持续验证奖励信号。

Abstract: We investigate automated rewards for training language models in conversational humor, focusing on reward exploits and countermeasures. Two approaches aim to capture understandable surprise and predicted audience amusement. Controlled tests show that an embedding-based surprise reward accepts word-shuffled replies as readily as witty ones. A fluency filter detects the shuffles, but the combined reward also rejects some witty replies and fails further validation. An audience model's predicted laughter is instead vulnerable to laughter cues in either speaker's messages. Normalizing these cues across speakers blocks the covered attacks, although unmatched expressions remain exploitable. Three reinforcement-learning runs evaluate training with successive reward revisions. The final run improves the combined evaluation score by 0.0903 and reduces zero-score sessions by 40%, but its humor-specific improvement remains below our preregistered target. These findings illustrate a broader challenge for automated reward design: countermeasures must block exploitable shortcuts while preserving the behavior the reward was intended to encourage.

</details>


### [87] [EviGraph: Proof-Carrying Selective Recommendation over Temporal Public-Service Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00212)
*Yixi Zhou,Sikun Wang,Lei Fan,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 EviGraph，通过区分“关键决策需求”与“可搁置信息”，在时间知识图谱上检索证据并判定推荐是否成立，从而在保证可靠性的同时减少公共服务的无效拒绝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共服务推荐依赖与请求的服务、范围和时间相匹配的证据，但把每个缺失细节都视为决定性因素会导致本可提供的推荐被过度拒绝。作者希望区分哪些信息是决策必须确立的（关键需求）、哪些可以暂时悬置，从而在可靠性与可用性之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出 EviGraph 框架：用语言智能体（language agent）将关键决策需求关联到时间知识图谱中的证据，再由确定性检查器（deterministic checker）验证推荐是否获得充分支持；通过区分必须确立的信息与可保留未决的信息来降低不必要的不响应。

Result: 在双语香港公共服务基准（含可执行政策引用）上评估表明：区分关键需求与可搁置信息能显著减少不必要的不响应；而额外增加验证反而会撤回本已成立的推荐，且并未提升决策质量。

Conclusion: 可靠的循证导航取决于明确“决策前必须确立什么”，而非简单地堆叠更多验证环节；过度验证可能适得其反。

Abstract: Public-service recommendations require evidence that matches the requested service, scope, and date. Yet treating every missing detail as decisive can withhold useful recommendations. We introduce EviGraph, which distinguishes critical decision requirements from information that can remain unresolved. A language agent links these requirements to evidence in a temporal knowledge graph, while a deterministic checker establishes whether a recommendation is supported. Evaluation on a bilingual Hong Kong public-service benchmark with executable policy references shows that this distinction reduces unnecessary abstention. Additional verification, however, can withdraw supported recommendations without improving decision quality. These findings suggest that reliable evidence-based navigation depends on specifying what must be established for a decision, rather than simply adding more verification.

</details>


### [88] [Build2SPARQL: A Large-Scale Text-to-SPARQL Benchmark Dataset for Building Knowledge Graph Querying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00224)
*Wooyoung Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Build2SPARQL，一个面向建筑知识图谱的大规模 text-to-SPARQL 基准，通过图遍历代码生成并验证 SPARQL 查询、由大模型仅生成自然语言问题，共产出 6,136 条可执行查询和 30,680 个问题，并有高人工验证保真度；检索增强使三款开源模型的精确匹配率从零样本 0.2–20% 提升至三样本检索的 56–65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑自动化系统正越来越多地用 Brick、ASHRAE 223P 等本体表示为语义知识图谱，这为 AI 应用（特别是让建筑运维人员通过语言代理以自然语言查询图谱的 text-to-SPARQL）提供了机器可读的基础；但该方向进展受限于缺少大规模自然语言/SPARQL 配对基准数据。

Method: 提出 KG-grounded（知识图谱锚定）生成管线：SPARQL 查询完全由图遍历代码生成并验证，大模型只负责生成对应的自然语言问题，从而让查询正确性不依赖模型行为。管线挖掘六类查询模式——线性链、分支、UNION、聚合、OPTIONAL、属性过滤，并在五种词汇语域（vocabulary registers）下改写每个查询。方法应用于 201 个建筑知识图谱（180 个 Brick、21 个 ASHRAE 223P）。

Result: 产出 6,136 条可执行 SPARQL 查询和 30,680 个自然语言问题。对 300 个问题做双人人工验证：语义保真度 98.8%、自然度 98.8%、操作合理性 84.0%。在三款开源权重语言模型上的检索增强评估将精确匹配准确率从零样本的 0.2–20% 提升至三样本检索的 56–65%。

Conclusion: Build2SPARQL 提供了一个高质量、大规模的建筑知识图谱 text-to-SPARQL 基准，其生成方式保证了查询正确性与模型解耦；实验显示检索增强能显著提升开源模型性能，表明该基准及方法可有效推动建筑领域语义查询的语言代理研究。

Abstract: Building automation systems are increasingly represented as semantic knowledge graphs (KGs) using ontologies such as Brick and ASHRAE 223P, creating a machine-readable substrate for artificial-intelligence applications. One promising application is translating natural-language questions into SPARQL (text-to-SPARQL), which would let building operators query these graphs through language agents, but progress is limited by the scarcity of large natural-language/SPARQL benchmarks. This paper presents Build2SPARQL, a large-scale benchmark for building KGs generated by a KG-grounded pipeline: SPARQL queries are produced and validated entirely by graph-traversal code, while large language models generate only the natural-language questions, keeping query correctness independent of model behavior. The pipeline mines six query-pattern families -- linear chains, branching, UNION, aggregation, OPTIONAL, and attribute-filtered -- and phrases each query across five vocabulary registers. Applied to 201 building KGs (180 Brick, 21 ASHRAE 223P), it yields 6,136 executable SPARQL queries and 30,680 questions. A two-rater human validation of 300 questions found 98.8% semantic fidelity, 98.8% naturalness, and 84.0% operational plausibility. A retrieval-augmented evaluation across three open-weight language models raised exact-match accuracy from 0.2-20% (zero-shot) to 56-65% (three-shot retrieved).

</details>


### [89] [Robust Is Salient: An Informed Adversary Moves the Optimal Signal onto the Salience Pole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00233)
*Cris Huynh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究对抗性信号博弈：当知情对手与发送者共享同一受限信号信道时，最能保护真相的信号即最能描述真相的信号。在 108 个验证项目上，抗对手最优解与先前的"显著性极点"完全重合；在 20 万条目的池中两者仅 2,748 项不同，且差异恰好落在先前的"显著性到贝叶斯坐标"未定义之处。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的显著性（salience）模型与贝叶斯推理模型在受限信号下存在理论张力，本文试图通过引入知情对手来统一并解释这一关系：为何"最能保护真相"与"最能描述真相"的信号会重合，以及该重合的结构性原因。

Method: 将对手引入强制选择任务（抽象自桌游《Deception: Murder in Hong Kong》）。对手知道目标、观察信号，并利用说服预算 β 论证最强错误答案。随 β 增大，最优信号从后验最大化选项转移到边际最大化选项；β=0 时该博弈还原为原始 oracle 模型（听者温度 τ=1）。在两个敌手框架下评估七个语言模型在 108 项上的选项变化，并与精确的零效应率对照。

Result: 抗对手最优解与显著性极点完全对齐（108/108 项）；在 20 万条目池中仅 2,748 项不同，且差异恰位于"显著性到贝叶斯坐标"未定义处。池中 18.2% 的项目在有限预算下最优解发生移动，且每项的临界预算精确。两个敌手框架使七个语言模型在 30-77/108 项上改变所选选项，远超精确零效应率；但无法判断该移动是朝向对手感知最优解还是朝向显著性，因为两者相同——这是结构性限制而非零结果。

Conclusion: 抗对手鲁棒性与显著性在结构上重合，导致任何测量都无法区分二者，构成对实证评估的结构性限制。建议在评估对手感知能力之前，先做一个廉价的诊断检查：验证鲁棒目标是否与评估项上的启发式目标重合。

Abstract: When an informed adversary shares the audience of a constrained signalling channel, the signal that best protects the truth is the signal that best describes it. On 108 confirmatory items, the adversary-robust optimum aligns exactly with the salience pole from prior work. Across a 200,000-item pool, the two differ on only 2,748 items --- lying exactly where the prior salience-to-Bayes coordinate is undefined. Where defined, robustness is achieved by moving from Bayesian discrimination entirely to salience. We show this by introducing an adversary to a forced-choice task (abstracted from Deception: Murder in Hong Kong). The adversary knows the target, observes the signal, and argues for the strongest wrong answer using a persuasion budget, $β$. As $β$ grows, the optimal signal shifts from the posterior-maximizing option to the margin-maximizing one; at $β= 0$, the game reproduces the original oracle model with a listener temperature of $τ= 1$. This effect is real: 18.2 percent of the pool has an optimum that shifts under a finite budget, and each item's critical budget is exact. This coincidence structurally limits empirical evaluation. Two adversary framings change the chosen option of seven language models on 30 to 77 of 108 items against an exact no-effect rate. Yet, no measurement can determine whether this movement is toward the adversary-aware optimum or toward salience, because the two options are identical. This is a structural limit, not a null result. The diagnostic check is cheap: before evaluating adversary-awareness, verify whether the robust target coincides with a heuristic target on the evaluation items.

</details>


### [90] [Conflicting Supervision Moves Commitment, Not Capability: A 12.29σ arrangement effect that is exactly zero under a convention-agnostic score](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00234)
*Wenhui Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究训练数据中两种互不兼容但各自正确的“惯例”（convention）的排序如何写入模型参数，核心发现是学习率调度器（而非数据排序本身）决定了排序效应的性质——衰减调度抑制排序效应，恒定学习率则在最后一步放大它；二者可分解为“排列(块周期)×调度(端点可给任一时刻的权重)”两个相乘因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前预训练中已报告“衰减学习率能缓和顺序效应”，但其机制、以及调度与数据排列两部分的分离尚未被说明和量化。作者希望揭示哪种惯例被模型“承诺”是由路径（排序与调度共同作用）写入参数的，并指出现有精确匹配基准（exact-match）无法观测到这一写入过程。

Method: 将同一语料以十种不同排序（保持预算、路径以外一切固定）训练，并在仅 lr_scheduler_type 不同的两个调度族下各跑两遍；恒定学习率族与单余弦调度族对照。通过分析模型对两种惯例的“分配份额”（allocation share）与对比度（contrast floors）来度量排序效应，并提出一个界，其中排列以“块周期”进入、调度以“端点能放在运行任一时刻的权重”进入，作为两个分开的相乘因子。

Result: 恒定学习率下，内部跨度达分配份额 0.2221、11.63 个对比度台阶，且随排列的“成块程度”单调变化；单余弦调度下同样十个排序仅落入两个可区分状态（而它们自身分辨率本可区分约十个）。跨十二个实验臂，acc_A+acc_B 在 9.7% 内恒定，而分配份额从 0.04 跑到 0.87，说明本文测得的 12.29σ 的排列切换在“惯例无关”度量下恰好为零——该守恒来自衰减调度族。在提示中标明惯例会瓦解这一切换，并达到并集上界的 87.5%。

Conclusion: “顺序重要”与“顺序不重要”是同一旋钮的两端；路径真正写入的是模型对某一惯例的承诺，而惯例无关的精确匹配基准无法看见它。学习率调度是决定排序效应幅度的平均算子，衰减调度通过避免“大步长与未收缩余项同时出现”来削弱排序效应。

Abstract: "Train a model on the same problems written under two incompatible conventions, both correct, and ask what the ordering of that data writes into the parameters. The learning-rate schedule is not a background condition for that question. It is the averaging operator, and it decides the answer. We prove a bound in which the arrangement and the schedule enter the ordering effect as separate multiplied factors: the arrangement only as a block period, the schedule only as how much weight the endpoint can place on any one moment of the run. A decaying schedule cannot put a large step size and an uncontracted remainder at the same moment; a constant one does exactly that at the last step. That decay moderates ordering effects has been reported in pretraining; the mechanism, the separation, and a controlled measurement of both halves are ours. Ten orderings of one corpus, one budget, everything but the path held fixed, run twice under families differing in lr_scheduler_type and nothing else: at a constant rate the interior spans 0.2221 in allocation, 11.63 contrast floors, monotone in how blocked the arrangement is. Under the single cosine every published arm uses, the same ten arms occupy two distinguishable states where their own resolution would allow about ten. "Order matters" and "order does not matter" are the two ends of one knob. What the path writes is which convention the model commits to, and no exact-match benchmark can see it. Across twelve arms acc_A+acc_B is constant to within 9.7% while the allocation share runs 0.04 to 0.87, so the 12.29-sigma arrangement switch this paper measures is exactly zero under a convention-agnostic metric. That conservation is quoted from the decayed family throughout, the constant-rate one being a noisier place to read it. Marking the convention in the prompt collapses the switch and reaches 87.5% of the union ceiling."

</details>


### [91] [Knowing When to Yield: Grounded Arbitration of User Corrections in Text-Based Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00282)
*Yezhou Cheng,Runjia Du,Zeming Liu,Hang Lyu,Zehua Yang,Bojun Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 GAVA（grounded correction arbitration），将具身智能体面对可能错误的纠正时的响应建模为接受、拒绝、检查世界、询问说话者四选一，并通过观察有界证据、合法探测与一步期望损失规则实现。在文本 ALFWorld 上，完全局部检查使 GAVA 与"总是验证"均达 100% 纠正准确率；GAVA 在同episode执行中降低了交互成本但在完美说话者下与成本阈值打平。训练期物体-位置先验在 340 个未见场景上显著降低交互与联合声明成本，且在 308 个非重叠已见场景上复现（置信区间排除零）。语义 GAVA 准确率约 98.8%/98.7%，所有方法均完成任务。结果支持声明成本下的选择性信息收集与语义先验，但未证明环境信息价值总体上优于澄清，且评估不含人类、视觉输入或物理机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当人的纠正可能出错时，具身智能体应如何响应（接受、拒绝、检查环境还是追问）缺乏一个形式化的仲裁框架与证据契约，需要在声明成本下权衡环境检验与澄清提问的价值，并证明其相对于"总是验证"等基线的成本优势。

Method: 将 grounded correction arbitration 形式化为接受/拒绝/检查世界/询问说话者的选择问题；GAVA 采用观察有界证据、合法探测（legal probes）和一步期望损失规则。实验在文本 ALFWorld 上进行，162 个检查点产生 972 组真假干预对；先做完整局部检查评估纠正准确率（100%），再评测同episode执行中的交互成本；引入仅训练期物体-位置先验与语义 GAVA；对策略、成本、基线和多重性计划冻结后进行预注册式复现，并用 95% checkpoint-bootstrap 置信区间检验。

Result: 完整局部检查下 GAVA 与总是验证均 100% 准确，确立的是证据契约而非比较优势；同episode执行中 GAVA 相较总是验证降低了交互成本，但在完美说话者下与成本阈值打平。训练期物体-位置先验使 GAVA 在 340 个未见场景上的交互成本与声明联合成本较均匀 GAVA 分别降低 0.490 和 0.420；该收益在 77 个非重叠已见检查点（308 场景）上复现为 0.595 和 0.517，95% bootstrap 置信区间均不含零。联合成本也优于相同先验的固定策略，但与匹配校准的无 VOI 比较不显著。语义 GAVA 两队列各犯 4 个事实错误（98.8%/98.7% 准确率），所有方法完成全部任务。

Conclusion: 研究表明在声明成本、语义先验下进行选择性信息收集是有效的，但未建立环境信息价值（VOI）相对澄清的普遍优势。研究结论受限于标准化声明、完整符号观测与受控说话者，未涵盖人类参与者、视觉输入或物理机器人，泛化性有限。

Abstract: How should an embodied agent respond when a person's correction may be wrong? We formulate grounded correction arbitration as a choice among accepting, rejecting, inspecting the world, and asking the speaker. GAVA implements this interface with observation-bounded evidence, legal probes, and a one-step expected-loss rule. In text-only ALFWorld, 162 checkpoints produce 972 paired true and false interventions. Complete local inspections give GAVA and always verify 100 percent correction accuracy, establishing the evidence contract rather than a comparative advantage. In same-episode execution, GAVA reduces interaction cost against always verify but ties a cost threshold under a perfect speaker. An exploratory training-only object-location prior lowers interaction and declared joint cost on 340 unseen scenarios by 0.490 and 0.420 relative to uniform GAVA. After freezing the policy, costs, baselines, and multiplicity plan, the gains replicate on 77 non-overlapping seen checkpoints, covering 308 scenarios: 0.595 and 0.517, with both 95 percent checkpoint-bootstrap confidence intervals excluding zero. Joint cost also improves over an identical-prior fixed policy, while the matched calibrated no-VOI comparison remains inconclusive. Semantic GAVA makes four factual errors in each cohort, corresponding to 98.8 percent and 98.7 percent accuracy, and all methods complete every task. Results support selective information gathering with semantic priors under declared costs, but do not establish a general advantage of environmental value of information over clarification. The study uses normalized claims, complete symbolic observations, and controlled speakers; it evaluates neither human participants, visual input, nor physical robots.

</details>


### [92] [Rules to Tools: Executable Checks for LLM Agents in Scientific Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00313)
*Jingjie Ning,Guojiang Zhao,Chen Xu,Shanshan Zhong,Xiaochuan Li,Ji Zeng,Guolin Ke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过对比科学编码智能体在"只读文字规范"与"配给可执行预备检查(R2T)"两种条件下的程序修复表现，发现预备检查能小幅提升修复完成率，但收益因任务而异，且伴随公开CPU开销上升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学编码智能体需依据方程、边界条件和输出要求来评估并修复其编写的程序。现有的仅文字形式的规范难以被程序化验证，作者希望通过为公开科学要求提供"预先制备的可执行检查"（Rules to Tools, R2T），取代智能体自行将规则转译为实现的做法，从而衡量这一工具化支持能否改善修复结果并降低智能体侧成本。

Method: 在匹配的 SciCode 修复组中，文字组与工具组共享相同的文字检查、起始程序、模型和预算；工具组额外获得一个可调用的检查实现。研究在多个队列上对比：两个任务ID队列（各30项）、一个8任务ID队列（16项）、一个更大的共享定义SciCode队列（24项/组），以及5个开发阶段暴露任务（用备选起始程序，10项）。同时设置"由源码经Python"的新增实验臂，并进行匹配的偏微分方程(PDE)对比，记录修复完成率、任务簇引导法(bootstrap)的95%置信区间、初始检查标记情况、模型输出量及公开CPU使用。

Result: 两个任务ID队列中，仅文字组完全修复26/30，配预备检查组29/30；其中3个任务ID偏向工具、1个偏向文字、11个持平。8任务ID队列为13/16对15/16，任务簇bootstrap的95%区间为[-12.5, 43.75]个百分点。更大的共享定义SciCode队列两组均持平为13/24。5个开发暴露任务为3/10对7/10。工具组在任务17、77、11上占优（初始检查仅标记任务17，任务77、11未报告违规）；任务37偏向文字且无初始违规报告。新增的"源码经Python"实验臂也达到15/16，与专用命令的汇总成绩持平。匹配PDE对比中，详细文字为23/24、检查为24/24，检查组报告模型输出量低31.2%。智能体侧输出节省因队列而异，而两个任务ID队列的公开CPU使用均上升。

Conclusion: 预先制备的可执行检查在多数设置下带来小幅修复率提升，但收益高度依赖任务，部分任务反而文字组更优；工具化支持降低了智能体报告的输出量，却增加了公开CPU消耗，说明其代价并未消失而是转移。研究成果量化了配预备检查条件下任务依赖的修复结果与智能体侧成本。

Abstract: Scientific coding agents receive equations, boundary conditions, and output requirements in writing, then must assess the programs they revise. Rules to Tools (R2T) supplies prepared executable checks of public scientific requirements. Matched SciCode repair groups share written checks, starting programs, model, and budgets; the tool group receives a callable implementation. Across two task-ID cohorts, complete repair is 26/30 with text and 29/30 with the prepared checks. Three task IDs favor tools, one favors text, and eleven tie. The eight-ID cohort scores 13/16 versus 15/16, with a task-cluster bootstrap 95% interval of [-12.5, 43.75] percentage points for the difference. The larger shared-definition SciCode cohort ties at 13/24 per group. Five development-exposed tasks with alternate starting programs score 3/10 versus 7/10. The tool group favors tasks 17, 77, and 11; initial checks flag task 17 and report no violation for tasks 77 and 11. Task 37 favors text and has no initial reported violation. A fresh source-through-Python arm also reaches 15/16, matching the dedicated command's aggregate. In a matched PDE comparison, detailed text scores 23/24 and checks score 24/24, with 31.2% lower reported model output for checks. Agent-side output savings vary by cohort, while public CPU use rises in both task-ID cohorts. These results measure task-dependent repair outcomes and agent-side costs with prepared checks.

</details>


### [93] [Predictive Credit: Measuring What Scientific Explanations Add to Experimental Forecasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00314)
*Jingjie Ning,Xueqi Li,Yibo Kong,Dongting Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究代理解释计划实验，但其"自然解释"带来的预测增益在当前测试分辨率下未能得到确认，仅特定直接文本方式在部分次级指标上显示改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究代理（research agents）通常会解释其计划进行的实验，但缺乏一种系统方法来度量这些解释是否真正带来"预测性信用"（predictive credit）——即解释内容能否实际提升对实验结果的预测能力。作者希望建立一个严格的协议，区分解释、匹配描述、捐赠者情境等不同信息源对预测的贡献。

Method: 采用配对预测（paired forecasts）设计，固定干预、预测者与结果变量，仅变换描述方式、匹配解释与捐赠者情境三要素；并通过五项检查追踪承诺、交付、预测增益、对齐性与已知信号吸收。在三个数据域验证：336个受控学习中的前瞻性状态、12个Tox21端点、24个OpenML任务。使用多版本模型（如v5、DeepSeek V4 Pro、DeepSeek V4 Flash）执行冻结的信用判定，并设置研究者撰写的机制正对照（positive control），以区间得分（interval score）、点准确度（point MAE）、覆盖率、等效性等指标评估。

Result: v5的冻结信用判定在多域中均不具结论性；Tox21预注册的ROC AUC区间得分损害检验未通过（D−M=−.0026，95%区间[−.0174,.0104]）；OpenML的联合形成、点等效与可重复性规则未满足。匹配点准确度相对描述未获确认，Tox21/OpenML种子-捐赠者区间跨越零。在DeepSeek V4 Pro下，匹配卡与捐赠者卡分别使次级Tox21漂移降低64.5%和59.1%；V4 Flash重放使匹配点MAE从.01823升到.02020，且未达匹配-捐赠者区间得分等效。OpenML全卡分配使名义80%区间宽度扩大21%，覆盖率49.3%（描述与内容为51.4%，覆盖66/144张卡）。直接文本Flash交付全部144条注释但无可检测的匹配点准确度增益。正对照降低点MAE 2.60个百分点。总体而言，自然解释的信用在测试的捐赠者分辨率下仍未获确认。

Conclusion: 该协议为研究代理基准和科学预测的"预测性信用"度量提供了可行框架，且正对照验证了方法灵敏度；但自然解释带来的预测增益在当前捐赠者分辨率下未获确认，提示需要更高的解释分辨率或更精细的机制分解才能量化解释的真正贡献。

Abstract: Research agents explain planned experiments. We measure predictive credit with paired forecasts sharing an intervention, forecaster, and outcome while varying description, matched explanation, and donor context. Five checks track commitment, delivery, predictive gain, alignment, and known-signal uptake. Across 336 prospective states in controlled learning, 12 Tox21 endpoints, and 24 OpenML tasks, v5's frozen credit decision was inconclusive. Tox21's preregistered ROC AUC interval-score harm test was unmet ($D-M=-.0026$, 95 percent interval [$-.0174$, .0104]); OpenML's joint formation, point-equivalence, and repeatability rule was unmet. Matched point-accuracy gains over description remained unconfirmed, and Tox21/OpenML seed-donor intervals spanned zero. Under requested DeepSeek V4 Pro, matched and donor cards reduced secondary Tox21 drift by 64.5 and 59.1 percent. A DeepSeek V4 Flash replay raised matched point MAE from .01823 to .02020 and missed matched-donor interval-score equivalence. OpenML full-card assignment widened nominal 80 percent intervals by 21 percent, with 49.3 percent coverage versus 51.4 percent for description and content in 66/144 cards. Direct-text Flash delivered all 144 notes without detectable matched point-accuracy gain. A researcher-authored mechanism positive control lowered point MAE by 2.60 percentage points versus description. The protocol measures predictive credit for research-agent benchmarks and scientific forecasting; natural-explanation credit remained unconfirmed at the tested donor resolutions.

</details>


### [94] [ContractRL: Shielded Group-Relative Policy Optimization for Auditable Tool-Call Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00328)
*Miaobo Hu,Shuhao Hu,Xiaobo Guo,Xin Wang,Bokun Wang,Yina Sa,Daren Zha,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ContractRL 是一个契约约束的序列化修复协议，将验证器引导的 JSON 修复建模为有界决策过程，通过 RFC-6902 动作掩码与预算机制实现局部修补，比全量重生成与监督微调更高效、更可审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化工具调用常在少量字段违反模式或执行契约时失败，而重新生成完整对象会扩大动作面且难以审计反复修复过程，因此需要一种局部、可控、可追溯的修复方法。

Method: 提出 ContractRL，将验证器引导的 JSON 修复建模为有界决策过程：每一步策略观察候选对象、带类型的验证器反馈、JSON Pointer、不可变修复历史与剩余预算；契约派生的动作掩码在执行确定性验证器转换前，过滤掉畸形或被 RFC-6902 禁止的操作。同时定义契约约束的组相对目标，用于补丁、重试与弃权决策，并在轨迹冻结前将规范目标与语义标签保持在在线状态之外；后续用策略优化（强化学习）进一步提升性能。

Result: 在相同验证器信息下，每种子 192 个案例、共 5 个种子，ContractRL 达到 0.9362 语义成功率、平均 34.4 生成 token，优于 Patch-SFT（0.9076，44.9 token）与全量重生成（0.9148，137.2 token）。策略优化将语义成功率从监督版 ContractRL 的 0.9186 提升至 0.9375。另一次三种子配对评估相对于 Patch-SFT 的语义差为 +0.0396（95% CI [+0.0137,+0.0662]，p=0.0039）。反馈、动作掩码、预算与模式偏移分析表明增益源于局部修正；对抗性与多轮评估刻画了剩余失败模式。

Conclusion: ContractRL 通过契约约束的局部序列化修复，在更少 token 下取得更高语义成功率，并提供可审计的修复轨迹；强化学习策略优化进一步带来显著提升，剩余失败模式主要见于对抗与多轮场景。

Abstract: Structured tool calls often fail after only a small number of fields violate a schema or an execution contract. Regenerating the complete object enlarges the action surface and makes repeated repair difficult to audit. We introduce ContractRL, a contract-constrained sequential repair protocol that models verifier-guided JSON repair as a bounded decision process. At each step the policy observes the candidate, typed verifier feedback, JSON Pointer, immutable repair history, and remaining budget; a contract-derived action mask filters malformed or prohibited RFC-6902 operations before a deterministic validator performs the transition. We specify a contract-constrained group-relative objective for patch, retry, and abstention decisions while keeping canonical targets and semantic labels outside the online state until trace freeze. Under identical verifier information, ContractRL attains 0.9362 semantic success with 34.4 generated tokens, compared with 0.9076 and 44.9 tokens for Patch-SFT and 0.9148 and 137.2 tokens for full regeneration over 192 cases per seed and five seeds. Policy optimization improves semantic success from 0.9186 for supervised ContractRL to 0.9375. A separate three-seed paired evaluation against Patch-SFT yields a semantic difference of +0.0396 (95% CI $[+0.0137,+0.0662], p=0.0039$). Feedback, action-mask, budget, and schema-shift analyses connect these gains to localized correction, while adversarial and multi-turn evaluations characterize the remaining failure modes.

</details>


### [95] [Mathematical Transfer in LLMs Follows Reasoning Approach More Than Topic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00331)
*Sajad Goudarzi,Samaneh Zamanifard,Seyed Amin Seyed Haeri,Moloud Nasiri,Hamed Rahimian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探讨数学训练数据的组织原则：按推理方法（共享解题方法）匹配是否比按主题（共享数学领域）匹配更能促进微调后的跨题迁移。通过两个平衡的2×2实验设计，结果一致显示"同方法不同主题"的数据在所有40个模型-目标比较中均优于"同主题不同方法"的数据，尽管同主题数据在表面相似度上更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为LLM选择数学训练数据时，通常按主题（topic）组织（如用概率题训练概率目标），但另一种思路是按推理方法（reasoning approach）组织（如共享解题方法的题目，即便数学领域不同）。作者希望确定哪一种匹配关系在微调后产生更大的迁移效果。

Method: 采用两个反平衡（counterbalanced）2×2设计：(1) 概率与组合数学 交叉 不变量推理与双重计数（2000题）；(2) 数论与几何 交叉 补集推理与鸽巢原理（800题）。每个设计中的每个单元格轮流作为留出目标（held-out target）：同方法（SA）来源与目标共享方法但主题不同，同主题（ST）来源与目标共享主题但方法不同。每个来源在各角色中各出现一次，使加性来源质量效应在等权聚合对比中相互抵消。在五个基础模型和每个设计三个训练种子上进行评估。

Result: SA在所有40个种子池化的模型-目标比较中均优于ST。主设计中模型层面优势为8.2至16.2个百分点（均值10.8），第二设计中为12.0至16.0个百分点（均值14.3）；全部十个模型层面的95%置信区间均不包含零。在两个设计中，ST来源在嵌入和词汇度量上与目标更相似，因此SA的优势与语句层面相似度的测量排序方向相反。

Conclusion: 在所评估的主题-方法组合范围内，推理方法作为数学迁移的匹配准则比主题更有效。这一发现提示，为提升LLM的数学能力迁移，应按共享解题方法而非共享数学领域来组织和选择训练数据。

Abstract: When selecting mathematical training data for LLMs, a natural organizing principle is topic: probability examples for probability targets. An alternative is reasoning approach: worked solutions that share a solution method with the target, even when the mathematical domain differs. We ask which relation produces greater transfer after fine-tuning. We evaluate two counterbalanced $2\times2$ designs: probability and combinatorics crossed with invariant reasoning and double counting (2,000 problems), and number theory and geometry crossed with complement and pigeonhole reasoning (800 problems). In each design, every cell serves as the held-out target in turn: same-approach (SA) sources share the target's method but change the topic, while same-topic (ST) sources share the topic but change the method. Every source appears once in each role, so additive source-quality effects cancel from the equally weighted aggregate contrast. Across five base models and three training seeds per design, SA outperforms ST in all 40 seed-pooled model--target comparisons. Model-level advantages range from 8.2 to 16.2 percentage points in the primary design (mean: 10.8) and from 12.0 to 16.0 in the second design (mean: 14.3); all ten model-level 95% confidence intervals exclude zero. In both designs, ST sources are more similar to targets under embedding and lexical measures, so the SA advantage runs opposite to the measured ordering of statement-level resemblance. These findings identify reasoning approach as a more effective matching criterion than topic for mathematical transfer across the evaluated topic--approach combinations.

</details>


### [96] [Fault-Tolerant Budget Conservation in Distributed Multi-Agent Delegation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00349)
*Genliang Zhu,Chu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并形式化验证了一种面向分布式多智能体委派的容错预算守恒机制：预算被量化为排他性托管信用，通过委派DAG流转，并借助签名调度许可、隔离区、网关核验、认证结算与永久退役等机制，在崩溃、重试、重复、分区、汇聚与迟到完成等故障下仍保持预算上限不被超支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源限制正成为跨并发且易故障工作节点委派任务的AI智能体的授权边界；现有的父子分配约束、仿射对象和分布式托管机制单独使用时，无法在回复丢失、超时后效果完成、消息重复、分支分区或DAG汇聚别名同一谱系等情形下防止超支。

Method: 将预算建模为可量化的资源向量，由排他性托管信用表示，并在委派DAG中流动。分发前，分支将信用转换为绑定谱系、纪元、规范化效果、最大收费、接收方和幂等键的操作预留；持久化带隔离区的签名调度许可，网关在首次接受前核验许可。不确定效果持续计费，直至认证结算、有围栏的权威无效果证明或永久退役。作者在显式中介、持久性、认证、规范化和网关假设下，证明所有权分区、账本与效果守恒、后代不放大、至多一次结算、迟到完成安全与分区限制等性质，并用有界TLA+检查、独立JavaScript探索器和崩溃注入的双进程SQLite实验验证，且能检出超时退款与历史证书验证突变体。

Result: 该机制在所评估的崩溃、重试、重复、分区、汇聚和迟到完成调度下均保持所发行预算上限。不可区分性结果表明，分区局部可用性要求排他性预分配；模型检查与实验能检测出超时退款和历史证书验证两类突变体。

Conclusion: 通过排他托管信用、签名调度许可、认证结算与永久退役等机制，可以实现分布式多智能体委派中的容错预算守恒，在多种故障与异常调度下仍保持预算上限，从而为AI智能体跨并发挥发性工作节点的资源授权提供可靠边界。

Abstract: Resource limits are becoming an authorization boundary for AI agents that delegate work across concurrent and failure-prone workers. Parent-child allocation constraints, affine objects, and distributed escrow do not by themselves prevent overspend when replies are lost, effects complete after timeout, messages repeat, branches partition, or DAG joins alias one lineage. We formalize fault-tolerant budget conservation for distributed multi-agent delegation. Budgets are quantized resource vectors represented by exclusive escrow credits that move through a delegation DAG. Before dispatch, a branch converts credit into an operation reservation bound to lineage, epoch, normalized effect, maximum charge, receiver, and idempotency key. It persists a signed dispatch permit with quarantine; the gateway verifies that permit before first acceptance. Uncertain effects remain charged until authenticated settlement, a fenced authoritative no-effect proof, or permanent retirement. We prove ownership partition, ledger and effect conservation, descendant non-amplification, at-most-once settlement, late-completion safety, and partition confinement under explicit mediation, durability, authentication, normalization, and gateway assumptions. An indistinguishability result shows that partition-local availability requires exclusive preallocation. Bounded TLA+ checking, an independent JavaScript explorer, and crash-injected two-process SQLite experiments exercise the declared scope and detect timeout-refund and historical-certificate-validation mutants. The mechanism preserves the issued budget bound across the evaluated crash, retry, duplicate, partition, join, and late-completion schedules.

</details>


### [97] [JusticeAxis: Benchmarking Legal Judgment between Rigid Rule Application and Ungrounded Discretion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00353)
*Zhengkai Tu,Mingda Zhang,Zijia Wang,Xiaoying Tang,Jimmy Huang*

Main category: cs.AI
