TL;DR: 本文提出将法律裁判形式化为"锚定于参考"的任务，并构建了包含256个跨国真实刑事案件及九份律师撰写裁判的基准JusticeAxis，以及可插拔的JusticeAgent框架；实验表明模型规模会使裁判失败呈现相反偏向，而JusticeAgent可将冻结的开源模型提升至商用水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律裁判方法在"僵化的法条匹配"与"无依据的自由裁量"之间摇摆：基准只评一个标签或评分量表，而真正能提供平衡的"经验"从未被验证。作者希望裁判既能锚定法条、又能锚定案件具体情形，并以可验证方式引入经验。

Method: 将法律裁判形式化为"参考锚定"任务：目标是生成单一决定，同时锚定于法条与案情。构建JusticeAxis基准（18国256个真实刑事案件，含音频、图像、文本证据，每案三份律师撰写裁判：真实裁判与两个失败情形各一份）。提出JusticeAgent框架：由要素智能体（element agents）认定事实，由法官智能体（judge agent）在携带"情形经验"的技能（skills）下适用法律；技能从执行轨迹中蒸馏，并仅在满足贝叶斯可信区间（Bayesian credible bounds）时才被采纳。

Result: 实验显示失败随模型规模呈现方向性转变：开源权重骨干模型漂向"无依据的理由"，而前沿（商用）模型漂向"法条默认值"。JusticeAgent作为简单而有效的插件，可将冻结的开源骨干模型提升至商用水平。

Conclusion: 通过把法律裁判锚定于法条与案情，并以贝叶斯可信区间约束从执行轨迹蒸馏的技能，JusticeAxis与JusticeAgent为可验证、平衡的法律裁判提供了基准与框架，有效解决了现有方法中僵化匹配与无依据裁量之间的失衡问题。

Abstract: A sound judgment applies the law to established facts and weighs the circumstances in which they arose. However, existing methods swing between rigid statute matching and ungrounded discretion, benchmarks score a label or a rubric, and the experience that would supply the balance stays unverified. We formalize legal judgment as a reference-anchored task, whose object is a single decision that stays tied to the statute and to the circumstances at once. We introduce JusticeAxis, 256 real-world criminal cases from 18 countries with audio, image, and text evidence, and three lawyer-written judgments for every case: the recorded one and one for each failure. We further propose JusticeAgent, a harness whose element agents establish the facts and whose judge agent applies the law under skills carrying experience of the circumstances. Skills are distilled from execution trajectories and admitted only under Bayesian credible bounds. Experiments show that failure turns direction with scale: open-weight backbones drift to unsupported grounds, frontier models to the statutory default. We further verify that JusticeAgent, as a simple yet effective plugin, carries a frozen open-weight backbone to commercial level. Project resources are available at https://github.com/beita6969/JusticeAxis.

</details>


### [98] [What Should an Agent Remember? Disentangling Retention from Retrieval in Bounded-Memory Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00366)
*Juli Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建流式召回基准，发现记忆评估易混淆保留与选择两个因素；保持访问方式固定时，查询感知选择仅提升15.5个百分点，而混合比较因改变历史访问夸大了68.7点的优势，其中53.2点来自访问差异；有界保留下各类选择均达保留上限，失败主要由淘汰而非排序导致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化智能体需同时决定"信息到达时保留什么"和"查询出现时呈现什么"，但现有记忆评估常将保留与选择规则混在一起比较，导致无法区分性能提升究竟来自保留策略还是选择策略，从而产生误导性结论。

Method: 构建流式召回基准，交叉组合保留规则与选择规则，在300个相同的种子片段(seeded episodes)上评估每种条件；通过控制变量法，分别固定访问方式(access)以单独考察选择(selection)的影响，并设置有界保留(bounded retention)条件，测试查询感知(query-aware)、稠密(dense)与预言(oracle)选择是否达到保留上限，同时区分淘汰(eviction)错误与排序(ranking)错误；最后在SQuAD上复现评估以验证结论泛化性。

Result: 在固定访问方式下，查询感知选择使所需事实召回率提升15.5个百分点(95% CI: 12.8–18.2)；而同时改变历史访问的混合比较报告了68.7个百分点的优势，其中53.2个百分点可归因于访问差异。在有界保留条件下，查询感知、稠密与预言选择均达到保留上限；有界近因(bounded recency)条件下观察到的319次失败全部由淘汰错误而非排序错误造成。当目标回退到足够远的过去时，召回率降至0%。在SQuAD上的复现保持了保留上限结论，并显示稠密检索在自然文本上可优于词法检索。

Conclusion: 有界记忆评估应固定访问方式，并将保留与选择分别报告，以避免混淆两个因素的贡献；同时，淘汰机制（而非排序）是制约召回的主要瓶颈，随着信息回退加深召回趋于零。

Abstract: A persistent agent must decide both what to retain as information arrives and what to surface once a query appears, yet memory evaluations can confound these decisions by comparing methods that differ in both retention and selection. We build a streaming-recall benchmark crossing retention and selection rules and evaluate every condition on the same 300 seeded episodes. Holding access fixed, query-aware selection improves required-fact recall by 15.5 percentage points (95% CI: 12.8 to 18.2), whereas a mixed comparison that also changes history access reports a 68.7-point advantage, of which 53.2 points are attributable to access. Under bounded retention, query-aware, dense, and oracle selection reach the retention ceiling, and all 319 observed failures in the bounded recency condition are caused by eviction rather than ranking errors. Recall falls to 0% as targets recede sufficiently far into the past. Repeating the evaluation on SQuAD preserves the retention ceiling while showing that dense retrieval can outperform lexical retrieval on natural text. These results show that bounded-memory evaluations should hold access fixed and report retention and selection separately.

</details>


### [99] [When Harnesses Lose the Signal: Causal Evaluation of Recovery in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00372)
*Shuyao Xiao,Shengling Wang,Xuan Chen,Ke Chao,Ming Cui,Feifei Qian,Chaoyang Mei,Fanlin Meng,Ziming Yu,Junxi Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将错误恢复视为因果决策问题：同一恢复操作既能挽救失败轨迹也能破坏本可成功的轨迹，并据此设计 Causal Intervention Router（CIR）实现选择性干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对 LLM 智能体错误恢复的评价只看平均任务成功率，掩盖了恢复操作的双刃剑效应——同一操作既可挽救失败轨迹，也可能干扰本应成功的轨迹。作者希望更细致地评估恢复的价值，并据此决定何时值得干预。

Method: 将恢复建模为因果决策问题：从相同执行状态出发，对比有无恢复的后续结果，分离"挽救"与"伤害"效应，并研究恢复价值随时间的演变。基于此提出 Causal Intervention Router（CIR），一个轻量级策略，利用恢复发生前可获得的（动作、环境）信息判断干预是否值得。

Result: 在长时程 ALFWorld 任务上使用 Qwen3-14B，CIR 将成功率从 70.33% 提升至 73.33%（+3.00 个百分点），且对所有观测正确的已评估轨迹不作任何改动。进一步对照实验表明，恢复的收益无法仅用环境返回的新观测来解释。

Conclusion: 论文提出了一种评估恢复并选择性应用恢复的实用方法：将恢复视为因果决策问题，用 CIR 只在值得时干预，从而在长时程任务中稳定提升成功率，同时避免对正确轨迹造成伤害。

Abstract: Large language model agents rely on external harnesses to pass information between the model and its environment and to recover from execution errors. Yet recovery is usually judged only by average task success. This hides an important tension. The same operation can rescue a failing trajectory or disrupt one that would otherwise succeed. We frame recovery as a causal decision problem. Starting from the same execution state, we compare what happens with and without recovery, separate rescue from harm, and study how the value of recovery changes over time. We then introduce the Causal Intervention Router (CIR), a lightweight policy that uses information available before recovery to decide when intervention is worthwhile. On long-horizon ALFWorld tasks with Qwen3-14B, CIR raises success from 70.33% to 73.33%, a gain of 3.00 percentage points. It leaves all evaluated trajectories with correct observations untouched. Additional controls show that the benefit of recovery cannot be explained solely by the new observation returned by the environment. These results provide a practical way to evaluate recovery and apply it selectively.

</details>


### [100] [Benchmarking Prompt Optimization of Large Language Models With Chess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00416)
*Timothée Lesort,Alejandra López de Aberasturi Gómez,Tristan Karch,Tom Veniat,Philippe Modard,Karl Tuyls,Ludovic Denoyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于 1,118 道 Lichess 国际象棋谜题的基准，用于研究冻结大语言模型的自动提示优化（APO），其可廉价、确定地评分，难度可调且资源可再生，并在六种 APO 算法与八个模型上验证了其挑战性、区分性、可再生性与可负担性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型能力提升，传统基准面临饱和、测试集污染以及评估昂贵昂贵等问题；而自动提示优化（APO）在整个搜索过程中反复评估，更需要一个评分廉价且确定、难度足够留有余地、且能随模型演进不断更新的基准。

Method: 作者构建了一个由 1,118 道 Lichess 谜题组成的国际象棋基准来研究冻结 LLM 的 APO（只优化提示、不更新权重）。该基准结合了廉价的精确匹配评分、基于引擎的替代走法评估，以及难度可调的可持续更新谜题供给；除孤立的测试项成功率外，还把解谜提升与同一领域内的短对局 rollout 联系起来。用该基准在八个目标模型上评估六种 APO 算法，测量基线强度、各模型对优化的响应程度，以及优化后提示的跨模型迁移性和对实际对局的迁移效果。

Result: 该基准被证明：(i) 具有挑战性——最强的被评估模型 Gemini 3.5 Flash（用作元模型）仅能解出约 55% 的谜题；(ii) 具有区分性——在不同方法和模型间显现出性能提升、持平与退化；(iii) 可再生——可用新谜题降低污染风险、可调难度以在模型进步时保留提升空间；(iv) 可负担——整个研究的运行成本约 800 美元。作者已发布谜题、优化与评估代码及数据集更新脚本。

Conclusion: 国际象棋是 APO 的理想基准：评分廉价而确定、难度足够大、可随模型演进持续更新且成本可控，能有效区分不同 APO 方法与模型的优化响应及迁移能力，为研究冻结 LLM 的提示优化提供了可持续的实验平台。

Abstract: Evaluating large language models becomes increasingly challenging as their capabilities advance: benchmarks can saturate, public test sets risk contamination, and assessing harder tasks can require expensive grading or execution infrastructure. These challenges are amplified in automatic prompt optimization (APO), where evaluation is repeated throughout the search for better prompts. Studying APO therefore requires a benchmark that is cheap and deterministic to score, hard enough to leave room for improvement, and renewable as models evolve. We introduce a chess benchmark built from 1,118 Lichess puzzles to study APO for frozen LLMs: we optimize their prompts without updating their model weights. Chess combines inexpensive exact-match scoring, engine-based evaluation of alternative moves, and a renewable supply of problems with adjustable difficulty. Unlike evaluations that report only success on isolated test items, the benchmark also connects puzzle-solving gains to short game-play rollouts within the same domain. We use it to evaluate six APO algorithms on eight target models, measuring not only baseline strength but also how much each model responds to optimization and whether optimized prompts transfer across models and to game play. Chess is thus a well-suited benchmark for APO: it is (i) challenging, as even the strongest evaluated model, Gemini 3.5 Flash (used as the meta-model), solves only about 55\% of puzzles; (ii) discriminative, revealing gains, unchanged performance, and regressions across methods and models; (iii) renewable, with fresh puzzles to reduce contamination risk and adjustable difficulty to maintain headroom as models improve; and (iv) affordable, as the complete study runs for around \$800. We release the puzzles, optimization and evaluation code, and dataset-renewal scripts (https://github.com/imec-ailabs/Automatic-Prompt-Optimization-with-Chess).

</details>


### [101] [JevSpawn: Adaptive Agentic Inference through Compositional Action Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00437)
*Haoyang Su,Weiran Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JevSpawn 提出一种组合式策略，将自然语言任务说明与有限概率探索连接起来，通过并行动作生成、反馈驱动的分支选择、表示修正及从保留备选中恢复，在不额外训练的情况下提升智能体推理速度与任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体逐 token 生成推理与动作，导致长交互缓慢且计算昂贵；Jev 风格模型虽能对有限字段提供快速概率预测，但要求字段预先指定，限制了需要从自然语言指令中动态推导并随交互调整可用动作的自主任务求解场景。

Method: 提出 JevSpawn 组合式策略：将并行动作生成（spawning）与反馈驱动的分支选择、表示修正、以及从保留备选方案中恢复相结合；利用共享动作结构与模型前缀减少重复生成和上下文计算，且无需额外训练。

Result: 在八个基准任务上，与七个智能体基线和 TypeSafe Jev 变体对比，JevSpawn 获得更优的任务性能和更快的导航（navigation）速度，验证其为结构化智能体推理的有前景方法。

Conclusion: JevSpawn 有效弥合了自然语言任务规范与有限概率探索之间的鸿沟，展示了无需训练的结构化智能体推理优势，为高效自主智能体提供了新方向。

Abstract: LLM agents generate intermediate reasoning and actions token by token, making extended interactions slow and computationally expensive. Jev-style models offer fast probabilistic predictions over finite fields, but require those fields to be specified in advance. This requirement limits autonomous task solving, where the available actions must be derived from natural language instructions and adapted through interaction. We introduce JevSpawn, a compositional policy that connects natural language task specifications to finite probabilistic exploration. Parallel action spawning is coupled with feedback driven branch selection, representation revision, and recovery from retained alternatives. Shared action structure and model prefixes reduce repeated generation and context computation without additional training. Evaluations on eight benchmark tasks against seven agent baselines and a TypeSafe Jev variant establish JevSpawn as a promising approach to structured agentic inference, with improved task performance and faster navigation.

</details>


### [102] [Frozen Scenes, Shifting Winners: Configuration Fragility in Text-to-3D Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00447)
*Anson Y. Lam,Shuqing Li,Michael R. Lyu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计文本到3D的渲染图像评估协议，发现相机设置和提示词措辞会显著影响评分稳定性与排名结论，但经全搜索的同时推断后，未确认生成器优劣的真正反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到3D评估通常采用渲染图像进行自动对齐评分，但评估协议中的相机设置和提示词措辞等可变因素是否影响评分与排名稳定性尚不清楚，缺乏系统性审计，导致结论的可靠性存疑。

Method: 从6个生成器收集300个冻结场景，系统变化8个渲染与提示词因素，评估19个对齐评估器加1个感知质量对照组，并施加4种定向场景退化测试；测量配置方差与生成器间方差，采用提示引导下界、成对协议边际包络及全搜索同时推断。

Result: 17/19个对齐评估器的峰值配置方差超过生成器间方差，11/19个提示引导下界高于1；排名比分值更稳定，但18/19个评估器在某种配置下改变点估计赢家；成对边际包络显示部分比较随设置改变方向，但任何反转都无法通过全搜索同时推断。

Conclusion: 观察到的赢家变化仅为描述性而非已确认的生成器优劣变化；所有评估器（含无提示对照组）对布局扰乱的定向判别力不超过67%平局调整值，说明敏感性有限。建议报告（生成器、分数、卡片ID），并采用协议依赖比较和选择感知不确定性。

Abstract: Can a text-to-3D leaderboard change when every generated scene stays fixed? We audit this question for rendered-image evaluation, where camera settings and caption wording become part of the measurement protocol. Across 300 frozen scenes from six generators, we vary eight render and caption factors for 19 alignment evaluators plus one perceptual-quality control, then test four targeted scene degradations. Peak configuration variance exceeds between-generator variance for 17/19 alignment evaluators, with prompt-bootstrap lower bounds above 1 for 11/19. Rankings are more stable than scores, yet 18/19 evaluators change their point-estimate winner under some configuration. Pairwise protocol margin envelopes show which comparisons keep their direction across the tested settings. Selected pairs have opposite pointwise intervals, but no reversal survives simultaneous inference over the full search. Thus the observed winner changes are descriptive, not confirmed changes in generator superiority. Sensitivity remains separate: no evaluator, even the prompt-free control, exceeds 67% tie-adjusted directional discrimination on layout scrambling, which is diagnostic rather than human-validated ground truth. The audit separates score stability, decision uncertainty, and targeted sensitivity, and recommends reporting (generator, score, card ID) with protocol-dependent comparisons and selection-aware uncertainty.

</details>


### [103] [Before Agents Decide: Epistemic Action in LLM-Based Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00511)
*Yizhi Liu,Balaji Padmanabhan,Siva Viswanathan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将认知科学中的"认知行动"（epistemic actions）概念引入LLM智能体设计，区分三类生产"决策就绪证据"的行动模式，并强调智能体设计需关注证据是否已准备好用于决策，而非仅关注决策本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体设计侧重于搜索与探索，但忽视了一个更早的问题——可用证据是否已足够支撑决策。现实中常存在必要证据缺失、证据形式遮蔽关键信息、或所需比较尚未建立等情况，因此需要系统性地思考如何产出"决策就绪的证据"。

Method: 作者借鉴认知科学中"认知行动"（为后续选择改善证据基础的行动）的理论，将其迁移到LLM智能体上，区分三种行动模式：获取缺失证据、转化已有证据的形式、探测系统以产生有揭示性的响应；并提出"认知脚手架"（epistemic scaffolding）概念，指使这些行动可执行且可审计的界面、工具与环境。

Result: 论文论证了智能体设计必须从"如何做决策"扩展到"如何生产决策就绪的证据"，通过认知行动的分类框架与认知脚手架概念，为LLM智能体在决策前的证据准备环节提供了理论支撑与设计方向。

Conclusion: 智能体设计不能只关注决策行为本身，而必须解决"决策就绪的证据是如何被生产出来"这一前提问题；引入认知行动与认知脚手架，使证据的获取、转化与探测成为可设计、可审计的组成部分。

Abstract: Before a difficult decision, people often act simply to understand the situation better. We turn an object to see another side, place alternatives next to each other, or change one condition and observe what happens. These actions may not complete the task, but they improve the evidence needed for the next choice. LLM-based agents can search and explore, yet agent design gives less attention to an earlier question: is the available evidence ready for the decision? Sometimes necessary evidence is missing. In other cases, the evidence is present but its form hides what matters, or the comparison needed to judge it does not yet exist. Cognitive science calls actions that improve the basis for a later choice epistemic actions. We bring this idea to LLM-based agents and distinguish three modes: acquiring missing evidence, transforming available evidence, and probing a system to create a revealing response. We use the term epistemic scaffolding for the interfaces, tools, and environments that make these actions possible and auditable. This paper argues that agent design must address how decision-ready evidence is produced.

</details>


### [104] [Ontology-Based Contextual AI Evaluations (OB-CAIE) Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00529)
*Julie Krugler Hollek,Michael Zargham,Mala Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于本体的情境化AI评估方法（OB-CAIE），通过明确界定测试范围、平衡人类专业判断与自动化，并提升评估测试环境的可复现性，从而增强AI评估的科学严谨性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI评估存在三大问题：测试覆盖范围不清晰导致科学严谨性不足、人类专业知识与自动化之间缺乏平衡、以及评估测试环境缺乏可复现性。OB-CAIE旨在解决这些问题，强化AI评估的科学方法基础。

Method: OB-CAIE方法论通过两个本体来刻画可处理的问题空间：领域特定本体（DSO，即"测什么"）和评估过程本体（EPO，即"如何测"）。一个问题空间可复用于一次或多次AI评估；方法论在特定环节以科学严谨的方式引入人类判断，尤其适用于人类反馈真实不可约减或机器不可替代的复杂领域。

Result: OB-CAIE的一个关键优势是，失败点可以在其规范的问题空间内被追溯、可视化和分析，从而显著提升AI评估的可解释性与可诊断性。

Conclusion: OB-CAIE通过先明确"将要测试什么"，弥补了现有AI评估在科学方法第一步上的不足，并借助DSO与EPO的双本体结构，在保证严谨性与可复现性的同时，为复杂领域保留了科学合理的人类判断空间。

Abstract: The ontology-based contextual AI evaluation (OB-CAIE) methodology was developed to address a lack of scientific rigor that arises from unclear testing coverage, to balance human expertise and automations, and to address a lack of reproducibility of AI evaluation testing environments. OB-CAIE strengthens the current state of AI evaluations by addressing the first step in the scientific method by clearly defining what will be tested. Two ontologies represent the tractable problem space in the OB-CAIE methodology: the Domain-Specific Ontology (DSO) and the Evaluation Process Ontology (EPO). The DSO is the what; the EPO is the how. An OB-CAIE problem space can be used for one or multiple AI evaluations. The OB-CAIE methodology allows for human judgment at specific points, in scientifically grounded ways, and in complex subject areas where human feedback is genuinely irreducible or machine irreplaceable. A key advantage of the OB-CAIE methodology is that failure points can be traced, visualized and analyzed within the canonical OB-CAIE methodology problem space.

</details>


### [105] [Science or Slop?: Benchmarking and Mitigating Scientific Slop in AI-Generated Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00531)
*Yerim Oh,Young-Jun Lee,Jaewoo Ahn,Gunhee Kim,Dongyeop Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"科学垃圾"(scientific slop)概念,即AI生成论文中全局科学推理断裂的模式,并通过构建SciSlopBench基准和SciSlopHarness框架,实现了对AI生成论文的高精度识别与负责任的修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成内容(常称"AI slop")在学术界日益普遍,但AI生成科学论文中的垃圾模式比普通文本更复杂,难以被现有基于token的AI检测器识别。这类论文各部分看似合理,但连接各部分的科学推理却存在断裂,可能误导读者对工作的评价。因此需要针对"科学垃圾"建立专门的基准和检测方法。

Method: 作者从结构(Structure)、论证(Argument)和制品(Artifacts)三个维度设计了六项度量来刻画"科学垃圾"。构建了SciSlopBench基准,包含390篇AI生成论文(以计算机科学为主,涵盖生命、社会与自然科学),每篇与研究问题和贡献类型匹配的人类撰写论文配对。进一步提出SciSlopHarness框架,引导固定的LLM仅在实验记录支持修改的地方修正垃圾模式,避免直接优化度量导致的reward hacking。

Result: 六项度量识别每对中AI论文的准确率达85.9%,优于Binoculars的68.7%。科学垃圾程度越高,ICLR评分越低,且在2017至2025年每一年都能以高于随机水平区分拒稿与录用论文。直接优化度量并不可行;标准修订会残留垃圾,直接针对垃圾的提示会触发reward hacking,而SciSlopHarness相比最强修订基线,将剩余AI-人类差距缩小63%,且无需人类参考目标。

Conclusion: AI生成科学论文在其全局推理中留下根本性痕迹;负责任的缓解措施需要严格的证据支撑,而非仅仅润色文字。该工作为检测和缓解AI生成科学论文中的"科学垃圾"提供了基准、度量与框架。

Abstract: AI-generated content, often called AI slop, is increasingly common everywhere, particularly in academia. Slop in AI-generated scientific papers, however, has more complex patterns that cannot be easily detected by existing token-based AI detectors. Each part of such a paper looks plausible while the scientific reasoning that connects the parts breaks down, which can mislead how readers assess the work. We benchmark these failures as scientific slop through six measures across Structure, Argument, and Artifacts. We construct SciSlopBench with 390 AI-generated papers, mostly in computer science but spanning the life, social, and natural sciences, each paired with a human-written paper matched by research problem and contribution type. Our measures identify the AI paper in each pair with 85.9% accuracy, compared with 68.7% for Binoculars. Higher scientific slop accompanies lower ICLR ratings and distinguishes rejected from accepted papers above chance in every year from 2017 to 2025. Reducing these patterns, however, is not as simple as directly optimizing the measures. We therefore propose SciSlopHarness, a harness-level framework that guides a fixed LLM to revise slop only where the experiment records support the change. While standard revisions leave residual slop and direct slop-aware prompting triggers reward hacking, SciSlopHarness reduces the remaining AI-human gap by 63% over the strongest revision baseline without requiring human reference targets. Overall, we demonstrate that AI-generated scientific papers leave fundamental traces in their global reasoning, and that responsible mitigation demands strict evidentiary grounding rather than mere prose refinement.

</details>


### [106] [Worse Together: How Performance Breaks Down in Multi-User Multi-Agent Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00583)
*Sahan Paliskara,Nattaput Namchittai,Andrew Lampinen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究多用户、多AI智能体在共享资源下的协调问题，发现各自为用户服务的智能体团队在群体结果上普遍劣于统一协调者，并识别出相关行为与缓解措施，同时发布MAMUBench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人们越来越多地把任务委托给AI智能体，不同用户的智能体会在共享资源（如代码库、日历、预算）上相遇。由于各智能体服务于目标不同的用户，协调往往失败，导致群体结果比单一智能体服务所有人更差。作者希望系统地量化这一多用户、多智能体场景下的协调失败程度及其成因。

Method: 作者在5个前沿模型、4个环境（API密钥/共享算力预算、诊所/共享日历、个人助理/共享团购预订、合并队列/共享发布截止）共77个场景中，比较三种设置：单一协调者（服务所有用户）、无通信信道的智能体团队、有通信信道的智能体团队，并以群体结果（如目标用户请求达成率）进行对比。

Result: 在所有环境中，智能体团队的群体结果均劣于协调者：无信道时在两个环境中彻底崩溃，即使有信道，协调开销也造成巨大差距。例如在个人助理环境中，协调者达成目标用户请求的频率约为团队的两倍。研究识别出与群体性能低下相关的行为：团队增大时的停滞、互相覆盖彼此操作、捏造主张等，并找到了有效但依赖具体环境的缓解措施，如设置团队负责人、明确的程序化指令、以及让智能体在提交前读取同伴消息的平台检查。

Conclusion: 多用户、多智能体间的协调是一个关键且普遍的失败点，当前前沿模型的智能体团队在群体层面表现不佳。作者将发布API密钥、诊所和个人助理三个环境构成的MAMUBench（共74个场景），用于评估多用户、多智能体协调能力，为后续研究和改进提供基准。

Abstract: People are increasingly delegating tasks to AI agents, and those agents are increasingly encountering other people's agents over shared resources such as a codebase, a calendar, or a budget. When each agent acts for a different user with different goals, coordination often fails, and the group ends up worse off than if a single agent had acted for everyone. We study this multi-user, multi-agent setting across five frontier models and 77 scenarios in four environments: an API key environment in which agents share a compute budget, a clinic in which they share a calendar, a personal assistant environment in which they share a group order or booking, and a merge queue in which they share a release cutoff. In each scenario, we compare a single agent that serves every user (a coordinator) to a team in which each agent serves one user, with and without a communication channel between the agents. Teams deliver worse group outcomes than the coordinator in every environment: without a channel, they completely collapse in two environments, and even with one, coordination overhead creates substantial gaps. For example, in the personal assistant environment, the coordinator fulfills a targeted user request about twice as often as teams. We identify distinct behaviors associated with this poor group-level performance, including stalling as teams grow, overriding each other's actions, and fabricating claims. We find effective but environment-specific mitigations, such as a team lead, explicit procedural instructions, and a platform check that makes an agent read its peers' messages before committing. We will release the API key, clinic, and personal assistant environments as MAMUBench, comprising 74 scenarios for evaluating multi-user, multi-agent coordination.

</details>


### [107] [Legal Research Bench: Measuring End-to-End Reliability in Long-Horizon Legal Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00609)
*Katrina Drozdov,Oliver Chen,Langston Nashold,Rayan Krishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Legal Research Bench (LRB)，一个由专家编写的 413 道开放式美国法律研究问题基准，用于评估语言模型智能体在法律检索工作流中的可靠性。评估 13 个前沿模型后，最强模型 Claude Opus 4.8 的完全正确率仅 42.9%，且更多推理轮次、工具调用和成本并不带来更高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律研究是耗时且核心的法律工作流，语言模型智能体天然契合这一检索密集型任务；但其价值取决于可靠性——遗漏权威、引用过时或结论错误都会使回答不可用。因此需要一个以“来源可验证、全通过”为标准的基准来衡量智能体是否真正可靠。

Method: 构建 LRB 基准：413 道由专家撰写的开放式美国法律研究问题，每题配有黄金答案、支持性权威依据和二元评分量表。在包含网络搜索、判例法检索、页面解析和检索工具的测试框架中评估 13 个前沿模型。采用“全通过评分 + 来源验证”方式——只有当回答满足所有必须标准且引用的权威来源可验证时才判为正确；并用专家律师验证 LLM 裁判的一致性。

Result: 智能体远未达到可靠水平：最强模型 Claude Opus 4.8 的完全正确率为 42.9%。不同法律领域的全通过率差异显著，要求调和冲突权威依据的问题得分更低。跨模型来看，更多推理轮次、工具调用次数和推理成本并不能预测更高的准确率。

Conclusion: 现有语言模型智能体在法律研究这一高可靠性要求的工作流中表现仍远不达标，可靠性的核心瓶颈尚未被计算资源投入所解决，LRB 为追踪该领域进展提供了严格的评估基准。

Abstract: Legal research is a core and time-consuming legal workflow. Lawyers must identify controlling authority, verify that it remains valid, reconcile statutes and cases, and synthesize a grounded answer. Language model agents are a natural fit for this retrieval-intensive workflow, and automating even part of it would be valuable. But that value depends on reliability: a single missing authority, stale citation, or wrong legal conclusion can make an otherwise plausible answer unusable. We introduce \textbf{Legal Research Bench} (LRB), a benchmark of 413 open-ended U.S. legal research questions written by experts, each paired with a gold answer, supporting authorities, and a binary grading rubric. We evaluate thirteen frontier models in a harness with web search, case-law search, page parsing, and retrieval tools. We score agent responses through all-pass grading with source verification, where a response is correct only if every required criterion is satisfied and its cited authorities verify. We also validate the LLM judge against expert attorneys ensuring that benchmark scores track attorney judgment. Agents remain far from reliable: among the models we tested, the strongest, Claude Opus 4.8, is fully correct on 42.9\% of questions. Performance also varies substantially by task setting: all-pass rates differ across areas of law and are lower on questions requiring reconciliation of conflicting authorities. Across models, more turns, tool calls, and inference cost do not predict higher accuracy.

</details>


### [108] [Spatial Strategies, Not Actions: Vector-Quantized Geodesics as Tools for LLM-Driven Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00613)
*Gabriel Turinici*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种结合几何工具与 LLM 作为高层编排器的架构，通过在网格世界中向量量化测地轨迹形成工具库，使低成本非推理配置达到与昂贵思维链版本相当的到达率，并将决策成本从分钟级降至秒级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体常被批评缺乏空间理解能力、主要依赖统计文本模式。作者希望通过引入几何工具来弥补这一缺陷，研究 LLM 在网格世界中的空间理解能力。

Method: 智能体先采集测地轨迹，对轨迹进行向量量化以提取代表性子集；离线阶段由 LLM 为每条选中轨迹关联自然语言描述，形成工具；在线阶段由 LLM 根据当前状态与目标选择合适的工具，底层控制则由执行该轨迹关联的原始动作完成。该方法将学习分为两层：工具发现通过无监督轨迹量化实现，推理与决策交由 LLM 完成。实验在部分可观测的动态 2D 网格环境中进行，使用开放视觉语言模型 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B，并配备智能体中心缩放工具与碰撞检测工具。

Result: 将几何派生的工具库与智能体中心缩放工具、碰撞检测工具结合后，一个快速、非推理（non-reasoning）配置的目标到达率与成本高得多的思维链版本相当，同时将单次决策成本从分钟级降低到秒级。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过在底层引入几何派生的动作原语/工具、由 LLM 承担高层编排，可以显著提升 LLM 智能体的空间理解能力，并以更低成本实现与复杂推理方法相当的导航性能。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) based agents are often criticized for lacking spatial understanding and mainly exploiting statistical text patterns. We investigate their spatial comprehension through an architecture combining geometrical tools with a LLM serving as a high-level orchestrator in grid-world environments. The agent first collects geodesic trajectories, which are then vector-quantized to extract a representative subset. Offline, the LLM associates a natural language description of the underlying behavioral patterns to each selected trajectory, making it a tool. Online, the LLM chooses the appropriate tool conditioned on the current state and goal. Low-level control is handled by primitive actions that execute the trajectory associated with the tool. From an agentic AI perspective, this approach separates learning into two levels: tool discovery is handled through unsupervised quantization of trajectories, while reasoning and decision-making are handled by the LLM. We test the approach in a partially observable dynamic 2D grid environment with an open vision-language model (Qwen3.6-35B-A3B). Pairing the geometry-derived tool library with an agent-centered zoom tool and a collision detection tool lets a fast, non-reasoning configuration match the goal-reaching rate of a much more costly chain-of-thought version, while cutting the cost of a decision from minutes to seconds.

</details>


### [109] [CompMat-Bench: Benchmarking AI Agents for Computational Materials Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00636)
*Chenmu Zhang,Levi Felix,Jun-Jie Zhang,Xingfu Li,Xuelian Jiang,Tao Jiang,Subhendu Mishra,Xixi Qin,Boris Yakobson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CompMat-Bench 是一个由 94 个计算材料学研究任务组成的基准，通过预先复现研究步骤并固定规则评分（无需 LLM 评判），在不重复昂贵模拟的情况下评估 AI 智能体完成研究步骤的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估 AI 智能体的科学研究能力受限于底层实验/计算所需的时间与资源。在计算材料研究中，跨智能体与试验重复相同的昂贵模拟会使评估变得不切实际，因此需要一种能在避免昂贵模拟的同时进行评估的方法。

Method: 提出 CompMat-Bench，包含 94 个源自近期已发表计算材料研究的任务，每个任务要求智能体朝研究目标完成一个研究步骤。作者预先复现这些研究步骤，并让智能体为昂贵模拟准备输入、分析输出；预先复现的输入与结果作为真实值，用于通过固定规则（非 LLM 评判）对智能体评分。基准支持四种评估条件：单任务与由相关任务组成的工作流，各自又分完全或简化的方法学指导。

Result: 在单任务且完全指导下，基于三种 LLM 的智能体在 94 个任务上的通过率为 66.0–90.4%，能够完成单个材料研究步骤。更长的工作流与简化的指导都会限制智能体表现，但方式因智能体而异：它们降低了较弱智能体的通过率，而最强的智能体只有在长工作流与简化指导同时出现时才会下降。失败分析将大多数失败归因于科学错误，而非软件使用错误。

Conclusion: CompMat-Bench 为比较智能体在真实材料研究步骤上的表现、以及分析智能体失败模式提供了基础，指出工作流长度与指导程度对智能体性能的影响具有智能体差异性。

Abstract: Evaluating AI agents on scientific research tasks is constrained by the time and resources required for the underlying experiments or calculations. In computational materials research, repeating the same expensive simulations across agents and trials can make evaluation impractical. We introduce CompMat-Bench, a benchmark of 94 tasks derived from recently published computational materials studies, each asking agents to complete a step toward achieving the study's scientific goal. We reproduce the research steps in advance and assess agents on preparing inputs and analyzing outputs for expensive simulations, so expensive simulations can be avoided during evaluation. The reproduced inputs and results serve as ground truth for grading agents with fixed rules, without an LLM judge. The benchmark supports four evaluation conditions: single tasks and workflows composed of related tasks, each with full or reduced methodological guidance. With full guidance on single tasks, agents based on three LLMs demonstrate the ability to complete individual materials research steps, with pass rates of 66.0-90.4% across 94 tasks. Both longer workflows and reduced guidance can limit agent performance, but in different ways for different agents: they lower the pass rates of the weaker agents, whereas the strongest agent falls only when a long workflow is combined with reduced guidance. Failure analysis attributes most failures to scientific errors rather than to errors in software usage. CompMat-Bench provides a basis for comparing agents on the steps of real materials research and for analyzing agent failure modes.

</details>


### [110] [Incident-Arena: Getting agents to the last nine of reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00648)
*Andre Fu,Malik Drabla,Leon Liu,Meji Abidoye,Marek Suppa,Lata Mishra,Adnan El Assadi,Yiyuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Incident-Arena，一个人工构建的、基于真实开源生产应用的智能体 SRE（站点可靠性工程）基准，包含 20 个任务，在临时 Kubernetes 集群中注入故障并用功能性验证器评估修复质量，结果显示前沿模型得分不足 64.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 编码智能体在工程中被广泛使用，但现有 agentic SRE 基准存在三大局限：(1) 环境不真实，多为玩具仓库；(2) 框架实现非标准；(3) 验证方式仅为简单的静态检查，无法反映真实生产事故响应的复杂度。业界缺少一个真实、标准化且能严格验证的基准来评估智能体处理生产事故的能力。

Method: 构建 Incident-Arena：20 个人工挑选、基于真实开源部署软件的任务；每个任务将生产应用部署到临时 Kubernetes 集群，从配置层到底层镜像注入故障，并按任务需求施加持续负载。提出新颖的功能性验证方法，超越静态检查，在保持系统级指标稳定的同时确保修复的安全性。

Result: 智能体试运行平均消耗 2.81M tokens、41 轮交互，远超现有基准，展现了长时程推理能力。在 20 个任务、3 种应用基板上，前沿模型得分低于 64.3%，失败涵盖诊断/定位错误、不完整修复以及不安全回归等类型。

Conclusion: Incident-Arena 揭示了当前前沿模型在真实生产事故响应上的显著不足，诊断、修复完整性与安全性仍是关键短板，为 agentic SRE 领域提供了更真实、标准化且严格验证的评估平台。

Abstract: AI coding agents are ubiquitous in engineering workflows amongst industry and academia. Yet, despite their use in app coding, relatively less attention has been paid to their ability to execute on production incident response. This emerging field, termed agentic site-reliability-engineering (SRE) contains benchmarks limited by (1) unrealistic environments, typically toy repositories (2) non-standard framework implementations and (3) simple static verifiers. We introduce Incident-Arena, a human-built benchmark of 20 carefully selected tasks grounded in real-world deployed open source software. Each task deploys a production application to an ephemeral Kubernetes cluster, injecting a fault from the config layer through underlying images, and a sustained load profile given the task requirements. We also present a novel verification method, going beyond static checks to functional verifiers, holding systems level metrics stable, while ensuring repairs are done safely. Agent trials run an average of 2.81M tokens and 41 turns, going beyond existing benchmarks, demonstrating agentic long horizon reasoning. Across 20 tasks and 3 application substrates, frontier models score below 64.3%, with failures extending from diagnosis/localization errors, through incomplete repairs and unsafe regressions.

</details>


### [111] [Agent Evaluation Reliability: More Tasks Won't (Always) Fix An Agent Leaderboard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00651)
*Michael Hardy,Ruhana Azam,Anka Reuel,Mykel Kochenderfer,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种贝叶斯方差分解框架，用于分析稀疏、不均衡的智能体排行榜，发现评测的可靠性高度依赖测量目标：系统级排名可靠但底层模型排名可靠性较低，脚手架选择会改变结论，单纯增加任务数量无法解决所有不确定性，而汇集多样基准可在更低成本下提升跨任务排名可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体评测日益用于比较大语言模型并指导部署决策，但排名不仅反映模型本身，还受脚手架、任务等评测条件影响，导致可靠性依赖于具体声明。现有评测存在"测的是部署系统还是底层模型"的模糊性，需要明确当前智能体评测究竟能可靠支持哪些结论，以及如何通过补充评测加以改进。

Method: 提出贝叶斯方差分解框架，将信号（与预期结论相关的性能差异）与噪声（与结论无关但仍会改变排名的变异）分离，并应用于 Holistic Agent Leaderboard 和 Harbor Index 的 22 个基准。通过固定"模型-脚手架"系统的排名可靠性、底层模型可靠性、脚手架间可靠性（衡量脚手架是否保持模型排名）以及任务预算与基准汇集策略的投影可靠性等指标进行诊断。

Result: 1) 固定模型-脚手架系统的排名可靠（0.935–0.994），但底层模型可靠性显著更低（0.148–0.841）；2) 脚手架选择会改变结论，脚手架效应在各评测间差异显著；3) 当不确定性由脚手架覆盖不足主导时，即使无限增加同类任务，模型排名可靠性最多仅提升 0.097；4) 汇集多样基准可将跨任务排名投影可靠性从 0.44 提升至 0.75（同等任务预算），并最多降低 83% 的投影成本。

Conclusion: 评测设计应遵循预期声明：先明确分数或排名代表的含义，诊断限制其可靠性的因素，再将评测预算投入到真正重要的不确定性来源上，而非盲目增加任务数量。

Abstract: Agent evaluations are increasingly used to compare LLMs and inform deployment decisions, yet ranks can reflect not only the model but also the effects of the evaluation conditions such as the scaffolds or tasks. This makes reliability claim-dependent: an evaluation that reliably ranks deployed systems may not reliably rank underlying models. We ask which conclusions current agent evaluations reliably support and what additional evaluation would improve them. We develop a Bayesian variance-decomposition framework for sparse, imbalanced agent leaderboards and apply it to 22 benchmarks from the Holistic Agent Leaderboard and Harbor Index. The framework separates signal, performance differences relevant to the intended claim, from noise, irrelevant variation that can still change rankings. We find: (1) Reliability depends on the measurement goal. Fixed model-scaffold systems are ranked reliably (0.935-0.994), while underlying-model reliability is substantially lower (0.148-0.841). (2) Scaffold choice can change conclusions. Inter-scaffold reliability measures whether scaffolds preserve model rankings, showing that scaffold effects vary substantially across evaluations. (3) More tasks cannot resolve all uncertainty. Even infinitely many similarly constructed tasks improve model-ranking reliability of a benchmark by at most 0.097 when uncertainty is dominated by limited scaffold coverage. (4) Pooling diverse benchmarks can improve cross-task rankings at lower cost. For rankings across diverse agentic tasks, pooling benchmarks raises projected reliability from 0.44 to 0.75 at the same task budget and can reduce projected cost by up to 83\%. Evaluation design should follow the intended claim: identify what a score or ranking should mean, diagnose what limits its reliability, and spend evaluation budget on the sources of uncertainty that matter.

</details>


### [112] [Learning Multiple Timescales for Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00849)
*Pedro Robles Dutenhefner,Dikshant Shehmar,Wagner Meira,Marlos C. Machado*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GITA 通过让单一价值函数显式以抽象步长 k 为条件，并在训练时聚合多个 k 的优势加权监督，同时保留局部分辨率和长程信号，在 OGBench 上显著超越现有离线目标条件强化学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线目标条件强化学习（GCRL）难以处理长时程任务。折扣因子会压缩远距离状态间的价值差异，使其低于函数逼近误差，导致智能体失去对状态排序的信号。时间抽象虽能恢复长程信号，但固定 k 无法兼顾：大 k 保留远距离价值差异却模糊邻近状态区分，小 k 则相反。

Method: 提出 Generalized Implicit Temporal Abstraction (GITA)，将单一价值函数显式以抽象步长 k 为条件，显式化大 k 与小 k 之间的权衡。训练时通过聚合多个 k 值的优势加权监督来训练单一策略，使得对某状态-目标对赋予更大正优势的 k 尺度对其更新贡献更强。由此无需在局部分辨率与长程信号之间取舍，也不必固定单一 k。

Result: 在 OGBench 上，GITA 超越广泛的离线 GCRL 基线，平均成功率相比 HIQL 提升 25 个百分点（相对提升 73%），相比最强的固定 k 方法 OTA 提升 7 个百分点（相对提升 14%）。

Conclusion: GITA 通过以 k 为条件的价值函数和多尺度优势聚合，同时保留了局部分辨率与长程信号，无需在 k 上做选择，从而在离线目标条件强化学习的长期任务中取得显著性能提升。

Abstract: Existing approaches to offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) struggle with long-horizon tasks. Discounting shrinks value differences between distant states until they fall below the function approximation error, leaving the agent with no signal for ranking states. Temporal abstraction, which treats k environment steps as a single transition, restores this signal at long range, but no single fixed k suits all state-goal distances: large k preserves value differences across long temporal distances while collapsing distinctions between nearby states, and small k does the reverse. We make this trade-off explicit and introduce Generalized Implicit Temporal Abstraction (GITA), which conditions a single value function on k. GITA trains one policy by aggregating advantage-weighted supervision across multiple k values, so scales assigning larger positive advantages to a state-goal pair contribute more strongly to its update. GITA does not need to choose between local resolution and long-range signal; it retains both without committing to a single k. On OGBench, GITA outperforms a broad range of offline GCRL baselines, raising average success rate across all tasks by 25 percentage points (73% relative improvement) over HIQL. It also improves over the strongest fixed-k method, OTA, by 7 percentage points (14% relative).

</details>


### [113] [When More Data Is Not Enough: The Context-Sufficiency Frontier in Generative AI Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00654)
*Merieme Askour,Ayoub Merimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“上下文充分性”理论，指出生成式AI个性化中，上下文的相关性比数量更重要，并通过实验证明相关上下文提升适切性，无关上下文则降低适切性并破坏检索稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统个性化依赖客户历史行为与偏好（客户证据），而生成式AI使服务方能在响应生成时实时注入情境信息（服务方上下文）。这种便捷性带来新问题：上下文越容易被提供，“越多越好”的假设不再成立，需要厘清上下文在何种数量与相关性下才是充分且不有害的。

Method: 开发“上下文充分性（context sufficiency）”理论，根据上下文与客户当前意图的相关性划分四种状态——不足（insufficiency）、充分（sufficiency）、饱和（saturation）、干扰（interference），并引入“上下文充分性前沿（Context-Sufficiency Frontier）”定位最小相关上下文集合。随后在一家大型家居零售商开展全因子实验，用生成式推荐系统验证理论。

Result: 实验结果显示：相关上下文提升了推荐适切性；无关上下文则降低了适切性，并破坏了检索的稳定性，证实“更多不等于更好”。

Conclusion: 该框架将个性化范式从“供给更多上下文”转向“识别当前交互真正需要什么”，并主张在整个服务流程中施加约束（enforcing constraints），而非无限堆砌上下文。

Abstract: Personalization has long relied on customer data to infer what an individual is likely to value. We call this customer evidence: the customer's historical behavior and preferences. Generative AI extends personalization by allowing providers to supply changing situational information at the moment a response is produced, without encoding every condition in advance. We define this provider-side context as information about what is possible, permitted, or advisable now. This flexibility creates a new problem: once context becomes easy to supply, more is not necessarily better. We develop a theory of context sufficiency in which the relevance of context to the customer's current intent matters more than its volume. The theory identifies four states, insufficiency, sufficiency, saturation, and interference, and introduces the Context-Sufficiency Frontier to locate the minimal relevant set. In a full-factorial experiment with a generative recommender at a large home-furnishing retailer, relevant context improved appropriateness, while irrelevant context reduced it and destabilized retrieval. The framework shifts personalization from supplying more context toward identifying what the current interaction actually requires and enforcing constraints throughout the service process.

</details>


### [114] [Backdoor Containment via Expert Quarantine and Shutdown in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00663)
*Jianwei Li,Min-Seon Kim,Jung-Eun Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全新的LLM后门防御策略"learn, but channel"（学习但疏导），通过Quarantined Expert Shutdown（QES）方法，在MoE式结构中利用LoRA分支和路由器，将后门行为引导到专用"隔离专家"中，部署时只需将隔离专家的路由权重置零即可消除后门，将攻击成功率从100%降至0-10%，同时基本保持下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM后门防御分两类：抑制后门学习（过滤毒数据或中断优化中的学习）或"先学习后净化"（修复权重或输入门控）。两者都旨在阻止后门形成或事后清除。作者认为存在第三种未被探索的路径：允许后门在训练中形成，但将其疏导至一个可隔离、可关闭的组件中，使部署时的缓解只需常数时间操作，无需检测触发词或更新模型权重。

Method: 提出Quarantined Expert Shutdown（QES）。在给定中毒数据集下，对基于Transformer的语言模型进行MoE式扩展：添加带路由的专家专用LoRA分支和轻量路由器。通过辅助路由目标（auxiliary routing objectives），将触发条件控制的行为吸引到指定"隔离专家"中，同时在其他部分保留良性能力。部署时，将隔离专家的路由权重置零（常数时间操作），无需触发筛选或权重再更新。

Result: 在两个任务、三种攻击、四个模型族的大多数设置下，方法将攻击成功率（ASR）从100%降至0-10%，下游实用性（utility）通常保持不变或仅受轻微影响。结果证明了"learn, but channel"在生成式LLM后门遏制中的可行性。

Conclusion: 作者确立了"learn, but channel"作为一种此前未被探索的后门遏制机制，QES以计算高效、部署时一次性关闭的方式实现后门隔离，在保持模型良性能力的同时大幅降低攻击成功率。

Abstract: Backdoored large language models (LLMs) can behave normally on benign inputs while producing attacker-specified outputs under hidden triggers. Existing defenses span four stages--prior-training, in-training, post-training, and inference-time--and share one of two underlying strategies: either suppress backdoor learning (by filtering poisoned data or interrupting its acquisition during optimization) or learn, then purify (by repairing model weights or gating inputs after a fully backdoored model has formed). We propose a third strategy, learn, but channel: allow backdoor formation during training but route it into a designated, quarantined component that can be disabled at deployment. To this end, we propose Quarantined Expert Shutdown QES, a computationally efficient containment strategy built in a regularization-steered MoE-like setting. Specifically, given a poisoned dataset, QES augments a Transformer-based language model with routed expert-specific LoRA branches and lightweight routers, and uses auxiliary routing objectives to attract trigger-conditioned behavior into a designated expert while preserving benign capability elsewhere. At deployment, mitigation reduces to a single constant-time operation: zeroing the quarantined expert's routing weight, without trigger screening or further updating model weights. Empirically, our methods reduce the attack success rate ASR from 100% to 0-10% on most settings across two tasks, three attacks, and four model families, while downstream utility is often preserved or only modestly affected. These results establish learn, but channel as a previously unexplored regime for backdoor containment in generative LLMs.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Variable Selection for Infectious Disease Transmission via Penalized Pairwise Accelerated Failure Time Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00176)
*Jeongjin Lee,Patrick M. Schnell,Grzegorz A. Rempala,Eben Kenah*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对传染病传播接触间隔（contact interval）的带惩罚的成对加速失效时间（pairwise AFT）模型，用自适应LASSO实现变量选择，并在理论上建立了其选择一致性与渐近正态性，最终用2009年洛杉矶县甲型H1N1流感家庭监测数据加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传染病传播数据中，因个体间传播诱导的依赖性违反了标准生存分析中事件独立的假设，导致传统的回归与变量选择方法不再适用。作者希望借助成对生存分析方法刻画有序对（ordered pair）的接触间隔，同时处理传染性与易感性协变量效应、种群内传播与外部感染源，并在大量候选协变量场景下实现变量选择。

Method: 作者提出带惩罚的成对加速失效时间模型：以接触间隔（第一个体进入传染期至其与第二个体首次传染性接触的时间）为结局；模型同时纳入传染性与易感性协变量，并联合表示种群内传播与外部感染源。变量选择采用自适应LASSO惩罚的最大似然估计，配合坐标下降算法，将分布的基线参数作为无惩罚的冗余（nuisance）参数处理。理论部分在profile似然正则条件及局部渐近机制下（信息在所有随访期内有传播风险的有序对中累积），建立了自适应LASSO估计量的选择一致性与渐近正态性。

Result: 模拟研究表明，与无惩罚估计量和普通LASSO相比，自适应LASSO在变量选择上更准确，估计精度更高。在2009年洛杉矶县甲型H1N1流感家庭监测数据的应用中，自适应LASSO得到稀疏且可解释的模型，并提升了被选协变量效应估计的精度，其中包括抗病毒预防（antiviral prophylaxis）对易感性的影响。

Conclusion: 所提出的带自适应LASSO惩罚的成对加速失效时间模型能够在传播诱导依赖的传染病数据中实现有效的变量选择与参数估计，具有理论保证（选择一致性、渐近正态性），并在真实流感监测数据中展现出稀疏性、可解释性与更高的估计精度，为传染病传播分析的回归建模提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: In infectious disease transmission, regression and variable selection for time-to-event outcomes are complicated by transmission-induced dependence that violates standard independence assumptions. Pairwise survival analysis addresses this dependence by modeling contact intervals in ordered pairs, defined as the time from the onset of infectiousness in the pair's first individual to their first infectious contact with the second individual. We propose a penalized pairwise accelerated failure time model for contact intervals that accommodates simultaneous covariate effects on infectiousness and susceptibility while jointly representing within-population transmission and infection from external sources. For variable selection with many candidate covariates, we develop adaptive LASSO penalized maximum likelihood estimation with a coordinate descent algorithm, treating distributional baseline parameters as unpenalized nuisance parameters. We establish selection consistency and asymptotic normality of the adaptive LASSO estimator under profile-likelihood regularity conditions in a local asymptotic regime in which information accumulates over all ordered pairs that are ever at risk of transmission during follow-up. Simulation studies show accurate variable selection and improved estimation accuracy compared with the unpenalized estimator and the LASSO. We illustrate the method using 2009 household influenza A (H1N1) surveillance data from Los Angeles County, where the adaptive LASSO produces a sparse, interpretable model and improves the precision of selected covariate effect estimates, including the effect of antiviral prophylaxis on susceptibility.

</details>


### [116] [Beyond Actor-Partner Associations: A Target-Trial Framework for Causal Spillover Effects in Longitudinal Dyadic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00181)
*Subir Hait*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文建立APIM式问题与因果二元组分析之间的桥梁，将关联性APIM系数在特定假设下等同于因果对比，揭示二元组处理下由成员倾向性乘积分解引发的精确单参数偏差delta(H)，并给出IPW与AIPW偏差的闭式表示，通过模拟、诊断及HRS养老退休数据应用说明偏差来源与支持不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: APIM虽描述一方暴露与自身及伴侣结局的关联，但其actor/partner系数只有在额外设计与识别假设下才具有因果性。作者旨在明确关联模型与因果表述的对应关系，并刻画二元组内干扰下，常见倾向性分解策略所引发的系统性偏差及其修正方法。

Method: 将APIM式问题形式化为二元组内干扰下的目标试验（dyadic target trials）；在联合可交换性、正性与齐次正确设定的处理条件均值下，将APIM各系数对应到因果对比；对二元处理定义delta(H)=Cov(A1,A2|H)，推导四格联合误差结构(+delta,-delta,-delta,+delta)及IPW对比偏差闭式解；将同一delta纳入AIPW偏差；用36场景模拟与真实边际分解诊断区分分解偏差与非线性模型偏差，并应用于HRS 2400对夫妻的退休转换与后续抑郁症状研究。

Result: 在较强依赖条件下，对真实边际进行分解会使溢出对比方向反转，并将交互作用膨胀至真实值近五倍；联合退休较罕见、残差处理依赖显著，估计结果对支持域与倾向性截断（flooring）敏感；灵活AIPW减少非线性模型偏差，但无法解决弱支持不稳定性；大的独立倾向性偏差主要源于分解偏差。

Conclusion: 本文贡献在于为从APIM问题到因果二元组分析提供设计与诊断桥梁，而非提出新的通用干扰或AIPW理论；强调在二元组干扰下分解边际倾向性会引入delta(H)偏差，需借助支持域诊断与因果目标试验框架审慎解读APIM系数。

Abstract: Actor-partner interdependence models (APIMs) describe how one member's exposure is associated with that member's and the partner's outcomes, but actor and partner coefficients are causal only under additional design and identification assumptions. We formulate APIM-style questions as dyadic target trials under within-dyad interference and make three links between the associational and causal formulations explicit. First, under joint exchangeability, positivity, and a homogeneous correctly specified treatment-related conditional mean, APIM actor, partner, interaction, and joint coefficients equal the corresponding causal contrasts. Second, for binary dyadic treatment, multiplying the true member-specific propensities induces an exact one-parameter discrepancy delta(H) = Cov(A_1, A_2 | H): the four joint-cell errors are (+delta, -delta, -delta, +delta), total variation is 2|delta|, and IPW contrast bias has a closed-form representation. Third, the same discrepancy affects AIPW through its product with outcome-regression error. A 36-scenario simulation and a true-marginal factorization diagnostic show that large separate-propensity bias under residual dependence is primarily factorization bias, while flexible AIPW reduces nonlinear-model bias but not weak-support instability. In the strongest dependence condition, factorizing even the true marginals reversed the spillover contrast and inflated the interaction to nearly five times its true value. We apply the framework to 2,400 Health and Retirement Study couples studying retirement transitions and later depressive symptoms. Joint retirement was rare, residual treatment dependence was substantial, and estimates were sensitive to support and propensity flooring. The contribution is a design-and-diagnostic bridge from APIM questions to causal dyadic analyses, not a new general interference or AIPW theory.

</details>


### [117] [Forecasting multiple density-valued time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00215)
*Han Lin Shang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对密度值时间序列（非负且积分为一、不构成线性向量空间）的建模难题，提出基于中心对数比变换（clr）与累积分布函数（CDF）的两种双射变换，将密度映射到无约束函数空间，并在该空间内采用多总体函数时间序列建模与性别/地区间差距建模两种方法，利用日本县级分年龄生命表死亡数据（1973–2024）评估一至二十步点预测与区间预测精度并给出建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密度值时间序列在实践中常见，但由于密度函数非负且积分为一，不构成线性向量空间，导致标准时间序列方法无法直接应用，需要先将其变换到无约束空间再进行建模与预测。

Method: 提出两种双射变换：基于中心对数比（clr）变换和基于累积分布函数（CDF）的变换，将密度映射到无约束函数空间。在该空间内采用两种方法：多总体函数时间序列建模（刻画总体间相关性）与性别和地区间的差距（gap）建模。利用日本县级分年龄生命表死亡计数数据（1973–2024）进行实证评估。

Result: 基于日本县级分年龄生命表死亡计数数据（1973–2024），评估并比较两种方法在一至二十步向前点预测与区间预测的精度，并给出一般性建议。

Conclusion: 通过恰当的变换可将有约束的密度值时间序列映射到无约束函数空间，从而应用标准函数时间序列方法建模；两种变换与两种建模方法各有适用场景，文中基于实证结果给出了一般性选择建议。

Abstract: Density-valued time series are common in practice. However, since these functions are non-negative and must integrate to one, they do not form a linear vector space, which complicates the direct application of standard time-series methods. To address these constraints, we introduce two transformations, based on the centered log-ratio transformation and the cumulative distribution function, for modeling and forecasting balanced panel data represented by multiple densities. Both transformations map densities bijectively into an unconstrained function space in which cross-sectional and serial dependence can be modeled directly. Within the resulting unconstrained space, we consider two approaches: multi-population functional time-series modeling to account for correlations among populations, and gap modeling between gender and region. Using Japanese subnational age-specific life-table death counts from 1973 to 2024, we evaluate and compare the one- to 20-step-ahead point and interval forecast accuracy of the two approaches for each transformation, and offer some general recommendations.

</details>


### [118] [Heteroskedastic Canonical Polyadic Tensor Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00498)
*Kyle Ritscher,Carlos Llosa-Vite*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出异方差CP分解（HCP），在CP分解基础上引入非恒定的低秩精度张量来建模逐元素变异性，并用交替分块坐标上升法同时恢复低秩均值张量和精度张量，计算复杂度与CP-ALS同阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的CP分解在最小化平方误差损失时，等价于假设高斯模型具有低秩均值张量且所有张量元素方差恒定。这一恒定方差假设在实际数据（如存在异方差噪声的数据）中往往不成立，导致估计不准确。为此需要一种能建模逐元素变异性（异方差）的低秩张量分解方法。

Method: 提出异方差CP分解（HCP），用一个非恒定、低秩的精度张量（方差的倒数）来建模逐元素变异性。基于高斯模型进行参数推断，并设计了一种交替分块坐标上升（alternating block-coordinate ascent）算法，迭代更新均值张量的因子和精度张量的因子，从含噪观测中同时恢复低秩均值张量和精度张量。

Result: 所提方法在计算上具有竞争力，因子更新的主导阶复杂度与CP-ALS相同。在合成实验和脑电图（EEG）应用中验证了HCP的有效性。

Conclusion: HCP通过引入低秩精度张量刻画异方差噪声，突破了传统CP分解的恒定方差假设，且计算开销与CP-ALS相当，在合成数据和EEG任务上表现出良好性能。

Abstract: When minimizing the squared-error loss, the popular CP decomposition can be interpreted as parameter inference in a Gaussian model with a low-rank mean tensor and constant variance across the tensor entries. We introduce heteroskedastic-CP (HCP), which models entrywise variability with a non-constant, low-rank precision tensor, and develop an alternating block-coordinate ascent method to recover both the low-rank mean and precision tensors from noisy observations. Our procedure is computationally competitive, with the same leading-order factor-update complexity as CP-ALS. We demonstrate HCP on synthetic experiments and an EEG application.

</details>


### [119] [Three-group variance-ratio tests for heteroscedasticity in linear regression: an exact null distribution and an outlier-resistant version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00550)
*Ahmed El-Kotory,Ebrahim Khaled Ebrahim*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对线性回归异方差性的稳健检验方法：按任意选定变量排序后将数据三等分，分别拟合回归并比较最大与最小误差尺度，从而在存在离群点、非单调方差及方差随时间变化等情况下仍保持良好的检验水平和功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异方差检验（如White检验、Goldfeld-Quandt检验）在数据含离群点、方差非单调、或方差沿回归变量之外的变量（如时间）变化时，会失去名义水平或功效，因此需要一个既稳健又灵活（可沿任意排序变量）的检验。

Method: 将观测按任意选定变量排序，等分为三组；对每组分别拟合回归，比较最大与最小误差尺度之比。当使用最小二乘拟合且误差正态时，该比值精确服从三组情形下的Hartley最大F分布；当使用最小截尾平方和（LTS）拟合时，该比值对沿排序方向的离群点具有稳健性，其平方近似为具有有效自由度的最大F比，并通过截尾方差的影响函数以闭式形式推导出自由度极限。采用析因模拟覆盖72种方差形态、排序变量、回归变量数、污染及样本量组合。

Result: 稳健检验的平均规模调整功效最高，达67%，优于次优检验的45%；当方差沿回归变量变化（所有检验均已知该变量）时，它优于异常值筛选后的White检验（67%对56%）；在重尾t3误差下优势相似（64%对35%）。它能检测沿时间变化的方差并定位方差发生变化的区间。按原文描述实现的两个已发表的稳健Goldfeld-Quandt检验版本未能维持其名义水平。方法已实现于R包KOTORY。

Conclusion: 所提出的稳健异方差检验在多种不利场景下（离群点、非单调方差、沿时间的方差变化、重尾误差）表现优于现有检验，兼具灵活性（可沿任意排序变量）与定位能力，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Tests for heteroscedasticity in linear regression lose their level or power in three situations: when the data contain outliers, when the variance is not monotone, and when it changes along a variable outside the regressors, such as time. The proposed tests sort the observations by any chosen variable, split them into three equal parts, fit the regression in each part and compare the largest with the smallest error scale. With least squares fits and normal errors, the ratio follows Hartley's maximum F distribution with three groups exactly. With least trimmed squares fits, the ratio resists outliers spread along the ordering. Its square is approximately a maximum F ratio with effective degrees of freedom, whose limit we derive in closed form from the influence function of the trimmed variance. A factorial simulation covered 72 settings of variance shape, ordering variable, number of regressors, contamination and sample size. The robust test had the highest mean size-adjusted power, 67%, against 45% for the next test; when the variance changed along a regressor, which all tests were given, it led White's test after an outlier screen, 67% against 56%. With heavy-tailed t3 errors its lead was similar, 64% against 35%. Unlike the tests built on the regressors, it can follow a variance changing along time, and it identifies the part of the data where the variance changes. Two published robust versions of the Goldfeld-Quandt test, as implemented from their descriptions, did not hold their nominal level. The R package KOTORY implements the methods.

</details>


### [120] [Probabilistic linkage with graphs: leveraging cluster structure to improve the accuracy of entity resolution at scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00566)
*Evelyn Lauren,Laura White,Trevor Graham Bell,Jacob Bor*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了11种图聚类算法在概率记录链接后的实体解析任务中的表现，发现来自相邻领域（Leiden、Louvain、标签传播）的算法比传统的传递闭包方法误差最多降低66%，且比实体解析领域常用的马尔可夫聚类快达40倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率记录链接能在标识符不完整或不完美时识别同一实体的记录，但其产生的成对链接存在噪声，需要解析为不同的个体。标准的传递闭包方法对每条链接独立施加固定阈值，随着人口规模增大，错误合并不同个体的风险随之上升，因此需要利用图结构来提升链接准确性。然而，相邻领域的图聚类算法虽解决相同的图聚类任务，却从未在实体解析场景中被系统评估过。

Method: 作者选取11种聚类算法——其中5种已在实体解析领域应用，6种来自相邻领域——在3个模拟数据集和1个真实世界数据集上进行评估，并针对链接图提出了一个定制化适配方案。

Result: 来自跨领域的算法（Leiden、Louvain、标签传播）准确性最高，相比传递闭包将链接误差最多降低66%；这3种算法运行速度比实体解析领域的领先方法马尔可夫聚类快达40倍，并能直接集成到现有的大规模处理流程中。

Conclusion: 相邻领域的图聚类算法在实体解析任务中具有显著优势，不仅准确率更高，而且运行效率大幅提升，为记录链接后的大规模实体解析提供了更优的替代方案。

Abstract: Probabilistic record linkage identifies records belonging to the same entity even when identifiers are incomplete or imperfect, and is increasingly used to conduct population-level inferences from administrative data at scale. Linkage produces noisy pairwise links that must then be resolved into distinct individuals. The standard approach, transitive closure, applies a fixed threshold to each link independently and assigns clusters from the resulting connected components. However, the risk of merging distinct individuals grows with population size, motivating approaches that use graph structure to improve linkage accuracy. Clustering algorithms from adjacent fields solve the same graph-clustering task in other domains, yet have never been systematically evaluated for entity resolution. We evaluated eleven clustering algorithms -- five established in entity resolution and six from adjacent fields -- across three simulated datasets and one real-world dataset, and propose an adaptation tailored to linkage graphs. Cross-field algorithms -- Leiden, Louvain, label propagation -- were among the most accurate, reducing linkage error by up to 66% relative to transitive closure. The three algorithms also ran up to 40 times faster than Markov clustering (a leading approach in the entity resolution literature) and integrate directly into existing pipelines at scale.

</details>


### [121] [Nonparametric Identification of Latent Dimension under Heavy-Tailed and Mixed-Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00588)
*Chetkar Jha*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于秩变换的非参数平行分析（Rank PA）方法，用于在高维混合信号和重尾误差分布下更可靠地估计潜变量维度 r，解决了传统平行分析因列置换无法破坏信号而导致的零谱膨胀问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主成分分析与因子分析高度依赖对潜变量维度 r 的正确识别，而作为金标准的平行分析（PA）在混合信号和/或重尾误差分布的高维情形下性能下降，因为列置换机制无法破坏信号，导致零谱被抬高，从而影响维度估计的准确性。

Method: 作者提出非参数化解决方案 Rank PA：先对数据矩阵 X 进行秩变换，利用秩变换后数据矩阵中的“有序程度”唯一保留潜变量维度，从而在置换下恢复谱可交换性；并从理论上证明 Rank PA 在放松条件下的一致性，既不需要因子的普遍性（pervasiveness），也不需要限制性的四阶矩假设。

Result: 理论证明 Rank PA 最多可以一致地估计 O(n^{1/3 - τ})（τ>0）个因子；数值模拟和对 FRED-MD 数据集的分析展示了该方法的稳健性能。

Conclusion: Rank PA 通过秩变换恢复置换下的谱可交换性，在高维混合信号和重尾误差分布条件下显著优于传统平行分析，为潜变量维度估计提供了稳健的非参数工具。

Abstract: Principal component analysis and factor analysis are foundational to the study of high-dimensional data, yet their efficacy depends entirely on correctly identifying the latent dimension $r$. While parallel analysis (PA) is widely regarded as the gold standard for this task, its performance suffers in high-dimensional regimes consisting of mixed signals and/or heavy-tailed error distributions. This arises because under the preceding conditions, column-wise permutation mechanism (or any modern variations) applied to the data matrix $\textbf{X}$ fails to destroy the signal, leading to an inflated null spectrum. To address this, we propose a nonparametric solution. We show that the latent dimension is uniquely preserved in the degree of order within the rank-transformed data matrix, which restores spectral exchangeability under permutation. We prove the consistency of Rank PA under relaxed conditions, requiring neither the pervasiveness of factors nor restrictive fourth moment assumptions. We establish that Rank PA can consistently estimate the number of factors up to the maximum of $O(n^{1/3 - τ})$ where $τ>0$. The robust performance of our remedy is demonstrated through numerical simulations and an analysis of the FRED-MD dataset.

</details>


### [122] [Foundation or Formula? A Simulation-Based Comparison of Google's TimesFM-3 and Classical Time-Series Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00589)
*Ebrahim Khaled Ebrahim,Somaia Mohamed Ali,Ahmed El-Kotory*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 该探索性研究在九个已知数据生成过程（模型训练语料中不可能包含）上，对比了时序基础模型 TimesFM-3 与经典方法（ARIMA、指数平滑等）。在 7200 条序列、12 步预测视界的预注册协议下，两类方法互不占优：TimesFM-3 最差情景误差不超过最优方法的 1.31 倍，而每个经典方法最差可达 2.2 倍以上；但经典方法的大失败多因仅有两个季节周期时自动方法退化为非季节模型。此外，该稳健性仅 TimesFM-3 独有，且仅限短视界，长视界、间歇性需求和 M4 等基准上各有不同表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共基准上难以判断时序基础模型是否应取代经典方法，因为几乎没有基准数据集完全不在任何模型的预训练语料中；因此需要通过已知数据生成过程（模型必定未见过）来进行无泄漏的公平比较。

Method: 在九个已知数据生成过程上生成 7200 条序列，采用预注册协议和 12 步预测视界，将 TimesFM-3 与经典方法（ARIMA、指数平滑等，含自动定阶版本）进行对比；并进一步考察季节周期数量、长视界（25–48 步）、间歇性需求、随机参数、重尾噪声、离群值、M4 月度序列及 101 条宏观时序等敏感性分析；同时与其他四个基础模型对比稳健性是否通用。

Result: 两类方法互不占优。TimesFM-3 误差最多为情景最优方法的 1.31 倍，而每个经典方法最差值至少为 2.2 倍；经典方法最大失败发生在仅有两个季节周期时（自动方法退化为非季节模型）。当有四个及以上周期时，自动 ARIMA 在季节 ARIMA 数据上比 TimesFM-3 准确 21–24%。该稳健性在五个基础模型中仅 TimesFM-3 具备，且只限于短视界：在 25–48 步上三个被测试的基础模型都预测饱和过程下降而实际水平持平，在其他情形自动 ARIMA 的最差表现更小。在间歇性需求上 TimesFM-3 相比基于历史的简单基准无优势。随机参数、重尾噪声和离群值不改变这些定性结论；在 1000 条 M4 月度序列官测期上 TimesFM-3 与第 5–7 名持平、落后于前四名；在 101 条训练期之后的宏观序列上与经典方法统计上无差异。

Conclusion: 在无泄漏的已知过程数据上，TimesFM-3 不具备全面替代经典方法的优势，其稳健性（最差情景误差可控）具有短视界、模型特定等限定条件；因研究的是黑箱模型的行为而非原因，且发现受限于所考察的过程与视界，结论是条件性的。

Abstract: Time-series foundation models such as Google's TimesFM-3 forecast series they were never trained on, but whether they should replace classical methods such as ARIMA and exponential smoothing is hard to settle on public benchmarks, because few benchmark datasets are absent from every model's pre-training corpus. This exploratory study compares TimesFM-3 with classical methods on data generated from nine known processes, which the model cannot have seen. Across 7200 series and a pre-registered protocol with a 12-step horizon, neither family dominates. TimesFM-3's error is never more than 1.31 times that of the best method in a scenario, whereas every classical method's is at least 2.2 times somewhere; the largest classical failures occur with only two seasonal cycles, where the automatic methods fall back to non-seasonal models. With four or more cycles automatic ARIMA is 21-24% more accurate than TimesFM-3 on seasonal ARIMA data. Among five foundation models this robustness is specific to TimesFM-3, and it holds only at short horizons: at steps 25 to 48 the three foundation models tested there forecast a decline on a saturating process whose level stays flat, and automatic ARIMA has the smaller worst case elsewhere. On intermittent demand TimesFM-3 shows no advantage over simple benchmarks built from the history. Randomised parameters, heavy-tailed noise and outliers leave these patterns qualitatively unchanged; on the official test period of 1000 M4 monthly series TimesFM-3 is level with the fifth- to seventh-ranked M4 entries, behind the four best, and on 101 macroeconomic series observed after the models' documented training data it is statistically level with the classical methods. The study characterises the behaviour of a black-box model, not the reasons for it, and its findings are conditional on the processes and horizons examined.

</details>


### [123] [Standardized Dynamic Borrowing: Uniform Control of Information Borrowing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00617)
*Huan Wang,Fei Wu,Yeh-Fong Chen*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出标准化动态借用（SDB）方法，通过将借用程度与经上确界标准化的贝叶斯因子绑定，为临床试验中的外部信息借用提供了透明、可预先指定且可控制的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态借用能利用外部信息提升临床试验效率，但现有方法缺乏透明、可预先设定的借用控制，难以在保证操作特征（功效与I类错误）的前提下解释和约束借用程度。

Method: SDB 结合一个"源信息"分量与一个"无借用"分量，并用经容许目标数据上确界标准化的贝叶斯因子更新二者的相对优势（odds）。无借用分量下的后验密度通过对目标数据似然归一化得到。源分量初始权重是其后验权重的尖锐一致上界，构成可解释的借用约束。在同属单参数正则指数族时，后验期望局部信息比有效样本量等于目标样本量加借用的源先验有效样本量减去非负异质性惩罚项。对正态结局，借用取决于一个标准化的源-目标冲突 Z 分数，并按标准正态核衰减。

Result: 作者联合选择初始权重与目标样本量以满足预先设定的功效与 I 类错误要求；正态与二项分布的示例表明 SDB 能将源-目标相容性转化为数据自适应的借用，且校准程序能在操作特征约束下预先指定源分量初始权重与目标样本量。

Conclusion: SDB 提供了一个透明框架，用于预先指定、控制并解释动态借用，使外部信息的借用程度具有可解释的约束与明确的操作特征保障。

Abstract: Dynamic borrowing can improve efficiency in clinical trials using external information, but existing methods may not offer transparent, prespecified control over borrowing. We introduce Standardized Dynamic Borrowing (SDB), which combines a source-informed component with a no-borrowing component and updates their relative odds using a Bayes factor standardized by its supremum over admissible target data. The posterior density under the no-borrowing component is obtained by normalizing the target-data likelihood. The prespecified source-component initial weight is a sharp uniform upper bound on its posterior weight, providing an interpretable borrowing constraint. When source and target models belong to the same one-parameter canonical exponential family, posterior expected local-information-ratio effective sample size equals target sample size plus borrowed source-prior effective sample size minus a nonnegative heterogeneity penalty. For normally distributed outcomes, borrowing depends on a standardized source-target conflict $Z$-score and decreases according to a standard normal kernel. We also jointly select the initial weight and target sample size to meet prespecified power and Type I error requirements. Normal and binomial illustrations demonstrate how SDB translates source-target compatibility into data-adaptive borrowing and how the calibration procedure prespecifies the source-component initial weight and target sample size under operating-characteristic constraints. These results provide a transparent framework for prespecifying, controlling, and interpreting dynamic borrowing.

</details>


### [124] [Discussion on "Principled type I error rate inflation in two-arm clinical trial designs with external control information borrowing" by Silvia Calderazzo, Manuel Wiesenfarth, Vivienn Weru, and Annette Kopp-Schneider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00711)
*Tim Friede,Christian Röver*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文是一篇讨论性评论，针对 Calderazzo 等人提出的贝叶斯借用外部对照数据的检验程序，论证其条件化方案实际意义有限，并建议通过调整似然函数来应对假设违背问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在涉及信息性先验的贝叶斯分析中，基于固定条件评估频率学派的第一类错误或功效并不能完全契合贝叶斯分析的目标；特别是在借用外部对照数据时，需避免同时对参数和数据条件化，作者希望厘清这一方法学争议。

Method: 作者以 Calderazzo 等人的示例案例为对象，分析其检验程序中的条件化方案，论证其实际局限，并提出若主要关切是假设违背，应通过修改似然函数而非调整条件化方式来应对。

Result: 论证得出两点：其一，该条件化方案在实际应用中意义有限；其二，假设违背问题更适合通过调整似然函数来解决。

Conclusion: 在借用外部对照数据的贝叶斯检验中，不应过分依赖基于固定条件的频率学派性质评估，而应更多关注模型假设本身，并通过适应性的似然函数构建来应对潜在的假设违背。

Abstract: Consideration of frequentist type-I error or power based on certain fixed conditions may not fully embrace the aims of a Bayesian analysis; this is particularly true in settings where informative priors are involved. Calderazzo et al. consider adaptations of a testing procedure involving Bayesian borrowing from external control data. Particular caution may be necessary here in order not to condition on parameters and data simultaneously. We consider their example case and argue that (a) the conditioning scheme may have limited practical implications, and (b) if the main concern are assumption violations, these might be better addressed by adapting the likelihood function.

</details>


### [125] [Factor Model Estimation for High-Dimensional Time Series with Heteroskedastic Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00766)
*Da Zhao,Jing Chen*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种融合同期协方差与滞后自协方差信息的因子载荷空间估计方法，利用 HeteroPCA 校正横截面异方差白噪声的污染，并给出估计一致性、收敛速率及实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于滞后自协方差的因子载荷估计方法未充分利用同期协方差信息；而在横截面异方差白噪声下，同期协方差矩阵被异质的对角噪声污染，难以直接使用。作者旨在结合两类协方差信息，并消除噪声污染，从而提升因子载荷空间的估计精度与收敛速率。

Method: 该方法将同期协方差矩阵与滞后自协方差信息合并为一个估计矩阵，针对横截面异方差白噪声带来的异质对角噪声污染，应用已有的 HeteroPCA 算法进行偏差校正，同时保留序列相依性信息。框架还支持在满足稀疏性与非相干性条件的已知条目集合上存在噪声污染的情形。在依赖观测的正则条件下建立一致性并推导收敛速率。

Result: 在正则条件下证明了估计量的一致性并给出收敛速率，识别出纳入同期协方差相较于仅用滞后自协方差估计量能够改善收敛速率的参数区域。模拟研究评估了载荷空间估计误差以及同期协方差引入和对角校正的作用；对 S&P 500 股票收益率的实证应用验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 结合同期协方差与滞后自协方差的估计框架，经 HeteroPCA 校正后能够在异方差噪声下有效估计因子载荷空间，并在特定参数区间取得更优收敛速率，具有理论和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Factor modeling provides a framework for extracting common components from high-dimensional time series. We develop an estimator of the factor loading space that combines contemporaneous covariance with lagged autocovariance information. Under cross-sectionally heteroskedastic white noise, the contemporaneous covariance contains heterogeneous diagonal noise contributions. We apply the existing HeteroPCA algorithm to the combined estimation matrix to correct this contamination while retaining information from serial dependence. The framework also accommodates noise contamination on a known set of entries subject to sparsity and incoherence conditions. Under regularity conditions for dependent observations, we establish consistency and derive convergence rates, identifying regimes in which incorporating contemporaneous covariance improves the rate relative to the lagged-autocovariance estimator. Simulations evaluate loading-space estimation error and the effects of contemporaneous covariance inclusion and diagonal correction. An application to S\&P 500 stock returns illustrates the method.

</details>


### [126] [Scalar-on-function regression with measurement error in the functional regressors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00867)
*Xiaochen Cai,Fenglin Xie,Todd Ogden*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对标量-函数回归中函数型协变量存在测量误差的问题，提出基于模拟外推（SIMEX）的方法来校正系数函数估计的衰减偏差，并通过模拟研究、多种扩展（复杂噪声、重复观测、广义响应、2D/3D函数型预测变量）以及扩散张量成像数据应用加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量-函数回归的现有方法大多隐含假设函数型协变量被精确观测，但实际中这些协变量常受测量误差污染，导致系数函数估计产生衰减。因此需要一种能处理函数型回归变量带误差的矫正方法。

Method: 提出模拟外推（SIMEX）方法：先估计误差方差，建立添加误差方差的序列与对应系数函数估计之间的关系，再外推至零误差情形。文中描述了三种外推估计系数函数序列的方法，并与基于平滑样条和函数型主成分分析的两种预平滑方法进行模拟对比。

Result: 模拟研究表明模拟外推方法能够校正测量误差引起的系数函数衰减；并在更复杂的噪声协方差结构、函数型预测变量的多次重复观测、广义响应以及2D和3D函数型预测变量等方向上进行了方法扩展。

Conclusion: 模拟外推方法可有效处理函数型协变量带测量误差的标量-函数回归问题，并通过扩散张量成像数据的应用展示了该方法的实用性。

Abstract: We consider the problem of scalar-on-function regression. Most existing methods implicitly assume that the functional covariates are observed exactly, but in practice, they are often contaminated by measurement error. Our goal, therefore, is to deal with the problem of scalar-on-function regression when the regressor functions are observed with error. In this paper, we propose a simulation-extrapolation method to correct for the attenuation of estimated coefficient functions caused by the error. The method first estimates the error variance, establishes the relationship between a sequence of added error variance and the corresponding estimates of coefficient functions, and then extrapolates to the zero-error. We describe three methods to extrapolate the sequence of estimated coefficient functions. In a simulation study, we compare the performance of the simulation-extrapolation method with two pre-smoothing methods based on smoothing splines and functional principal component analysis. Next, we discuss the extension of the method in several directions, allowing for more complex noise covariance structures, multiple replications of functional predictors, generalized responses, and 2D and 3D functional predictors. Finally, we illustrate the methods by an application to diffusion tensor imaging data.

</details>


### [127] [Modeling Bipartite Dynamic Networks: An Additive and Multiplicative Effects Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00900)
*Jing Luo*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对纵向二分网络（bipartite networks）发展了一套加性-乘性效应（AME）建模框架，并提出基于块坐标下降与平方迭代法的估计程序，在模拟和全球生产网络应用中验证了其优于传统方法的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二分网络（如国家-产品、作者-论文等两类不同行动者间的互动）呈现的依赖模式与单模网络不同，现有模型难以恰当刻画其结构；同时纵向数据下行动者节点的加性/乘性潜效应会随时间演变，需要专门针对二分纵向结构的模型来准确估计系数并恢复数据生成过程。

Method: 提出二分动态 AME（additive and multiplicative effects）模型：首先区分并明确二分网络的依赖结构及相应建模假设；其次引入模型并开发基于块坐标下降（block coordinate descent）的估计流程；再次融入平方迭代法（squared iterative method）提升计算效率；并提供 R 包 RAMEN 以方便实现。

Result: 模拟研究和全球生产网络的应用表明，该模型改进了系数估计、更准确地恢复数据生成过程，并更好地捕捉乘法潜结构。模型揭示出各国在全球生产参与中随时间演变的模式，这些模式无法被观测协变量或静态设定所捕获。

Conclusion: 所提出的二分动态 AME 模型及其高效估计算法能有效刻画纵向二分网络的依赖结构，在估计精度与潜结构恢复上优于既有方法，并成功揭示全球生产网络中未被观测协变量解释的动态演变规律，具有较强的理论与应用价值。

Abstract: Researchers frequently study interactions between two distinct types of actors, represented as bipartite networks. These networks exhibit dependence patterns that differ from those in one-mode networks and therefore require models tailored to their structure. This paper develops an additive and multiplicative effects (AME) framework for longitudinal bipartite data. First, I distinguish the dependence structure and specify the corresponding modeling assumptions. Second, I introduce the bipartite dynamic AME model and develop an estimation procedure based on block coordinate descent. Third, I incorporate a squared iterative method to improve computational efficiency. Using simulations and an application to global production networks, I show that the model improves coefficient estimation, more accurately recovers the data-generating process, and better captures the multiplicative latent structure. The model reveals evolving patterns in countries' global production engagement that are not captured by observed covariates or static specifications. I provide an R package, RAMEN, to facilitate implementation.

</details>


### [128] [Quantile Social Autoregressive Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00933)
*Liyuan Wang,Danyang Huang,Wei Lan,Chih-Ling Tsai*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出分位数社会自回归模型，用同伴响应的经验分位数替代传统的线性平均，从而捕捉极端同伴行为的影响，并给出了估计方法、识别条件和大样本理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有同伴效应建模主要依赖线性均值模型（linear-in-means），对同伴响应取平均会掩盖极端同伴行为对个体行为的影响。为克服这一局限，需要一种能够刻画同伴分布中不同分位段（尤其是极端行为）影响的建模框架。

Method: 提出基于"分位数社会规范"（quantile social norm，即同伴响应的经验分位数）的分位数社会自回归模型；将分位数水平本身作为未知参数由数据直接估计，以识别影响个体行为最强的同伴分布区段。估计方面引入基于伪响应工具变量（pseudo-response instruments）的新矩条件；因分位数社会规范非光滑，对工具变量与残差施加核平滑以保证有效统计推断。此外，建立均衡存在唯一性与识别条件，并证明估计量的一致性与渐近正态性，通过蒙特卡洛实验与实证应用进行验证。

Result: 论证了模型的均衡存在唯一性并推导出识别条件；所提核平滑估计量满足一致性与渐近正态性；蒙特卡洛实验表明有限样本表现良好，实证应用展示了估计出的分位数水平及其对应同伴效应的可解释性。

Conclusion: 分位数社会自回归模型突破了线性均值模型的限制，能够识别并量化同伴响应分布中特定分位段（包括极端行为）对个体行为的影响，并具备完善的统计推断理论基础。

Abstract: Research on modelling peer effects has predominantly relied on linear-in-means models. However, averaging peer responses prevents these models from capturing the effects of extreme peer behavior. To address this limitation, we propose a quantile social autoregressive model based on a novel quantile social norm, which uses empirical quantiles of peer responses. By treating the quantile level as an unknown parameter estimated directly from the data, our approach identifies which segment of the peer response distribution most strongly influences individual behavior. To estimate the model, we introduce new moment conditions using pseudo-response instruments. Because the quantile social norm is nonsmooth, we apply kernel smoothing to the instruments and residuals, ensuring valid statistical inference. Additionally, we establish equilibrium existence and uniqueness and derive the identification conditions. Furthermore, we prove the consistency and asymptotic normality of our proposed estimator. Finally, Monte Carlo experiments examine the finite-sample performance of the estimator, and an empirical application illustrates the interpretation of the estimated quantile levels and their corresponding peer effects.

</details>


### [129] [Testing Procedures for Strict Pleiotropy in Genetic Association Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00962)
*Eva Biswas,Nilanjan Chatterjee,Zheyu Wang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于GWAS z-score的统计检验方法，用于识别"严格多效性"（strict pleiotropy）SNP，即同时与多个性状关联的变异位点，并推断其所关联的最少性状数目，可应用于独立或相关性状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多效性是基因组生物学的重要特征，现有方法多侧重于检验SNP是否至少与一个性状相关，而能够识别"严格多效性"（与多于一个性状相关）的方法很少，且要么局限于少量性状，要么随性状数增加在计算上变得困难。

Method: 作者开发了两种检验程序，均仅依赖GWAS提供的z-score，适用于独立及相关性状：（1）频率学派方法，控制族错误率（family-wise error rate，FWER）；（2）基于局部错误发现率（local false discovery rate，lfdr）的方法，在控制FDR的同时提供更高的检验功效。

Result: 通过大量模拟研究，将所提方法与现有严格多效性检验方法进行性能比较，验证了其有效性；并将方法应用于阿尔茨海默病相关生物标志物，以区分代表标志物特异性变异的SNP与疾病相关生物学变异的SNP。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够有效、可扩展地识别严格多效性SNP并推断其关联的最少性状数目，在FWER或FDR控制下具有良好功效，为多性状GWAS分析提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Pleiotropy, the phenomenon in which a single genetic variant influences multiple traits, is a fundamental feature of genome biology and an important consideration in statistical genetics. Many statistical methods exist that leverage pleiotropy to increase the power of association tests, but they are often designed to identify SNPs which are associated with at least one of the underlying traits. Only a limited number of methods exist for identification of ``strict pleiotropy'', i.e. those associated with more than one trait, but these methods are either restricted to a small number of traits or become computationally challenging as the number of traits increases. In this paper, we develop two testing procedures to identify SNPs under strict pleiotropy and determine the corresponding minimum number of associated traits. Both methods rely only on z-scores available from genome-wide association studies (GWAS) and are applicable to independent as well as correlated traits. The first procedure is frequentist in nature and is designed to control the family-wise-error rate. The second procedure is based on the local false discovery rate and provides improved power while maintaining FDR control. We conduct extensive simulation studies to compare the performance of the proposed methods with alternative methods for testing strict pleiotropy. Finally, we demonstrate an application of the proposed methods to Alzheimer's disease-related biomarkers for the goal of distinguishing SNPs that may represent biomarker-specific variations from those that are related to disease-relevant biology.

</details>


### [130] [Augmented Patient Preference Incorporated Reinforcement Learning (APP-RL) to Estimate the Optimal Dynamic Treatment Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01044)
*Yingchao Zhong,Lu Wang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出 APP-RL 方法，通过数据增强将患者的潜在偏好纳入基于树的强化学习框架，用于多阶段、多治疗情境下的动态治疗方案估计，兼顾个性化与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态治疗方案（DTR）文献通常只纳入患者的病史信息，而忽略患者自身的偏好，这可能导致所估计的最优治疗方案与患者真实意愿不符，进而影响依从性和治疗效果。

Method: 提出 APP-RL（Augmented Patient Preference incorporated Reinforcement Learning）方法：先基于患者对问卷的回答推导其潜在偏好的后验分布（数据增强），再利用估计到的偏好对多个结局进行加权，以此识别每个阶段的最优个性化决策；针对多阶段问题，在每个阶段生长一棵决策树，并通过逆向归纳（backward induction）递归地实现算法。

Result: 所提方法稳健、高效，能给出可解释的动态治疗方案估计；通过模拟研究对其有限样本表现进行了全面评估。

Conclusion: APP-RL 将患者偏好融入树基强化学习以估计多阶段最优动态治疗方案，既保留了决策树的可解释性，又使方案更贴合患者个体偏好，具有良好的稳健性与效率。

Abstract: Dynamic treatment regimes (DTRs) are sequential decision rules that individualize treatments to each patient at each treatment stage adapting to their past clinical course. Existing literature typically accommodates each individual's medical history, but overlooks a patient's preferences. We propose a method that incorporates a patient's latent preferences through data augmentation into a tree-based reinforcement learning method to estimate optimal dynamic treatment regimes for multi-stage, multi-treatment settings. For each patient at each stage, we derive the posterior distribution of preferences given responses to a questionnaire, and then subsequently weight multiple outcomes with the estimated preferences to identify the optimal stage-wise personalized decision. For multiple stage situations, we grow a decision tree at each stage and implement the algorithm recursively using backward induction. Our proposed method, named Augmented Patient Preference incorporated Reinforcement Learning (APP-RL) is robust, efficient, and leads to interpretable DTR estimation. The finite-sample performances of the proposed method has been thoroughly evaluated through simulation studies.

</details>


### [131] [Polylogarithmic Sparsity of Randomly Reweighted NPMLEs for Gaussian Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01088)
*Hansheng Jiang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文证明：对高斯位置混合模型的非参数最大似然估计(NPMLE)施加一个趋于零的随机扰动，可获得精确的多对数级稀疏性，且所得随机重加权估计量在高维下原子数仅为O((log n/log log n)^d + log n)，同时保持近似最优的似然和参数化（至多对数因子）的Hellinger收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典NPMLE的混合分布可能不唯一，且其支撑原子数的传统界随样本量n线性增长，缺乏稀疏性保证。作者希望在不借助支撑惩罚项的条件下，构造一个唯一且稀疏的估计量，同时不损失统计效率。

Method: 对似然施加一个随n趋于零的随机扰动，得到随机重加权NPMLE：最大化带独立Gamma权重的加权似然，权重在n增大时集中于1。核心证明基于"正核混合的有效维度原理"：拟合值的低维变化控制每一个极大值点集的极值点的支撑，从而导出精确的多对数级原子数界和唯一性。

Result: 以高概率，(1) 随机重加权NPMLE唯一；(2) 在d维下原子数为O{(log n/log log n)^d + log n}；(3) 几乎最大化普通似然；(4) 混合密度的Hellinger估计速率达到参数化水平，仅多出对数因子。稀疏性对估计量本身成立（而非其近似），且无需支撑惩罚。数值实验验证其Hellinger风险与支撑大小与普通NPMLE相当。

Conclusion: 通过对似然施加消失的随机扰动，可在不牺牲统计效率的前提下严格实现唯一且多对数稀疏的高斯混合NPMLE，稀疏界远优于线性界，并为正核混合估计的支撑控制提供了新的有效维度理论工具。

Abstract: The nonparametric maximum likelihood estimator (NPMLE) of a Gaussian location mixture maximizes the likelihood over the infinite-dimensional space of mixing distributions. The maximizing mixing distribution can be nonunique, and the classical bound on its number of atoms grows linearly with the sample size $n$. We show that a vanishingly small random perturbation of the likelihood yields exact polylogarithmic sparsity. The resulting randomly reweighted NPMLE maximizes a weighted likelihood whose independent weights, taken to be Gamma in our analysis, concentrate around one as $n$ grows.
