With high probability, it is unique, has $O\{(\log n/\log\log n)^d+\log n\}$ atoms in dimension $d$, nearly maximizes the ordinary likelihood, and estimates the mixture density at a Hellinger rate that is parametric up to logarithmic factors. This sparsity holds for the estimator itself, not for an approximation of it, and requires no support penalty. The proof rests on an effective-dimension principle for positive kernel mixtures: low-dimensional variation of the fitted values controls the support of every extreme point of the set of maximizers. Numerical illustrations verify that the reweighted NPMLE has Hellinger risk and support size comparable to those of the ordinary NPMLE.

</details>


### [132] [Certified Alpha Capacity: Statistical Evidence, Economic Lifetime, and Arbitrage under Decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01115)
*Nicolò Bonacorsi*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究交易信号能否在其经济价值衰减之前积累足够统计证据以供可靠部署，通过在统一的 Kullback-Leibler 信息尺度上度量统计证据与剩余机会，得出高斯模型下的精确可行性阈值及更一般情形下的信息下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交易信号存在一个根本矛盾：统计验证需要时间与数据，但信号的经济价值会随市场套利活动而衰减，因此需要判断信号能否在失效前被可靠确证。

Method: 将统计证据与剩余交易机会统一映射到 Kullback-Leibler 信息尺度；在正则高斯模型下推导精确的可行性阈值，并给出超越该模型的通用信息论下界；进一步分析有限信息预算所引发的生存前沿、搜索惩罚与市场均衡。

Result: 有限信息预算会产生生存前沿和搜索惩罚，并形成一种市场均衡，即套利活动会缩短可确证机会的存续期；对资金费率数据的回顾性研究验证了持续性的作用。

Conclusion: 通过统一的信息尺度可以严格刻画交易信号"确证速度 vs. 价值衰减"的可行性边界，说明套利行为本身会缩短机会寿命，持续性在可部署信号的判定中起关键作用。

Abstract: In this paper we study whether a trading signal can accumulate enough statistical evidence for reliable deployment before its economic value decays. We solve the problem by measuring statistical evidence and remaining opportunity on a common Kullback-Leibler information scale, obtaining an exact feasibility threshold in a canonical Gaussian model and general information lower bounds beyond it. We show that finite information budgets generate survival frontiers, search penalties, and a market equilibrium in which arbitrage activity shortens the lifetime of certifiable opportunities; a retrospective funding-rate study illustrates the role of persistence.

</details>


### [133] [Spatio-temporal coarse-grained Hawkes processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01151)
*Tomoya Uda,Shinsuke Koyama*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出时空粗粒度Hawkes过程，用于直接建模时空聚合的计数数据，无需引入潜事件时间或位置；通过构造有效激发核并纳入同一时间bin内的内部激发，实现了比传统分箱泊松近似更高阶的逼近，并给出矩估计及应用到地震ETAS模型的实证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时空Hawkes过程的推断需要每个事件的发生时间和位置，但在许多应用中观测数据仅为按时间区间和空间区域聚合的计数。作者希望绕开潜变量（事件时间/位置）的引入，直接对聚合计数数据进行建模与推断，同时保持对底层连续时空Hawkes过程的高精度近似。

Method: 提出时空粗粒度Hawkes过程这一离散时间计数模型：通过在时空bin上对时间和空间触发效应求平均来构造有效激发核，并显式纳入同一时间bin内的内部激发。作者刻画了该过程的一阶和二阶性质，并在时间与空间离散化同时趋于精细的联合极限下，推导其平稳均值与自协方差相对于对应聚合Hawkes过程的渐近逼近误差。

Result: 理论分析表明，由于计入了同一时间bin内的内部激发，该模型相比传统分箱泊松近似提供了更高阶的逼近。数值实验验证了理论结果，并显示在较大范围的聚合尺度上都能实现精确近似。

Conclusion: 作者进一步提出基于矩的估计方法，并将该框架通过聚合的ETAS模型应用于地震发生数据。结果表明，所提方法为时空聚合事件数据的推断与预测提供了一种计算高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal Hawkes processes are widely used to model self-exciting event data, but conventional inference methods require access to the occurrence time and location of every event. In many applications, however, observations are available only as counts aggregated over temporal intervals and spatial regions. We propose a spatio-temporal coarse-grained Hawkes process, a discrete-time count model that approximates aggregated observations generated by an underlying spatio-temporal Hawkes process. The proposed model constructs effective excitation kernels by averaging the temporal and spatial triggering effects over spatio-temporal bins while explicitly incorporating excitation occurring within the same temporal bin. This formulation enables direct modeling of aggregated count data without introducing latent event times or locations. We characterize the first- and second-order properties of the proposed process and derive asymptotic approximation errors for the stationary mean and autocovariance relative to those of the corresponding aggregated Hawkes process in the joint limit of fine temporal and spatial discretization. The analysis shows that the proposed model provides a higher-order approximation than the conventional binned Poisson approximation by accounting for intra-bin excitation. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical results and demonstrate accurate approximation over a broad range of aggregation scales. We further develop a moment-based estimation procedure and apply the proposed framework to earthquake occurrence data through an aggregated Epidemic-Type Aftershock Sequence (ETAS) model. The results indicate that the proposed approach provides a computationally efficient alternative for inference and prediction from spatio-temporally aggregated event data.

</details>


### [134] [Testing and segmentation of joint and individual components in integrative multi-source factor models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01313)
*Kyoowon Kim,Sungkyu Jung*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出MSSAT（Multi-Source Sequential Alignment Test），一种基于几何对齐与渐近假设检验的无重采样顺序检验方法，用于从多源数据中分离共享（联合）结构与源特异性（个体）变异，在准确性与计算速度上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多源数据整合中分离共享结构与源特异变异是一项基础任务。现有联合-个体模型依赖计算密集的优化或松弛的谱界，导致分离精度欠佳、可扩展性差，亟需一种既准确又高效的方法。

Method: 提出MSSAT，利用“真实联合成分在不同数据源间表现为紧密对齐的得分子空间”这一几何观察，推导对齐统计量的渐近零分布，构建一个严格的、免重采样的顺序检验程序，以准确估计联合秩（joint rank）。

Result: 大量模拟实验以及真实数据应用（含TCGA多组学数据集）表明，与竞争方法相比，MSSAT实现了更高的分离准确度，且计算速度显著更快。

Conclusion: MSSAT通过几何对齐与渐近检验框架，有效解决了联合-个体分离中精度与可扩展性难以兼顾的问题，在多源数据整合任务中具有显著优势和应用价值。

Abstract: Disentangling shared (joint) structures from source-specific (individual) variations is a fundamental task in multi-source data integration. Existing joint-individual models often rely on computationally intensive optimization or loose spectral bounds, leading to suboptimal separation accuracy and poor scalability. In this paper, we propose the Multi-Source Sequential Alignment Test (MSSAT). MSSAT leverages the geometric observation that true joint components manifest as closely aligned score subspaces across different data sources. By deriving the asymptotic null distribution of our alignment statistic, we develop a rigorous, resampling-free sequential testing procedure to accurately estimate the joint rank. Extensive simulations and real data applications, including a TCGA multi-omics dataset, demonstrate that MSSAT achieves superior separation accuracy and substantially faster computation compared to competing methods.

</details>


### [135] [Efficient Bayesian inference for multiple network data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01532)
*Francesco Barile,Sara Capozio,Bernardo Nipoti*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究中心化 Erdős–Rényi 分布的分布性质，并针对多网络数据提出一种半共轭贝叶斯方法；模拟显示 Gibbs 采样与经验贝叶斯均能准确恢复网络摘要，其中经验贝叶斯随网络规模高效扩展，并提供 R 包 BayesCER 实现该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多网络（multiple network）数据的贝叶斯建模缺乏高效且可扩展的推断方法，且中心化 Erdős–Rényi 分布的分布性质尚未被充分研究，限制了其在贝叶斯框架下的应用。

Method: 首先考察中心化 Erdős–Rényi 分布的分布性质；随后提出一种针对多网络数据的半共轭贝叶斯方法，分别采用 Gibbs 采样与经验贝叶斯进行推断，并配套实现 R 包 BayesCER。

Result: 模拟实验表明，Gibbs 采样和经验贝叶斯都能准确恢复网络摘要统计量，且经验贝叶斯方法随网络规模增大具有良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 基于中心化 Erdős–Rényi 分布构造的半共轭贝叶斯方法能够有效、可扩展地处理多网络数据，配套的 BayesCER 软件包便于实践应用。

Abstract: We investigate distributional properties of the centered Erdős--Rényi distribution (Lunagòmez et al., 2021) and propose a semi-conjugate Bayesian approach to multiple network data. In simulations, both Gibbs sampling and empirical Bayes accurately recover network summaries, with the latter scaling efficiently with network size. As a companion to this note, we provide the R package BayesCER, which implements the proposed methodology.

</details>


### [136] [Dynamic Spatial Bayesian Machine Learning Model: Applications to Intergenerational Economic Mobility and Geographic Income Inequality in the United States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00072)
*Hammed A. Olayinka,Saheed O. Olayemi*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合马蹄(Horseshoe)收缩的动态空间面板贝叶斯加性回归树模型(DSP-BART-HS)，用于高维时空面板数据，并在9种数据生成场景、各200次重复下与结构空间计量、非参数机器学习和区域小域估计等方法进行全面比较，结果显示该模型在所有场景中均为最优或与最优无显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理高维时空面板数据时存在局限：传统的区域-时间聚合比较器在个体层面非线性主导结果方差时性能严重退化，并且缺乏自动变量选择与灵活推断能力。作者希望构建一个能同时应对不规则空间拓扑、密集政策效应和非线性个体交互的灵活框架。

Method: 开发DSP-BART-HS模型，将动态空间面板结构、BART（贝叶斯加性回归树）树集成与Horseshoe收缩先验相结合，实现自动变量选择。通过9种数据生成场景（各200次重复，涵盖不规则空间拓扑、密集政策效应、非线性个体交互等）与结构空间计量、非参数机器学习、小域估计等方法进行联合评估，并利用零训练区域空间留出检验空间扩散机制。

Result: DSP-BART-HS在每一个场景中均为最优或与最优估计量在统计上无显著差异。传统区域-时间聚合方法在个体非线性的作用占主导时性能落后三倍以上。模型通过空间扩散机制在零训练区域空间留出下仍保持强预测精度。在美国县级面板应用（代际经济流动性和地理收入不平等）中，在真实未见区域、随机和时间留出下均表现良好。但局限性在于：时间外推场景下，较轻的区域自回归设定MTS-CAR-X具有稳健且持续的优势。

Conclusion: DSP-BART-HS为分层时空面板数据提供了强预测性能、自动变量选择和灵活推断，弥补了传统方法在个体非线性下的不足；但在时间外推方面不如较轻的MTS-CAR-X设定。

Abstract: We develop a Dynamic Spatial Panel Bayesian Additive Regression Trees model with Horseshoe shrinkage (DSP-BART-HS) for high-dimensional spatio-temporal panel data. We jointly evaluate the model against a comprehensive suite of structural spatial econometrics, non-parametric machine learning methods, and small-area estimators across nine data-generating scenarios spanning 200 replicates each, including irregular spatial topologies, dense policy effects, and non-linear individual-level interactions. DSP-BART-HS is the best or statistically indistinguishable from the best estimator in every scenario. Conventional region-time-aggregate comparators suffer severe performance degradation -- trailing by a factor of three or more -- whenever individual-level non-linearity drives outcome variance, a limitation this framework's tree-ensemble design directly addresses. The model also maintains strong predictive accuracy under a zero-training-region spatial holdout via its spatial diffusion mechanism. We demonstrate practical utility across two U.S. county-level panel applications -- intergenerational economic mobility and geographic income inequality -- under genuine unseen-region, random, and temporal holdouts, with results validated through repeated-split uncertainty quantification and paired significance testing against every comparator. One clear limitation emerges: under temporal extrapolation specifically, a lighter per-region autoregressive specification (MTS-CAR-X) achieves a robust, and consistent advantage. DSP-BART-HS nonetheless establishes strong predictive performance, automated variable selection, and flexible inference for hierarchical spatio-temporal panel data.

</details>


### [137] [Identifying Panel Conditioning with Refreshment Samples: Sharp Bounds and Design Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01654)
*Shoki Okubo*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究面板数据中的“面板条件作用”（panel conditioning，即重复参与调查改变了后续测量），将其建模为重访时的确定性单调映射，并刻画在无限制退出（attrition）下，补充样本（refreshment sample）究竟能识别什么。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 补充样本是应对面板流失（panel attrition）的标准手段，但其背后的识别结果通常假定“参与调查不改变测量”。当这一假定不成立（存在面板条件作用）且退出又不受限制时，传统识别结论失效，因此需要刻画补充样本在无限制退出下对面板条件作用所能识别的确切内容。

Method: 将面板条件作用建模为重访时的一个确定性单调映射；在退出不受限制的设定下，推导候选映射与数据一致的充分必要条件（留存放缩后的幸存者隐含潜在结果分布被补充样本分布“集合支配”），并给出能由显式退出过程合理化该映射的条件。进一步借助密度比条件刻画识别集，讨论无退出时在潜在结果支撑上的点识别情形；对无限制映射给出幸存者均值效应的修剪界（trimming bounds）；对全类别报告条目将模型退化为“无条件作用”检验；并考察同队列内其他波次、退出模式与进入波次条目对识别集的影响。

Result: 无退出时映射在潜在结果支撑上可被点识别；有退出时由密度比条件决定识别集，且仅靠尾部行为无法确定该集合；无限制映射下幸存者均值效应为熟悉的修剪界；全类别报告条目时模型等价于无条件作用检验。在同队列内，其他波次、退出模式与进入波次条目不改变识别集（除非有跨波次选择限制）。在显式选择限制下，生存匹配、对称匹配与进入波次校正可识别幸存者效应，并给出这些方法的偏差及识别条件作用路径曲率的秩条件。

Conclusion: 在面板流失无限制且存在面板条件作用的情况下，补充样本只能部分识别条件作用映射；其识别集由密度比条件刻画，而尾部行为不足以单独确定。通过施加跨波次的显式选择限制并采用生存匹配、对称匹配或进入波次校正，可以识别幸存者效应与条件作用路径的曲率。作者用日本面板数据对上述结论进行了说明。

Abstract: Refreshment samples are the standard remedy for panel attrition, and the identification results behind them maintain that participation does not change measurement. We characterize what a refreshment sample identifies about panel conditioning, modelled as a deterministic monotone map at reinterview, when attrition is unrestricted. A candidate map is consistent with the data if and only if the retention-scaled distribution of the stayers' implied latent outcomes is setwise dominated by the refreshment distribution; every such map is rationalized by an explicit attrition process. Without attrition the map is identified on the latent-outcome support; with attrition, a density-ratio condition governs the identified set, and tail behaviour alone does not determine it. For an unrestricted map the survivors' mean effect has the familiar trimming bounds; for an item with all categories reported, the model reduces to a test of no conditioning. Within a cohort, other waves, dropout patterns and entry-wave items leave the set unchanged unless restrictions link selection across waves. Under explicit selection restrictions, survival matching, symmetric matching and entry-wave correction identify survivor effects. We derive their biases and give rank conditions under which refreshment schedules identify curvature in the conditioning path. A Japanese panel illustrates the results.

</details>


### [138] [Markov chain Monte Carlo for predictively oriented posteriors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01818)
*Yann McLatchie,Louis Sharrock,David T. Frazier,Jeremias Knoblauch*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对预测导向后验（predictively oriented posterior）无法显式计算密度、难以用传统蒙特卡洛采样的难题，推导出一个可逐点求值密度、且近似误差快速衰减的近似，并在流行病学、空间统计和低能核物理案例中加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测导向后验即使在模型误设下也能提供有原则的不确定性量化，但由于缺乏显式密度，无法采用经典蒙特卡洛采样算法进行计算，这限制了其实际应用。

Method: 作者推导了预测导向后验的一个近似形式，使该近似的密度可以被逐点（point-wise）求值，从而可用标准蒙特卡洛方法采样；同时证明其近似误差随尺度快速衰减。

Result: 所提出的近似具有可显式求值的密度，且近似误差迅速衰减，使其可通过经典蒙特卡洛算法有效计算。

Conclusion: 该方法在流行病学、空间统计和低能核物理等多个领域的案例研究中得到验证，表明该近似方案能够在不牺牲误差衰减速度的前提下，为预测导向后验提供可计算性。

Abstract: The predictively oriented posterior offers principled uncertainty quantification, even under model misspecification. However, it does not admit an explicit density and therefore cannot be computed using classical Monte Carlo sampling algorithms. We remedy this by deriving an approximation to the predictively oriented posterior whose density can be evaluated point-wise, and whose approximation error decays rapidly. These results are illustrated on case studies from epidemiology, spatial statistics, and low-energy nuclear physics.

</details>


### [139] [Generalized Engression Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01823)
*Xinwei Shen,Zijian Guo,Francis Bach*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出广义 engression 模型（generalized engression），这是一个统一的非参数分布回归框架，可对连续、二元、分类、有序、排名等任意混合类型且存在条件依赖的多元结果估计其给定协变量下的条件联合分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元结果往往包含多种数据类型（连续、二元、分类、有序、排名），且各坐标之间存在条件依赖。现有方法通常针对单一结果类型设计，且大多只估计每个坐标的均值等概要量，而非结果向量的联合分布。因此需要一种能统一处理任意类型结果并直接建模联合分布的方法。

Method: 该方法建立在 engression——一种基于评分规则（scoring rule）的深度生成模型——之上，引入数据类型特定的链接函数（data-type-specific link function）和随机扰动（stochastic perturbation）来平滑损失，从而即使在链接不连续的情况下也能进行基于梯度的训练。同时建立了针对连续、离散和混合结果的通用表示（universal representation）结果。

Result: 在模拟实验和两项实际应用中（社区生态学基准中的 242 个物种，以及一个 17 维混合类型健康结果），该方法在边际评分上与类型特定模型相当，在联合分布上优于它们，并且达到或超过专门构建的最先进联合物种分布模型（JSDM）。

Conclusion: 广义 engression 为任意类型结果提供了统一且有效的分布回归框架，能同时改进联合分布估计并保持边际表现，配套软件已以 Python 提供。

Abstract: We consider estimating the conditional distribution of a multivariate outcome given covariates when its coordinates may be continuous, binary, categorical, ordinal or rankings, and are conditionally dependent on one another. Different statistical methods have been developed for each outcome type, and most of them target a summary of the conditional distribution, such as the mean of each coordinate, rather than the joint distribution of the outcome vector. We develop generalized engression models, a unified nonparametric distributional regression framework for outcomes of any type. The proposed method builds upon engression, a scoring-rule-based deep generative model, and introduces a data-type-specific link function and a stochastic perturbation that smooths the loss, enabling gradient-based training even with discontinuous links. We establish universal representation results for continuous, discrete and mixed outcomes. In simulations and in two applications, 242 species in a community ecology benchmark and a 17-dimensional mixed-type health outcome, the method matches type-specific models on marginal scores, improves on them on the joint distribution, and matches or exceeds purpose-built state-of-the-art joint species distribution models. Software is available in Python.

</details>


### [140] [Transportability methods for failure-time outcomes under assumptions for relative effect measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02041)
*Sarah E Robertson,Lan Wen,Lawson Ung,Jon A Steingrimsson,Issa J Dahabreh*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出在相对尺度（风险比）上进行可交换性假设的识别方法（风险比可移植性），用于将试验结果外推到目标人群，并在肺癌筛查的实例中证明其优于传统的分布可移植性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分临床领域，相对效应量比绝对效应量在不同人群间更稳定，因此相对尺度上的可交换性假设可能比绝对尺度上的更合理。然而，现有将试验结果外推到目标人群的方法大多依赖强且常不切实际的分布可交换性（分布可移植性）假设。

Method: 作者提出在更合理的相对尺度可交换性假设下、针对失效时间结局（failure-time outcomes）的识别结果，主要聚焦于风险比（risk ratio transportability）。并将该方法应用于估计筛查策略对全因死亡率的影响：使用国家肺筛查试验（NLST）数据，外推到来自国家健康访谈调查（NHIS）的具有全国代表性的目标人群。

Result: 由于NHIS提供了全因死亡率数据且无筛查混杂，可将分布可移植性分析结果与观测到的基线风险做证伪评估，发现分布可移植性方法显著低估了死亡风险；而风险比可移植性方法产生的估计合理，且与观测基线风险相符。

Conclusion: 在分布可移植性失效的某些情境下，基于相对尺度的假设可能更为合理，风险比可移植性方法能提供更准确的效应外推估计。

Abstract: In certain clinical areas, relative effect measures are believed to remain more constant across populations than absolute measures. This suggests that exchangeability assumptions on the relative scale may be more plausible than those on the absolute scale. Despite this, most methods for extending trial results to a target population rely on strong and often implausible distributional exchangeability assumptions between the trial and target population (distributional transportability).
  We propose identification results for failure-time outcomes under a more plausible assumption of exchangeability on the relative scale, focusing primarily on the risk ratio (risk ratio transportability). We applied these methods to estimate the effect of screening strategies on all-cause mortality, using data from the National Lung Screening Trial (NLST) to a nationally representative target population from the National Health Interview Survey (NHIS). All-cause mortality data was available from NHIS, along with no confounding of screening, allowing a falsification assessment of the distributional transportability analysis against the observed baseline risk.We found that this approach substantially underestimated mortality risk. In comparison, the risk ratio transportability approach produced reasonable estimates that matched the baseline risk.Thus, assumptions on relative scales may be more plausible in certain settings where distributional transportability fails.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [Reverse Item Response Theory for Sparsity-Robust Ranking in Fragmented Cancer Drug-Response Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00002)
*Jung Min Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"反向项目反应理论（reverse IRT）"，将癌症类型视为具有耐药能力的潜在"受试者"、药物视为具有逃避难度的"题目"，用于药敏数据分析，在GDSC2大规模数据上验证其稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药敏数据（如GDSC2）存在大量缺失与碎片化评估，传统简单平均法在稀疏数据下估计稳健性差；作者希望引入IRT框架，将癌症类型与药物映射到同一潜在尺度，以更稳健地估计癌症类型级别的体外耐药性与药物的广谱活性。

Method: 采用反向IRT模型，以癌症类型为潜在"受试者"（耐药能力）、药物为"题目"（逃避难度），在242,036个GDSC2药敏测量上估计共享潜在尺度；并通过四种缺失机制（MCAR、癌症偏向稀疏、药物偏向稀疏等，最高60%缺失）验证、留出预测（Brier分数）与五法对比、Bootstrap置信区间、以及PRISM跨平台复现（方向一致性、秩相关）进行评估。

Result: 在60%缺失率下，reverse IRT较简单平均的潜在排序恢复优势为Δρ=+0.089至+0.095（MCAR、癌症偏向、药物偏向多种稀疏情形）；留出预测中IRT取得五法中最佳Brier分数；Bootstrap显示28种癌症中19种具有稳定的耐药/敏感分类；PRISM跨平台复现方向一致性82%，但秩相关仅ρ=0.25。

Conclusion: reverse IRT的贡献在于提供了一种在碎片化评估下具有方法论稳健性的药敏分析框架，而非构建一个普适的临床耐药"排行榜"；其跨平台秩相关较弱（ρ=0.25）提示需谨慎解释为普遍临床耐药排序。

Abstract: We introduce reverse Item Response Theory (IRT) to pharmacogenomic drug-response analysis by treating cancer types as latent "subjects" with resistance ability and drugs as "items" with evasion difficulty. Applied to 242,036 drug sensitivity measurements from the Genomics of Drug Sensitivity in Cancer (GDSC2) database, the model estimates cancer-type-level in-vitro resistance and drug-level broad activity on a shared latent scale. Validation across four missingness regimes demonstrates that reverse IRT better recovers the full-data latent ranking than simple averaging, with advantages of Delta-rho = +0.089 to +0.095 at 60% missingness under MCAR, cancer-biased, and drug-biased sparsity. Held-out prediction confirms IRT achieves the best Brier score among five evaluated methods. Bootstrap confidence intervals show 19 of 28 cancer types have stable resistant/sensitive classifications. Cross-platform PRISM replication shows 82% directional agreement but weak rank-order correlation (rho = 0.25), indicating the contribution is methodological robustness under fragmented evaluation, not a universal clinical resistance leaderboard.

</details>


### [142] [How Far is Adam from Natural Gradient Descent?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00004)
*Vihaan Paka-Hegde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过尺度不变的γ(Δθ)度量，量化了Adam（含动量）与真实自然梯度下降（NGD）之间的几何偏差，发现该偏差具有上下文依赖性：良态条件下偏差小，病态及非凸网络中偏差急剧增大（最高约10³），但偏差增大并不损害最终目标最小化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adam虽为深度学习标准优化器，但其与自然梯度下降（NGD）的几何关系仍存在未解问题。作者旨在厘清Adam完整更新规则（含动量）作为对角经验Fisher近似的结构性偏离来源，并定量评估其在真实损失景观中偏离NGD的程度及其对优化的实际影响。

Method: 将Adam（含动量）的完整更新规则分解为对角经验Fisher近似，并识别出三个结构性误差来源：对角截断、经验标签替换与时间滞后。引入尺度不变度量γ(Δθ)量化与真实NGD的几何偏差，在四种损失景观上实验：良态线性回归、病态线性回归、逻辑回归以及非凸小神经网络。同时对比标准经验Fisher（EF）与改进经验Fisher（iEF）的路径稳定性。

Result: Adam的几何轨迹高度依赖情境：在良态条件下偏差较低，但在病态条件下显著上升，在神经网络中偏差达到约10³。更高的几何漂移与较慢的初始优化相关，但不会损害最终目标最小化——Adam始终收敛到低损失。改进经验Fisher（iEF）轨迹比标准经验Fisher（EF）更稳定，后者频繁振荡或发散。

Conclusion: Adam的实际优化能力可能并非源于对自然梯度路径的紧密追踪，而是源于结构近似误差与动量平滑之间的平衡。这一发现重新诠释了Adam成功的原因，提示其优势来自近似与平滑的综合效应，而非对NGD的忠实近似。

Abstract: Adam is the standard optimizer in deep learning, yet its geometric relationship to natural gradient descent (NGD) contains unresolved questions. We study Adam's full update rule, including momentum, as a diagonal empirical Fisher approximation subject to diagonal truncation, empirical label substitution, and temporal lag. Using the scale-invariant $γ(Δθ)$ metric, we measure Adam's geometric deviation from true NGD across four loss landscapes: well-conditioned linear regression, ill-conditioned linear regression, logistic regression, and a non-convex small neural network. Adam's geometric trajectory is context-dependent. Deviation remains low in well-conditioned settings but rises significantly under ill-conditioning, reaching misalignments of $\approx 10^3$ in the neural network. Higher geometric drift correlates with slower initial optimization but does not degrade final objective minimization; Adam consistently reaches low loss. Furthermore, the improved empirical Fisher (iEF) tracks more stable paths than the standard empirical Fisher (EF), which frequently oscillates or diverges. Our results suggest Adam's practical optimization power may stem from a balance of structural approximation errors and momentum smoothing rather than close tracking of the natural gradient path.

</details>


### [143] [FourierQK: Filter Shape, Admissibility and the Leakage-Coverage Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00009)
*Athanasios Zeris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过受控消融实验系统研究 FourierQK 中频率滤波器的最优形状，发现振荡带通结构（去除直流与奈奎斯特分量）、段落尺度的窄带宽高斯/Mexican Hat 滤波器在双向注意力下效果最佳，而时域因果 Morlet 无法超越标准点积注意力，从而明确了 FourierQK 适用于编码器式场景、自回归生成需因果谱变体的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作（Zeris, 2026e）发现用带通滤波内积替代 Q/K 点积能显著提升注意力性能，但尚未回答"何种滤波器形状最优及其原因"这一自然追问。作者希望系统刻画滤波器性质（直流抑制、奈奎斯特抑制、带宽、中心频率、多尺度覆盖）对性能的影响，以理解带通结构的必要性与适用边界。

Method: 在字符级语言建模（TinyShakespeare，6 层 GPT）上开展受控消融实验，检验五类滤波器属性假设：对比直流/奈奎斯特分量保留与抑制、不同带宽（σ）与中心频率（段落尺度~70 token）的单尺度滤波器、可采纳（零均值 Mexican Hat DOG, m=2）与非可采纳（Gaussian）滤波器、窄带/宽带对双边 FFT 泄漏的影响，以及因果时域 Morlet（K=128 抽头覆盖 T=256 上下文的 50%）在字符尺度的表现。

Result: (1) 直流与奈奎斯特分量有害（val≈2.0，等同相位随机化），证明振荡带通结构必不可少；(2) 最优单尺度带宽为 σ≈2 bins、中心在段落尺度，相较 BASE-DOT 带来 Δ=+1.15 nats 的净增益；(3) 零均值 Mexican Hat DOG 优于同尺度高斯，并对双边 FFT 泄漏提供部分防护；(4) 双边 FFT 泄漏随谱覆盖单调增长——窄带（gap>+4）干净、宽带（gap<+2）泄漏；(5) 字符尺度的因果时域 Morlet 无法超越 BASE-DOT。

Conclusion: 综合发现 (1)-(5)，FourierQK 在双向注意力设定（编码器式，如 BERT）中有效，因为训练与推理时均有全序列上下文；而自回归生成需要因果谱变体（如 MorletQK，解码器式，如 GPT）。

Abstract: Frequency-collapse attention [Zeris, 2026e] achieves large gains over standard dot-product attention by replacing the Q/K dot product with a bandpass-filtered inner product at a learned frequency. A natural follow-up question is: which filter shape works best, and why? We test five hypotheses about filter properties -- DC suppression, Nyquist suppression, bandwidth, centre frequency, and multi-scale coverage -- using a controlled ablation on character-level language modelling (TinyShakespeare, 6-layer GPT). Our main findings are: (1) DC and Nyquist components are actively harmful (val ~= 2.0, equivalent to phase randomisation), confirming that oscillatory bandpass structure is essential, not just any low-dimensional spectral summary; (2) the optimal single-scale bandwidth is sigma ~= 2 bins centred at paragraph scale (~70 tokens), giving a clean gain of Delta = +1.15 nats over BASE-DOT; (3) admissible filters (zero-mean, Mexican Hat DOG m = 2) outperform non-admissible Gaussians at the same scale and provide partial protection against bilateral FFT leakage; (4) bilateral FFT leakage scales monotonically with spectral coverage -- narrowband filters (gap > +4) are clean, wideband filters (gap < +2) are leaky; and (5) causal time-domain Morlet at character scale cannot beat BASE-DOT (K=128 taps covers 50% of T=256 context), motivating word-level experiments in the companion MorletQK paper [Zeris, 2026f]. Together, findings (1)-(5) characterise FourierQK as effective in bidirectional attention settings (encoder-style, e.g. BERT), where full-sequence context is available at both training and inference time; autoregressive generation requires a causal spectral variant such as MorletQK [Zeris, 2026f] (decoder-style, e.g. GPT). Code available at: https://github.com/AthanasiosZeris/energy-gated-attention

</details>


### [144] [Integrating Fairness and Explainability in a Multiple Instance Reinforcement Learning System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00035)
*Bente Hinkenhuis,Seyed Sahand Mohammadi Ziabari,Ali Mohammed Mansoor Alsahag*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究将强化学习多实例学习（RL-MIL）、对抗性去偏与偏好条件超网络结合的学生风险预测框架，发现虽然基线RL-MIL分类性能强，但两个超网络变体均出现模式坍缩，偏好权重无法系统性地控制公平性-性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生表现预测需要兼具准确性、可解释性与公平性。MIL将学生建模为弱标签交互的"包"，适合教育场景，但直接纳入人口统计学信息会带来不公平预测的风险，因此需要在可解释的RL-MIL流程中引入公平性控制机制。

Method: 提出多目标框架：RL-MIL中每个学生被表示为一袋弱标签交互实例，RL智能体选取有信息量的实例供下游分类；结合对抗性去偏（以Equalized Odds为目标）与偏好条件超网络（将用户定义偏好标量作为条件，动态生成网络参数）。评估两个超网络变体，考察偏好权重能否连续控制公平性-性能权衡。

Result: 基线RL-MIL取得强分类性能，但两个超网络扩展均出现模式坍缩：改变偏好权重几乎不产生沿公平性-性能前沿的系统性移动。失败源于目标主导（objective dominance）、条件机制下梯度传播弱、以及动态生成参数间的相互作用。

Conclusion: 公平性目标可以纳入可解释的RL-MIL流程，但仅靠偏好条件化不能保证可控的多目标行为。稳健的公平RL-MIL需要显式的梯度平衡、目标分离和稳定性分析机制。

Abstract: Predicting student performance from educational interaction data requires models that are both accurate and sufficiently transparent to support meaningful intervention, while demographic information introduces an additional risk of unfair predictions. This study investigates a multi-objective framework that combines reinforcement learning-based multiple instance learning (RL-MIL), adversarial debiasing, and preference-conditioned hypernetworks for student-at-risk prediction. MIL represents each student as a bag of weakly labeled interactions, while an RL agent selects informative instances for downstream classification. Two hypernetwork variants are evaluated to determine whether a user-defined preference scalar can continuously control the trade-off between predictive performance and Equalized Odds. The underlying RL-MIL baseline achieves strong classification performance, but both hypernetwork extensions exhibit mode collapse: changing the preference weight produces little systematic movement along the intended fairness-performance frontier. The failure is associated with objective dominance, weak gradient propagation through the conditioning mechanism, and interactions between dynamically generated parameters. The results show that fairness objectives can be incorporated into an interpretable RL-MIL pipeline, but preference conditioning alone does not guarantee controllable multi-objective behavior. Robust fair RL-MIL therefore requires explicit mechanisms for gradient balancing, objective separation, and stability analysis.

</details>


### [145] [Fast Polynomial Transcendentals for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00049)
*Robert Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在 NVIDIA Blackwell GPU 上，使用短多项式程序加速大语言模型中的特殊函数单元（SFU）操作，替换原生 sigmoid、tanh、SiLU 等激活函数，取得了显著的吞吐提升，同时保持相近的训练损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU 各代在矩阵、特殊函数和内存流水线上的扩展速率不一致，导致内核瓶颈随硬件演化而变化；FlashAttention-4 在 Blackwell 上暴露了注意力中的这种不平衡。作者希望测试短多项式程序能否加速 LLM 中其他依赖 SFU 的操作，以缓解 SFU 成为瓶颈的问题。

Method: 首先在隔离的 IEEE FP16 扫描中，对比 PyTorch 原生评估与打包的融合乘加（FMA）多项式程序，覆盖 L2 驻留和 HBM 驻留工作集。随后，在四个 GB200 集成任务中用 3 阶或 4 阶 BF16 多项式程序替换原生 sigmoid、tanh 和 SiLU：密集 SiLU、tanh-softcapped 注意力、sigmoid 注意力，以及路由专家 SwiGLU。多项式程序结合了解析对称性、目标格式舍入和消耗内核内的打包算术。

Result: 隔离路径在 L2 中提速 1.19–2.19 倍，在 HBM 中提速 1.00–1.70 倍。密集 SiLU、tanh-softcapped 注意力和路由专家替换分别将完整训练步吞吐提升 2.7%、2.9% 和 8.0%；sigmoid 注意力替换将完整注意力前向提升 7.4%，完整 GPU 步提升 0.3%。在约 1000 亿 token 的常见训练时长下，最终平滑训练损失差异（多项式减原生）在四个任务中介于 -0.107 到 +0.079 之间。

Conclusion: 短多项式程序能够有效加速 LLM 中的 SFU 操作，在 Blackwell GPU 上带来明显的端到端吞吐提升，同时训练损失差异很小（既有改善也有轻微退化），验证了该方法的实用性与可行性。

Abstract: Graphics processing unit (GPU) generations scale matrix, special-function, and memory pipelines at different rates, so kernel bottlenecks move as hardware evolves. FlashAttention-4 exposed this imbalance inside attention on NVIDIA Blackwell. We test whether short polynomial programs can accelerate other special-function-unit (SFU) operations in large language models (LLMs). We first compare native PyTorch evaluation with packed fused multiply--add (FMA) programs in an isolated IEEE binary16 (FP16) sweep spanning L2-resident and high-bandwidth-memory (HBM)-resident working sets. We then replace native sigmoid, tanh, and sigmoid linear unit (SiLU) with degree-3 or degree-4 bfloat16 (BF16) programs in four GB200 integration tasks: dense SiLU, tanh-softcapped attention, sigmoid attention, and routed-expert Swish-gated linear unit (SwiGLU). The programs combine analytical symmetry, target-format rounding, and packed arithmetic inside consuming kernels. The isolated paths improve by 1.19--2.19x in L2 and 1.00--1.70x in HBM. The dense-SiLU, tanh-softcapped-attention, and routed-expert substitutions improve complete training-step throughput by 2.7\%, 2.9\%, and 8.0\%, respectively. The sigmoid-attention substitution improves complete-attention forward by 7.4\% and the complete GPU step by 0.3\%. Same-checkpoint open-weight ablations and one paired pre-training comparison per task extend the evaluation to model behavior. At common horizons near 100 billion tokens, the final smoothed training-loss differences (polynomial minus native) range from $-0.107$ to $+0.079$ across the four tasks.

</details>


### [146] [SW-KAN: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks with Stieltjes-Wigert q-Orthogonal Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00050)
*Amirhosein Azarpour,Seyyed Moein Kazemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SW-KAN，一种基于 Stieltjes-Wigert q-正交多项式的 Kolmogorov-Arnold 网络，通过平滑指数映射和数值稳定的三项递推，解决多项式 KAN 中无界输入与有界正交基之间的域不匹配问题，在图像分类与函数逼近任务上取得更优的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多项式的 KAN 变体虽降低了原始 B 样条实现的计算开销，却引入一个被忽视的根本问题：无界实值输入与正交多项式基的有界（或半无限）支撑之间存在域不匹配。作者旨在解决这一域不匹配，同时保持计算效率、数值稳定性和可解释性。

Method: 提出 Stieltjes-Wigert KAN（SW-KAN），在边（edge）上采用定义于半无限域 (0, ∞) 的 Stieltjes-Wigert q-正交多项式作为可学习一元函数。核心技术包括：1）引入平滑的 exponential-of-tanh 映射，稳定弥合域差距并保持梯度良态；2）利用数值稳定的三项递推将多项式展开以 O(N) 计算，无需特殊函数调用；3）利用 Stieltjes-Wigert 多项式的对数正态权重结构与可学习 q 参数作为归纳偏置。

Result: 在图像分类和连续函数逼近的全面实验中，SW-KAN 在多个任务上实现更优的精度-效率权衡，优于既有多项式 KAN 基线；在特征维度降低和训练数据受限等资源受限条件下仍保持稳健性能，并能以极少的参数逼近复杂多元函数。

Conclusion: SW-KAN 通过 Stieltjes-Wigert q-正交多项式、平滑域映射与 O(N) 递推求值，有效解决了多项式 KAN 的域不匹配与计算效率问题，展现出对复杂多元函数的强表征能力和在资源受限场景下的鲁棒性，是高效函数逼近与分类的一个有吸引力的替代方案。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) represent a paradigmatic shift in deep learning by replacing fixed node activations with learnable univariate functions on edges, offering enhanced interpretability and parameter efficiency. While recent polynomial-based KAN variants have addressed the computational overhead of original B-spline implementations, they introduce a fundamental yet underexplored challenge: the domain mismatch between unbounded real-valued inputs and the bounded or semi-infinite support of orthogonal polynomial bases. To address this limitation, we propose the Stieltjes-Wigert Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (SW-KAN), a novel architecture that employs Stieltjes-Wigert q-orthogonal polynomials defined on the semi-infinite domain (0, infinity). We introduce a smooth exponential-of-tanh mapping that stably bridges the domain gap while preserving well-conditioned gradients, and leverage a numerically stable three-term recurrence that evaluates polynomial expansions in O(N) operations without special-function calls. Through comprehensive experiments spanning image classification and continuous function approximation, we demonstrate that SW-KAN achieves superior accuracy-efficiency trade-offs across diverse tasks. The log-normal weight structure and learnable q-parameter of Stieltjes-Wigert polynomials provide a distinct inductive bias that enables robust performance under resource-constrained conditions, including reduced feature dimensionality and limited training data. The proposed architecture not only outperforms established polynomial KAN baselines on standard benchmarks but also exhibits strong representational capacity for approximating complex multivariate functions with remarkably few parameters, making it a compelling alternative for efficient function approximation and classification in resource-constrained settings.

</details>


### [147] [Format-Aware Fusion for Fast FP4 Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00053)
*Robert Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"格式感知融合"技术，通过协同设计量化生产者、规模域与消费者布局，使FP4 Tensor Core在Llama-3 8B预训练中达到37.9K tokens/s/GPU，并在保持甚至超越bfloat16训练损失的同时实现高模型FLOP利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FP4 Tensor Core虽能加速矩阵乘法，但规模计算、操作数打包、布局构建及保存的反向状态等开销可能抵消其性能收益，亟需系统性的协同设计来释放FP4的真实加速潜力。

Method: 提出"格式感知融合"（format-aware fusion），将每个量化生产者与其规模域（原生mxfp、全局nvfp、协作线程组局部nvfp）和消费者布局协同设计；采用行梯度随机舍入与固定符号32值Hadamard权重梯度预处理；在Llama-3系列8B模型上通过1600亿token预训练进行评测，使用bfloat16输出投影和编译的交叉熵。

Result: 在相同加速器对比中，bfloat16达18.8K、Transformer Engine nvfp达27.6K tokens/s/GPU，而最快自定义路线达37.9K。mxfp配合行梯度随机舍入与Hadamard权重梯度预处理达37.2K tokens/s/GPU（bfloat16模型FLOP利用率的86.3%），训练损失终点高于原始bfloat16 2.11%。含四个末尾bfloat16块的Transformer Engine方案在27.1K tokens/s/GPU下比bfloat16高0.87%。下游排名与训练损失排名不一致，表明FP4结果共同取决于规模契约、操作数与执行路径。

Conclusion: FP4加速效果的实现依赖规模契约、操作数格式与执行路径的协同优化；格式感知融合能在显著提升吞吐的同时保持或改善训练质量，是释放FP4 Tensor Core潜力的有效途径。

Abstract: Four-bit floating-point (FP4) Tensor Cores accelerate matrix multiplication, but scale computation, operand packing, layout construction, and saved backward state can erase the gain. We present \emph{format-aware fusion}, which co-designs each quantization producer with its scale domain and consumer layout for native \mxfp{}, global \nvfp{}, and cooperative-thread-array-local \nvfp{}. We evaluate Llama-3-family 8B pretraining through 160 billion tokens using bfloat16 output projections and compiled cross entropy. In matched same-accelerator probes, bfloat16 and Transformer Engine \nvfp{} reach 18.8K and 27.6K tokens/s/GPU, while our fastest custom route reaches 37.9K. \mxfp{} with row-gradient stochastic rounding and fixed-sign 32-value Hadamard weight-gradient preconditioning reaches 37.2K tokens/s/GPU (86.3\% bfloat16 model FLOP utilization) and ends 2.11\% above the raw bfloat16 training-loss endpoint. A Transformer Engine recipe with four final bfloat16 blocks ends 0.87\% above bfloat16 at 27.1K tokens/s/GPU. Downstream rankings differ from training-loss rankings, showing that FP4 outcomes depend jointly on scale contract, operand, and execution path.

</details>


### [148] ["very likely" Means "uncertain"? How LLMs Diverge from Humans in Linguistic Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00083)
*Jinhao Duan,Zicheng Liu,Zijie Liu,Kaidi Xu,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 人类用语言标记（如"可能""很可能"）表达不确定性，但LLM的不确定性量化大多依赖似然或一致性信号。本文发现LLM编码的言语不确定性的数值水平与人类存在显著差异，并提出一种基于优化的算法METHODNAME，直接从LLM输出学习最优的不确定性词表映射，从而在标记层面比较人类与LLM的置信语义，揭示系统性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从认知角度看，准确的言语不确定性反映元认知监控，即"知道自己不知道"，以支持调节和信息寻求。然而现有LLM不确定性量化依赖代价高昂的似然或一致性信号，既未考虑人类如何用言语标记表达不确定性，也无法直接解释LLM输出的言语标记究竟传达了多大概率质量。作者希望揭示LLM在言语不确定性量化上与人类的差异，并验证言语标记能否可靠量化LLM的不确定性。

Method: 作者从心理学与决策科学文献中整理出人类不确定性标记语料库，并以此为基准评估LLM。观察到LLM将言语不确定性与数值水平的对应关系与人类明显不同后，提出一种新颖的基于优化的算法METHODNAME：直接从LLM输出中学习最优的不确定性画像，通过拟合"标记—不确定性"映射来最好地解释经验正确率，从而发现每个言语标记应传递的概率质量，而非通过重复采样来估计不确定性。

Result: 实验表明，LLM编码言语不确定性所用的数值水平与人类存在实质性差异。METHODNAME能够在标记层面直接比较人类与LLM的置信语义，成功分离这种不匹配，并揭示两者在言语表达上的系统性置信度偏差。

Conclusion: LLM在言语不确定性量化上与人类存在系统性分歧，单纯依赖似然或一致性信号不足以刻画其言语标记的置信语义。METHODNAME提供了一种直接、标记级别的比较手段，可发现每个标记应传达的概率质量，为可靠量化LLM不确定性并理解其与人类认知的差异提供了新途径。

Abstract: Humans express uncertainty verbally via markers (e.g., "possible," "likely"), yet most LLM uncertainty quantification (UQ) relies on costing likelihood- or consistency-based signals. From a cognitive perspective, accurate verbal uncertainty reflects metacognitive monitoring, representing knowledge boundaries ("knowing that you don't know") to support regulation and information seeking. In this paper, we investigate how LLMs diverge from humans in verbal uncertainty quantification and whether verbal markers can reliably quantify LLM uncertainty. We curate a corpus of human uncertainty markers from psychology and decision-science literature and benchmark LLMs against it. We observe that LLMs encode verbal uncertainty with numerical levels that differ substantially from those of humans. We then introduce METHODNAME, a novel optimization-based algorithm that learns an optimal uncertainty profile over uncertainty markers directly from LLM outputs. By fitting a marker-uncertainty mapping to best explain empirical correctness, METHODNAME discovers how much probability mass each verbal marker should convey, rather than estimating uncertainty via repeated sampling. METHODNAME enables a direct, marker-level comparison of confidence semantics between humans and LLMs, disentangling mismatch and revealing systematic confidence disparities in verbal expressions.

</details>


### [149] [Nous: Learning and Certifying Memory Decisions Before Source Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00094)
*Pranav Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将"学习决策"与"校准信息源"进行分离，证明在信念型智能体记忆中，学习并认证一个贝叶斯决策所需记录数可比源校准少一个数量级（平方级差距），并给出可用的有限样本认证证书及实验结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信念型智能体记忆需要基于当前状态做出可靠决策，但其证据往往含噪声、被复制或过时。传统观点认为必须先校准信息源可靠性才能改进决策，作者质疑这一必要性，希望分离学习、校准与修订认证，探究在不恢复源可靠性的前提下能否学习并证明有用决策。

Method: 作者在一个四模型隐藏马尔可夫族上分离三类任务：学习未知贝叶斯决策、认证其相对信息性基准的改进、以及固定精度源估计。进而推广到更广模型类与"未知身份加背景"报告通道，刻画策略改进的清晰可识别区间，并利用可观测见证区域构造有限样本证书，无需纯类别锚点。鲁棒性扩展容许有界历史依赖错误设定与条件复制，借助分裂训练见证适用于任意历史空间。最后将策略边界收据与 Nous Dimensions 集成，在三个外部 MiniGrid 记忆环境中引入噪声报告接口进行测试。

Result: 在四模型隐藏马尔可夫族中，学习未知贝叶斯决策需 Θ(l^-2) 条记录，认证其改进需 O(l^-2) 条同观测律的新记录，而固定精度源估计需 Θ(l^-4)（l 为持续性，趋于零），即学习与认证有用决策所需记录可比源校准少一个平方级因子。更广模型类保持该决策速率与源下界。实验在 45,000 条留出可变状态历史与 9,000 个回合上，新证书相对常数基准接受 9/9 改进、相对后写胜出接受 4/9 改进，而早期证书在 MiniGrid 中全部拒绝；强既有推理基线保持有竞争力或更优。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个统计层面的解释：记忆决策可以在不恢复源可靠性的前提下被学习与证明，且所需样本量显著少于源校准；但作者明确指出其并非一个普适更优的记忆算法。

Abstract: Belief-based agent memory needs reliable decisions about current state, yet its evidence may be noisy, copied, or stale. Must a memory calibrate its sources before it can improve its decisions? We separate learning, calibration, and revision certification. On one four-model hidden Markov family, learning an unknown Bayes decision requires Theta(l^-2) records and certifying its improvement over an informative incumbent takes O(l^-2) fresh records from the same observation law, while fixed-precision source estimation requires Theta(l^-4) as persistence l vanishes. Thus learning and certifying useful decisions can require quadratically fewer records than source calibration. A broader model class retains the decision rate and source lower bound. Under an unknown identity-plus-background report channel, we characterize the sharp identified interval for policy improvement and derive a finite-sample certificate using observable witness regions, without pure-class anchors. A robustness extension tolerates bounded history-dependent misspecification and conditional copying; split-trained witnesses apply to arbitrary history spaces with explicit power conditions. We integrate policy-bound receipts with Nous Dimensions and test 45,000 held-out mutable-state histories and 9,000 episodes in three external MiniGrid memory environments with an introduced noisy-report interface. The new certificate accepts 9/9 improvements over a constant incumbent and 4/9 over last-write-wins, versus none for the earlier certificate in MiniGrid. Strong established inference baselines remain competitive or better. The result is a statistical account of when memory decisions can be learned and justified without recovering source reliability, not a universally superior memory algorithm.

</details>


### [150] [One Mastery Threshold Does Not Fit All Knowledge Tracing Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00095)
*Xianghui Meng,Yujing Zhang,Jionghao Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 同一数值的掌握阈值在不同知识追踪模型下会产生截然不同的进阶决策。本文比较6种知识追踪模型与12个阈值，发现贝叶斯知识追踪（BKT）对阈值变化不敏感，而神经模型更敏感，最佳平衡阈值因模型与教学设置而异，且更严阈值未必缩小差距、反而可能限制弱势学生的进阶机会。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教学系统使用掌握阈值来决定学生何时停止练习并进阶，但相同的数值阈值在底层知识追踪模型改变时会产生不同决策，导致公平性与教学效果的隐患。作者希望系统评估不同模型与阈值组合对进阶决策的影响，并为阈值校准提供依据。

Method: 在四个公开教育数据集上考察六种知识追踪模型，评估从0.50到0.99共12个阈值，从进阶后表现、进阶覆盖率、练习负担、以及不同先前表现群体间的差异等方面进行分析，并在30种预定义教学设置下识别能平衡表现、额外练习与进阶机会的最佳阈值。

Result: BKT对阈值变化相对不敏感，而神经模型随阈值升高变得更具选择性；这一差异部分源于模型输出含义不同（BKT估计潜在掌握概率，神经模型估计下一次作答正确的概率）。最佳平衡阈值在不同模型与设置间差异显著，半数设置中神经模型与BKT的阈值相差超过0.10；更严格的阈值未能可靠缩小表现差距，反而可能不成比例地限制进阶，强先验学生进阶次数最高可达弱先验学生的3.26倍。

Conclusion: 当知识追踪模型或教学优先级改变时，掌握阈值应重新校准，并应依据其对表现、练习、进阶与机会公平的影响进行评估，而非沿用固定数值阈值。

Abstract: Tutoring systems use mastery thresholds to decide when students can stop practicing and advance, but the same numerical threshold can lead to very different decisions when the underlying knowledge tracing (KT) model changes. We examine six KT models across four public educational datasets and evaluate 12 thresholds from 0.50 to 0.99 using post-advancement performance, advancement coverage, practice burden, and disparities across prior-performance groups. We also identify thresholds that balance performance, extra practice, and advancement under 30 predefined instructional settings. Bayesian Knowledge Tracing (BKT) is relatively insensitive to threshold changes, while neural models become much more selective as thresholds increase. This partly reflects different model outputs: BKT estimates latent mastery probability, whereas neural models estimate the probability of a correct next response, so the same cutoff does not represent the same level of mastery. The best-balanced threshold varied substantially across models and settings. In half of the tested settings, neural models and BKT differed by more than 0.10 in their selected thresholds, although this gap became smaller when greater priority was placed on reducing extra practice and allowing more students to advance. Stricter thresholds also did not reliably reduce performance gaps and could disproportionately restrict advancement, with stronger-prior students advancing up to 3.26 times as often as weaker-prior students. These results show that mastery thresholds should be recalibrated when the KT model or instructional priorities change and evaluated by their effects on performance, practice, advancement, and access.

</details>


### [151] [Uncertainty-Aware Learning from Multi-Expert Interval Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00102)
*Samira Alkaee Taleghan,Younghyun Koo,Andrew P. Barrett,Farnoush Banaei-Kashani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种保留多位专家区间标签的方法，将标签不确定性分解为标签内不精确性与专家间差异，并通过分解匹配使预测不确定性各分量与标签侧来源对应，在海冰浓度任务上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多机器学习应用依赖专家标注，而合格专家对同一观测可能给出不同但都合理的解读，这种专家间差异反映真实分歧或模糊性而非标注错误。当专家还报告区间而非精确值时，监督信号同时包含两种标签不确定性：标签内不精确性与专家间差异。现有方法将二者割裂处理：多专家方法把标签压缩为共识，区间目标方法只输出单一预测，预测不确定性方法很少对照真实专家分歧验证其不确定性估计。

Method: 首先将异构标签词汇表统一到公共概率标签空间，分离编码差异与专家判断；其次保留个体标签区间，用混合Beta分布建模，并用Cramér距离目标函数进行训练；第三，将预测不确定性分解为分量内、分量间和模型不确定性三个部分，并评估它们是否分别对应标签内不确定性、标签间不确定性和模型误差；由于这一对应关系并不自动成立，提出分解匹配（decomposition matching）来对齐预测分量与其预期的标签侧来源。

Result: 在海冰浓度任务上，该方法相比硬标签将MAE降低31%，并优于聚合、区间分布和区间回归等基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过保留个体专家区间、分离标签内不精确性与专家间差异，并验证对应的预测不确定性分量，该方法有效利用了专家标注中的两类不确定性，在预测精度和不确定性校准上均取得显著提升。

Abstract: Many machine learning (ML) applications rely on expert labels, and qualified experts may provide different but plausible interpretations of the same observation. Such variation across expert labels may reflect genuine disagreement or ambiguity rather than annotation error. When individual experts additionally report intervals rather than exact values, the supervision contains two distinct sources of label uncertainty: within-label imprecision and between-expert variation. Existing methods treat these forms separately: multi-expert approaches collapse labels to a consensus, interval-target methods often yield a single prediction, and predictive-uncertainty methods rarely validate their uncertainty estimates against observed expert disagreement. To address this problem, we propose an approach that preserves individual expert intervals, separates within-label imprecision from between-expert variation, and validates the corresponding predictive uncertainty components. First, heterogeneous label vocabularies are harmonized into a common probabilistic label space, separating encoding differences from expert judgement. Second, individual label intervals are retained and modeled with a mixture of Beta distributions trained using a proper Cramér-distance objective, preserving distinct expert-reported labels. Third, we decompose predictive uncertainty into within-component, between-component, and model uncertainty, and evaluate whether these components correspond to within-label uncertainty, between-label uncertainty, and model error, respectively. Because this correspondence is not guaranteed, we introduce decomposition matching, which aligns the predictive components to their intended label-side sources. On sea-ice concentration the model reduces MAE by 31\% over hard labels and outperforms aggregation, interval-distribution, and interval-regression baselines.

</details>


### [152] [The Hidden Costs of 99% Accuracy: A Trustworthiness Audit of the Telco Customer Churn Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00118)
*Soumyadeep Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对IBM Telco客户流失基准数据集进行可信度审计，揭示四个被精度/F1主导报告所掩盖的问题：SMOTE数据泄漏、冗余特征误导SHAP、温度缩放校准失效，以及F1最优阈值远非成本最优，并提出了四要素报告清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该基准数据集上客户流失预测的测试准确率普遍超过95%，最高被引研究达99.01%，但现有以准确率和F1为核心的报告范式掩盖了多项可信度缺陷（数据泄漏、特征冗余、校准失真、阈值选择不当），导致研究结论不可靠且无法支撑成本敏感的决策。

Method: 在n=7,043的IBM Telco数据集上开展四项审计：(1)跨10个分类器和15个种子，对比SMOTE在划分前/划分后的效果，并直接测量合成训练点中来自测试集最近邻插值的比例；(2)检验TotalCharges≈tenure×MonthlyCharges的冗余性（R²=0.999），结合TreeSHAP归因排序，提出R²>0.95的预建模冗余诊断；(3)进行15种子校准审计，比较等渗回归、温度缩放等方法；(4)在50美元挽留成本和24个月CLV代理下计算成本最优决策阈值，与F1最优阈值对比。并在Iranian Telecom和Bank Customer数据集上做域内/跨域复制验证。

Result: 1) 划分前SMOTE使流失类F1虚高13.1个百分点（每个分类器Wilcoxon p<10^-4），但类加权同序无虚高，定位为SMOTE几何构造所致，约36%的合成训练点是测试集实例的最近邻插值；2) 移除TotalCharges仅使准确率变化<0.2个百分点，但TreeSHAP将其排在第9位平均绝对归因，严重误导SHAP解释；3) 等渗回归是最稳健的默认校准方法，温度缩放在预测概率呈双峰的类加权树集成上失效；4) 成本最优阈值约为F1最优阈值的1/5到1/10，每千客户节省约77,000美元。F1在域内和跨域均泛化；F2仅在电信域内泛化。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示该基准数据集的高准确率掩盖了严重的数据泄漏、特征冗余与校准缺陷，并据此归纳出四要素报告清单（管道披露、冗余诊断、校准审计、成本敏感阈值），同时发布可复现实现，以提升客户流失预测研究的可信度。

Abstract: Customer churn prediction on the IBM Telco Customer Churn benchmark (n = 7,043) routinely reports test accuracies above 95%, with the most cited published study reporting 99.01%. We audit this benchmark for four trustworthiness failures invisible to the accuracy- and F1-centred reporting that dominates the literature. First, pre-split SMOTE inflates churn-class F1 by 13.1 percentage points across ten classifiers and fifteen seeds (Wilcoxon p < 10^-4 per classifier); the same leaky pipeline ordering paired with class weighting yields no inflation, isolating the effect to SMOTE's geometric construction. We measure the mechanism directly: approximately 36% of synthetic training points are nearest-neighbour interpolations of test-set instances. Second, the TotalCharges field is approximately determined by tenure multiplied by MonthlyCharges (R2 = 0.999); removing it changes accuracy by less than 0.2 percentage points, yet TreeSHAP ranks it ninth in mean absolute attribution - a pattern that materially corrupts SHAP-based interpretation. We propose an R2 > 0.95 pre-modelling diagnostic. Third, in a 15-seed calibration audit, isotonic regression is the strongest default; temperature scaling fails on class-weighted tree ensembles whose predicted-probability distribution is bimodal. Fourth, the cost-optimal decision threshold (under a 50 USD retention offer and 24-month CLV proxy) is approximately 5-10 times lower than the F1-optimal threshold, saving approximately 77,000 USD per 1,000 customers. We replicate F1 and F2 on Iranian Telecom Churn (within domain) and Bank Customer Churn (across domain): F1 generalises; F2 generalises only within telecom. We synthesise these findings into a four-component reporting checklist - pipeline disclosure, redundancy diagnostic, calibration audit, and cost-sensitive thresholds - and release a reproducible implementation.

</details>


### [153] [Generalized Biomedicine Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00120)
*Luyao Tang,Yingkai Yang,Hanqi Chen,Jiewei Zheng,Chaoqi Chen,Cheng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出广义生物医学发现（GBD）这一新范式，并构建覆盖长尾、异常与分类法感知发现的统一基准，同时提出认知启发的SCAN方法，通过预测抑制、惊奇诱发显著性与互补整合来提升医学影像中的新颖概念发现能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实临床中医学影像面临开放世界偏移：长尾罕见病、被正常解剖主导的细微病灶、以及层级分类法。而现有开放世界范式大多假设平坦且类别均衡的标签空间，无法满足生物医学的实际需求。核心洞察是：占主导地位的已知模式构成视觉流形，会掩盖细微的新颖性。

Method: 受专家诊断启发，提出SCAN（Surprise-evoked Complementary AccommodatioN）方法，遵循认知启发的感知流程：①预测性抑制（predictive suppression），过滤预期中的正常模式；②惊奇诱发显著性（surprise-evoked salience），突出意外偏差；③互补整合（complementary accommodation），将这些偏移融入全局表示。同时构建统一基准，覆盖长尾、异常与分类法感知三类发现任务。

Result: 大量实验表明，SCAN在提升新颖概念发现能力的同时，基本保留了已建立的临床知识，并且可插入现有架构，更好地权衡医学影像中的已知-未知（known-unknown）平衡。

Conclusion: SCAN通过认知启发的三阶段流程有效应对生物医学领域的开放世界挑战，在发现新颖概念与保留既有知识之间取得良好平衡，并具备良好的架构可移植性。

Abstract: In real-world clinical practice, medical images face open-world shifts: (i) long-tailed rare diseases, (ii) subtle lesions dominated by normal anatomy, and (iii) hierarchical taxonomies. Yet most open-world paradigms assume flat, balanced label spaces, leaving these biomedical demands unresolved. We introduce Generalized Biomedicine Discovery (GBD) and a unified benchmark spanning long-tail, anomaly, and taxonomy-aware discovery. Our key insight is that dominant known patterns form a visual manifold that masks subtle novelty. Inspired by expert diagnosis, we propose SCAN (Surprise-evoked Complementary AccommodatioN), which follows a cognition-inspired perceptual progression: it applies predictive suppression to filter expected norms, triggers surprise-evoked salience to highlight unexpected deviations, and performs complementary accommodation to integrate these shifts into global representations. Extensive experiments show that SCAN improves novel concept discovery while generally preserving established clinical knowledge, and it plugs into existing architectures to better navigate the known-unknown trade-off in medical imaging. Code is available at https://github.com/lytang63/generalized-biomedicine-discovery.

</details>


### [154] [Classification Based on Association Rules Algorithm for Breast Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00174)
*Ali Alsalama,Ahmed Kubba,Ghaith Jamjoum,Zaher Al Aghbari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于加权关联规则分类的新型乳腺癌分类数据挖掘方法，包含规则生成、规则剪枝和规则预测三个核心算法，并在多个测试样本上验证了可行性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺癌是全球女性死亡率的重要致因，发病率在各癌种中位居前列，早期检测需求迫切。传统方法存在可解释性不足的问题，而关联规则挖掘能产出医学专业人员易于理解的结果，因此研究者日益倾向于基于关联规则的分类方法。

Method: 提出一种基于加权分类策略的关联规则数据挖掘技术，实现包含三个核心算法：规则生成（识别频繁项集并生成关联规则）、规则剪枝（依据特定标准剔除规则，并按对训练数据的影响分为主、次两组）、规则预测（运用剪枝后的规则对测试数据进行分类）。

Result: 该预测算法在多个测试样本上进行了测试，结果表明该方法的可行性和性能得到验证。

Conclusion: 基于加权关联规则的分类方法能够有效用于乳腺癌分类，兼具良好的分类性能与可解释性，为早期检测提供了一种可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Breast cancer is a significant contributor to female mortality across the world, displaying one of the highest oc currence rates among the various cancer types. In response to the need for early breast cancer detection, researchers have increasingly turned to association rule-based classification as a favored method. Association Rule mining is a data mining approach which offers the benefit of yielding results that are readily understandable for medical professionals. This paper introduces a novel association rule-based data mining technique for breast cancer classification based on a weighted classification approach. This implementation employs three core algorithms: Rule Generation, Rule Pruning, and Rule Prediction. Rule Generation identifies frequent itemsets and creates association rules. Rule Pruning eliminates rules using specific criteria and separates them into major and minor groups based on their influence on training data. Rule Prediction applies the pruned rules to classify test data. The final prediction algorithm was tested on several testing samples to show the feasibility and performance of the approach.

</details>


### [155] [Four Ways to Grow a Classifier and Why One of Them Cannot Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00180)
*Cagri Temel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过统一的实验协议系统考察了四种构造式（constructive）增长决策在树结构与构造式模型中各自"买到"了什么，发现其中一种（将软决策树每个叶子都加深为门）在数学上买不到任何东西，并给出了精确诊断与一行修复方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造式分类器在训练中不断添加结构（加深树、增加隐藏单元、分裂叶子等），但这些增长决策的实际收益缺乏统一的定量诊断。作者试图回答四种常见增长决策各自究竟带来了什么收益，并特别审查其中一种看似自然但可能完全无效的做法。

Method: 在树结构与构造式模型上采用统一的固定协议进行测量。针对"深度化软决策树"这一做法：将每个叶子变为一个门（gate），两个子节点继承父节点的类别分布，从而保持函数不变。作者证明了这种构造使每个新门的梯度恒等于零；当门取1/2时，两个子节点获得完全相同的梯度，导致新增层永远无法学习。修复方案是对子节点施加很小的随机扰动（其大小几乎不影响效果），实用规则为：添加参数后断言其梯度非零。其余三种决策分别测量：向残差拟合新隐藏单元后再安装、按最大期望误差分裂叶子、以及要求节点在接收更具表达力的分裂前达到统计显著性。

Result: 被诊断的深度化做法在三个种子、五折交叉验证下，相比同深度从零训练，在Iris上损失19.6个准确率点、Wine上损失19.1、Digits上损失55.6；小随机扰动即可修复。向残差拟合新单元再安装，在每份数据集上获得更小的网络但未带来更高准确率，且在Digits上显著损失准确率。按最大期望误差分裂叶子获得稀疏性（以3.7次分裂达到0.885，而完整深度六树需63个分裂），但在更难题上损失4.3个点。要求统计显著性后再提升节点表达力买不到任何东西：树反而更大且更不准确。所有数字均由测量脚本直接插入。

Conclusion: 四种增长决策中，只有"将叶子全部加深为门"这一做法在数学上完全无效——不是梯度弱而是梯度根本不存在（与Net2Net用噪声打破的对称性、或陡峭下降用二阶信息逃离的鞍点不同）。修复只需一个小的随机扰动。其余三种决策各带来一项收益（更小网络、稀疏性、或宣称的统计保证），而后两者在部分情况下有代价，统计显著性要求则毫无收益。

Abstract: Constructive classifiers add structure while they train: a level to a tree, a unit to a hidden layer, a split at a leaf. This paper asks what each of four such growth decisions actually buys, measured under one fixed protocol in tree-structured and constructive models, and gives an exact diagnosis and a fix for the one that buys nothing.
  The diagnosis concerns the most natural way to deepen a soft decision tree: turn every leaf into a gate whose two children inherit the parent's class distribution, so that the function is unchanged. I prove that this leaves the gradient of every new gate identically zero and, with the gate at 1/2, gives the two children identical gradients, so the added level can never learn. Unlike the symmetry that Net2Net breaks with noise or the saddle point that splitting steepest descent escapes with second-order information, first-order information here is not weak but absent. Over three seeds of five-fold cross-validation the construction loses 19.6 accuracy points on Iris, 19.1 on Wine and 55.6 on Digits against the same depth trained from scratch. The fix is a small random perturbation of the children, whose size barely matters. The practical rule is one line in a test: after adding parameters, assert that their gradient is nonzero.
  The other three decisions each buy one thing. Fitting a new hidden unit to the residual error before installing it buys a smaller network on every dataset, though not a more accurate one, and on Digits it costs accuracy significantly. Splitting the leaf with the largest expected error buys sparsity, reaching 0.885 with 3.7 splits where a complete depth-six tree uses 63, but loses 4.3 points on a harder problem. Requiring statistical significance before a node receives a more expressive split buys nothing: the tree gets larger and less accurate.
  Every number in the paper is inserted from the measurement script.

</details>


### [156] [Uncertainty-Aware RL-Controlled Adaptive 3D Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00188)
*Alpay Ozkan,Tunc Ozan Aydin,Marc Pollefeys,Jelena Trisovic,Daniel Barath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于语义熵、几何曲率与纹理丰富度的自适应体素细分框架，并引入强化学习智能体在用户指定的内存预算下学习体素细分策略，以取代手工调参，从而在三维重建中实现更高的几何精度、语义一致性与更优的内存-精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定分辨率的体素网格效率低下：均匀区域浪费内存，复杂区域丢失细节。现有自适应方法（如 MAP-ADAPT）依赖几何启发式和用户定义语义类别列表，需要专家调参、缺乏对未知物体的泛化能力，且无法显式控制内存占用。

Method: 提出一种自适应框架，利用语义熵（量化标签不确定性）结合几何曲率和纹理丰富度作为场景复杂度信号，进行有原则的分辨率分配，摆脱对语义分类体系（taxonomy）的依赖；进一步引入强化学习智能体，在用户指定的目标内存预算下学习体素细分策略，将手工调参阈值替换为单一直观的控制参数，从而显式地控制精度-内存权衡，生成多分辨率 TSDF（截断符号距离场）。

Result: 在合成与真实世界数据集上，相较于 MAP-ADAPT 和固定分辨率基线，所提出的多分辨率 TSDF 实现了更高的几何精度、更好的语义一致性以及更优的内存-精度权衡效果。

Conclusion: 该自适应框架通过结合语义熵等场景复杂度线索与强化学习驱动的内存预算控制，有效解决了固定分辨率和现有自适应方法在效率、泛化与可控性上的不足，并提供开源代码与模型（https://github.com/alpayozkan/UnRL）。

Abstract: Voxel-based volumetric mapping is fundamental to 3D reconstruction, yet fixed-resolution grids remain inherently inefficient - wasting memory in uniform regions and losing detail in complex ones. Existing adaptive methods, such as MAP-ADAPT, partially address this by varying resolution based on geometry and user-defined semantic class lists, but these heuristics require expert tuning, lack generalization to unseen objects, and provide no explicit mechanism to control memory usage. We propose an adaptive framework that refines voxels based on semantic entropy, which captures label uncertainty, together with geometric curvature and texture richness as scene complexity cues, yielding principled resolution allocation without reliance on semantic taxonomies. To make the accuracy-memory trade-off explicit and user-controlled, we further introduce a reinforcement learning agent that learns voxel subdivision policies under a user-specified target memory budget, replacing hand-tuned thresholds with a single intuitive control parameter. The resulting multi-resolution TSDF achieves higher geometric accuracy, better semantic consistency, and improved memory-accuracy trade-offs compared to MAP-ADAPT and fixed-resolution baselines on both synthetic and real-world datasets. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/alpayozkan/UnRL.

</details>


### [157] [Energy Time-Series Imputation with Differentially Private Diffusion Models via Clipping-Aware Objective Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00209)
*Huizhen Huang,Yu Li,Tao Huang,Chen Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向隐私敏感电力时间序列插补的差分隐私扩散模型方法，通过时步感知的目标条件化（v-预测、静态损失加权与扩散调度感知动态加权）缓解低信噪比时步下的大梯度裁剪问题，在五个真实能源数据集上稳定提升插补效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源时间序列细粒度测量包含敏感时间信息，需在差分隐私约束下可靠恢复缺失值。扩散模型配 DP-SGD 是可行框架，但余弦扩散调度下晚时步处于低 SNR 条件，标准 ε-预测会诱发大的预裁剪梯度，更易被定阈裁剪，从而损失优化信号。

Method: 将目标函数与裁剪的交互建模为定阈 DP-SGD 下的优化问题，提出时步感知的目标条件化：采用 v-预测抑制晚时步梯度放大，使用静态损失加权作为均匀缩放控制，并引入扩散调度感知的动态加权在裁剪前施加更强衰减。

Result: 在五个真实能源时间序列数据集上，针对随机点缺失、连续块缺失、持续中断及多种缺失严重度进行评估；在匹配的 DP-SGD 设定下，方法一致优于 ε-预测基线，梯度诊断显示上尾预裁剪梯度范数更低、裁剪比例更小、低 SNR 晚时步衰减更强。

Conclusion: 裁剪感知的目标条件化能有效缓解低 SNR 时步的梯度放大与裁剪损失，从而在差分隐私约束下稳定提升能源时间序列插补效用，验证了该思路对隐私敏感能源监测分析的实际价值。

Abstract: Reliable recovery of missing measurements is important for monitoring and analysis in energy time-series systems, where fine-grained measurements may contain sensitive temporal information. Diffusion models trained with differentially private stochastic gradient descent (DP-SGD) provide a promising framework for privacy-sensitive energy time-series imputation. Under cosine diffusion schedules, late timesteps correspond to low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) conditions, where standard $\varepsilon$-prediction can induce large pre-clipping gradients. Such gradients are more likely to be clipped, reducing the retained optimization signal. The artificial intelligence (AI) contribution lies in formulating this objective--clipping interaction as an objective optimization problem under fixed-threshold DP-SGD and developing timestep-aware objective conditioning for diffusion-based energy time-series imputation. The method adopts $v$-prediction to mitigate late-timestep gradient amplification, uses static loss weighting as a uniform-scaling control, and introduces diffusion-schedule-aware dynamic weighting for stronger attenuation before clipping. For the engineering application, we evaluate the method on five real-world energy time-series datasets across random point missingness, contiguous block missingness, persistent outages, and multiple missing-data severities. Under matched DP-SGD settings, the proposed method consistently improves imputation utility over the $\varepsilon$-prediction baseline. Gradient diagnostics reveal lower upper-tail pre-clipping gradient norms, reduced clipping fractions, and stronger attenuation at late low-SNR timesteps, supporting the effectiveness of clipping-aware objective conditioning for energy time-series imputation.

</details>


### [158] [Useful to Whom? Sample Value Is Defined Only Relative to the Learner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00221)
*Yangze Liu,Xiao-Long Yin,Zhongyi Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究核心集选择中"易样本优先"与"几何覆盖"两种准则的胜负分界是否取决于目标学习器，通过冻结子集的对照实验证明该分界会随网络宽度、输入网格、步长以及架构类型（CNN vs. ViT）而移动，并指出选择策略的预算区间需针对目标学习器评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究中易优先与覆盖优先各自的优势预算区间由某个交叉边界分隔，但这一边界究竟由数据固有属性决定，还是会随训练所用的学习器改变，尚不明确。作者希望厘清"哪些数据对训练有用"这一问题的答案是否具有学习者依赖性。

Method: 采用冻结子集干预法：先固定选定样本子集不变，仅改变训练学习器，观察交叉边界的移动。在低分辨率 ImageNet-100 上，通过加倍 ResNet-18 宽度、放大输入网格（信息量不变）、调整下采样步长等控制变量实验；并在原生 224px ImageNet-1k 协议下，用 LFrac 与 EL2N 两种探针比较 CNN 与 ViT 的学习表现。

Result: 在低分辨率 ImageNet-100 上，ResNet-18 宽度加倍使每类样本的交叉边界从 57 移至 85；放大网格使边界左移，且该左移随宽度增加而减弱；仅移除最后一层下采样步长即可复现左移。原生 224px 下，宽度效应较小且依赖探针：LFrac 几乎平坦，EL2N 微弱右移。将卷积系统换成 ViT 后，覆盖准则在全测量范围内胜出，即使易样本子集来自卷积代理。

Conclusion: 通过冻结子集干预确立了核心集选择准则的优势边界具有学习者依赖性，并识别出宽度、网格、步长与架构等可移动边界的网络结构因素，但未得出普适的缩放定律。实践启示是：选择策略的优选预算区间必须结合目标学习器进行评估。

Abstract: What kind of data does a model need in order to learn? Coreset selection makes this question concrete: under a budget, keep the samples most useful for training. Easy-first and geometric coverage criteria can win in different budget regimes, separated by a crossover boundary. We ask whether this boundary is fixed by the data or changes with the target learner. Controlled experiments freeze the selected subsets and manipulate only the training learner. On low-resolution ImageNet-100, doubling ResNet-18's width moves the crossover from 57 to 85 samples per class: the learner changes the relative value of the same samples. A wider sweep reveals an interaction between input grid and capacity. Enlarging the grid while retaining the same image information shifts the boundary left, and this shift weakens as width increases. Stride controls reproduce and reverse the grid effect without changing the input grid; removing only the last downsampling stride is sufficient to recover the leftward shift. Under the native-224px ImageNet-1k protocol, width effects are smaller and depend on the probe: LFrac remains nearly flat, while EL2N shifts modestly right. Swapping the convolutional learning system for a ViT makes coverage win throughout the measured range, even when the easy subsets come from the convolutional proxy. These results establish learner dependence through frozen-subset interventions and identify network structure that can move the boundary. They do not yield a universal scaling law. Their practical implication is direct: a selection strategy's preferred budget regime must be evaluated with respect to the target learner.

</details>


### [159] [Constant-Memory Recall: Learned Associations in a Fixed Matrix State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00232)
*Samuel Larson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个带有固定token特定键偏置的小型DeltaNet变体，用于记忆每序列32个新键值对。仅用32 KiB的循环矩阵状态即可在三个训练种子上达到99.95%的平均准确率；即使在查询前填充至1,798个token的无关内容，召回仍近乎完美。而参数匹配的向量模型和Transformer基线始终接近随机水平，导致无法进行有效的记忆效率比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定大小的循环记忆在推理时限制了存储增长，但其成功的召回能力高度依赖于具体任务和训练方式。作者希望探究在固定循环状态容量下，模型能否可靠地记住并召回序列中的键值对，同时检验何种架构与训练设置能实现有效记忆。

Method: 作者选取一个带有固定token特定键偏置的小型DeltaNet变体，训练其每序列记住32个新键值对；循环矩阵状态仅32 KiB。评估时在序列的值中进行选择，考察召回准确率。同时引入填充token将查询前的上下文延长至1,798个token以测试抗干扰能力，并通过清零第一个记忆块、48对探索性测试（仅用主训练预算的四分之一）以及参数匹配的向量模型和Transformer基线（含额外训练搜索）进行对照。

Result: 在32 KiB循环状态下，模型于三个训练种子取得99.95%的平均准确率；当填充内容将查询前上下文拉长到1,798个token且不增加配对时，召回仍近乎完美。清零第一个记忆块会破坏召回。48对探索性测试在用四分之一训练预算后仍接近随机，未能定位到容量上限。参数匹配的向量模型和Transformer基线始终接近随机，即便Transformer经过额外训练搜索也未能改善，这一基线失败使记忆效率对比无法完成。

Conclusion: 该DeltaNet变体能够在极小且固定的循环状态下近乎完美地完成键值对记忆与召回，并对长距离无关上下文具有鲁棒性；但由于参数匹配的向量与Transformer基线始终无法学会该任务，作者无法将所提方法的记忆效率与这些基线进行有意义的比较，这一悬而未决的问题仍需进一步解决。

Abstract: Fixed-size recurrent memory limits storage growth during inference, but successful recall depends on the task and training. We study a small DeltaNet variant with fixed token-specific key biases, trained to remember 32 new key-value pairings per sequence. With 32 KiB of recurrent matrix state, it achieves 99.95% mean accuracy across three training seeds when choosing among the sequence's values. Recall remains near perfect when filler extends the pre-query context to 1,798 tokens without adding pairings. Zeroing the first memory block removes this recall. An exploratory 48-pair test remains near chance after one quarter of the primary training budget and does not locate a capacity limit. Parameter-matched vector and Transformer baselines remain near chance, including the Transformer after additional training searches. This unresolved baseline failure prevents a memory-efficiency comparison.

</details>


### [160] [How Many Categories Are Enough? Distribution-Free Certification Limits for Few-Shot Anomaly Thresholds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00236)
*Gia Huy Thai,Nguyen Thai Anh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究少样本异常检测在部署时所需的、具有受控误报率(FAR)的报警阈值是否可由有限的正常样本证据来认证。通过在MVTec与VisA数据集上的实验与理论推导，作者表明目标类别自身的留一法校准分辨率有限且对分布偏移脆弱，并推得一个类别数量可行性下界（95%无分布UCB至少需14/29/59个类别），进而提出CRESS协议，发现仅用3-4个认证类别时所有配置都返回失效闭合阈值τ*=0，揭示了源证据支持可迁移可靠性声明的能力边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本异常检测器通常用排序指标（如AUROC）评判，但实际部署需要一个误报率受控的报警阈值。作者质疑：为一个未见过的类别认证这样一个阈值，究竟需要多少张正常图像、或多少个类别的正常证据？现有依赖目标类别LOIO校准的做法在图像数量少时分辨率受限且对分布偏移敏感，而跨类别迁移的证据是否足以支撑可靠性声明也缺乏定量刻画。

Method: 1) 使用冻结的DINOv2+PCA残差排序器，在15个MVTec与12个VisA类别、四种腐蚀类型上，检验目标类别留一图像跳出(LOIO)校准的行为；2) 推导类别计数可行性calculus：在零类别损失、不计多重性的前提下，证明任何确定性、一致有效、无分布的95%置信上界(UCB)所需的最少iid类别抽取数；3) 提出CRESS协议，将源类别划分为参考、提案、认证三个互斥角色，并对960个冻结配置进行图像单元与类别单元两种分析，评估其能否选出正阈值及UCB大小。

Result: 目标类别LOIO校准：秩值最小为1/(k+1)，在k=4、名义α=0.20时，Gaussian腐蚀的MVTec上经验FAR达0.341，为名义水平的1.7倍，说明分辨率受限且偏移脆弱。类别计数下界：任何确定性、一致有效、无分布的95% UCB在α=0.20/0.10/0.05时至少需要14/29/59个iid类别，且这些计数必要而非充分。CRESS协议：仅3-4个认证类别时，全部960个冻结配置返回失效闭合阈值τ*=0，最小类别级UCB为0.950；图像单元分析中，36.7%至60.3%的目标单元能选出正阈值，但该界仅适用于选定的源混合分布，而非新类别抽取的边际风险。

Conclusion: 本文给出了一个定量的可行性边界：目标类别自身证据不足以在低图像量下认证可靠阈值，而跨类别证据在少量类别下只能给出失效闭合（τ*=0）的保守结论。作者贡献在于厘清了估计目标(estimand)——源混合条件的可靠性界不同于新类别的边际风险——并提出CRESS协议以明确源证据何时能、何时不能支持可迁移的可靠性声明。

Abstract: Few-shot anomaly detectors are judged by ranking metrics, yet deployment requires an alarm threshold with a controlled false-alarm rate (FAR). We ask how much normal evidence, in images or category units, is needed to certify such a threshold for an unseen category. Using a frozen DINOv2 principal component analysis (PCA) residual ranker on 15 MVTec and 12 VisA categories under four corruption types, we show that target-only leave-one-image-out (LOIO) calibration is resolution-limited and shift-fragile: rank values cannot fall below $1/(k+1)$, and at the attainable level $α=0.20$, empirical FAR reaches 0.341 on Gaussian-corrupted MVTec at $k=4$, 1.7 times the nominal level. A category-count feasibility calculus is then derived: even with all-zero category losses and no multiplicity charged, any deterministic, uniformly valid, distribution-free 95% upper confidence bound (UCB) requires at least 14, 29, and 59 independent and identically distributed (iid) category draws at $α=0.20$, $0.10$, and $0.05$; these counts are necessary but not sufficient. The Cross-category Reliability Estimation with Source Support (CRESS) protocol splits source categories into disjoint reference, proposal, and certification roles. With only three or four certification categories, all 960 frozen configurations return the fail-closed threshold $τ^\star=0$, and the smallest category-level UCB is 0.950. Image-unit analyses of the same archive select positive thresholds in 36.7% to 60.3% of target cells; these bounds hold for the selected source mixture, not for the marginal risk of a new-category draw. The contribution is a quantitative feasibility boundary and an estimand-aware protocol specifying when source evidence can, and cannot, support a transferable reliability claim.

</details>


### [161] [Partial AUC Maximization from Positive-unlabeled Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00284)
*Atsutoshi Kumagai,Tomoharu Iwata,Taishi Nishiyama,Hiroshi Takahashi,Kazuki Adachi,Yasuhiro Fujiwara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅利用正样本与无标签数据（PU learning）来最大化 ROC 曲线部分面积（pAUC）的方法，无需标签化的负样本，并在十个真实数据集上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: pAUC 是关注特定假阳性率范围内的二分类性能指标，在网络安全、医疗、广告等领域有重要应用。现有 pAUC 最大化方法通常需要标注的正、负样本，但实际上负样本常因隐私顾虑或标注成本高而难以获取。

Method: 在经验风险最小化框架下，作者证明 pAUC（含其依赖 FPR 的阈值）可仅用正类和边缘（marginal）密度表示，并从 PU 数据推导出经验估计量；随后通过最大化平滑化的经验 pAUC 估计量来训练分类器。

Result: 在十个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，所提方法能够有效利用 PU 数据最大化 pAUC，证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文成功提出并验证了一种无需负样本、仅依赖正样本和无标签数据的 pAUC 最大化方法，为难以获取负样本的实际应用场景提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: The partial area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (pAUC) is an important performance metric for binary classification that summarizes true positive rates within a specific range of false positive rates (FPRs). Classifiers that achieve high pAUC need to be obtained in many real-world applications such as cybersecurity, medical care, and advertising. Although many methods for maximizing the pAUC have been proposed, they typically require both labeled positive and negative data for training. However, in practice, labeled negative data are often difficult to collect due to privacy concerns or the need for high expertise to annotate them. In this paper, we propose a method for maximizing the pAUC from positive and unlabeled (PU) data without negative data. Within an empirical risk minimization framework, we show that the pAUC, including its FPR-dependent thresholds, can be represented using only the positive and marginal densities, and derive an empirical estimator from PU data. A classifier is then trained by maximizing the derived smoothed empirical pAUC estimator. We experimentally demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method with ten real-world datasets.

</details>


### [162] [Contingent Exposure Routing for Financial AI: Outage Risk and the Cost of Indivisible Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00239)
*Shivam Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究模型故障转移（failover）下的订单路由问题：当某金融端点失效时，备份路由会改变哪些机构共享决策误差，从而影响价格位移风险。作者通过局部市场冲击响应矩阵刻画中断依赖路由，并证明共享备份会导致集中度下限、平衡降险随存活端点增长而递减；对不可分割决策给出随机化路由的精确二阶矩与条件期望取整等算法，并在大规模合成实验与真实API回放中验证其降险效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型故障转移虽能恢复系统可用性，但会改变共享决策误差的金融机构组合，从而加剧集中风险与价格位移。现有研究缺乏对"中断依赖路由"下风险的量化与可审计的压力测试方法，因此需要一种能测量并降低故障转移集中度风险的框架。

Method: 通过局部市场冲击响应矩阵形式化中断依赖路由，研究期望平方价格位移；构建对称构造证明共享备份存在O(1)集中度下限，而平衡降险风险随存活端点数反比下降；对不可分割决策推导独立随机化路由的精确二阶矩与有效敞口粒度，分析其与分数分配的差距；提出条件期望取整（无耦合配额的有界智能体）与保持端点数目的交换过程（swap procedure），并对照对偶下界评估。实验覆盖60个合成组合网络、11,340次情景评估，以及1,024条真实API响应回放。

Result: 在独立误差的中心反馈设定下，交换过程对单端点移除降险6.57%、双端点移除降险10.53%；在API回放再平衡任务上，样本外降险为3.30%（成对bootstrap区间2.07–4.57%）。强对齐误差、劣质端点与不可分割性会限制分散化收益。

Conclusion: 本文贡献在于提供一个可审计的路由压力测试与实现分析框架，而非对真实市场崩盘概率的估计；共享备份的集中度下限、平衡降险的可扩展性以及不可分割性下的精确分析共同刻画了故障转移路由的风险结构。

Abstract: Model failover restores availability, but changes which financial institutions share decision errors. We formulate outage-contingent routing through a local market-impact response matrix and study expected squared price displacement. A symmetric construction shows that a shared backup can leave an order-one concentration floor as the number of primary endpoints grows, while balanced fallback risk decreases inversely with the surviving endpoint count. For indivisible decisions, we derive the exact second moment of independent randomized routing and an effective-exposure granularity that determines its gap from fractional allocation. Conditional-expectation rounding gives a finite-agent bound without coupled quotas; a separate swap procedure preserves endpoint counts and is assessed against dual lower bounds. Across 60 synthetic portfolio networks and 11,340 scenario evaluations, the latter reduces risk by 6.57% and 10.53% for single and double endpoint removals at the central feedback setting with independent errors. A replay of 1,024 recorded API responses on constructed rebalancing tasks gives a smaller held-out reduction of 3.30% (paired bootstrap interval 2.07--4.57%). Strongly aligned errors, inferior endpoints, and indivisibility limit diversification. The contribution is an auditable routing stress test and implementation analysis, not an estimate of real-market crash probabilities.

</details>


### [163] [M$^2$Weather: A Benchmark for Joint Multi-Station and Multi-Variable Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00370)
*Rongwen Li,Xiao Wang,Mingyang Wang,Hongwu Liu,Changjian Chen,Zhuo Tang,Kenli Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 M²Weather 基准，用于联合多站点、多变量天气预测，通过多尺度数据、统一评估协议和即插即用适配器，系统评估站点-变量关系建模的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 站点天气预报同时受跨站点空间依赖和多气象变量间强物理耦合的影响，但现有研究往往孤立地考虑这两类关系，且使用不同数据集与实验设置，难以系统评估其各自及联合的贡献。

Method: 构建 M²Weather 基准：经多准则质量控制和站点分层，收集 2809 个高质量站点及 5 个物理耦合气象变量，覆盖法国、欧洲、全球三个空间尺度；建立统一的训练与评估协议；设计轻量即插即用适配器，可在不重新训练的情况下向已训练模型引入缺失的站点或变量关系；并在 16 个代表性模型上系统评测。

Result: 系统评估显示联合建模站点与变量关系带来收益；补充缺失关系后，所有适配模型在三个数据集上 MSE 均进一步下降，证明跨站点与跨变量的互补信息是提升站点天气预报的重要资源。

Conclusion: 联合建模站点和变量关系及其互补信息对提升站点天气预报具有重要意义，M²Weather 基准和适配器为公平、高效地研究站点-变量关系提供了统一平台。

Abstract: Station weather forecasting is fundamentally shaped by both complex spatial dependencies across stations and strong physical coupling among weather variables. However, existing studies often consider these relationships separately and use different datasets and experimental settings, hindering systematic assessment of their individual and joint contributions. In this paper, we introduce $M^2$Weather, a benchmark for joint multi-station and multi-variable weather forecasting. Through multi-criteria quality control and station stratification, we collect 2,809 high-quality stations with 5 physically coupled weather variables across three spatial scales: France, Europe, and Global. This multi-scale design lets us examine whether conclusions persist from national to global station networks. We also introduce unified training and evaluation protocols to enable fair comparison of different station-variable modeling paradigms. To further examine the benefits of modeling station-variable relationships, we design a lightweight, plug-and-play adapter. With a trained weather forecasting model, this adapter can introduce missing station or variable relationships without retraining the model. This enables fair and efficient investigation of station-variable relationships. Systematic evaluation of 16 representative models shows the benefits of jointly modeling station and variable relationships. Completing missing relationships further reduces MSE for all adapted models on all three datasets. Together, these results identify the complementary information across stations and variables as an important resource for improving station weather forecasting. Our code can be obtained at https://github.com/hnu-vis/M2-Weather.

</details>


### [164] [The Null Is the Hard Part: Exact Tests for Memorization in Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00251)
*Sushovan Majhi,Pramita Bagchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对生成模型记忆（memorization）审计方法提出批判与改进：由于现有审计缺乏零分布（null distribution），仅靠阈值判读相似度分数会产生错误结论。作者为整体模型和单张图像分别构造了零分布，并据此对 MemBench 及数据复制（data-copying）测试进行重新评估，发现许多此前的“记忆”认定并不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆审计方法仅将相似度分数与固定阈值比较，没有零分布作为对照，导致假阳性泛滥、结论可能错误。作者认为记忆审计的关键难点在于建立正确的零假设（null），即如何区分“真正的复制”与偶然相似。

Method: 作者提供两类零分布：(1) 整体模型层面——训练图像与留出图像在给定模型样本下可交换，通过重标记（relabelling）构造置换检验，对任意统计量精确成立（当留出图像为随机划分时）；(2) 单张图像层面——用随机图像排名与匹配对照（matched controls）、独立生成重采样等方法校准，并结合小尺度交欧拉特征曲线（Intersection Euler Characteristic Profile, IECP）这类尺度受限统计量，同时采用错误发现率（FDR）控制与校准最大值。

Result: 在零分布校准下，MemBench 的缓解措施实际效果被夸大：随机提示扰动下三分之二的认证图像不再被检出，注意力缩放下六分之五不再被检出，嵌入优化下全部不再被检出。对 24 个生成器的数据复制测试，通过其自身零推断仅有 10 个被“标记”为复制，而邻域偏好统计量在 7 个模型上仍触发、限制在近重复尺度的计数统计量则在 0 个模型上触发（McNemar p=0.016）。对单张图像的方法，随机图像排名在 2365 个对照中产生 596 个假发现；独立生成重采样使零分布过窄三倍。经匹配对照校准后，61 张 MemBench 图像中有 36 张以 5% FDR 被认证，留出对照仅在 0.01% 的校准划分中被认证，每张图像两个生成即恢复该计数；校准最大值认证 46 张。作者推荐小尺度交欧拉特征曲线（IECP）质量作为尺度受限统计量，它还可统计被复制的不同图像数量并检验两个模型是否复制相同图像。

Conclusion: 记忆审计必须在适当的零分布下进行，否则阈值判读会产生误导性结论。作者提供的置换检验与对照校准方法能有效控制假发现，并揭示此前对生成模型记忆程度的估计明显偏高。他们推荐使用小尺度 IECP 作为尺度受限的稳健统计量来审计并比较记忆行为。

Abstract: Memorization audits of generative models read similarity scores against thresholds, with no null distribution, and the conclusions they support can be wrong. By MemBench's rule, the benchmark's mitigations roughly halve Stable Diffusion's memorization; audited with false-discovery control, two thirds of the certified images are no longer detected under random prompt perturbations, five sixths under attention rescaling, and all of them under embedding optimization. The field's data-copying test, read against its own null, flags ten of twenty-four generators that reproduce nothing. We argue that for memorization the null is the hard part, and supply two. For a whole model, training and held-out images are exchangeable given its samples, and relabelling them is a permutation test, exact for any statistic when the held-out images are a random split; under it, a nearest-neighbour preference still fires on seven of those twenty-four, and a count restricted to the near-duplicate scale on none (McNemar p=0.016). For single images, the natural nulls fail twice, measurably: ranking an image among random images yields 596 false discoveries among 2,365 controls, and resampling independent generations makes the null three times too narrow. Calibrated against matched controls, the audit certifies 36 of 61 MemBench images at 5% false-discovery rate, held-out controls are certified in 0.01% of calibration splits, and on this benchmark two generations per image recover that count. A calibrated maximum, which reads occasional rather than typical copying, certifies 46. As the scale-restricted statistic we recommend the small-scale mass of the Intersection Euler Characteristic Profile, which also counts distinct images copied and tests whether two models copy the same ones.

</details>


### [165] [STCFormer: Adaptive Spatio-Temporal Modeling with Dynamic Cluster Transformer for Station-based Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00377)
*Rongwen Li,Haixin Xie,Mingyang Wang,Hongwu Liu,Kun Fang,Changjian Chen,Zhuo Tang,Kenli Li*

Main category: cs.LG
