TL;DR: 本文提出 STCFormer，一种自适应时空 Transformer，在每个时间片内根据站点局部演化动态分组，并结合簇内局部注意力与跨簇全局注意力，在八个气象预测任务上取得最低 24 小时 MSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的站点天气预报需要建模站点间复杂的空间依赖，但现有聚类式选择性建模存在两大不足：观测窗口内共享的分组会掩盖站点关系的局部变化，且仅依赖簇内交互会丢失重要的全局上下文。同时，选择性交互相比稠密连接的理论优势尚未被充分理解。

Method: 提出 STCFormer，其核心是 Cluster-Guided Attention Block：在每个时间片内依据站点局部演化自适应分组；簇内使用细粒度局部注意力，跨簇则通过区域状态摘要进行全局注意力，使每个站点可获取簇外信息。作者还推导了簇条件局部注意力的 Lipschitz 上界不高于全连接情形，据此设计 InfoLoss 以增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在三个真实气象数据集、八个温度和风速预测任务上，STCFormer 在所有八个任务中取得最低的 24 小时均方误差，并在跨指标与预测时长的 48 项比较中有 47 项排名第一或第二。消融实验和案例研究验证了局部自适应分组与局部-全局互补交互的有效性。

Conclusion: STCFormer 通过动态局部自适应分组和互补的局部-全局注意力机制，有效捕捉站点间复杂空间依赖，兼具理论鲁棒性优势与实验性能提升，为气象预测中的选择性建模提供了新思路。

Abstract: Station-based weather forecasting supports daily life and economic activity, yet accurate forecasts require modeling complex spatial dependencies among stations. Recent clustering-based selective modeling offers a promising alternative to dense inter-station interactions. However, a grouping shared across an observation window may obscure local changes in station relationships, while intra-cluster interactions alone may miss important global context. The theoretical advantages of selective interactions over dense connectivity also remain insufficiently understood. We therefore propose STCFormer, an adaptive spatio-temporal Transformer that dynamically groups stations according to their local evolution within each temporal patch. Its Cluster-Guided Attention Block combines fine-grained local attention within clusters and global attention over regional state summaries, allowing each station to access information beyond its own cluster. We further show that a derived Lipschitz upper bound for cluster-conditioned local attention is no larger than its fully connected counterpart, explaining a potential robustness benefit and motivating the design of InfoLoss. Experiments on three real-world weather datasets spanning eight temperature and wind forecasting tasks show that STCFormer achieves the lowest 24-hour mean squared error on all eight tasks and ranks first or second in 47 of 48 comparisons across metrics and forecasting horizons. Ablations and case studies further confirm the benefits of locally adaptive grouping and complementary local-global interactions. Our code can be obtained at https://github.com/hnu-vis/STCFormer.

</details>


### [166] [Sharp Oracle-Regret Tradeoffs for Projection-Free Online Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00254)
*Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文刻画了当可行集仅能通过线性优化预言机（oracle）访问时，在线凸优化可达到的遗憾下界与匹配算法，给出了维度无关的极小极大遗憾精确阶为 Θ(GD·max{√T, T/(1+min{Q,BT})^{1/4}})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线凸优化通常假设可行集完全已知，但许多实际场景中学习者只能通过"精确线性优化预言机"访问可行集，且每轮必须保持可行性。这带来两个核心困难：一是可行集未知导致的信息受限，二是为保证可行性必须把动作限制在已获得信息所决定的凸包内。本文旨在在这一受限预言机模型下，给出遗憾的精确最优阶及相应最优算法。

Method: 作者采用"构造性下界 + 匹配上界算法"的两部分策略。下界侧：先证明普遍可行性迫使每一步动作落在"给定内切球与历史预言机回复"构成的凸包中；再利用一个固定体（fixed-body）构造，将每一阶段的新方向耦合到同一个共享单纯形上，使得虽然所有损失函数共享同一最小点，但每次获取有用回复都需付出重复代价。上界侧：提出一种带块的"计数近似梯度法"（counted approximate-gradient method），通过交错安排各块实现与下界匹配的遗憾速率。此外，针对规定光滑度 β，给出解析构造的曲率相关下界，并确定了一般刻画仍保持紧致的光滑度阈值。

Result: 对凸 G-Lipschitz 损失、直径至多 D、总预言机调用预算 Q、每轮严格上限 B 次调用的设定，得到维度无关的极小极大期望遗憾为 Θ(GD·max{√T, T/(1+min{Q,BT})^{1/4}})。该下界对任意随机化学习者也成立。两个特例：每轮一次调用时遗憾为 T^{3/4}；达到 √T 遗憾需要二次方的总预算。对光滑度 β，得到曲率相关下界及阈值：超过该阈值时一般刻画仍为最优。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了线性优化预言机受限下、且要求每轮可行的在线凸优化的遗憾复杂度，给出了匹配的上下界，并统一了"总预算"与"每轮严格上限"两类调用约束；关于光滑度的扩展进一步明确了广义结论的适用范围。

Abstract: We characterize the regret attainable in online convex optimization when access to the feasible set is limited to an exact linear optimization oracle. The learner is given an inscribed ball and a diameter bound and must remain feasible on every consistent instance. For convex $G$-Lipschitz losses, diameter at most $D$, a total allowance of $Q$ oracle calls, and a strict limit of $B$ calls per round, the dimension-free minimax expected regret is $Θ(GD\max\{\sqrt T,T/(1+\min\{Q,BT\})^{1/4}\})$. The lower bound applies to arbitrary randomized learners. Universal feasibility first forces each action into the hull of the supplied ball and the preceding oracle replies. A fixed-body construction then couples fresh phase directions to a shared simplex, making useful replies costly repeatedly even though all losses have a common minimizer. A counted approximate-gradient method with interleaved blocks attains the matching rate. Total-budget and strict per-round guarantees follow as special cases, including the $T^{3/4}$ rate with one call per round and the quadratic total budget needed for $\sqrt T$ regret. For prescribed smoothness $β$, an analytic construction yields a curvature-dependent lower bound and identifies the threshold above which the general characterization remains sharp.

</details>


### [167] [FAER: Auditable Utility-Aligned Trajectory Replay for Language Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00385)
*Miaobo Hu,Shuhao Hu,Xiaobo Guo,Xin Wang,Bokun Wang,Tianshu Fu,Daren Zha,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 FAER，一个可审计的全轨迹重放框架，用于弥合重放选择器在格式反馈、置信度、新鲜度或响应长度等信号与下游学习者效用之间的差距，并在 GSM8K 上验证了其序列选择优于均匀与格式反馈基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重放选择器通常依据格式反馈、置信度、新鲜度或响应长度等指标对缓存轨迹排序，但这些指标与缓存级正确性以及下游学习器的实际效用是不同的目标，存在“选择-学习差距”。作者希望形式化这一差距，并提供可审计、训练免费或学习器感知的轨迹选择机制，使选择结果与下游学习者效用对齐。

Method: 作者形式化“选择-学习差距”，提出 FAER 可审计全轨迹重放框架。其固定选择器作为协议基线（训练免费）；FAER-UTILITY 是在不相交的校准块上拟合的学习器感知选择器。方法中报告归一化梯度对齐作为基线，并引入一次性优化器感知的虚拟更新以提供幅度感知的效用曲面。审计合约在评估标签联结前冻结已观测字段与重放轨迹，确保选择过程可审计。

Result: 在 GSM8K 与 Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct 上的匹配学习器研究表明，128 次更新下固定选择器质量 0.6329，均匀为 0.5482，格式反馈为 0.6037。仅元数据交叉拟合校准在 8 个种子下达到 0.6476±0.0139（中位数 0.6481；配对 95% 置信区间 [+0.079,+0.122]），目标运行 63,276 tokens，记录总成本 189,642 tokens、3.48 GPU 小时（含校准）。完成的 FAER-UTILITY 行达到 0.6624，目标运行 62,844 tokens、4.26 GPU 小时。格式反馈选中的记录正确率为 0.6953，而固定选择器为 0.3594，尽管下游排序不同。完成的对比报告还呈现了学习器感知消融、同种子差距、策略优化行以及严格零样本迁移。

Conclusion: FAER 框架通过可审计的固定选择器与学习器感知的 FAER-UTILITY，有效弥合了高速缓存轨迹选择与下游学习者效用之间的差距。实验表明 FAER 方法在固定与学习器感知设置下均优于均匀与格式反馈基线，且学习者感知校准带来稳定提升；同时揭示了格式反馈所选记录的高缓存正确率与下游效用排序并不一致，凸显了显式对齐选择器与学习者效用的必要性。

Abstract: Replay selectors often rank cached trajectories by format feedback, confidence, freshness, or response length, although cache-level correctness and downstream learner utility are distinct objectives. We formalize this selection-to-learning gap and introduce FAER as an auditable full-trajectory replay framework. Its training-free fixed selector is a protocol baseline; FAER-UTILITY is the learner-aware selector fitted on disjoint calibration blocks. The normalized gradient alignment is reported as a baseline, while a disposable optimizer-aware virtual update supplies a magnitude-aware utility surface. The audit contract freezes observed fields and replay traces before evaluation labels are joined. On GSM8K with Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct, the matched learner study reports quality 0.6329 for the fixed selector, compared with 0.5482 for uniform and 0.6037 for format-feedback under 128 updates. Metadata-only cross-fitted calibration reaches $0.6476\!\pm\!0.0139$ over eight seeds (median 0.6481; paired 95% interval $[+0.079,+0.122]$) at 63,276 target-run tokens; its recorded full cost is 189,642 tokens and 3.48 GPU-hours including calibration. The completed FAER-UTILITY row reaches 0.6624 at 62,844 target-run tokens and 4.26 GPU-hours. Format-feedback selects records with correctness 0.6953, compared with 0.3594 for the fixed selector, despite the different downstream ranking. The completed comparison surfaces report the learner-aware ablation, same-seed gap, policy-optimization rows, and strict zero-shot transfer.

</details>


### [168] [Verification Pulses and the Cost of Escaping Wrong Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00256)
*Shivam Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在异步二元寄存器中，有限验证预算的时机与指向如何影响系统能否从错误共识的吸引域中恢复。作者推导了越过盆地边界所需的最小验证强度，给出了有限种群后续达成错误共识的精确生灭概率，并识别出临界预算窗口（首项 N log(x0/b)，修正项 √N 量级），同时区分了重复验证目标与验证停止后自主放大两部分的方差贡献。实证方面校准了 13,392 条语言模型响应，发现不同拟合响应机制下所有调整后的调度对比区间均包含零，且均值预测误差虽小却可能严重低估终端共识占用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部验证可以纠正个体输出，却可能仍使一个自我强化的种群停留在错误共识的吸引域内。作者希望理解在固定验证预算下，何时以及如何投放验证（时机与指向）才能有效触发从错误共识中的恢复，并揭示均值层面的预测误差为何会掩盖终端共识的严重低估。

Method: 对一般非线性响应，在峰值验证约束下推导跨越盆地边界所需的最小"燃料"；对有限种群采用精确的生灭过程计算脉冲验证后仍走向错误共识的概率；主渐近分析给出临界预算窗口（首项与 √N 阶修正，分解重复验证目标与验证停止后自主放大的方差）；并结合前瞻性实验记录 13,392 条语言模型响应（含校准与 108 条留出轨迹），进行调度对比的区间估计及分布审计。

Result: 在 16 个多数更新槽位、14 个初始错误的情景下，9 次随机检查即跨过平均场预算阈值，但精确模型中需要 23 次才能达到 95% 的最终恢复概率。校准产生了不同的拟合响应机制，但全部四个调整后的调度比较区间均含零；分布审计发现，温和的均值预测误差可能掩盖对终端共识占用率的严重低估。

Conclusion: 结果支持在明确规定的更新合约下进行风险校准的重置调度，同时将其与"指向不同目标的检查"和"无限制证据广播"显式区分开来，强调验证预算的时机与指向对自我强化系统中的错误共识恢复具有决定性作用。

Abstract: External verification can correct individual outputs while leaving a self-reinforcing population in the basin of a wrong consensus. We study how the timing and addressing of a fixed verification budget affect recovery in an asynchronous binary register. For a general nonlinear response, we derive the minimum fuel required to cross a basin boundary under a peak verification constraint. For a finite population, an exact birth--death calculation gives the probability of subsequent wrong consensus after a pulse. Our main asymptotic result identifies the critical budget window: a leading term $N\log(x_0/b)$ and a correction of order $\sqrt N$, with separate variance contributions from repeated verification targets and autonomous amplification after verification stops. The distinction is substantial: with 16 majority-updated slots and 14 initially wrong, 9 random checks cross the mean-field budget threshold, whereas 23 are required for 95% eventual recovery in the exact model. A prospectively specified experiment records 13,392 language-model responses, including calibration and 108 held-out trajectories. Calibration produces different fitted response regimes, but all four adjusted schedule-comparison intervals include zero. A distributional audit also finds that modest mean-prediction error can conceal a large underestimate of terminal consensus occupancy. The results support risk-calibrated reset scheduling under a specified update contract, while explicitly separating it from distinct-target checking and unrestricted evidence broadcast.

</details>


### [169] [MatrixReward: Reward from Rubric Matrix for Open-Ended Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00389)
*Zihan Shen,Qi Liu,Zixuan Yang,Yiqun Chen,Chenglong Zhao,Xiaozhao Wang,Lei He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MatrixReward 提出了一种基于"rollout×评分维度"胜率矩阵的奖励构建方法，通过矩阵列离散度与列间相关性动态赋予评分维度权重，再结合正负理想画像计算每个生成样本的相对贴近度奖励，在四个开放式问答基准上以 Qwen3-8B 取得 63.02 的平均分，超过最强基线约 2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式生成任务缺乏标准答案，需要有效的奖励机制。现有的逐点评分量表（pointwise scoring rubric）只能给出单一分数，无法反映同一提示下不同样本答案之间的相对优劣；将多个评分维度的判断简单合并为单一分数还可能掩盖样本间的差异。因此需要一种能从相对比较中构建更合理奖励的方法。

Method: 提出 MatrixReward。首先在每个评分维度（rubric）下对同一提示采样得到的所有响应进行两两比较，构建 rollout×rubric 的胜率矩阵。利用矩阵每一列的离散度衡量该维度对当前样本的区分能力，利用列间相关性识别重复冗余的维度，二者共同导出数据驱动的维度权重；再与该维度的先验权重结合。对矩阵做列归一化和加权后，取各维度观测到的最大值和最小值分别构成正理想画像与负理想画像，计算每个 rollout 到这两个理想画像的相对距离，得到"相对贴近度"质量奖励。

Result: 在四个开放式问答基准上使用 Qwen3-8B 进行评估，MatrixReward 取得平均 63.02 分，比最强基线高出约 2.0%。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，基于相对比较构建的矩阵能够为开放式生成强化学习构造更合理的奖励，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Open-ended query generation lacks standard answers, thus necessitating an effective reward mechanism. Pointwise scoring rubrics provide limited information about the relative quality of sample answers under the same prompt; merging multiple rubric judgments into a single score may also mask the differences between these answers. We propose MatrixReward, which constructs rewards from a rollout-by-rubric win-rate matrix obtained by comparing every pair of sampled responses under each rubric. The spread of each matrix column captures how strongly that rubric distinguishes the current rollouts, while correlations between columns reveal rubric repetition; together, these statistics yield data-dependent rubric weights. We combine these weights with the prior weights of rubrics. After column normalization and weighting, the observed per-rubric maxima and minima define positive and negative ideal profiles. Each rollout's distances to these two ideals determine its relative-closeness quality reward. Evaluated using Qwen3-8B on four open-ended query-answering benchmarks, MatrixReward achieves an average score of 63.02, outperforming the strongest baseline by approximately 2.0%. These results support the idea that matrices derived from relative comparisons can be used to construct rewards more reasonably for open-ended generative reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [170] [Attention Manifolds: Steering or Blocking Language Models by Editing Learned B-Spline Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00257)
*Naveen Mysore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"注意力流形"（attention manifolds）：在注意力机制中引入可学习的 2D B 样条曲面，使每个 value 维度依据 query-key 交互被调制，而非所有接收者共享同一 value 向量。该方法在 LLaMA 1B/3B 上以 0.3% 的参数开销将 WikiText-2 困惑度降低 2–2.5 点，显著改变模糊/多义输入的输出，并展示了几何化的模型编辑与安全导向的"注意力墙"能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 transformer 注意力中，源 token 向所有接收者发送相同的 value 向量，query 只决定"关注多少"而不决定"提取什么"。这限制了注意力对不同 query-key 交互下内容选择与信息调制的能力，尤其在歧义、多义词场景下表现不足。

Method: 引入注意力流形 $S_d(q_d, k_d)$：每个 value 维度 d 对应一个可学习的张量积三次 B 样条曲面（2D），根据该维度的 query、key 交互调制 value。曲面初始化为零以保留预训练行为。应用于 LLaMA 3.2-1B-Instruct 和 3B-Instruct，仅增加约 0.3% 参数。此外，通过翻转某层系数实现可编辑的几何操控；通过将系数设为 -1 构造"注意力墙"阻断特定维度的 value 流动。

Result: WikiText-2 验证困惑度下降 2–2.5 点。在 112 个多样化 prompt 上，曲面改变了 69%（1B）至 83%（3B）的贪心解码输出，歧义与多义输入下改变率达 94–100%。输出质量提升体现在：修正事实错误、提高精确度、增加具体性（引用了圣奥古斯丁并归因，且两个规模一致）。机械可编辑性：翻转某层系数改变 10 个 prompt 中 9 个的贪心输出（KL≈0.010）。安全实验：层宽"注意力墙"将爆炸装置 prompt 从具体操作说明重定向为一般性教育内容。

Conclusion: 注意力流形提供了一种低开销、可插拔且几何可解释的机制来调制注意力提取的内容，能提升语言模型质量、支持精确的模型编辑与安全导向的流形塑形，初步表明其在安全对齐方面的潜力。

Abstract: In standard transformer attention, a source token sends the same value vector to every receiver. The query determines \emph{how much} to attend but not \emph{what} to extract. This work introduces \textbf{attention manifolds}: learned 2D B-spline surfaces $S_d(q_d, k_d)$ that modulate each value dimension based on the query-key interaction. Each surface is a tensor-product cubic B-spline initialized to zero, preserving pretrained behavior. Applied to LLaMA 3.2-1B-Instruct and 3B-Instruct, attention manifolds reduce WikiText-2 validation perplexity by 2--2.5 points with 0.3\% parameter overhead. Across 112 diverse prompts, surfaces change greedy-decoded output for 69\% (1B) to 83\% (3B) of cases, with the strongest effects on ambiguous and polysemous inputs (94--100\% change rate). The surfaces improve output quality: correcting factual errors (\emph{``the CAP theorem has three main components''} $\to$ \emph{``it is impossible to guarantee all three''}), increasing precision (\emph{``impossible to know certain properties''} $\to$ \emph{``impossible to know both position and momentum''}), and adding specificity (a generic quote $\to$ an attributed Saint Augustine citation, consistently at both scales). The learned surfaces are also mechanically editable: inverting a layer's coefficients changes greedy output for 9/10 prompts (KL~0.010), providing a geometric mechanism for model steering. Setting surface coefficients to $-1$ creates ``attention walls'' that block value flow through specific dimensions. In a preliminary experiment, a layer-wide wall redirects an explosive-device prompt from specific instructions to general educational content, suggesting a path toward safety-oriented manifold shaping.

</details>


### [171] [RACE: Residual-Aware Test-Time Adaptation for Neighbor-Rich Time-Series Foundation Model Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00405)
*Hao-Nan Shi,Tong Wu,Chen-Cong Sun,Yuan Jiang,Han-Jia Ye,De-Chuan Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RACE框架，在测试时利用同领域历史邻居序列的证据来修正时间序列基础模型（TSFM）的预测误差，无需修改骨干模型，且通过轻量级门控机制决定何时应用修正，实现跨骨干模型的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型逐序列推理不适合"邻居丰富"的预测场景（如血糖监测、Web/云负载），因为这些场景的历史序列共享有用结构但邻居相关性不等。现有方法要么针对每个目标领域微调TSFM（成本高、跨骨干迁移性差），要么简单拼接检索到的序列而不验证其是否支持当前预测。核心挑战是邻居序列间存在相互矛盾的残差证据，且残差模式随TSFM和预测任务而变化。

Method: 将问题形式化为测试时邻居缩放（test-time neighborhood scaling），在不修改骨干模型的前提下利用同领域邻居证据。提出两阶段框架RACE：第一阶段检索与查询兼容的邻居，将其残差对齐到查询尺度，聚合一致性证据形成免训练的RACE-TF修正；第二阶段（完整RACE）使用轻量级、领域特定的门控（Gate）判断何时应用修正有益，并提供可跨TSFM骨干复用的训练工作流。

Result: 在四个TSFM上，RACE在两个主领域（CGM与Web/云负载）的所有三个领域聚合指标上均有提升，高误差查询上的增益最大。在任一领域内，基于一个TSFM训练的门控可无需适配直接迁移到其他骨干，构成的流水线在全部72个跨骨干指标比较上均优于匹配的冻结目标。

Conclusion: RACE验证了免训练的测试时残差修正结合轻量级门控能有效利用同领域邻居证据，在多个TSFM和领域上稳定提升预测性能，且门控组件具备出色的跨骨干迁移能力，避免了逐领域微调的成本。

Abstract: Time-series foundation models (TSFMs) perform strongly across forecasting tasks, but their per-series inference is ill-suited to neighbor-rich forecasting, where each query has access to related but nonidentical historical series. Continuous glucose monitoring (CGM) and Web/cloud workloads exemplify this setting: CGM trajectories share physiological patterns but vary across individuals, devices, and conditions, while Web/cloud workloads combine common operating regimes with non-stationarity, heavy tails, and bursts. These histories share useful structure, yet neighbors are not equally relevant. Existing methods either fine-tune TSFMs for each target domain, incurring additional costs and offering limited transferability across backbones, or append retrieved series without verifying whether they support the current forecast. The key challenges are conflicting residual evidence from neighboring series and residual patterns that vary across TSFMs and forecasting tasks. We formulate test-time neighborhood scaling: using same-domain neighbor evidence without modifying the backbone. We propose RACE (Residual-Aware Correction of Forecasting Errors), a two-stage framework for using historical neighbors. We first retrieve query-compatible neighbors, align their residuals to the query scale, and aggregate coherent evidence into the training-free RACE-TF correction. Full RACE then uses a lightweight, domain-specific Gate to determine when applying the correction is beneficial, with a reusable training workflow across TSFM backbones. Across four TSFMs, RACE improves all three domain-aggregate metrics on both primary domains, with the largest gains on high-error queries. Within each domain, a Gate trained on one TSFM transfers to other backbones without adaptation, and the resulting pipeline improves all 72 cross-backbone metric comparisons over the matched frozen targets.

</details>


### [172] [Large Language Bayes Is Not Reparameterisation-Invariant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00265)
*Jian Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出 Large Language Bayes (LLB) 通过对语言模型采样的候选概率程序做近似推断、并以指数化证据下界为权重求平均来回答问题，但该权重对程序的书写方式(参数化)不敏感。证据下界不具备对数边际似然的参数化不变性，导致同一测度在不同参数化下权重相差数倍、贝叶斯因子被颠倒、模型后验与下游推断产生误差；作者用希尔伯特距离界定该误差，并提出只验证自己生成的参数化（而非统计检测等价程序）来消除这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLB 的模型平均权重依赖证据下界，而证据下界对参数化敏感，这威胁到 LLB 方法的正确性：同一模型的不同写法会得到不同权重，进而扭曲贝叶斯模型比较和下游推断。作者旨在量化这一缺陷、刻画其对模型选择与下游目标的影响，并寻求规避途径。

Method: 作者从理论与实验两方面入手：(1) 指出对数边际似然具有参数化不变性而证据下界没有，并设计 centered 与非 centered 参数化(如 eight schools 及高维漏斗)的对照实验；(2) 量化权重差异，包括重要性加权佐证及用匹配后验矩的全协方差高斯重现 LLB 实际推断；(3) 对六种语言模型生成的 360 个程序统计 centered 比例；(4) 用下界亏缺(证据下界与对数边际似然之差)的散布 Δ 推导出已知的锐利希尔伯特距离上界来控制模型后验与下游目标的误差；(5) 对比"统计检测等价程序"与"验证自己生成的参数化"两种策略，指出前者在有限样本预算下会误合并不同模型，而后者可行并关闭漏洞。

Result: eight schools 上 centered 与非 centered 程序是同一测度(至 5.7×10⁻¹⁴)，但权重差 6.1×；重要性加权降至 2.2×；用匹配后验矩的全协方差高斯重现 LLB 实际推断后仍差 1.9×，在 64 维中达 8.9×。跨似然族、维度与漏斗严重度，差异最大达 31.9× 且会改变符号，故不存在统一更优的写法。该差异会颠倒对八个自然竞争模型的贝叶斯因子。误差受 Δ 经希尔伯特距离上界控制。六种语言模型生成的 360 个程序中 centered 比例在 0% 到 100% 之间且在同一模型内稳定。

Conclusion: LLB 的权重依赖参数化写法，证据下界不具备对数边际似然的参数化不变性，导致权重差异、贝叶斯因子反转及模型后验/下游误差，且无普遍最优的写法。但该误差可由下界亏缺散布 Δ 经希尔伯特距离精确上界控制；统计检测等价程序在有限样本下不可靠，而验证自身生成的参数化(而非第三方程序)可以规避 LLB 权重对参数化的敏感性，从而关闭该漏洞。

Abstract: Large Language Bayes (LLB) answers an informal modelling question by sampling candidate probabilistic programs from a language model, running approximate inference on each, and averaging them with weights proportional to an exponentiated evidence bound. We show that this weighting depends on how a model is written. The log marginal likelihood is invariant to reparameterisation; the evidence bound is not. On eight schools the centered and non-centered programs are the same measure to $5.7\times10^{-14}$, yet their weights differ by $6.1\times$; importance weighting reduces this only to $2.2\times$, and reproducing the inference LLB actually runs, a full-covariance Gaussian matched to the posterior moments, still leaves $1.9\times$ on eight schools and $8.9\times$ in $64$ dimensions. Across likelihood families, dimensions and funnel severities the discrepancy reaches $31.9\times$ and reverses sign, so no single writing is uniformly preferable. It inverts Bayes factors against eight natural competitors, and the induced error in the model posterior, and in any downstream target, is controlled by the spread $Δ$ of the bound shortfalls through a known sharp Hilbert-distance bound. Across $360$ programs from six language models the parameterisation written ranges from $0\%$ to $100\%$ centered and is stable within a model. Detecting equivalent programs statistically can falsely merge genuinely different models at practical sample budgets; verifying reparameterisations we generate ourselves cannot, and closes the window.

</details>


### [173] [VANDAM: Viewing a nucleotide sequence with DNA molecular priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00411)
*Jeremy Levy,Ariel Larey,Yury Nahshan,Raizy Kellerman,Elay Dahan,Amit Bleiweiss,Guy Leib,Omri Nayshool,Dan Ofer,Tal Zinger,Dan Dominissini,Gideon Rechavi,Marissa Wirth,Simon Lee,Dung Hoang,Noam D. Beckmann,Shane O'Connell,Nicole Bussola,Alexander W. Charney,Yoli Shavit,Nati Daniel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VANDAM 是一个将 DNA 分子先验引入基因组基础模型（GFM）训练的框架，通过预测区域分子属性并在有功能标签时注入局部特征，持续提升了多种架构和下游任务的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基因组基础模型采用"DNA 即字符串"的抽象，以掩码 token 预测为预训练目标，并未显式建模对生物学功能至关重要的生化、结构与物理特性。由于许多分子属性可通过现有生物物理模型从序列估算，其价值不在于作为独立模态，而在于提供可被训练目标显式利用的先验。

Method: 提出 VANDAM 框架，在自监督训练中从池化表示预测区域分子属性；当存在功能标签且可奖励保留分子先验时，在输入端额外注入局部特征，从而与基于 token 的目标形成互补。

Result: VANDAM 在四个架构家族和九个留出（held-out）基因组任务上一致提升了下游性能。探针实验进一步表明，分子先验的使用可泛化到其他未见过的分子属性。

Conclusion: 通过显式利用从序列估算得到的分子先验来扩展 GFM 训练，可以补充 token 级目标，增强模型的生物学表征能力，并使其泛化到未见分子属性。

Abstract: Contemporary Genomic Foundation Models (GFMs) rely on a DNA-as-a-string paradigm that employs masked token prediction objectives for pretraining. However, this abstraction does not explicitly model the biochemical, structural, and physical properties essential to biological function. Many molecular properties can be estimated from sequence using established biophysical models, so their utility lies not in providing an independent modality, but in introducing priors that training objectives can explicitly exploit. We introduce VANDAM, a framework that extends the training of GFMs with DNA molecular priors. In self-supervised training, VANDAM predicts regional molecular properties from pooled representations. When functional labels are available and can reward retaining molecular priors, local features are additionally injected at the input. VANDAM consistently improves downstream performance across four architecture families and nine held-out genomic tasks by complementing token-based objectives. Probing experiments further demonstrate that the use of molecular priors generalizes to other unseen molecular properties.

</details>


### [174] [MOVE: Multimodal Open-world Verification and Expansion for Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00268)
*Zekai Chen,Jiayang Xing,Xun Wu,Miao Zhang,Xunkai Li,Kairui Yang,Zhengyu Wu,Xu Wang,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MOVE，一个多模态开放世界类验证与扩展框架，通过联合视觉、文本和图上下文识别未知节点，利用多模态 LLM 生成候选类，并仅在候选类得到多模态证据一致支持时才扩展类空间，从而避免引入冗余类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态图学习面临部署后新类涌现而模型标签空间固定的根本挑战。现有方法只检测未知节点并用 LLM 生成候选类描述，却无法判断现有类是否足以覆盖这些节点，也无法判断生成的类是否足够可靠以扩展类空间，因而存在未充分利用多模态信息、LLM 描述不完整以及引入冗余类别三大问题。

Method: 提出 MOVE 框架：第一，联合考虑视觉 token、文本属性和图上下文来识别无法归入现有类的节点；第二，利用多模态 LLM 生成候选类别；第三，仅当候选类得到多模态证据的一致支持时才选择性地扩展类空间，避免引入多余类别。

Result: 实验表明，MOVE 在未知识别、开放域标注和下游图学习任务上取得平均 11.87% 的提升。

Conclusion: MOVE 有效解决了多模态开放世界场景下类验证与扩展问题，通过多模态证据一致性和选择性扩展机制，显著优于现有方法，验证了联合多模态信息与谨慎类别扩展的重要性。

Abstract: Multimodal graph learning faces a fundamental challenge: new classes may emerge after deployment, while models are trained with a fixed label space. Existing approaches typically detect unknown nodes and use LLMs to generate candidate class descriptions, but they do not determine whether existing classes are insufficient to cover these nodes or whether a generated class is reliable enough to expand the class space. Our empirical study reveals three challenges: multimodal information beyond individual modalities is required for unknown-node identification, LLM-generated class descriptions may not fully capture multimodal class characteristics, and directly adding candidate classes can introduce redundant categories. Based on these observations, we propose MOVE, a multimodal open-world class verification and expansion framework. MOVE identifies nodes that cannot be assigned to existing classes by jointly considering visual tokens, textual attributes, and graph context, leverages a multimodal LLM to generate candidate classes, and selectively expands the class space only when candidates are consistently supported by multimodal evidence without introducing unnecessary categories. Experiments demonstrate that MOVE achieves an average improvement of 11.87\% across unknown recognition, open-domain annotation, and downstream graph learning tasks.

</details>


### [175] [IrekoGPT: Turning Structured Pruning into Post-Hoc Slimmable LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00426)
*Pietro Moriello,Pietro Buzzega,Angelo Porrello,Simone Calderara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IrekoGPT 提出一种后训练(post-hoc)方法，将预训练 LLM 转换为宽度可在推理时动态调整的 slimmable 模型：保留 SliceGPT 的投影矩阵而不剪枝，使同一模型可暴露不同宽度的嵌套子网络，并通过多压缩比逐层校准和岭回归校正提升鲁棒性，在 Llama 与 Qwen 上优于朴素 PCA 瘦身方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型压缩方法（如 PCA 或 SliceGPT 等剪枝方案）通常为单一压缩比优化，推理时宽度固定，无法灵活适应不同计算资源；同时朴素 PCA 瘦身在高压缩比下性能下降明显。作者希望在不重新训练的前提下，将预训练 LLM 转换为一个宽度可调的 slimmable 模型，并提升高压缩比下的鲁棒性。

Method: 以 SliceGPT 为基础，但保留其投影矩阵而不进行剪枝，从而在同一模型中暴露不同宽度的嵌套子网络；通过在多个压缩比下逐层校准(layer-wise calibration)提升鲁棒性；对下游线性层采用无梯度的岭回归(ridge regression)进行校正。该方法属于后训练(post-hoc)转换，无需梯度训练。

Result: 在 Llama 和 Qwen 系列模型上的初步实验表明，IrekoGPT 优于基于朴素 PCA 的瘦身方法，且在高压缩比下提升最大。代码已开源。

Conclusion: IrekoGPT 能够将预训练 LLM 高效转换为宽度可调的 slimmable 模型，在保持后训练、无梯度、单一模型多宽度的同时，显著改善高压缩比下的表现，为灵活部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We introduce IrekoGPT, a post-hoc method for converting pretrained LLMs into slimmable models whose width can be adjusted at inference time. Building on SliceGPT, we retain its projection matrices without pruning them, allowing a single model to expose nested subnetworks at different widths. We improve robustness by calibrating each layer across multiple compression ratios, and correct downstream linear layers through gradient-free ridge regression. Across Llama and Qwen models, preliminary results show improvements over naive PCA-based slimming, with the largest gains at high compression. Code is available at https://github.com/aimagelab/IrekoGPT

</details>


### [176] [Coupling Perception and Reasoning in Federated Multimodal Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00277)
*Zekai Chen,Xun Wu,Hailin Zhang,Xunkai Li,Yu Liu,Kairui Yang,Muyan Huang,Xuaner Chen,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦多模态图基础模型（GFM）的适配问题，发现多模态编码器与图神经网络（GNN）的适配并非相互独立，二者存在显著的"配对敏感性"。为此提出 FedCORE 框架，用共享低维潜在状态统一编码器与 GNN 的更新，将配对差距从 30.6 降至 5.9，降幅达 80.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦 GFM 适配方法主要更新图侧模块而冻结多模态编码器，只调整了"信息如何传播"而固定了"信息提取什么"。作者通过实证发现编码器与 GNN 适配并不独立：编码器适配受图关系影响，且跨客户端模块交换暴露出分别参数化的编码器与 GNN 更新之间存在显著的配对敏感性。这一现象促使作者提出统一二者适配的方案。

Method: 提出 FedCORE 框架：通过一个共享的低维潜在状态来表示编码器与 GNN 的更新，从多模态信号和结构信号中联合优化该核心，并直接在共享状态空间中进行联邦演化，从而保持感知适配（编码器）与推理适配（GNN）之间的兼容性。

Result: 大量实验表明 FedCORE 将编码器—GNN 配对差距从 30.6 降至 5.9，相对独立联合适配实现了 80.7% 的降幅。

Conclusion: 联邦多模态图基础模型适配中编码器与 GNN 的更新并非独立，两者存在强配对关系；FedCORE 通过在共享低维潜在空间中联合演化二者更新，有效缓解了配对不兼容问题，显著缩小编码器—GNN 配对差距。

Abstract: Federated multimodal graph foundation models (GFMs) aim to adapt pretrained multimodal models to decentralized graph data, where each client owns a private multimodal graph and cannot share raw information. These models typically combine a multimodal Encoder that extracts semantic evidence from heterogeneous modalities and a graph neural network (GNN) that performs relational reasoning over graph structures. However, existing federated GFM adaptation methods mainly update graph-side modules while keeping the multimodal Encoder frozen, limiting adaptation to \emph{how information is propagated} while fixing \emph{what information is extracted}. Through empirical studies, we reveal that Encoder and GNN adaptations are not independent: Encoder adaptation is affected by graph relations, while cross-client module swapping reveals substantial pairing sensitivity between separately parameterized Encoder and GNN updates. Motivated by this observation, we propose \textbf{FedCORE}, a federated adaptation framework that represents Encoder and GNN updates through a shared low-dimensional latent state. FedCORE jointly optimizes this core from multimodal and structural signals and performs federated evolution directly in the shared state space, preserving compatibility between perception and reasoning adaptations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FedCORE reduces the Encoder--GNN pairing gap from $30.6$ to $5.9$, corresponding to an $80.7\%$ reduction over independent joint adaptation.

</details>


### [177] [Exact information accounting for SGD methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00446)
*Akshay Balsubramani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种对随机梯度下降(SGD)及其变体的精确信息论分析，将预条件SGD步骤视为高斯贝叶斯模型的后验均值更新，其单步遗憾可分解为内在时间代价与比较器信息变化之和。该分解可推广为关于目标函数本身的恒等式，并能统一解释收敛、鞍点逃逸、平坦性与泛化、学习率调度、自适应优化器及各种SGD变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准几何分析难以精确刻画SGD的动力学行为，尤其是无法统一解释收敛速度、平坦性—泛化联系、各类变体与优化器的效果差异。作者希望给出一种精确的、信息论视角的替代框架，用一个统一恒等式覆盖这些现象。

Method: 将预条件SGD步骤解释为高斯贝叶斯模型的后验均值更新，推导单步遗憾的精确分解（内在时间代价 + 比较器信息变化），并扩展为关于目标函数的恒等式。通过在合成与真实训练运行上测量恒等式各项，验证其解释力；对梯度自由SGD讨论曲率预条件应如何进入更新；并给出基于锐度的泛化证书及其在参数规模下的失效条件。

Result: 在真实网络上，该恒等式将经典收敛界的松弛归因于被其推导所丢弃的项，并能区分达到相同训练损失的优化器——这种区分遵循其步数与步的一致性；其与泛化优劣的关系在不同网络间存在差异。对梯度自由SGD，恒等式确定了曲率预条件应如何进入更新。基于锐度且带数据无关各向同性先验的泛化证书在网络规模下是空洞的，除非曲率谱在所有参数上几乎平坦。

Conclusion: 信息论恒等式为理解和统一SGD变体的行为提供了精确工具，揭示了经典收敛界的松弛来源并能够区分优化器；但该框架下基于锐度的泛化证书揭示了对平坦性—泛化联系的朴素解释在网络规模下不成立，除非曲率谱接近平坦。

Abstract: As an alternative to the standard geometric analyses, we give an exact, information-theoretic analysis of stochastic gradient descent (SGD) and its variants. We show that a preconditioned SGD step is the posterior-mean update of a Gaussian Bayes model, and that its one-step regret splits into an intrinsic-time cost and a change in comparator information. The split extends to an identity for the objective itself. Convex convergence, strict-saddle-point escape, the link between flatness and generalization, the standard learning-rate schedules, adaptive optimizers, and the noisy, momentum, heavy-tailed, and gradient-free variants of SGD each correspond to a term or a special case of this identity. We measure its terms on synthetic and real training runs. On real networks it attributes the slack of classical convergence bounds to the terms their derivations drop and separates optimizers that reach the same training loss. That separation follows the number and consistency of their steps. Its relation to which of them generalizes better differs between networks. For gradient-free SGD the identity determines how a curvature preconditioner should enter the update. The sharpness-based generalization certificate it yields, with a data-independent isotropic prior, is vacuous at network scale unless the curvature spectrum is nearly flat across all parameters.

</details>


### [178] [Stable and Counterfactually Robust Physical World Models from Imposed Structure and Learned Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00280)
*Yufeng Wang,Parivesh Priye,Lu Wei,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究世界模型需要硬编码多少通用物理结构、学习多少系统特定物理，从而同时满足四条性质；结果表明通过约9000参数的结构化模型即可在电磁腔、粒子网格、浅水三个系统中恢复本构函数、分离守恒/耗散世界并泛化到未见参数干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统从轨迹学习的世界模型并不内置物理定律，可能凭空制造能量、长时推演漂移或发散，并且在物理参数改变时仍沿用训练时观察到的规律。作者希望弄清：为同时满足（1）满足第二定律的耗散、（2）对物理参数干预的响应正确、（3）稳定至训练时长100倍、（4）抗扰动鲁棒，需要把多少通用物理结构硬编码进模型，又有多少系统特定物理可从数据习得。

Method: 引入的通用结构包括：动力学由学习到的能量经一个固定的可逆算子取梯度生成；能量被限制在有界（confining）函数类内；设置一个只能移除能量、绝不注入能量的单向端口；驱动通道已知；被干预参数经一个可分离映射进入系统。模型从数据中学习能量泛函、本构关系、耗散率及各耦合项。作者在电磁腔、particle-in-cell网格、浅水流体三个系统上验证，模型规模约9000参数。

Result: 模型以单位斜率恢复本构函数；用同一组权重即可将守恒世界与耗散世界区分开（相差四个数量级）；能将符号、幅值、速率及重力等干预外推到未见过的取值（同等容量但无该结构的模型表现仅相当或差于随机）；非线性本构律被恢复且曲率保持，对留出干预的预测比收敛的线性模型好2-17倍。

Conclusion: 少量通用物理结构（基于学习能量梯度的动力学、限定的能量类、单向耗散端口、已知驱动通道、参数的可分离介入）足以让世界模型同时具备第二定律相容耗散、正确的参数干预响应、超长时稳定与抗扰动鲁棒，并可准确恢复系统特定的本构关系与耦合；而缺乏这些结构的同容量模型则无法实现。

Abstract: A world model learns to forecast how a physical system evolves from recorded trajectories, yet the systems it imitates obey physical laws that are neither fully supplied nor reliably respected. The model may create energy, drift or diverge over long rollouts, and answer a changed law query using the law observed during training. We ask how much general physical structure must be hard coded into a world model, and how much system-specific physics can then be learned from data, for four properties to hold simultaneously: second law compatible dissipation, correct responses to interventions on physical parameters, stability out to one hundred times the training horizon, and robustness to disturbances. The imposed structure is general: dynamics are generated from the gradient of a learned energy through a fixed reversible operator, the energy is restricted to a confining class, a one way port can remove energy but never inject it, the drive channel is known, and the intervened parameter enters through a separable map. The model learns the energy functional, constitutive relations, dissipation rate, and couplings. Across an electromagnetic cavity, a particle in cell grid, and a shallow-water fluid, models with roughly nine thousand parameters recover constitutive functions with unit slope, separate conserving from dissipating worlds by four orders of magnitude using a single set of weights, and transfer changes in sign, magnitude, rate, and gravity to unseen values, where equal-capacity models without the same structure perform at chance or worse. A nonlinear constitutive law is recovered with its curvature preserved and predicts a held-out intervention $2$-$17\times$ better than a converged linear model.

</details>


### [179] [Grand Canonical Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00683)
*Andreas Burger,Malte Franke,Luka Mucko,Kjell Jorner,Alan Aspuru-Guzik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Grand Canonical Generators (GCG)，将 Boltzmann 生成器扩展到巨正则系综，包含两种设计（条件化可变尺寸生成模型，以及将巨正则分布分解为粒子数分布）与正则 Boltzmann 密度，并验证了其在 Lennard-Jones 流体和分子筛中甲烷吸附上的巨正则可观测量复现能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Boltzmann 生成器主要针对固定粒子数的正则系综，难以处理粒子数可变的系统（如吸附、相变等）。作者希望将生成式建模推广到巨正则系综，以建模可变化学势下粒子数可变的物理系统。

Method: 提出两种设计：第一种将可变尺寸生成模型以化学势为条件，联合采样粒子数与构型；第二种将巨正则分布分解为粒子数分布与对应正则 Boltzmann 密度的乘积，并将化学势的线性依赖解析编码，同时给出可计算的似然以支持自归一化重要性采样（SNIS）。因子化形式可复用任意现有的正则系综 Boltzmann 生成器。

Result: 在 Lennard-Jones 流体和分子筛中的甲烷吸附两个系统上，GCG 准确复现巨正则可观测量，并展示了对不同化学势的泛化能力，且可通过 SNIS 和巨正则蒙特卡洛进行修正。

Conclusion: GCG 成功将 Boltzmann 生成器推广到巨正则系综，其因子化设计具备解析的化学势依赖、可处理的似然以及与其他正则生成器的兼容性，为可变粒子数系统的生成式建模提供了有效框架。

Abstract: We introduce Grand Canonical Generators (GCG), a generative framework that extends Boltzmann generators to the grand canonical ensemble. We present two designs. The first conditions a variable-size generative model on the chemical potential, sampling particle number and configuration jointly. The second factorizes the grand canonical distribution into a particle-number distribution and the corresponding canonical Boltzmann density. This factorized formulation can use any existing Boltzmann generator for the canonical component, encodes the known linear chemical-potential dependence analytically, and yields a tractable likelihood that supports self-normalized importance sampling (SNIS). Empirically, GCG accurately reproduces grand canonical observables on a Lennard--Jones fluid and methane adsorption in a zeolite, demonstrating generalization across chemical potentials and correction via SNIS and grand canonical Monte Carlo.

</details>


### [180] [Bellman-Certified Rounding for Sparse Policy Deployment in MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00325)
*Zhaojun Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在有限MDP中，将连续策略（行混合）舍入为稀疏二元策略时能保留多少折扣回报，并通过可复用的包络与局部曲率积分给出舍入前后的保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续策略优化会把更新扩散到许多状态上，但实际部署只允许少数完整的状态级改动；策略依赖的访问分布使各行编辑相互耦合，且长时程使全局曲率界过于保守，因此需要更紧致、可计算的舍入保证。

Method: 从2d+2次Bellman求解中得到可复用的包络，提供舍入前的统一与候选特定保证；利用每次交换的秩二有理表示，支持沿实际轨迹的加权曲率积分；并证明线性维度依赖在预算扩展时不可避免，精确全局曲率阈值化是困难的。

Result: 候选特定界将舍入前认证覆盖率从48.2%提升到74.1%；在γ=0.95时，局部积分将耦合实例上的界-损失比中位数从402.3降至2.08。

Conclusion: 候选特定保证与局部曲率积分显著收紧舍入误差界，大幅提升认证覆盖率并降低界-损失比，验证了该方法在结构化与耦合问题上的有效性。

Abstract: Continuous policy optimization may spread an update across many states, even when deployment permits only a few complete state-level changes. We study how much discounted return can be retained when continuous row mixtures are rounded to sparse binary policies in finite MDPs. Policy-dependent visitation couples the row edits, while long horizons make global curvature bounds conservative. From $2d+2$ Bellman solves, we derive reusable envelopes that support uniform and candidate-specific guarantees before rounding. A rank-two rational representation of each exchange further permits weighted curvature integration along the realized trajectory. We prove that linear dimension dependence is unavoidable when the budget scales, and that exact global curvature thresholding is hard. Candidate-specific bounds raise pre-rounding certification coverage from $48.2\%$ to $74.1\%$ on the structured suite. At $γ=0.95$, local integration lowers the median bound-to-loss ratio from $402.3$ to $2.08$ on coupled instances.

</details>


### [181] [Beyond Diagonal State Space Models: Exact Non-Abelian Group Tracking, Solvability Barriers, and Geometric Physical Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00329)
*Zeyu Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出选择性状态空间模型（如 Mamba、S4D、LRU）受限于转移矩阵可交换性与低导出长度的仿射变换群，无法有效表示非可解单群（如 A_5）上的组合子。作者提出非交换状态空间模型 NC-SSM，将状态转移提升到紧致李群 SU(2)、SO(3) 与 SO(N)，并通过封闭形式的 Euler-Rodrigues 映射和有理 Cayley 变换实现严格的保范等距。配合 Hopf 纤维化 Bloch 投影读出、四元数并行前缀扫描和恒等门控 Lie SSM，NC-SSM 在 14 个实验基准上取得显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对角化 SSM 的转移矩阵满足可交换性，且可解仿射变换群的导出长度不超过 2，导致多层对角网络在模拟非交换组合子时需要指数级电路深度，在 A_5 等非可解单群上出现严重优化退化。因此需要一种能原生表示非交换群结构、同时保持稳定等距性质的序列模型。

Method: 提出 NC-SSM，将状态转移结构提升到紧致李群：基于 SU(2) 的 NC-SSM、对应的实正交 SO(3)-SSM，以及任意维的 Cayley-SSM（SO(N)）。利用封闭形式的 Euler-Rodrigues 映射和有理 Cayley 变换实现精确的保范等距（||U_t|| = 1）。配套技术包括：纯 Hopf 纤维化 Bloch 投影读出（S^3/{±1} ≅ S^2 ≅ SO(3)）以消除符号歧义；真正的四元数并行前缀扫描（T=2048 时 9.06 倍加速）；以及 Identity-Gated Lie SSM 以消除稀疏语法相位漂移。

Result: 在 14 个实验基准上：(1) NC-SSM 在 S_3、D_4、Q_8 及单群 A_5 上实现 100% 追踪，而 3 层深度对角基线崩溃至 6.60%（p = 8.81e-4）；(2) Cayley-SO(5)-SSM 突破 Klein 1884 年关于对称群 S_5 的上限（50.92% vs 对角 5.25%，p = 0.0015，相对 SO(3) 方差降低 7.8 倍）；(3) SO(3)-SSM 在 300 步内保持黎曼流形（漂移 < 3.12e-6，优势超过 580,000 倍），实现 0.04 度航位推算误差与主动切向去噪；(4) NC-SSM 在 Dyck-2 上达 74.36%，深度 AST 作用域追踪达 30.26%（p = 0.0081）；(5) 消融实验证实严格等距在数学上是无损长程联想记忆的必要条件。

Conclusion: 通过将状态转移提升到紧致李群并利用保范等距的封闭形式参数化，NC-SSM 突破了传统对角 SSM 面对非可解群时的表示能力瓶颈，在群追踪、黎曼流形保持、结构化语法任务上均显著优于基线。结果表明严格的保范等距对于无损长程联想记忆具有数学上的必要性，为序列建模提供了一条基于李群结构的新路径。

Abstract: Selective state space models (SSMs), such as Mamba, S4D, and LRU, are bounded by transition matrix commutativity (A_t A_t' = A_t' A_t) and solvable affine transformation groups (Aff_D of derived length <= 2). Consequently, stacked multi-layer diagonal networks face severe optimization degradation on non-solvable simple groups such as A_5 due to the exponential circuit emulation depth required to simulate non-abelian commutators. We propose Non-Commutative State Space Models (NC-SSM), their real-orthogonal counterpart SO(3)-SSM, and arbitrary-dimension Cayley-SSM, lifting state transitions to compact Lie groups SU(2), SO(3), and SO(N). Via closed-form Euler-Rodrigues maps and rational Cayley transforms, NC-SSM achieves exact norm-preserving isometry (||U_t|| = 1). We introduce pure Hopf-fibration Bloch projective readouts (S^3/{+-1} =~ S^2 =~ SO(3)) to eliminate sign ambiguity, true quaternion parallel prefix scans (9.06x speedup at T=2048), and Identity-Gated Lie SSMs to eliminate sparse syntax phase drift. Extensive benchmarks across 14 experimental regimes show: (1) NC-SSM achieves 100% tracking on S_3, D_4, Q_8 and simple group A_5, where a 3-layer deep diagonal baseline collapses to 6.60% (p = 8.81e-4); (2) Cayley-SO(5)-SSM breaks Klein's 1884 ceiling on symmetric group S_5 (50.92% vs diagonal 5.25%, p = 0.0015, delivering 7.8x variance reduction over SO(3)); (3) SO(3)-SSM preserves Riemannian manifolds across 300 steps (< 3.12e-6 drift, > 580,000x advantage), achieving 0.04 deg dead-reckoning error and active tangent denoising; (4) NC-SSM achieves 74.36% on Dyck-2 and 30.26% on deep AST scope tracking (p = 0.0081); and (5) ablation confirms strict isometry is mathematically necessary for lossless long-range associative memory.

</details>


### [182] [The Weakest Link: Distilling LLM Reasoning with Worst-Case Constrained Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00332)
*Matthieu Zimmer,Xiaotong Ji,Tu Nguyen,Haitham Bou-Ammar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将推理蒸馏建模为带最坏情况约束的强化学习问题，通过一种无需增广状态的约束MDP奖励变换，在保证任务奖励的同时强制每一步前缀都满足教师对数似然约束，从而在数学推理和代码生成任务上显著扩展了准确率-保真度帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理蒸馏方法存在根本矛盾：纯任务奖励优化（如GRPO）会导致"奖励黑客"（答案正确但中间逻辑错误），而基于KL散度等软惩罚的正则化会稀释任务性能，且平均化的约束允许学生在某一步严重违反逻辑而在其他步靠高教师一致性来"补偿"。作者认为推理链的有效性取决于最薄弱的环节，因此需要最坏情况（逐前缀）约束而非平均约束。

Method: 将推理蒸馏形式化为约束强化学习：在轨迹每个前缀的教师对数似然上施加最坏情况约束，最大化任务奖励。为避免对偶拉格朗日求解器的高昂成本以及Saute等状态增广方法对测试时教师的依赖，作者推导出一种无增广（unaugmented）约束MDP，其奖励变换保留硬约束语义，支持将策略梯度低方差分解为单步项与长期项，并在惩罚极限下几乎必然满足最坏情况约束。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成任务上的大量实验表明，该方法显著扩展了准确率-保真度帕累托前沿；在匹配纯RL高最终答案正确率（Final Answer Correctness）的同时大幅降低教师约束违反，最终所有评测设置中取得最高的严格推理成功率（Reasoning Success Rate）。

Conclusion: 通过将蒸馏重构为带逐前缀最坏情况约束的约束RL，并采用无增广、低方差的奖励变换方案，该方法有效克服了奖励黑客与平均化正则的缺陷，在保持任务性能的同时大幅提升推理链的逻辑保真度，实现了更优的准确率-保真度权衡。

Abstract: Distilling the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) into smaller students is a central challenge for efficient deployment. Current approaches face a fundamental tension: optimizing purely for verifiable task rewards (e.g., via GRPO) leads to reward hacking, where students arrive at correct final answers through flawed intermediate logic, while regularizing with soft divergence penalties against a teacher (e.g., KL-based distillation) dilutes task performance and, critically, allows the student to compensate for severe logical violations at one step with high teacher agreement at others. We argue that this averaging is fundamentally misaligned with the nature of reasoning: a chain-of-thought is only as valid as its weakest link. Motivated by this observation, we formulate reasoning distillation as a constrained reinforcement learning problem in which the task reward is maximized subject to a worst-case constraint on the teacher log-likelihood along every prefix of the trajectory. To avoid the prohibitive cost of dual Lagrangian solvers and the test-time teacher dependence of state-augmented methods such as Saute, we derive an unaugmented constrained MDP whose reward transformation preserves the hard-constraint semantics, admits a low-variance policy gradient decomposition into single-step and long-term terms, and provably satisfies the worst-case constraint almost surely in the penalty limit. Through extensive experiments on mathematical reasoning and code generation tasks, we demonstrate that our method significantly expands the accuracy-fidelity Pareto front. By matching the high Final Answer Correctness of pure RL and drastically reducing teacher constraint violations, we ultimately achieve the highest rigorous Reasoning Success Rate across all evaluated settings.

</details>


### [183] [Signal-Noise Factorization Isolates Nuisance Variation into Removable Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00751)
*Sakin Kirti,Joel Zylberberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种强化表征几何属性的正则化器（信号-噪声分解 SNF 与信号-信号分解 SSF），发现 SNF 正则化能显著提升分类性能（尤其在分布外图像畸变场景下获益更大），并揭示其机制在于将噪声表征隔离到与类别信号正交的独立子空间，从而可被识别并投影剔除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期理论工作表明，深度神经网络表征几何中包含影响推理能力的基本属性：信号-噪声分解（SNF）和信号-信号分解（SSF）。作者希望建立强化这两类几何属性的正则化器，探究它们如何塑造表征几何，并理解其对计算机视觉（特别是生物医学、含分布外畸变）任务性能的影响机制。

Method: 构建分别强化 SNF 与 SSF 的正则化器，与 L2 正则化基线对比，在 CIFAR-100 分类任务以及经 MedMNIST-C 五种严重级别畸变处理的 BloodMNIST 数据集上训练并评估；随后分析不同正则化机制下的噪声子空间（nuisance subspace），在留出数据上估计由畸变主导的方向并将其从表征中投影剔除，以验证几何隔离带来的性能增益。

Result: 在 CIFAR-100 上，强化 SNF 提升模型性能，而强化 SSF 未带来提升。在 BloodMNIST（MedMNIST-C 五种严重级别）上，SNF 正则化带来更大的性能提升。分析表明，SNF 正则化模型将噪声表征在独立子空间中，与类别相关信号分离；据此估计并剔除畸变主导方向后，准确率获得显著提升。结论显示，隔离干扰变量与类别变量比维持类别变量的分解表征更重要。

Conclusion: 强制执行信号-噪声分解的正则化器能显著提升在推理时包含分布外图像畸变的计算机视觉任务性能；其有效性源于将干扰变量与类别变量隔离，而非维持类别变量内部的分解表征。这一发现对塑造表征以提升模型鲁棒性具有指导意义。

Abstract: Recent theoretical work identified fundamental properties of representation geometry that shape inference ability of deep neural networks. These include signal-noise factorization (SNF), the ability to segregate signal from noise, and signal-signal factorization (SSF), the ability to segregate task-specific and task-irrelevant signals. Here, we built regularizers that reinforce these two properties during training. We compared networks trained with these regularizers to $L_2$-regularized baseline networks on the CIFAR-100 classification task to understand how our regularizers shape representation geometry and impact performance on a well-known computer vision baseline. Enhancing SNF via regularization improved model performance but enhancing SSF did not. Motivated by biomedical applications, we investigated how our regularizers affected performance on the BloodMNIST dataset treated with MedMNIST-C corruptions at five severity levels, and found even larger performance gains using the SNF regularizer. To understand the mechanism by which SNF-regularization produces improved performance, we analyzed the nuisance subspaces across regularization regimes, finding that the SNF-regularized models represent noise in distinct subspaces, separate from class-relevant signal. Because this geometry is explicit, the dominant corruption-induced directions can be estimated on held-out data and projected out of the representations. This manipulation led to a substantial gain in accuracy. These results show that regularizers that enforce signal-noise factorization can produce substantial improvements on computer vision tasks that contain out-of-distribution image distortions at inference time. They also highlight how shaping representations affects model performance: isolating nuisance variables from categorical ones is more important than maintaining factorized representations of categorical variables.

</details>


### [184] [Benchmarking System One decision models against trained classifiers and language models for automated decision gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00346)
*Amir Rafe,Subasish Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了类型化决策模型（System One 模型）、监督分类器和生成式语言模型在自动化决策门控任务中的表现，并给出了随条件变化的设计规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件需要将分支决策交给模型，而模型必须能输出一个可声明的选项及其可被阈值化的概率。现有实现要么是监督分类器，要么是生成式语言模型，但都难以在低标签/无标签、风险控制、可靠性和成本等条件下满足需求，因此需要系统性地评估类型化决策模型作为替代方案的可行性与设计准则。

Method: 在匹配条件下，用一个测试框架向来自六个家族的八个决策模型检查点（含托管模型 Jev）和两个生成式对比模型发送相同的语义请求；同时在同一工作流、意图和社会科学条目上训练并评测监督分类器和零样本分类器。实验维度包括：有无标签、生成式模型的选项键似然读取、校准温度、5% 域内风险阈值下的域外接受率、yes/no 对调翻转率，以及意图训练的第一阶段升级到 Jev 的成本-精度权衡。

Result: 1) 模型类别排名依条件而定：有标签时，小型训练分类器在意图上最准，在工作流上与最佳决策模型无显著差异；无标签时，除基于编码器的检查点外，所有决策模型在工作流和意图上均超过零样本蕴含分类器。2) 通过选项键似然，更大的生成式模型在工作流和意图上与 Jev 持平，并在 5% 风险下接受更多工作流决策；微调后的决策检查点比未微调骨干在意图精度上有所提升。3) 在少量选项上拟合的存储温度在选项增多时校准误差上升；即便为 5% 域内风险设置留出阈值，Jev 仍接受 0.310 的域外请求。4) 对调 yes/no 使 Jev 每百个答案翻转 50.5 个，而微调检查点降低了其骨干在社会科学条目上的翻转率。5) 意图训练的第一阶段升级到 Jev 的系统，在满图形处理器利用率下以 Jev 的 0.43 成本匹配其精度。

Conclusion: 自动化决策门控的设计规则必须以任务条件（有无标签、风险容忍度、选项规模、成本约束）为依据：无标签场景偏好的决策模型、有标签场景的小型分类器、生成式模型的选项键似然读取，以及级联升级架构等均有其适应的条件区间。

Abstract: Software that hands branching decisions to a model needs a declared option and a probability it can threshold. Typed decision models, also called System One models, return such probabilities without generating text, while supervised classifiers and generative language models are the established alternatives. Under matched conditions, one harness sends eight decision-model checkpoints from six families, including the hosted model Jev, and two generative comparators the same semantic requests, and scores supervised and zero-shot classifiers on the same workflow, intent and social-science items. The ranking of the model classes depends on the conditions. With the task's own labels, small trained classifiers are the most accurate on intents and not significantly different from the best decision models on workflows. Without labels, every decision model except the encoder-based checkpoints exceeds a zero-shot entailment classifier on workflows and intents. Read through option-key likelihoods, a larger generative model is level with Jev on workflows and intents and accepts more workflow decisions at five percent risk, and fine-tuned decision checkpoints gain intent accuracy over their untuned backbones. Stored temperatures fitted on few options raise calibration error with many options, and a held-out threshold for five percent in-scope risk still lets Jev accept 0.310 of out-of-scope requests. Swapping yes and no flips 50.5 answers per hundred for Jev, while fine-tuned checkpoints cut their backbones' social-science flips. An intent-trained first stage escalating to Jev matches its accuracy at 0.43 of its cost at full graphics-processor utilization. The results yield condition-dependent design rules for automated decision gates.

</details>


### [185] [Scalable Multi-Task Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00758)
*Allen Tran,Jia Wan,Nathan Kallus,Aurélien Bibaut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多任务逆强化学习方法，在共享环境、不同奖励的多智能体数据上利用低秩假设进行联合学习，以缓解覆盖要求并实现新环境下多任务奖励恢复与策略学习的可扩展评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逆强化学习通过迁移奖励实现反事实评估，但迁移要求目标环境对智能体状态占有的充分覆盖。目标环境会影响状态占有，使得单一任务的覆盖条件严格且难以满足。作者希望借助多任务数据共享与低秩结构，缓解覆盖要求（各任务无需访问所有状态，只要其他任务覆盖即可），并降低多任务评估的计算开销。

Method: 提出一种多任务逆强化学习方法，在同一环境中汇集多个具有不同奖励的智能体的数据，并在奖励函数上施加低秩假设。利用数据的跨任务共享，使得单个任务即便未访问某些状态也能通过其他任务的数据完成奖励恢复。由于低秩结构，计算密集的规划复杂度随秩而非任务数量增长，从而实现多任务在目标环境下的可扩展评估。

Result: 提供了奖励恢复和新环境下策略学习的有限样本保证。实验表明，该方法在有限覆盖下具有鲁棒性，能够在各任务的支持集内及支持集外恢复奖励，迁移到目标环境时的遗憾低于基线方法，且随着任务数量增长，其相对逐任务方法的计算优势不断扩大。

Conclusion: 该多任务低秩逆强化学习方法通过跨任务数据共享有效缓解覆盖约束，并在保证奖励恢复与策略学习质量的同时实现计算可扩展性，在迁移评估与多任务规模增长场景下优于逐任务基线方法。

Abstract: By learning transferable rewards, inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) enables counterfactual evaluation of agents under modified environments. Such transfer places strict requirements on coverage since target environments affect agents' state occupancy. We propose a multi-task IRL method that pools data across multiple agents with different rewards in the same environment under a low-rank assumption. In addition to alleviating coverage requirements, so each task need not visit every state as long as others do, the method offers scalable evaluation of multiple tasks under new environments as computationally intensive planning scales with rank rather than the number of tasks. We provide finite sample guarantees on reward recovery and on policy learning in new environments. Experiments show our method is robust to limited coverage, recovers rewards on and off of each task's support, transfers to target environments at lower regret than baselines, with its computational advantage over per-task methods widening as tasks grow.

</details>


### [186] [Deep Learning for Anomaly Detection in Railway Systems: A Structured Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00363)
*Ammar Bouketta,Smail Niar,Hamza Ouarnoughi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了基于深度学习的铁路系统异常检测方法，提出统一分类框架，涵盖异常定位、数据表示、传感模态与时间特性，并梳理了分类、预测、重构及混合学习等范式，同时讨论数据挑战、评估实践、部署约束与决策导向框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代铁路系统的安全可靠运行日益依赖数据驱动的监测与智能故障检测；随着车辆与基础设施多源异构传感器数据的增长，深度学习成为铁路异常检测的有效范式，但现有方法庞杂、缺乏统一结构化的梳理与选型指导。

Method: 采用结构化综述方法，建立统一分类体系（涵盖异常位置、数据表示与表现形式、感知模态、时间特性）；将卷积、循环、注意力架构、自编码器、生成对抗网络和Transformer等方法归纳为基于分类、基于预测、基于重构及混合的四类学习范式；并分析数据挑战、评估指标与部署实践（边缘-云架构、计算约束、硬件感知优化），最终提出决策导向框架。

Result: 形成了一套将异常特性、数据属性与运行约束映射到合适检测范式与部署配置的决策导向框架，并系统梳理了各类深度学习方法在铁路异常检测中的适用场景。

Conclusion: 该综述为铁路异常检测深度学习方案的选型与部署提供了结构化参考，并指出了迈向可靠、可扩展智能监测系统所面临的开放性挑战。

Abstract: Ensuring safe and reliable operation of modern railway systems increasingly relies on data-driven monitoring and intelligent fault detection. Deep learning has emerged as an effective paradigm for railway anomaly detection, driven by the growing availability of heterogeneous sensor data from rolling stock and infrastructure. This paper presents a structured survey of deep learning-based anomaly detection approaches for railway systems. The surveyed methods are organized using a unified taxonomy covering anomaly location, data representation and manifestation, sensing modality, and temporal characteristics. Existing approaches, including convolutional, recurrent and attention-based architectures, autoencoders, generative adversarial networks, and transformers, are structured into classification-based, prediction-based, reconstruction-based, and hybrid learning paradigms. The survey also examines data-centric challenges, evaluation practices, performance metrics, and practical deployment aspects, including edge-cloud architectures, computational constraints, and hardware-aware optimization. Finally, a decision-oriented framework links anomaly characteristics, data properties, and operational constraints to suitable detection paradigms and deployment configurations. This work provides a structured reference for selecting and deploying deep learning solutions for railway anomaly detection and highlights open challenges toward reliable and scalable intelligent monitoring systems.

</details>


### [187] [Manifold-Constrained Initial Noise Optimization for Efficient Generative Model Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00365)
*Jinho Chang,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ZeNOVA，一种基于零阶优化的稳定高效初始噪声对齐方法，通过退火软值引导、流形约束的高斯超球面 Langevin 动力学和 Metropolis-Hastings 跳跃，在图像和视频生成模型上优于所有零阶基线，实现黑盒奖励对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒸馏和流图模型使确定性一步/少步生成成为可能，催生了对高斯分布初始噪声进行直接优化的奖励对齐方法；但现有方法多依赖一阶梯度信息，在黑盒奖励（无可微梯度）场景下不可用或不稳定、低效，因此需要一种无梯度的稳定高效初始噪声对齐方法。

Method: 提出 ZeNOVA：1) 退火软值引导，解决黑盒场景下现有算法的核心挑战；2) 流形约束的高斯超球面 Langevin 动力学，利用高斯先验的几何结构；3) Metropolis-Hastings 跳跃，保证采样稳定性与正确性。

Result: 在图像与视频生成模型上的大量实验表明，ZeNOVA 在将初始噪声优化至更高奖励方面，相比所有被评估的零阶基线更加稳定且显著更优，并验证了其对高斯先验几何的利用及对多种黑盒奖励对齐的实际适用性。

Conclusion: ZeNOVA 为黑盒奖励对齐提供了一种无需梯度、稳定且高效的初始噪声优化方案，展现出对图像与视频生成任务的广泛实用性，有望推动黑盒奖励场景下生成模型对齐的发展。

Abstract: Recent advances in distillation and flow-map models have enabled deterministic one- or few-step generation for high-quality data, facilitating a new branch of reward alignment approaches that directly optimize the initial noise from a Gaussian distribution. However, most existing initial-noise optimization methods rely on first-order gradient information, which is either inapplicable or suffers from instability and inefficiency in black-box reward scenarios. Here, we introduce ZeNOVA, a stable and efficient initial noise alignment method in a gradient-free manner. Specifically, we address existing algorithms' major challenge in black-box scenarios through annealed soft-value guidance, manifold-constrained hyperspherical Langevin dynamics, and Metropolis-Hastings jumping. Extensive experiments on image and video generative models show that ZeNOVA outperforms all evaluated zeroth-order baselines by optimizing the initial noise toward higher rewards substantially more stably while exploiting the geometry of the Gaussian prior, demonstrating its practical applicability to various black-box reward alignment.

</details>


### [188] [Platonic Task Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00929)
*Junghwan Park,Woojin Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“柏拉图任务向量”的假设，认为不同模型针对同一任务产生的参数更新是同一模型无关对象的“影子”，并设计“通用任务描述符”（Universal Task Descriptors）来让任务知识跨异构模型转移和组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相同任务的模型行为趋于一致，但产生这些行为的参数更新没有公共坐标系，导致权重空间的任务算术只能局限于单一模型，无法在缺乏结构对应的不同架构间迁移。作者希望刻画并利用一个共享的、模型无关的任务表示。

Method: 借鉴柏拉图的洞穴寓言，假设模型特定的更新是同一“柏拉图任务向量”的影子。针对图像/音频编码器与文本编码器配对的模型，引入通用任务描述符：一种与架构和嵌入维度无关的矩阵，记录任务的功能效应并支持矩阵形式的加法和取反。将描述符迁入目标模型即编辑目标，使其在任务的未标注探测图像与类别名提示上复现该描述符，无需逐图标签。实现方式有两种：其一，将描述符分解为图像嵌入上的位移场，通过一次最小二乘求解得到可折叠进目标模型最后一层的线性算子作为权重编辑，且因线性性可按带符号和组合任意强度；其二，在相同目标上训练低秩适配器，可到达所有层并联合拟合组合，代价是每次编辑需一次优化。

Result: 异构模型仅部分共享该对象，存在与共享分量范数相当、模型特定的残差，但跨模型迁移仍保留目标模型自身描述符增益的74%–80%。在六个模型族、八个分类任务以及一个音频-文本设置上的实验表明，两种实现下任务知识均能在异构模型间转移和组合。

Conclusion: 通过模型无关的通用任务描述符，任务知识可以在异构模型之间转移与组合，验证了“柏拉图任务向量”假设的可行性，并以两种实现路径（线性权重编辑与低秩适配器）落地。

Abstract: Models specialized for the same task converge to similar behavior, yet the parameter updates that produce it share no common coordinate system, so weight-space task arithmetic stays confined to a single model and cannot cross architectures without a structural correspondence. Drawing on Plato's allegory of the cave, we hypothesize that these model-specific updates are shadows of one shared, model-agnostic object, which we call the platonic task vector. To make it operational for models that pair an image or audio encoder with a text encoder, we introduce Universal Task Descriptors: matrices whose shape is independent of architecture and embedding dimension, which record a task's functional effect and support addition and negation as matrix operations. Transferring a descriptor into a target means editing the target until it reproduces the descriptor on the task's unlabeled probe images and class-name prompts, requiring no per-image labels. We realize this edit in two ways. First, the descriptor factorizes into a shift field on image embeddings, so a single least-squares solve yields a linear operator that folds into the target's last layer as a weight edit; by linearity, a bank of such operators admits any composition at any strength as a signed sum. Second, a low-rank adapter trained on the same objective reaches every layer and fits compositions jointly, at the cost of one optimization per edit. Heterogeneous models share this object only partially, with a model-specific residual comparable in norm to the shared component, yet cross-model transfer still retains 74-80 percent of the gain of the target's own descriptors. Experiments across six model families, eight classification tasks, and an audio-text setting show that task knowledge transfers and composes across heterogeneous models under both realizations.

</details>


### [189] [MoRA: MoE Pruning via Router Bias Learning and Expert Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00367)
*Yushuai Sun,Zikun Zhou,Lin Gao,Jun Yu,Wenjie Pei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MoRA，一种结构化 MoE 专家剪枝框架，通过可学习路由偏置与专家近似机制，在保证性能的同时有效压缩专家池，实验表明其优于现有剪枝算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 模型虽能通过稀疏激活降低每 token 计算量，但部署时仍需将完整专家池加载进内存。结构化专家剪枝可减少内存占用，但现有方法存在两大缺陷：专家排序准则与模型性能对齐不佳，或有效专家子集搜索计算代价过高；此外，普遍忽视了保留专家之间路由行为的冗余。作者旨在设计一种更高效、性能对齐且能进一步利用剩余专家补偿剪枝损失的剪枝框架。

Method: 提出 MoRA 框架，包含两部分：(1) 为每个专家引入可学习路由偏置，通过最小化语言建模损失与路由多样性正则项联合优化。学习到的偏置使路由概率分布更尖锐，从而识别对模型性能关键的专家，同时鼓励选择路由偏好多样的专家；(2) 引入剪枝后增强的专家近似机制，利用保留专家通过仿射变换近似被剪枝专家的输出，进一步提升剪枝后模型性能。

Result: 在 Qwen3-30B-A3B、DeepSeek-V2-Lite 和 Moonlight-16B-A3B 三个 MoE 模型上分别移除每层 25% 和 50% 的路由专家，在九个零样本基准上的广泛实验表明，MoRA 优于现有最优剪枝算法。

Conclusion: MoRA 通过可学习路由偏置和路由多样性正则化实现与性能对齐、计算高效的专家剪枝，同时结合专家近似机制补偿剪枝损失，在多个模型和基准上取得最优效果，证明了该框架对 MoE 内存压缩的有效性。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models enable parameter scaling with limited per-token computation by activating only a small subset of experts for each token, but deploying them still requires loading the complete expert pool into memory. Structured expert pruning can effectively reduce the memory usage by removing experts. However, existing pruning methods either use expert ranking criteria that are not well aligned with model performance or rely on effective expert subset searching that is computationally expensive. Moreover, these methods typically overlook the routing-behavior redundancy among the retained experts. In this paper, we propose MoE Pruning via Router Bias Learning and Expert Approximation (MoRA), a framework for structured MoE expert pruning. We introduce a learnable router bias for each expert and optimize these biases by minimizing the language-modeling loss and a routing-diversity regularizer. The learned router biases sharpen the routing probability distributions to identify experts critical to model performance while encouraging the selection of experts with diverse routing preferences. In addition, we introduce an expert approximation mechanism as a post-pruning enhancement. It leverages the remaining experts to approximate the outputs of pruned experts by affine transformation, further improving the performance of the pruned model. We evaluate MoRA on Qwen3-30B-A3B, DeepSeek-V2-Lite, and Moonlight-16B-A3B, removing 25\% and 50\% of the routed experts in each MoE layer. Extensive experiments on nine zero-shot benchmarks show that MoRA outperforms state-of-the-art pruning algorithms. Our code will be released.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [Weighted Data Selection: Sharp Upper-Half and Five-Dimensional Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00101)
*Zhongxuan Liu,Hongzhi Wang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文对有限加权最小二乘的最小范数学习器，证明了重加权训练支撑所保留风险量的精确定律 Γ_d(n)=3−n/d（在 ⌈3d/2⌉≤n≤2d−1 范围内），并通过下限构造与 Lean 4 形式化验证证明了其最优性；进一步在 (d,n)=(5,6) 的小预算情形下证明 Γ_5(6)=11/5，并与单纯形块预测吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在限定训练样本预算下，通过对训练支撑进行重加权（子集/权重选择）后，最小范数最小二乘学习器仍会保留多大的风险（即不可消除的剩余风险下界）。目标是给出给定维度 d 与样本数 n 之间风险的精确量化，并刻画达到该下界的支撑结构。

Method: 对有限加权最小二乘采用最小范数学习器；利用保持完整特征张成的选择（selection）覆盖所有观测特征秩；通过平衡单纯形锚点实现降维、正权重重提升与独立线压缩来收紧风险上界，并用平移坐标对构造匹配的下界；在 Lean 4 中形式化验证整个数据集级上界与锐性构造。对 (d,n)=(5,6) 情形，使用电路覆盖、二阶矩比较与电路平面概率刻画锐剩余量 6/5，借助极面几何处理共享秩三电路，并从 5=3+2 的单纯形块分解推导 Γ_5(6)=11/5。

Result: 证明了在 ⌈3d/2⌉≤n≤2d−1 全区间内精确定律 Γ_d(n)=3−n/d，且上下界匹配（最优）；在 (d,n)=(5,6) 时证明 Γ_5(6)=11/5，与任意交互构型上的单纯形块预测一致，锐剩余量为 6/5。完整的数据库级上界与锐性构造已在 Lean 4 中通过机器验证。

Conclusion: 在中等预算选择问题中，风险保留量由精确定律 Γ_d(n)=3−n/d 刻画，并由单纯形块前沿统一连接；该结果兼具最优性证明与形式化验证保证，为小样本重加权学习的最小风险提供了封闭形式的精确刻画。

Abstract: How much risk does a small reweighted training support retain? For finite weighted least squares with the minimum-norm learner, we prove the exact law $Γ_d(n)=3-n/d$ throughout $\lceil3d/2\rceil\leq n\leq2d-1$. The guarantee covers every observed feature rank and uses selections that preserve the full feature span. Balanced simplex anchors reduce dimension; positive-weight lifting and independent-line compression close the risk bound. Shifted coordinate pairs attain the matching lower bound. The complete dataset-level upper bound and sharpness construction are verified in Lean 4. At the smaller budget $(d,n)=(5,6)$, we also prove $Γ_5(6)=11/5$, matching the simplex-block prediction from $5=3+2$ over arbitrary interacting configurations. Circuit covers, comparison second moments, and circuit-plane probabilities give the sharp excess $6/5$, while polar-face geometry resolves shared rank-three circuits. The general simplex-block frontier connects these laws within the intermediate-budget selection problem.

</details>


### [191] [Transferable Graph Metanetworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00420)
*Yuxin Ma,Adir Dayan,Yam Eitan,Haggai Maron,Soledad Villar*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出可迁移图元网络（Transferable Graph Metanetworks），通过引入不变性与连续性原则改造图元网络，使其对不同宽度的输入神经网络具有尺寸泛化能力，并在μP参数化下实现了最多42倍训练宽度的稳健泛化，同时给出理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有权重空间网络多在同一或少数固定尺寸的网络上训练评估，难以泛化到更大尺寸的输入网络，导致“小网络训练、大网络评估”的效率优势未被实现。作者希望实现元网络跨输入网络宽度的尺寸泛化。

Method: 提出可迁移图元网络，在传统图元网络范式中加入一组修改，遵循两条原则：一是对“不同宽度网络表示同一函数的不同方式”保持不变性；二是连续性，使表示相似函数的权重获得相似预测。此外研究了输入网络从随机初始化独立训练时尺寸泛化的可行性。

Result: 修改显著提升了所有测试任务上的尺寸泛化性能。在最大更新参数化（μP）训练的输入网络上表现最强，泛化稳定保持到训练宽度的42倍。理论上利用无限宽度极限理论证明了模型在μP输入上的尺寸泛化保证，并解释了在其他参数化下失败的原因。

Conclusion: 通过不变性与连续性原则的改造，图元网络能够实现跨宽度的尺寸泛化，尤其在μP参数化下表现出色并具有理论保证，初步释放了小网络训练、大网络评估的效率潜力。

Abstract: A weight space network (or metanetwork) takes the weights of another neural network as input and predicts properties of it. Most prior work trains such models on input networks of one or a few fixed sizes and evaluates them in-distribution. The few attempts at out-of-distribution size generalization remain limited in scope and have achieved only modest success. Consequently, the potential efficiency gains of training on small networks and evaluating on much larger ones remain largely unrealized. We propose Transferable Graph Metanetworks, which extend the graph metanetwork paradigm with a set of modifications that make performance transferable across input networks of different widths. The modifications follow two principles: invariance to the ways in which networks of different widths represent the same function, and continuity, such that weights representing similar functions receive similar predictions. We further study whether size generalization is possible for input networks trained independently from random initialization. Empirically, our modifications significantly improve size generalization on every task we consider. Performance is strongest on input networks trained under the maximal-update parameterization ($μ$P), where it remains robust up to $42\times$ the training width. Theoretically, we explain these observations with infinite-width limit theory: we prove size-generalization guarantees for our model on $μ$P-trained inputs, and explain why it can fail under other parameterizations.

</details>


### [192] [Learning to Cover Locally: Graph Neural Combinatorial Optimization under a Hard Information Horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00422)
*Johannes F. Loevenich,Thies Moehlenhof,Laurin Holz,Maxime Schwarzer,Tobias Huerten,Roberto Rigolin F. Lopes*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究硬信息视界（horizon）下的组合优化：每个节点仅依据自身 k 跳邻域决定其全局解的份额，且这些承诺必须组合成全局可行解。作者以 OLSRv2 的加权多点中继（MPR）选择（NP 难的 2 跳覆盖问题）为实例，从理论和实验两方面证明：信息视界（而非模型容量）才是决定性能的最关键变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经组合优化默认采用能读取整个实例的集中式求解器，但许多真实分布式场景（如路由协议）中，每个节点只能看到局部信息，即存在硬性信息视界。作者希望探究在此约束下，局部决策如何组合成全局可行解，以及信息视界对求解质量的根本性限制。

Method: 将问题形式化为"局部集合覆盖"，并以 OLSRv2（RFC 7181）的加权 MPR 选择为具体实例（其 2 跳视界由协议本身强制规定，而非建模者自选）。理论层面：证明任何视界少一跳的确定性选择器要么覆盖失败、要么偏离最优达 Δ 倍；并证明在决定节点读取的 L 层 GNN 恰好等价于 L 跳选择器（容量无法弥补半径）；而深度 O(Δ) 的 GNN 可复现 RFC 7181 覆盖贪心，宽度 O(c_max·Δ) 时可复现其度量感知加权版本，两者均继承 (1+ln Δ₂) 近似比。实验层面：使用带覆盖补全解码器的 3 层 GATv2，从 CP-SAT 最优解进行行为克隆，并与单跳受限版本、贪心基线、真实网络迁移实验对比。

Result: 理论上确立了信息视界与 GNN 深度/宽度的精确对应关系，以及视界不足时的下界。实验中，3 层 GATv2 达到 cost/opt = 1.030±0.001（贪心为 1.138），在 100% 覆盖下弥合 79.1% 的差距；而将同一学习器限制为单跳（相同实例、解码器和示范）后劣化至 1.344，远差于贪心。迁移检验中：OLSRv2 未修改的选择代码在 200/200 个单位代价实例上与基数贪心一致；在 40,308 个真实营级机动实例上，冻结模型在完全覆盖下弥合 48% 的差距。

Conclusion: 信息视界（而非模型容量）是决定神经组合优化求解质量的最重要变量：在协议强制视界内，GNN 能以多项式深度/宽度精确复现并继承经典贪心的近似保证，并通过学习进一步逼近最优；但一旦视界缩短一跳，任何方法（无论容量多大）都会遭受根本性下界损失。

Abstract: Neural combinatorial optimization typically assumes a centralized solver that reads the whole instance. We study the opposite: combinatorial optimization under a hard information horizon, where every node commits to its share of a global solution seeing only its $k$-hop neighborhood, and those commitments must compose into a globally feasible solution. We formalize this as local set cover and instantiate it on weighted multipoint relay (MPR) selection, the NP-hard 2-hop covering problem of the Optimized Link State Routing Protocol version 2 (OLSRv2) routing protocol (RFC~7181), whose horizon is imposed by the protocol, not chosen by the modeler. We prove two results. Any deterministic selector whose horizon is one hop short must either fail coverage or land a factor $Δ$ from optimal, and an $L$-layer graph neural network (GNN) read out at the deciding node is exactly an $L$-hop selector, so capacity cannot buy back radius. Conversely, at the horizon a \ac{GNN} of depth $O(Δ)$ reproduces the RFC~7181 covering greedy, and at width $O(c_{\max}Δ)$ its metric-aware weighted analogue, inheriting the $(1+\lnΔ_2)$-approximation in both cases. Empirically, a 3-layer \ac{GATv2} with a coverage-completing decoder, behavior-cloned from the CP-SAT optimum, reaches $\text{cost}/\text{opt}=1.030\pm0.001$ against greedy's $1.138$, closing $79.1\%$ of the gap at $100\%$ coverage. Restricting the same learner to one hop, on identical instances with the same decoder and demonstrations, collapses it to $1.344$, far worse than greedy. Two transfer checks target real-world networks. OLSRv2's unmodified selection code matches our cardinality greedy on $200/200$ unit-cost instances, and on $40{,}308$ instances of real battalion mobility the frozen model closes $48\%$ of the gap at full coverage. The information horizon, not the model capacity, is the most significant variable.

</details>


### [193] [Target-Dependent Limits of Causal Repair: A Leading-Log Frontier in a Gaussian Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00424)
*Qinchuan Cheng,Jiaqi Liu,Ruixuan Xie*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文在标量高斯因果实验中，将"因果预测器潜在改进的估计"与"实际习得修复的收益评估"区分开来，刻画了学习与评估联合优化下的评估误差下界及前沿指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 即使知道一个因果预测器能带来多大潜在改善，也未必能揭示实际习得的"修复"所带来的收益。此前缺少对这种"潜在改进可估"与"实现修复实际增益可估"之间差距的量化刻画。

Method: 在已知干预几何的标量高斯因果实验中，辅助数据以有界污染的方式识别效应量，诊断信息用于识别方向。评估目标为已实现训练修复相对拟合参考的平方损失增益；在均匀学习MSE η约束下联合优化学习器与评估器，排除"不做任何修复"的平凡解。进一步刻画大信息量（magnitude-rich）区间的尖前导对数前沿，并用诊断-弃权规则在未知讨厌参数下达到该指数；再通过精确高斯模拟将该前沿迁移至自适应采样。

Result: 在通常的1/k学习尺度上，即使oracle潜势能以更快速率估计，任何可行学习器仍存在k^{-2}的评估下界。在magnitude-rich区间，评估指数达到min{ℓ_k, 2kη_k/U}（到一阶相对阶），其中ℓ_k=log(1/(k²E_k))，E_k为辅助精度。诊断-弃权规则在未知讨厌参数下达到该指数。有限网格实验区分了符号尾部抑制与总MSE，并暴露了有限预算下的保守行为。

Conclusion: 该结果表明评估目标如何改变此类实验中的信息需求，属于这一特定实验的刻画，而非一般性的因果可识别性声明。

Abstract: Knowing how much a causal predictor could improve need not reveal the gain of the repair actually learned. We quantify this gap in a scalar Gaussian causal experiment with known intervention geometry: auxiliary data identify effect magnitude up to bounded contamination, while diagnostics identify direction. The target is the squared-loss gain of the realized trained repair relative to a fitted reference. Jointly optimizing the learner and assessor under uniform learning MSE $η$ avoids the trivial solution of making no repair. At the usual $1/k$ learning scale, every feasible learner incurs a $k^{-2}$ assessment floor, even when oracle potential is estimable at a faster rate. In the magnitude-rich regime, we characterize a sharp leading-log frontier: the assessment exponent is $\min{\ell_k,2kη_k/U}$ to first relative order, where $\ell_k=\log(1/(k^2E_k))$ and $E_k$ is auxiliary precision. A diagnostic-abstention rule attains this exponent with unknown nuisance parameters. We also bound the critical allowance window and transfer the frontier to adaptive sampling by exact Gaussian simulation. Finite-grid experiments distinguish sign-tail suppression from total MSE and expose conservative finite-budget behavior. The result isolates how the assessment target changes information requirements in this experiment; it is not a general causal identifiability claim.

</details>


### [194] [ChainLoRA: Geometry-Preserving Task Vector Merging for Continual Learning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00431)
*Hang Yin,Haozhe Wang,Yuhua Luo,Zhangqi Pan,Xiaoxing Wang,Junchi Yan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: ChainLoRA 是一种无回放的持续参数高效微调框架，通过链式更新的任务向量几何，结合训练阶段的单侧正交性代理与合并阶段的自适应 SVD 合并（含 Procrustes 对齐），在参数预算受限下有效平衡知识保留与新任务适应，在多个基准上取得最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的持续参数高效微调必须在“保留旧知识、适应新任务、严格参数预算”三者之间取得平衡。现有方法要么依赖回放（存储旧样本，成本高），要么在无回放条件下出现灾难性遗忘或参数利用效率低下。作者希望从参数合并的几何视角出发，提出一种无需回放、且能随任务流增长保持恒定开销的持续微调方案。

Method: 提出 ChainLoRA 框架，核心包含两部分：(1) 链式更新训练——将遗忘问题形式化为任务更新之间可度量的几何交互，区分“方向重叠”与“系数耦合”；训练时仅依赖上一个载体（carrier）进行初始化并施加单侧正交性代理，使历史状态足迹与正则化开销随任务流增长保持恒定；(2) 流后自适应 SVD 合并——合并阶段用 Adaptive SVD 提取共享载体，并通过 Procrustes 对齐（Procrustes adaptation）将其对准最新任务，理论上实现共享分量与任务特定分量的几何近似分离；单侧代理进一步约束任务间干扰，有效秩惩罚促进持续学习中任务子空间的高效利用。

Result: 实验表明，ChainLoRA 在 Large 与 SuperNI 两个基准上，于所评估的无回放方法中取得最先进性能；在 Standard CL 上保持竞争力；并且在全部三个基准上，其平均分数几乎最接近所评估的回放方法。

Conclusion: ChainLoRA 从参数合并的几何视角刻画遗忘，通过链式更新训练与自适应 SVD 合并（含 Procrustes 对齐、单侧正交性代理、有效秩惩罚），在不依赖回放的情况下实现了旧知识保留与新任务适应的良好平衡，且计算/存储开销不随任务流线性增长，验证了几何分离视角对持续微调的有效性。

Abstract: Continual parameter-efficient fine-tuning for large language models (LLMs) must balance retention of previously acquired knowledge, adaptation to new tasks, and strict parameter budgets. We present \textbf{ChainLoRA}, a replay-free continual merging framework built on chain-updated task-vector geometry. From a parameter-merging perspective, we formulate a geometric view of forgetting through a measurable interaction between task updates, separating directional overlap from coefficient coupling. Building on this view, ChainLoRA combines chain-updated training with post-stream adaptive SVD merging. During training, initialization and a one-sided orthogonality proxy use only the last carrier, keeping their historical-state footprint and regularization overhead constant as the task stream grows. At merging time, Adaptive SVD extracts a shared carrier and aligns it to the latest task through Procrustes adaptation. Our theoretical analysis shows that Procrustes adaptation facilitates geometric approximate separation of shared and task-specific components. The one-sided proxy further bounds inter-task interference. An effective-rank penalty additionally promotes efficient utilization of the task subspace during continual learning. Experiments show that ChainLoRA achieves state-of-the-art performance among the evaluated replay-free methods on the Large and SuperNI benchmarks, while remaining competitive on Standard CL and attaining almost the closest average scores to the evaluated replay-based method across all three benchmarks.

</details>


### [195] [Fractional Laplace Neural Operators: Exact Architectures, an Expressivity Frontier at Criticality, and Certified Stability for Memory-Driven Network Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00515)
*Mauricio Herrera-Marín*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出分数阶拉普拉斯神经算子（fLNO），将Volterra预解式的非有理拉普拉斯符号结构嵌入可学习的函数空间映射中，兼顾精确记忆结构、物理坐标与稳定性保证，并具有竞争性精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子学习函数空间间的映射，而遗传网络动力学由具有非有理拉普拉斯符号的Volterra预解式描述。现有有限有理实现无法复现由分支点产生的非整数临界渐近行为，亟需一种能嵌入精确记忆结构与分数阶结构的算子学习架构。

Method: 作者提出分数阶拉普拉斯神经算子（fLNO），对可交换的激励-拉普拉斯对，用一个块图-谱层精确表示完整线性Volterra解算子；建立有限有理实现的可表达性前沿（在紧频率窗口内几何逼近分数记忆、半直线上最优有理速率为根指数）；推导可训练的、由构造强制规定稳定裕度的参数化方法；并用图子极限（graphon）迁移定理区分真正的算子一致性与参数共享。

Result: 在通用数据基准上，正有理算子在有限时域内可达到或超过fLNO精度；在受控的近临界实验中，fLNO以远少参数更忠实地恢复分支坐标；无约束有理拟合可跨越稳定边界，而经认证的参数化不能。四参数谱律无需重训练即可从48节点图迁移到192节点图，相对误差0.51–0.62%。应用于智利余震序列及智利与21个意大利地区的更新模型，展示了结构化推断与显式不确定性。

Conclusion: 本文贡献是一种算子学习架构，在该架构中，精确的记忆结构、物理坐标与稳定性保证可与具有竞争力的精度共存，为具备分数记忆的遗传动力学提供结构化、可迁移且稳定保证的学习方案。

Abstract: Neural operators learn maps between function spaces, while hereditary network dynamics are described by Volterra resolvents with non-rational Laplace symbols. We introduce a fractional Laplace neural operator (fLNO) that embeds this structure in the learned map. For commuting excitation--Laplacian pairs, one block graph-spectral layer represents the full linear Volterra solution operator exactly. We establish an expressivity frontier for finite rational realizations: they approximate fractional memory geometrically on compact frequency windows, but cannot reproduce the non-integer critical asymptotics generated by a branch point, and on the half-line the best rational rate is root-exponential. The same theory yields trainable parametrizations that enforce a prescribed stability margin by construction, and a graphon-transfer theorem separates genuine operator consistency from parameter sharing. In a common-data benchmark, positive rational operators can match or exceed fLNO accuracy on finite horizons, whereas in controlled near-critical experiments fLNO recovers the branching coordinate more faithfully with far fewer parameters; unconstrained rational fits can cross the stability boundary, while certified parametrizations cannot. A four-parameter spectral law transfers without retraining from graphs of size 48 to 192 with 0.51--0.62% relative error. Applications to Chilean aftershock sequences and to renewal models for Chile and 21 Italian regions illustrate structured inference with explicit uncertainty. The contribution is an operator-learning architecture in which exact memory structure, physical coordinates and stability guarantees coexist with competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [196] [Adaptive Conformal Prediction for Image Regression Models with Application to an Inertial Confinement Fusion Emulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00535)
*Carrie J. Lei-Cramer,Michael S. Jones,Laura J. Wendelberger*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出ACPNN，一种基于最近邻的输入自适应共形预测框架，用于图像回归任务，通过高斯过程及ARD核学习的缩放距离度量定义邻域，实现局部自适应、计算成本低的预测不确定性量化，并在惯性约束聚变扩散模型仿真上验证了其可靠性与自适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学机器学习中，黑盒图像模型越来越多地被用于高风险决策场景，但大多数方法只提供点估计而缺乏预测不确定性的量化；同时模型内部结构难以访问和解释，难以评估输入空间不同区域的可靠性。因此迫切需要能提供随输入变化（input-dependent）不确定性估计的方法，以指导模型开发和下游实验。

Method: 提出 Adaptive Conformal Prediction using Nearest Neighbors (ACPNN)，一种输入自适应的共形预测框架，专用于图像回归。其利用邻近样本的信息生成局部自适应的不确定性估计，并保持较低的计算开销；邻域结构通过一种缩放距离度量来定义，该度量由带自动相关性确定（ARD）核的高斯过程学习得到。

Result: 在用于模拟惯性约束聚变（ICF）的扩散模型上验证了ACPNN的有效性，结果表明该方法能够实现可靠且自适应的不确定性量化。

Conclusion: ACPNN为图像回归提供了一种输入自适应、低计算成本且可靠的预测不确定性量化方案，能够有效支持科学机器学习中的模型开发和下游实验决策。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification is critical in scientific machine learning, where black-box, image-based models are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings. In many such applications, model outputs inform costly decisions, yet most methods provide only point estimates without quantifying predictive uncertainty. This challenge is compounded by the limited accessibility and interpretability of model internals, making it difficult to assess reliability across different regions of the input space. As a result, there is a growing need for methods that can provide input-dependent uncertainty estimates to guide both model development and downstream experimentation. To address this need, we propose Adaptive Conformal Prediction using Nearest Neighbors (ACPNN), an input-adaptive conformal framework for image regression. ACPNN leverages information from neighboring samples to produce locally adaptive uncertainty estimates while maintaining low computational cost. The neighborhood structure is defined using a scaled distance metric learned via a Gaussian Process with an automatic relevance determination (ARD) kernel. We demonstrate the effectiveness of ACPNN on a diffusion model for emulating inertial confinement fusion (ICF) simulations, showing that it achieves reliable and adaptive uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [197] [Learning to Price Electricity for Optimal Demand Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00755)
*Jing Shang,Mohammad Mehrabi,Xinyang Zhou,Mahmoud Saleh,Andrey Bernstein,Stefan Wager*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于神经网络的上下文能源定价算法，将定价建模为Stackelberg博弈，利用平均场解表示，学习从上下文特征到可行价格信号的约束映射，能更有效地利用天气、日出日落、星期模式等丰富信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时变电力价格可用于引导消费者需求响应，使能源需求更好地匹配可再生能源供给。但最优价格随时间变化，依赖天气预测、日出日落时间、星期模式等复杂信号，现有方法无法高效利用这类丰富的上下文信息。

Method: 提出基于神经网络的上下文能源定价算法：将定价建模为Stackelberg博弈，借助Mehrabi等（2024）的平均场解表示，学习从上下文特征到可行价格信号的受约束映射。

Result: 在多个美国城市的能源电网模拟中验证了方法，结果显示引入上下文信息能显著提高需求响应项目的价值。

Conclusion: 通过神经网络与平均场解的约束映射，能有效利用复杂上下文信号进行最优定价，从而提升需求响应项目的经济价值。
