Abstract: There is considerable interest in using time-varying electricity prices to shape consumer demand response, and better align energy demand with renewable production. However, optimal prices generally vary over time in response to complex signals such as weather forecasts, sunrise/sunset times, and day-of-week patterns; and existing methods are not able to make efficient use of such rich contextual information. Here, we propose a neural-network-based algorithm for contextual energy pricing, modeling pricing as a Stackelberg game and leveraging a mean-field solution representation from Mehrabi et al.~(2024). The approach learns constrained mappings from contextual features to feasible price signals. We validate our approach by simulating the energy grid in several US cities, and show that incorporating contextual information can considerably increase the value of the demand response programs.

</details>


### [198] [Inference for stochastic differential equations driven by weighted sub-fractional Brownian motion using neural networks and the Euler approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00793)
*J. H. Ramirez-Gonzalez*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究在固定观测时域 T 上、由加权次分数布朗族高斯过程驱动的随机微分方程中，漂移系数、扩散系数与噪声协方差（含协方差指数与时间权重）的估计问题。作者基于欧拉近似重建高斯驱动增量，利用联合密度构造轨迹似然，并用神经网络与径向基函数表示漂移、扩散及归一化时间权重，通过似然剖面估计协方差指数与扩散尺度，在二十种系数设定下与两种神经网络方法对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的随机微分方程参数估计大多针对标准布朗运动驱动，而现实中许多噪声过程仍具长期依赖与非马尔可夫性质。加权次分数布朗族通过引入时间权重 f 与协方差指数 β，能更为灵活地刻画具有变异性与长记忆性的驱动噪声，但相应的漂移、扩散与噪声协方差的联合估计（尤其在同一短时域、单条轨迹、已知初值条件下）仍缺乏系统且可计算的方法，因此需要构建相应的统计估计框架。

Method: 作者针对 dX_t=a(X_t)dt+σ(X_t)dZ_t^{β,f}（0≤t≤T，已知 x_0）这一模型，先利用欧拉近似由观测到的状态转移重建高斯驱动增量，再利用这些增量的联合密度构造轨迹似然函数；漂移系数、扩散系数及归一化时间权重分别用神经网络和径向基函数进行参数化表示，而协方差指数 β 与扩散尺度 σ 则通过似然剖面（profile likelihood）进行估计。最后在二十种系数设定下，将所提方法与两种基于神经网络的对比方法在同一批仿真轨迹上进行数值比较。

Result: 该方法能够在离散观测下同时估计漂移、扩散与噪声协方差（含协方差指数与时间权重），并通过数值实验在二十种系数设定下与两种神经网络替代方法进行了对比验证，展示了所提似然估计框架的性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于欧拉近似与轨迹似然的估计框架，能够有效处理由加权次分数布朗族高斯过程驱动的随机微分方程，并在多种系数设定下表现出与现神经网络方法可比或更优的性能，为具有长记忆与变异性噪声的非马尔可夫随机系统提供了一种可计算的联合估计途径。

Abstract: We consider the estimation of drift, diffusion, and noise covariance from discrete observations of stochastic differential equations driven by Gaussian processes. For a fixed observation horizon $T>0$ and a known initial state $x_0\in\mathbb R$, we study \begin{equation*}
  dX_t=a(X_t)\,dt+σ(X_t)\,dZ_t^{β,f},
  \qquad X_0=x_0,\quad 0\leq t\leq T. \end{equation*} \smallskip\noindent Here $a:\mathbb R\to\mathbb R$ is the drift coefficient, $σ:\mathbb R\to(0,\infty)$ is the diffusion coefficient, and $Z^{β,f}$ is a centered Gaussian process from the weighted sub-fractional Brownian family, with covariance \begin{equation*}
  \operatorname{Cov}(Z_s^{β,f},Z_t^{β,f})
  =\int_0^{s\wedge t} f(r)q_β(s-r,t-r)\,dr,
  \qquad 0\leq s,t\leq T. \end{equation*} \smallskip\noindent Here $s\wedge t=\min\{s,t\}$. The temporal weight $f:[0,T]\to[0,\infty)$ is measurable, bounded, and positive almost everywhere, and $β\in(0,2)$ is the covariance exponent. For $u,v\geq0$, the kernel is $q_β(u,v)=[u^β+v^β-(u+v)^β]/(1-β)$ when $β\ne1$. Its continuous extension at $β=1$ is $q_1(u,v)=(u+v)\log(u+v)-u\log u-v\log v$, with $0\log0=0$.
  Using the Euler approximation, we reconstruct the Gaussian driving increments from observed transitions and use their joint density to obtain a trajectory likelihood. Neural and radial-basis representations model the drift, diffusion, and normalized temporal weight, while a likelihood profile estimates the covariance exponent and diffusion scale. We compare the method with two neural alternatives on the same simulated trajectories in twenty coefficient settings.

</details>


### [199] [Block Optimism for Nonstationary Bandits with Latent Linear Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00911)
*Taehyun Hwang,Hyunjun Choi,Heesang Ann,Min-hwan Oh*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究带有潜在线性动态的内生非平稳随机赌博机问题，通过自适应块级乐观性方法将遗憾界从已有的 $\tilde{O}(T^{2/3})$ 提升至 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$，是该模型下首个达到 $\sqrt{T}$ 量级遗憾保证的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在该模型中，动作同时影响即时奖励和未观测潜在状态的未来演化，奖励关于动作与潜在状态是双线性的，从而产生历史依赖的奖励与非平凡的长期规划问题。已有 explore-then-commit 方法通过均匀探索估计潜在动态、再承诺一个优化的开环动作序列，只能达到 $\tilde{O}(T^{2/3})$ 遗憾。作者希望改进这一速率。

Method: 提出自适应块级乐观性（adaptive block-level optimism）方法。关键步骤是循环近似（cyclic approximation）：在稳定动态下，无限记忆的奖励过程可以被截断，开环基准可通过优化一个有限记忆的块级代理来近似。基于这一归约，作者设计了一种基于 UCB 的块算法，为截断动态参数维护置信集，并乐观地选择动作块。

Result: 证明了 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ 量级的遗憾界，相比同一模型下此前的 $\tilde{O}(T^{2/3})$ 保证有显著改进。作者称这是具有双线性奖励观测与开环动作序列基准的潜在线性动态赌博机中首个 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ 遗憾保证。

Conclusion: 通过循环近似将无限记忆过程截断为有限记忆块级代理，并采用块级 UCB 乐观性，可将遗憾从 $\tilde{O}(T^{2/3})$ 提升至 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$，达到该类潜在动态赌博机问题的最优量级（首个 $\sqrt{T}$ 保证）。

Abstract: We study an endogenous nonstationary stochastic bandit problem with latent linear dynamics, where actions affect both immediate rewards and the future evolution of an unobserved latent state. Rewards are bilinear in the current action and latent state, inducing history-dependent rewards and a nontrivial long-horizon planning problem. The existing explore-then-commit approach achieves $\tilde{O}(T^{2/3})$ regret by uniformly exploring to estimate the latent dynamics and then committing to an optimized open-loop action sequence. We show that this rate can be improved via adaptive block-level optimism. Our key step is a cyclic approximation: under stable dynamics, the infinite-memory reward process can be truncated, and the open-loop benchmark can be approximated by optimizing a finite-memory block-level proxy. Building on this reduction, we propose a UCB-based block algorithm that maintains confidence sets for the truncated dynamics parameters and selects blocks optimistically. We prove a regret bound of order $\tilde{O}(\sqrt T)$, significantly improving over the previous $\tilde{O}(T^{2/3})$ guarantee for the same model. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first $\tilde{O}(\sqrt T)$ regret guarantee for latent linear-dynamics bandits with bilinear reward observations and an open-loop action-sequence benchmark.

</details>


### [200] [The Price of Correlated Tests: How Strict Should a Model Release Gate Be?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00993)
*Marco Pollanen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文把机器学习模型发布前的测试门槛建模为一个设计问题：通过选择模型必须通过的测试数量，使放行模型达到既定的可靠性目标，同时尽可能保留更多优质模型，并给出可验证的认证方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型上线前通常要通过一整套自动化测试。要求全部通过看似安全，但会误杀许多本可服务好用户的模型，且并未说明通过测试的模型究竟有多可信。因此需要一种既满足可靠性目标、又不牺牲过多好模型的放行门槛设计方法。

Method: 采用两类潜因子（latent-factor）模型，将两类成本显式化，并把每种计算化约为一维积分。证明当两类共享相同的潜相关时，更严格的门槛总能提高可靠性，因此保留最多好模型的门槛是仍能满足可靠性目标的最宽松门槛。在“全通过”门槛下，模型内可实现任何未达完美的可靠性目标，但随测试套件规模增大，保留下来的好模型占比趋于零，测试之间的相关性决定了代价。此外，给出基于精确二项界限的验证流程，即便门槛从固定候选清单中选出，也能用标注数据对其认证。

Result: 在某种配置下，99%的可靠性目标仅需8个独立测试即可达到；但当潜相关为0.3时需要74个测试，潜相关为0.5时需5,182个测试，此时门槛保留下来的好模型不足十分之一。

Conclusion: 发布门槛应在可靠性与保留好模型数量之间权衡：最宽松且仍达标的门槛最优；测试相关性极大抬高达标成本，门槛设计需考虑测试相关性；同时通过精确二项界限可在有标注数据时对门槛进行统计认证。

Abstract: Before a machine learning model ships, it often has to pass a suite of automated tests. Requiring every test to pass looks safe, yet it can reject many models that would have served users well, and it does not say how trustworthy a passing model actually is. We treat the release gate as a design problem: choose how many tests a model must pass so that cleared models meet a stated reliability target, while keeping as many good models as possible. A two-class latent-factor model makes both costs explicit and reduces each calculation to a one-dimensional integral. We prove that when both classes share the same latent correlation, a stricter gate always raises reliability, so the gate that keeps the most good models is the most lenient one that still meets the target. Under pass-all gating, any reliability target short of perfection is attainable within the model, but the share of good models kept tends to zero as the suite grows. Correlation between tests sets the price. In one configuration, a 99 percent target needs 8 independent tests, but 74 tests at a latent correlation of 0.3 and 5,182 at 0.5, where the gate keeps fewer than one good model in ten. We also give a validation procedure, built on exact binomial bounds, that certifies a gate from labelled data even when the gate is chosen from a fixed shortlist.

</details>


### [201] [Tolerance-Based Fairness Auditing: Violation Certification and Sensitivity Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01005)
*Jie Tang,Chuanlong Xie,Lixing Zhu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的、基于容差阈值的算法公平性审计框架，针对“违规认证”与“敏感性筛查”两类互补目标分别设计经验似然检验方法，以实现对群体差异是否超出可容忍限度的统计判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI广泛部署，算法不公平问题引发对透明度与公平性审计的强烈需求；而实际中可容忍的不公平程度取决于具体法律、伦理或应用情境，因此在给定容差阈值下，如何判定群体差异是否越过允许界限成为关键统计问题。

Method: 作者构建统一框架覆盖两个互补审计目标：(1)违规认证——开发约束经验似然检验，采用最小不利点（least-favorable-point）校准，并可结合虚假标记率（false flagging rate）控制用于多子群同时审计；(2)敏感性筛查——开发分裂经验似然与调整分裂经验似然检验，利用自适应边界代理（adaptive boundary-proxy）原则，用于预警场景。

Result: 数值实验展示两类程序在错误控制与敏感性之间呈现不同的权衡取舍；COMPAS数据集分析验证了该框架在预测性公平性审计中的可行性。

Conclusion: 所提统一容差审计框架能有效区分并服务于“控制虚假违规声明”与“减少漏报违规”两种目标，为特定阈值下的算法公平性审计提供了形式化的统计工具，并通过实例验证其实际应用价值。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence is increasingly deployed, algorithmic unfairness has raised growing concerns and intensified demands for transparent fairness auditing. In practice, the tolerable degree of algorithmic unfairness depends on the specific legal, ethical, or application context. Given a prespecified tolerance threshold, an important statistical question is how to determine whether a group disparity exceeds the allowable tolerance across different auditing objectives. To address this problem, we develop a unified tolerance-based fairness auditing framework for two complementary auditing objectives: violation certification, which prioritizes control of false violation declarations, and sensitivity screening, which prioritizes reducing missed violations. For the first objective, we develop a constrained empirical likelihood test for formal settings that uses least-favorable-point calibration and can be combined with false flagging rate control for simultaneous subgroup auditing. For the second objective, we develop split empirical likelihood and adjusted split empirical likelihood tests using an adaptive boundary-proxy principle for early-warning settings. Numerical experiments show the distinct error-control--sensitivity trade-offs of these procedures. A COMPAS analysis illustrates the framework in predictive fairness auditing.

</details>


### [202] [Posterior sampling by source-space MCMC via prior-based few-step transport maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01034)
*Hoang Phuc Hau Luu,Marcelo Hartmann,Zhongjian Wang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种源空间广义贝叶斯推断框架：用一/少步改进的 MeanFlow (iMF) 映射表示隐式先验，并在其高斯源空间进行后验采样，同时给出 Wasserstein 误差界，结合并行回火与混合 split HMC 提升采样效率，并在 CLIP 引导的图像生成任务中得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代贝叶斯推断越来越多地使用信息丰富但仅以样本形式隐含表示的先验（如历史集合、模拟器输出、预训练生成模型）；同样的问题也出现在测试时引导（广义贝叶斯）任务中，即用显式正权重（如指数化奖励）对隐式先验进行倾斜。直接对这些隐式先验做后验采样计算困难，因此需要高效且稳定的采样框架。

Method: 本文提出源空间（source-space）广义贝叶斯推断框架：1）用一或几步改进的 MeanFlow (iMF) 映射表示先验，将其变换到简单的高斯源空间；2）在该源空间中进行后验采样；3）建立精确后验与学习后验之间的 Wasserstein 误差界，将其分解为训练次优误差与模型类逼近误差（基于联合总体 iMF 与辅助速度损失）；4）在源空间中采用带预条件 Crank-Nicolson 更新的并行回火（parallel tempering），并引入混合 split Hamiltonian Monte Carlo 变体以提升采样效率。

Result: 合成实验表明，该框架能准确且高效地逼近后验分布；CLIP 引导的 ImageNet 实验表明，该方法能够将预训练的 iMF 图像先验引导至符合文本指定偏好的方向。

Conclusion: 所提出的源空间广义贝叶斯推断框架通过 iMF 先验传输与理论误差保证，实现了对隐式先验的高效、稳定后验采样，并在合成任务与 CLIP 引导的图像生成任务中验证了其准确性和有效性。

Abstract: Bayesian inference increasingly uses informative but implicit priors represented only by samples, such as historical ensembles, simulator outputs, and pretrained generative models. The same computational problem appears in the test-time guidance task (generalized Bayes), where an explicit positive weight, e.g., an exponentiated reward, tilts an implicit prior. We develop a framework for source-space generalized Bayesian inference that combines inexpensive few-step prior transports with posterior stability guarantees. Specifically, we represent the prior using a one- or few-step improved MeanFlow (iMF) map and perform posterior sampling in its Gaussian source space. We establish Wasserstein error bounds between the exact and learned posteriors in terms of the joint population iMF and auxiliary-velocity loss, decomposed into training suboptimality and model-class approximation error. In the iMF source space, we adopt parallel tempering with preconditioned Crank-Nicolson updates and introduce a hybrid variant that incorporates split Hamiltonian Monte Carlo to improve sampling efficiency. Synthetic experiments show that the proposed framework can approximate posterior distributions accurately and efficiently, while CLIP-guided ImageNet experiments demonstrate its ability to steer a pretrained iMF image prior toward text-specified preferences.

</details>


### [203] [Optimal Transport Meets Reinforcement Learning: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01413)
*Yujie Zhu,Charles A. Hepburn,Matthew Thorpe,Giovanni Montana*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该文是一篇综述，系统梳理了最优传输（OT）在强化学习（RL）目标函数与算法中的应用，涵盖方法分类、动机、实际考量与开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL 算法常需比较概率分布（如策略诱导的状态访问分布、专家数据分布、动作分布、转移分布等），但常用散度在分布重叠较弱时失效（常见于模仿学习、离线 RL 和分布偏移下的部署）。最优传输通过“移动概率质量”并编码任务几何的地面成本，提供了更有效的替代度量。

Method: 本文对使用 OT 的 RL 方法进行系统综述，对每种方法明确：OT 扮演的角色、被比较的分布、采用的 OT 公式化形式、以及对时序结构的处理方式；并在此基础上进行分类。

Result: 建立了现有 OT-RL 方法的分类体系，辨析了不同 OT 选择背后的动机，总结了成本设计、计算复杂度等实践考量，并指出了若干开放问题：可扩展的轨迹级传输、质量不匹配的原则性处理、以及 OT 正则化 RL 的理论分析。

Conclusion: 最优传输为处理 RL 中弱重叠分布比较提供了有力工具，但轨迹级传输的可扩展性、质量不匹配处理及理论保证仍是未来研究的关键方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms frequently compare probability distributions, such as state visitation distributions induced by policies and experts, action distributions from learned policies and offline datasets, or transition distributions from learned models and environments. However, commonly used divergences may become ineffective when these distributions overlap weakly, which is frequently encountered in imitation learning, offline RL, and deployment under distribution shift. Optimal transport (OT) offers an alternative by measuring the cost of \emph{moving} probability mass from one distribution to another under a ground cost that encodes task geometry. This survey covers how OT is used inside RL objectives and algorithms. For each method, we identify: the role OT plays, the distributions compared, the OT formulation used, and the treatment of temporal structure. Beyond categorising existing methods, we discuss the motivations behind different OT choices, practical considerations such as cost design and computational challenges, and highlight open problems including scalable trajectory-level transport, principled handling of mass mismatch, and theoretical analysis for OT-regularised RL.

</details>


### [204] [Zero Flux: Flow-Based Comparison of High-Dimensional Discrete Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01472)
*Leyang Wang,Yakun Wang,Song Liu,Taiji Suzuki*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 Zero Flux 准则，将连续域中基于流匹配（flow matching）训练向量场、并以其在中点是否消失来判定两分布是否相同的思路，推广到高维离散分布。该准则基于局部概率通量，在独立耦合（independent coupling）下，中点处所有局部概率通量消失当且仅当两分布相同，并可将联合分布差异分解为局部贡献、支持从样本高效估计，且给出了有限样本误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较高维离散分布一直很困难：状态空间随维度指数增长，且变量间交互结构变化复杂。近期工作提出在连续分布间用流匹配训练向量场，并以中点处向量场是否消失作为两分布相同的充要判据，但这一基于流的准则不能直接用于离散分布。

Method: 将连续域的流匹配判据推广到离散域，提出基于局部概率通量的 Zero Flux 准则；在独立耦合条件下证明：中点处所有局部概率通量消失当且仅当两分布相同；将该差异分解为更小的局部贡献，并可从样本中高效估计，同时建立估计器的有限样本误差界。

Result: 在合成数据与真实分类数据上的实验表明，该方法能可靠恢复稀疏依赖信号，并在高维情形下稳定追踪分布漂移。

Conclusion: Zero Flux 准则为高维离散分布比较提供了一种可分解、可高效估计且有理论误差保证的差异度量，在稀疏依赖检测与分布漂移追踪等任务中表现可靠稳定。

Abstract: Comparing two high-dimensional discrete distributions has always been a challenging task due to the exponentially growing state space and complex changes in interactions. A recent work suggests comparing distributions through a vector field trained using flow matching between two continuous distributions. The resulting vector field at mid-point vanishes if and only if two distributions identical. However, such a flow-based criterion does not naturally apply to discrete distributions. We extend this principle to the discrete domain and introduce the \emph{Zero Flux} criterion, a discrepancy based on local probability fluxes. Under independent coupling, we show that all local probability fluxes vanish at the midpoint if and only if two distributions are the same. This discrepancy decomposes the joint distributional difference into smaller, local contributions and can be efficiently estimated from samples. We establish finite sample error bounds for our estimator. Experiments on synthetic and real categorical data demonstrate reliable recovery of sparse dependence signals and stable tracking of distribution shifts in high dimensions.

</details>


### [205] [The hidden advantage of mask resampling: a theory of masked autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01578)
*Jorge Medina Moreira,Lorenzo Bardone,Lenka Zdeborová*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过高维线性模型从理论上解释了掩码预测（masked prediction）为何能学到非掩码重建（等价于 PCA）学不到的有用表示，并揭示了掩码重采样（mask diversity）带来的统计优势，进而通过移除标准图像增强（随机裁剪、翻转）的实验验证了动态掩码相对于静态掩码的下游优势，同时在 BERT 上也观察到了掩码多样性的收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：为什么掩码预测能学到无掩码重建（等价于 PCA）无法学到的有用表示？作者希望在高维、含共享潜在结构且噪声异质的数据模型下，从理论上定量刻画掩码线性重建为何、以及在何种条件下能在线性样本复杂度内恢复潜在特征，同时厘清"掩码预测目标"本身与"掩码多样性"各自的作用。

Method: 作者建立了一个掩码自编码器（MAE）的高维模型，数据具有共享潜在结构和异质噪声，分析掩码线性重建的样本复杂度下界，证明其在 PCA 失效的机制下仍能以线性样本量恢复潜在特征。理论中引入每个样本固定的 K 个掩码集合，刻画掩码重采样对特征恢复与下游性能的影响，并识别出掩码多样性越高样本复杂度越低的机制。在此基础上，用标准的 CNN 自编码器和视觉 Transformer 做实验，对比移除/保留随机裁剪与翻转前后的动态掩码与静态掩码效果，并辅以 BERT 语言任务试点。

Result: 理论上证明，在无掩码线性重建（PCA）失败的参数区间，掩码线性重建仍能以线性样本复杂度恢复潜在特征；更大的掩码多样性可降低样本复杂度。实验发现，标准图像训练中的随机裁剪和翻转会通过"即使补丁掩码固定也持续更新预测任务"的方式掩盖掩码重采样的优势；移除这些变换后，动态掩码在 CNN 自编码器和 ViT 上均展现出相对静态掩码的下游优势，BERT 试点也显示更大掩码多样性对下游语言任务有益。

Conclusion: 该工作分离了掩码预测目标本身的收益与掩码多样性的收益，表明掩码预测通过利用带有异质噪声的共享潜在结构而优于 PCA，掩码多样性进一步降低样本复杂度；同时证明可处理的数学理论能够指导实验设计，从而揭示被标准训练实践（如随机裁剪翻转）所掩盖的掩码多样性优势。

Abstract: Why can masked prediction learn useful representations that unmasked reconstruction misses? We study this question in a high-dimensional model of a masked autoencoder (MAE) trained on data with shared latent structure and heterogeneous noise. We prove that masked linear reconstruction can recover the latent feature at linear sample complexity in regimes where unmasked linear reconstruction, equivalent to PCA, fails. The analysis also quantifies the statistical advantage of mask resampling, an established ingredient of masked pretraining. By introducing a fixed collection of $K$ masks per sample, we characterize its effect on feature recovery and downstream performance, identifying regimes where greater mask diversity lowers sample complexity. Guided by this prediction, we find that random cropping and flipping in standard image-training pipelines can obscure the advantage of mask resampling by renewing the prediction task even when the patch mask is fixed. Removing these transformations reveals a downstream advantage for dynamic over static masking in CNN autoencoders and vision transformers. A complementary BERT pilot finds benefits from greater mask diversity on downstream language tasks. Our results separate the benefit of the masked prediction objective from that of mask diversity, and show how a tractable theory can guide experiments that uncover advantages hidden by standard training practices.

</details>


### [206] [Error-Corrected Inference-Time Scaling for Imperfect Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01933)
*Zuokai Wen,Louis Grenioux,Weinan E,Jiequn Han*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出能量基Feynman-Kac校正器（EBFKC），无需额外训练即可在推理时校正不完美扩散模型的采样误差，使之精确逼近目标分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时缩放方法依赖蒙特卡洛采样（增加粒子数），但其前提是预训练扩散模型是精确的。实际上模型因数据和训练限制而存在误差，单纯增加粒子只能降低蒙特卡洛误差，无法消除终点与目标分布的不匹配，也无法消除追踪预设概率路径时的误差。

Method: 作者针对能量基扩散模型提出EBFKC框架：首先推导Feynman-Kac动力学，即便模型不完美也能在连续时间群体极限下精确追踪预设路径；再用带方差控制引导的序列蒙特卡洛近似这些动力学。为消除终点不匹配，利用预训练能量作为扩散路径上的代理，并逐步融合已学能量与目标终端能量之间的差异。

Result: 在混合高斯模型、粒子系统、丙氨酸二肽和丙氨酸四肽上的实验表明，在退火和奖励倾斜条件下，该方法能紧密匹配目标分布及分子自由能剖面，而标准推理时缩放基线仍保留显著采样误差。

Conclusion: EBFKC能够在推理时、不额外训练地校正不完美能量基扩散模型的误差，比单纯增加粒子的推理时缩放基线采样更准确。

Abstract: Inference-time scaling adapts pretrained diffusion models to new sampling tasks without additional training. Existing methods rely primarily on Monte Carlo sampling with more particles, yet are premised on the pretrained model being exact. In practice, data and training limitations make the model imperfect, and these methods inherit its error. More particles reduce Monte Carlo error but cannot remove the mismatch between the endpoint and the desired target or the error in tracking the prescribed probability path. We introduce the Energy-based Feynman-Kac Corrector (EBFKC), a framework for energy-based diffusion models that corrects these errors on the fly given a reference energy. We first derive Feynman-Kac dynamics that track a prescribed path exactly in the continuous-time population limit even when the model is imperfect, and approximate these dynamics using sequential Monte Carlo with variance-controlling guidance. To remove the endpoint mismatch, we use the pretrained energy as a surrogate along the diffusion path and progressively incorporate the discrepancy between the learned and target terminal energies. Experiments on Gaussian mixture models, particle systems, alanine dipeptide, and alanine tetrapeptide show that our method closely matches target distributions and molecular free-energy profiles under annealing and reward tilting, whereas standard inference-time scaling baselines retain substantial sampling errors.

</details>


### [207] [Wasserstein Gradient Flows and Forward-Only Diffusion Are Not Enough for Multimodal Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02081)
*Daniel McBride,Pratik Khandagale,Cristina Garcia-Cardona,Yen Ting Lin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文论证基于Wasserstein梯度流（WGF）与纯前向扩散过程（FODP）的采样算法，其指数收敛保证被普遍误读为能高效采样复杂多模分布；作者通过JKO格式与Otto微积分证明这两类方法的密度演化等价，因此同样受亚稳态与慢混合制约，在良分离多模情形下混合时间随谱隙缩小而指数增长，且对数线性退火无法消除该指数标度，揭示这是局部梯度输运机制的结构性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量WGF与FODP采样算法以指数收敛的理论保证为依据，宣称能高效采样复杂多模态分布，但这一解读缺乏对多模情形下实际收敛速率的严格审视。作者旨在纠正当下对该类方法高效性的普遍误读，澄清理论保证与实际多模采样能力之间的根本差距。

Method: 作者运用Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto（JKO）格式与Otto微积分，证明标准WGF采样动力学与过阻尼前向扩散具有相同的密度演化，从而继承其亚稳态与慢混合行为；进而采用谱分析与平均首达时间（MFPT）两种互补工具分析该采样族，考察良分离多模下的谱隙与模态间跃迁；并对常用的对数线性退火调度进行分析，检验引入中间分布能否改变总输运时间的指数标度。

Result: 结果表明，标准WGF与过阻尼前向扩散密度演化等价，良分离多模会导致谱隙极小、模态间跃迁罕见，混合时间呈指数增长；所研究对数线性退火调度引入中间分布后，总输运时间仍保持指数标度，无法解除该限制。该局限源于纯粹局部、梯度驱动的输运机制本身，属结构性而非实现细节问题。

Conclusion: 作者认为这是标准WGF与FODP采样在高效处理多模分布上的根本局限，并呼吁未来开发本质非局部的输运机制以实现高效多模态采样。

Abstract: There has been a proliferation of sampling algorithms based on Wasserstein gradient flows (WGF) and forward-only diffusion processes (FODP), often accompanied by theoretical guarantees of exponentially fast convergence to the target distribution. These guarantees are frequently interpreted as evidence that such methods can efficiently sample complex multimodal distributions, often supported by empirical results. In this work, we argue that this interpretation is fundamentally misleading. By invoking the Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto (JKO) scheme and Otto calculus, we establish that the canonical WGF sampling dynamics and overdamped forward diffusion share the same density evolution and therefore inherit the same metastability and slow-mixing phenomena long understood in nonequilibrium statistical physics. We analyze this family of samplers using two complementary tools -- spectral analysis and mean first-passage time (MFPT) analysis -- and show that well-separated multimodality can induce exponentially long mixing times associated with small spectral gaps and rare inter-mode transitions. For the commonly adopted log-linear annealing schedule studied here, we find that introducing intermediate distributions does not remove the exponential scaling of the total transport time. The limitation is structural rather than implementation-specific: purely local, gradient-driven transport mechanisms can require exponentially long times to transport probability mass across well-separated modes. We argue that this represents a fundamental limitation of WGF- and FODP-based sampling in their standard forms, and motivates future development of fundamentally nonlocal mechanisms for efficient multimodal sampling.

</details>
